### [1] [Mellin Moments of Proton Unpolarized GPDs at Nonzero Skewness from Lattice QCD with a Neural-Network Parametrization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29726)
*Min-Huan Chu,Manuel Colaço,Krzysztof Cichy,Martha Constantinou,Arif Sarkar*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 利用格点量子色动力学（lattice QCD）结合短距离展开与多项式性约束，通过人工神经网络拟合提取非极化同位旋矢量质子广义形状因子，并得到夸克总角动量 $J^{u-d}=0.296(11)(7)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非零偏度（skewness）下的质子广义部分子分布（GPDs）及其梅林矩（Mellin moments）所对应的广义形状因子（GFFs）缺乏格点第一性原理的确定，而其对理解质子角动量与碰撞参数分布具有重要意义。

Method: 方法上结合梅林矩在偏度 $\xi$ 上的多项式性（polynomiality）与短距离展开（short-distance expansion）框架，将格点提取的约化 Ioffe 时间分布（ITDs）用 GFFs 表示；每个 GFF 以不变动量转移平方 $t$ 为自变量的连续函数形式用人工神经网络（ANN）参数化后进行拟合。

Result: 确定了进入前四个梅林矩的十四个 GFFs（包括依赖偏度的 GFFs），并得到同位旋矢量组合的夸克总角动量 $J^{u-d}=0.296(11)(7)$（$\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$ 方案，$\mu=2$ GeV），与基于非局域算符的先前格点结果一致且总不确定度更小；同时给出非极化与横向极化质子中前三阶梅林矩的碰撞参数分布。

Conclusion: 研究识别并讨论了该提取过程中的主要系统不确定度，以及非零偏度区间特有的挑战，为后续非零偏度 GPD 研究提供了方法论基础与定量参考。

Abstract: We use lattice QCD to determine the generalized form factors (GFFs) entering the first four Mellin moments of the unpolarized isovector $u-d$ proton GPDs at nonzero skewness. The framework combines the polynomiality of the moments in the skewness $ξ$, with the short-distance expansion formalism, allowing the lattice-extracted reduced Ioffe-time distributions (ITDs) to be expressed in terms of GFFs. In order to extract the GFFs by fitting these relations to the lattice data, we parameterize each GFF with an artificial neural network (ANN) as a continuous function of the invariant momentum transfer squared $t$, which accommodates the kinematic coverage of the data in a flexible way. With this fit, we determine the fourteen GFFs that enter the first four Mellin moments, including the skewness-dependent GFFs. From the extracted GFFs, we obtain the quark total angular momentum of the isovector combination, $J^{u-d}=0.296(11)(7)$ in the $\overline{\mathrm{MS}}$ scheme at $μ=2~\mathrm{GeV}$, in agreement with previous lattice determinations based on nonlocal operators and with a smaller total uncertainty. We also determine the impact-parameter distributions of the first three Mellin moments in an unpolarized and in a transversely polarized proton. We finally identify and discuss the principal systematic uncertainties which enter this extraction, and the challenges specific to the nonzero-skewness regime.

</details>

### [2] [Sharp Quantitative Convergence of Compactly Supported Aggregation-Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29000)
*Yunhong Deng*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文在一维情形下证明了具有次线性吸引核（sublinear attraction kernel）与高于二次的退化扩散（degenerate diffusion）的非线性聚集-扩散方程（nonlinear aggregation-diffusion equations）的定量收敛性，即紧支撑密度以指数速率收敛到唯一稳态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此类模型的稳态已被已知为唯一、紧支撑且径向递减的概率分布，但解向该稳态的收敛速率缺乏定量刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 在一维设定下，针对紧支撑密度（compactly supported densities）进行研究，结合次线性吸引核与高于二次的退化扩散条件进行分析。

Result: 证明了解以指数速度（exponentially fast）收敛到唯一稳态。

Conclusion: 在一维、次线性吸引核与高于二次退化扩散的条件下，聚集-扩散方程的解具有向唯一紧支撑稳态的指数收敛性。

Abstract: We establish the quantitative convergence of nonlinear aggregation-diffusion equations in dimension one, with sublinear attraction kernel and degenerate diffusion higher than quadratic. In these models, steady state is known to be the unique, compactly supported, and radial decreasing probability distribution. In this paper, we work with compactly supported densities in dimension one, we prove these solutions converge to the steady state exponentially fast.

</details>


### [3] [A spinor-adapted geometric approach to nonlinear Dirac systems on the Schwarzschild black hole background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29406)
*Seokchang Hong*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 本文将旋量适配几何方法从Minkowski时空小扰动推广至Schwarzschild背景，建立旋量场的局部能量衰减估计与加权能量层级，并证明半线性张量波—Dirac模型及Lorenz规范下Maxwell—Dirac系统的全局小数据稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有旋量适配几何方法仅适用于Minkowski时空小扰动，需检验其在黑洞背景（Schwarzschild）上的有效性；同时Einstein—Dirac方程的波规范约化引出张量波—Dirac耦合模型，其一阶Dirac与二阶张量波耦合存在表观导数损失问题。

Method: 将Dafermos–Rodnianski物理空间方法适配到Dirac情形，建立积分局部能量衰减估计（integrated local energy decay）与r^p加权能量层级（energy hierarchy）；构造旋量适配能量层级，结合Dirac流能量、旋量波能量、Kosmann李导数与Clifford代数零结构（null structure）闭合非线性估计，且不依赖最高阶Dirac流估计。

Result: 证明了Schwarzschild背景下半线性张量波—Dirac模型的全局小数据稳定性，且无需对张量波方程附加独立零条件（null condition）；并在零电荷假设下、Lorenz规范中证明Maxwell—Dirac系统的全局小数据稳定性。

Conclusion: 旋量结构在黑洞背景上仍本质必要，非线性闭合根本依赖Dirac方程内蕴结构；旋量适配物理空间方法可推广至真实规范耦合的Dirac系统。

Abstract: Building on our previous work, we extend the spinor-adapted geometric method for nonlinear Dirac systems from small perturbations of Minkowski spacetime to the Schwarzschild background. As a linear foundation, we establish integrated local energy decay estimates and an \(r^p\)-weighted energy hierarchy for spinor fields, adapting the physical-space approach of Dafermos and Rodnianski to the Dirac setting.
  The main focus of the paper is nonlinear. We consider a semilinear tensorial wave--Dirac model motivated by the wave-gauge reduction of the Einstein--Dirac system and prove global small-data stability. A principal difficulty is the apparent loss of derivatives arising from the coupling between the first-order Dirac equation and the second-order tensorial wave equation. We overcome this difficulty through a spinor-adapted energy hierarchy that exploits the complementary roles of the Dirac-current energy and the spinorial wave energy. In particular, the nonlinear estimates are closed without requiring the top-order Dirac-current estimate.
  The nonlinear analysis demonstrates that the spinorial structures remain essential on the black-hole background. The combination of Dirac-current energy, spinorial wave energy, Kosmann Lie derivatives, and the Clifford-algebraic null structure enables us to close the nonlinear estimates without imposing an independent null condition on the tensorial wave equation. Thus, the nonlinear closure relies fundamentally on structures intrinsic to the Dirac equation.
  A second main result is the global small-data stability of the Maxwell--Dirac system in the Lorenz gauge on the Schwarzschild background, under a vanishing-charge assumption. This extends the nonlinear stability theory to a genuine gauge-coupled Dirac system and demonstrates the applicability of the spinor-adapted physical-space approach beyond the tensorial wave--Dirac model.

</details>


### [4] [Maximal velocity estimates for Hardy operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29513)
*Atsuhide Ishida,Konstantin Merz*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文证明含次临界 Hardy 位势（Hardy potential）的分数阶拉普拉斯算子（fractional Laplacian）的最大速度估计（maximal velocity estimates），并据此重新证明带 Coulomb 衰减（Coulomb decay）的长程扰动下波算子（wave operator）不存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数阶拉普拉斯算子加上次临界 Hardy 位势所构成的 Hardy 算子（Hardy operator），其位势强度与长程扰动的相互作用会影响波算子（wave operator）的可定义性。本文旨在建立最大速度估计，并为长程扰动下波算子不存在性提供新的证明路径。

Method: 论文先证明含次临界 Hardy 位势、且可能含外部位势（external potentials）的分数阶拉普拉斯算子的最大速度估计。随后将所得估计作为工具，应用于研究带与不带长程扰动的 Hardy 算子之间的波算子交织（intertwining）问题。

Result: 论文得到了含次临界 Hardy 位势的分数阶拉普拉斯算子的最大速度估计。作为应用，给出了一个关于波算子不存在性的新证明：该波算子用于将 Hardy 算子与带 Coulomb 衰减长程扰动的对应算子相相互交织。

Conclusion: 最大速度估计是研究分数阶 Hardy 算子（fractional Hardy operator）散射性质（scattering properties）的有效工具。Coulomb 衰减型长程扰动下波算子的不存在性，可由该估计以不同于既有工作的方式得到。摘要未给出定量常数、条件细节或适用维度范围。

Abstract: We prove maximal velocity estimates for the fractional Laplacian with an added subcritical Hardy potential, called Hardy operator, and possibly external potentials. As an application, we give another proof of the nonexistence of the wave operator intertwining Hardy operators with and without long-range perturbations with Coulomb decay.

</details>

### [5] [Poisson Clustering and Seed Effect of PBHs: Implications for Microlensing, Gravitational Waves, Little Red Dots and Ultra-Faint Dwarf Galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28723)
*Bernard Carr,Sebastien Clesse,Juan Garcia-Bellido,Michael Hawkins,Florian Kuhnel*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该文综合泊松效应（Poisson effect）、种子效应（seed effect）、动力学加热、潮汐瓦解与碰撞三种机制，考察扩展质量函数下原初黑洞（primordial black holes, PBHs）团簇的形成与存续，并据此重新评估微引力透镜与引力波对PBH暗物质占比的限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PBH的存在会通过种子效应与泊松效应显著影响宇宙结构形成；扩展质量函数可能导致较小质量的暗物质维里化束缚黑洞团簇及更大质量的中心黑洞，因此需厘清这些效应如何影响现有暗物质占比限制。

Method: 文中结合三种效应：泊松与种子涨落引起的不可避的小尺度非均匀性引力束缚；约10⁶ M⊙以下所有团簇因动力学加热（dynamical heating）而被稀释；团簇在外银河晕（如朝麦哲伦云方向）存活、而在中心晕区因潮汐瓦解（tidal disruption）与碰撞被破坏。据此分析对微引力透镜巡天与引力波事件给出的PBH暗物质占比限制的影响。

Result: 具有预期质量与半径的PBH团簇不太可能放宽微引力透镜限制，但银河系自转曲线的下降或许可以，从而允许大量暗物质为在量子色动力学（quantum chromodynamics, QCD）相变时期形成的太阳质量PBH；此外，团簇内形成的双星可主导并合率，若PBH构成暗物质，则可能与LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA的引力波限制相容。

Conclusion: 超暗弱矮星系（Ultra-Faint Dwarf Galaxies）与小红点（Little Red Dots）可能由种子效应与泊松效应共同产生；若原初扰动具有指数非高斯尾（exponential non-Gaussian tails），团簇程度将超过泊松预言，从而为太阳质量PBH解释暗物质开辟新可能。

Abstract: The existence of primordial black holes (PBHs) could have significant effects on the formation of cosmic structures through both the seed effect and the Poisson effect. For an extended PBH mass function, this should lead to dark virialised bound clusters of black holes with modest mass, together with central black holes of larger mass. We consider the combination of three effects: the inevitable gravitational binding of small-scale inhomogeneities due to Poisson and seed fluctuations; the dilution through dynamical heating of all clusters smaller than around $10^{6}\,M_{\odot}$; the survival of clusters in the outer Galactic halo (i.e. towards the Magellanic Clouds) but their destruction in the central halo region through tidal disruption and collisions. We examine the implications of this for the constraints on the fraction of dark matter in PBHs from microlensing surveys and gravitational-wave events. Although the clustering of PBHs with the expected mass and radius is unlikely to relax the microlensing limits, the decline of the Galactic rotation curve might do so, allowing much of the dark matter to be in solar-mass PBHs formed at the quantum chromodynamics transition. We also emphasise that binary merger rates must be revised: binaries formed in PBH clusters can dominate the merger rate, which may be compatible with the LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA gravitational-wave limits if PBHs provide the dark matter. More positively, Ultra-Faint Dwarf Galaxies and Little Red Dots might arise through a combination of the seed and Poisson effects. Our conclusions could be modified for PBHs produced from primordial fluctuations with exponential non-Gaussian tails, since the clustering would then exceed the Poissonian prediction, opening up new possibilities to explain dark matter with solar-mass PBHs.

</details>


### [6] [Cosmological Gravitational Wave Collider Physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29885)
*Sukannya Bhattacharya,Matteo Fasiello,Anish Ghoshal,Gianmassimo Tasinato*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 在有效场论框架下研究无质量引力子与带立方自相互作用的大质量自旋二场σ_ij螺旋度二模式的混合，给出张量功率谱修正与树级双谱的新解析表达式；在m_σ>3H/2区域发现含对数振荡(k_L/k_S)^{3/2±iμ}的非解析挤压贡献，并导出短尺度张量功率谱的四极调制及其与模型参数的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原初张量非高斯性（primordial tensor non-Gaussianity）可用于探测暴胀期间存在的大质量粒子；本文旨在考察大质量自旋二场（massive spin-two field）在此探测中的作用。

Method: 采用有效场论（effective field theory）方法，处理无质量引力子与大质量自旋二场σ_ij螺旋度二模式（helicity-two mode）的混合，并引入立方自相互作用（cubic self-interactions）；在声速（sound speed）恒定的情形下计算领先阶张量功率谱修正与树级双谱（tree-level bispectrum），并进一步分析缓慢变化的声速对挤压信号（squeezed signal）的影响。

Result: 得到张量功率谱修正与树级双谱的新型解析表达式；在m_σ>3H/2区域发现非解析挤压贡献，呈对数振荡，形式为(k_L/k_S)^{3/2±iμ}，其中μ=√(m_σ²/H²−9/4)，振荡频率由质量m_σ确定，而不同引力子螺旋度构型（graviton helicity configurations）的相对振幅由极化收缩（polarisation contractions）决定；同一混合机制亦对功率谱与双谱产生修正，并给出短尺度张量功率谱的四极调制（quadrupolar modulation）及其振幅与模型参数的关系。

Conclusion: 结果将大质量自旋二场在宇宙学引力波对撞机（cosmological gravitational wave collider）中的两点、三点及四极张量信号联系起来，并表明缓慢变化的声速会改变挤压信号。

Abstract: Primordial tensor non-Gaussianity can probe massive particles present during inflation. We investigate this possibility in an effective field theory where the massless graviton mixes with the helicity-two mode of an additional massive spin-two field $σ_{ij}$ with cubic self-interactions. For constant sound speed, we compute the leading tensor power-spectrum correction and the tree-level bispectrum, finding novel analytical expressions for these quantities. In the $m_σ>3H/2$ regime, we find a nonanalytic squeezed contribution that exhibits logarithmic oscillations with characteristic dependence $(k_L/k_S)^{3/2\pm iμ}$, where $μ=\sqrt{m_σ^2/H^2-9/4}$. The oscillation frequency is set by the mass $m_σ$, while the polarisation contractions determine the relative amplitudes of the different graviton helicity configurations. The same mixing generates corrections to the power spectrum and the bispectrum. We derive the associated quadrupolar modulation of short-scale tensor power spectrum and relate its amplitude to the model parameters. Finally, we explore how a slowly varying sound speed modifies the squeezed signal.~These results connect the two-point, three-point, and quadrupolar tensor signatures of massive spin-two fields in the cosmological gravitational wave collider.

</details>


### [7] [Parity-odd CMB Correlations induced by a Chiral Gravitational Wave Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29182)
*Baoyu Xu,Yun-Long Zhang*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该论文提出手征引力波背景（chiral gravitational wave background, GWB）的弱透镜效应（weak lensing effects）可作为在宇宙微波背景（cosmic microwave background, CMB）中产生宇称奇（parity-odd）关联的新机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索手征引力波背景是否能够在CMB中产生宇称奇关联，并将其作为观测手征GWB的潜在探针，因为此类背景可由多种理论情形产生。

Method: 方法上同时考虑光子偏折（photon deflection）与偏振旋转（polarization rotation），推导由此产生的宇称奇EB与TB角功率谱（angular power spectra）。

Result: 针对代表性的GWB能量密度谱（energy density spectra），研究发现部分参数选择能很好地拟合现有观测测量结果。

Conclusion: 结论是手征GWB的弱透镜效应为手征GWB提供了一种潜在的观测探针，该机制可适用于广泛的理論情形。

Abstract: We investigate weak lensing effects of a chiral gravitational wave background (GWB) as a new mechanism for generating parity-odd correlations in the cosmic microwave background (CMB). Accounting for both photon deflection and polarization rotation, we derive the resulting parity-odd EB and TB angular power spectra. For representative GWB energy density spectra, we find parameter choices that provide good fits to existing measurements. This mechanism provides a potential observational probe of chiral GWB, which can arise in a wide range of theoretical scenarios.

</details>


### [8] [Magnetic monopoles produced by first-order phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29349)
*Yuefeng Di,Ligong Bian,Rong-Gen Cai*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该工作首次对一阶相变（first-order phase transition）中的磁单极子（magnetic monopole）产生进行三维格点模拟（3D lattice simulation），涵盖湮灭阶段与膨胀宇宙中的标度演化（scaling regime），并给出磁场与引力波（gravitational wave）功率谱及单极子质量约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前缺乏对相变产生磁单极子的三维格点模拟，未能定量刻画初始气泡（bubble）数量与残余单极子丰度（abundance）的关系，也未评估单极子动力学产生的磁场与引力波谱，故难以判断其能否解释原初宇宙磁场（primordial cosmic magnetic field）及宇宙学约束下的质量范围。

Method: 采用 Press-Ryden-Spergel 方法进行三维格点模拟，捕捉磁单极子在一阶相变中的产生、随后的湮灭过程以及膨胀宇宙中标度区的进入；据此定量建立初始气泡数与残余单极子丰度之间的关系，并计算单极子动力学源生的磁场与引力波功率谱（power spectrum）。

Result: 磁场谱在单极子尺度 w_M 之外呈陡峭的 k^{-5} 衰减；引力波谱的峰值对应于平均单极子间距 d_M，其低波数侧与高波数侧分别遵循 k^3 与 k^{-3} 标度。所得磁场强度被耀变体（blazar）观测排除，故磁单极子不能作为原初宇宙磁场的起源。在 Parker 约束下 m_M≲2.18×10^11 GeV，在暗物质（dark matter）丰度约束下 m_M≲1.58×10^12 GeV。

Conclusion: 磁单极子在膨胀宇宙中的标度演化可被定量模拟，其源生磁场与引力波谱具有可区分的特征标度；但磁场幅度受耀变体观测限制，单极子无法解释原初磁场，其质量上限由 Parker 约束与暗物质丰度共同限定，其中 Parker 约束更严格。

Abstract: We present the first 3D lattice simulations of magnetic monopole production from a first-order phase transition, using the Press-Ryden-Spergel method, capturing the subsequent annihilation stage and the entry into the scaling regime in an expanding universe. We establish the quantitative relationship between the initial bubble number and the residual monopole abundance, and calculate the magnetic field and gravitational wave power spectra sourced by monopole dynamics. Crucially, we find that the magnetic field spectrum displays a steep $k^{-5}$ decline beyond the monopole size $w_M$, whereas the peak of the gravitational wave spectrum aligns with the scale of the mean monopole separation $d_M$, governed by $k^3$ and $k^{-3}$ scalings on the low- and high-wavenumber sides of the peak, respectively. However, since the resulting magnetic field strength is excluded by blazar observations, magnetic monopoles cannot be the origin of primordial cosmic magnetic fields. Finally, we determine the allowed monopole mass under the constraints of the Parker bound ($m_M\lesssim2.18\times10^{11}\,\mathrm{GeV}$) and dark matter abundance ($m_M\lesssim1.58\times10^{12}\,\mathrm{GeV}$).

</details>

### [9] [Two-stage Slowdown of Vacancy-mediated Diffusion in a Lattice Gas Model with Quenched Disorder and Spins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29931)
*Qiang Zhai*

Main category: cond-mat.dis-nn

TL;DR: 论文提出一种空间淬火随机相互作用、自旋淬火于粒子的格点气模型（lattice gas model），在平衡态下研究空位介导扩散（vacancy-mediated diffusion），并观察到均方位移与空间重叠函数的两阶段减速现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构建可刻画类玻璃动力学系统中层级动力学（hierarchical dynamics）的新模型框架，通过淬火无序与空位扩散机制考察减速与反关联行为。

Method: 采用格点气模型，将随机相互作用在空间中淬火、自旋淬火到粒子上，在平衡态下分析空位介导的扩散过程，考察均方位移（mean squared displacement）、空间重叠（spatial overlaps）及空位存活函数（survival function）。

Result: 均方位移与空间重叠均呈现两阶段减速（two-stage slowdown）；平台（plateaus）源于相应长度尺度上的反关联（anti-correlation），且该反关联随滞后时间（lag time）增加迅速减弱；空位存活函数呈宽而有限的分布，提示大时间极限下空位为正常扩散（normal diffusion）。

Conclusion: 该模型可作为理解类玻璃动力学系统中层级动力学的新平台，为淬火无序与空位扩散耦合下的多阶段弛豫提供可控研究框架。

Abstract: We present a lattice gas model with random interactions quenched in space and spins quenched to the particles. The vacancy-mediated diffusion is studied in equilibrium. A two-stage slowdown is witnessed in both the mean squared displacement and the spatial overlaps. We demonstrate that the plateaus are due to anti-correlation at the corresponding length scale. The anti-correlation diminishes quickly with increasing lag time. A broad and yet finite distribution of the survival function of vacancies suggests their normal diffusion at large time limit. Our model can serve as a new playground for understanding hierarchical dynamics in glassy-like dynamical systems.

</details>

### [10] [How Far Can Foundation Models Be Distilled for Efficient Jet Tagging?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29439)
*Jay Agarwal,Siddharth Khare,Dhruv Kumar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究知识蒸馏（knowledge distillation）在喷注标记（jet tagging）任务中的作用：将六个架构、对称性与预训练方式各异的教师模型蒸馏到五个小型学生模型，结果表明蒸馏收益主要取决于教师强度，且2万参数的学生模型可超越所有已发表的从零训练的喷注标记器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型强子对撞机（Large Hadron Collider）实验对推理成本（inference cost）有严格限制，而引入更多物理知识或大规模预训练（pretraining）的模型虽性能强但代价高，因此需借助知识蒸馏将其性能迁移到可廉价运行的小模型，并明确教师何种性质对学生有益、学生可缩小到何种程度。

Method: 在喷注标记基准上，选取六个在架构、对称性（symmetry）与预训练上存在差异的教师模型（含两个基础模型 foundation models），蒸馏至五个小型学生模型；随后对两个最强蒸馏学生（Transformer 与 L-GATr-slim）在宽度与深度上逐步缩减，并分析学生学习的内容，同时对推理成本进行基准测试。

Result: 每个教师都能提升每个学生，增益主要由教师性能强度决定；仅2万参数的蒸馏 L-GATr-slim 以远低于任一教师的推理成本超越所有已发表的从零训练喷注标记器，即使仅2千参数也能与参数量为其千倍的 ParT 相当；同尺寸学生的推理速度可相差近一个数量级（order of magnitude）。

Conclusion: 知识蒸馏可将强教师模型的性能有效压缩至极小且推理廉价的学生模型，学生规模的下限远低于常规预期；同等参数量的学生间速度差异显著，说明架构与实现同样关键。作者公开了代码与全部训练模型权重。

Abstract: Jet tagging is one of the most important applications of machine learning at the Large Hadron Collider. Recent models build in more physics knowledge or rely on large-scale pretraining, but the experiments place strict limits on their inference cost. Knowledge distillation can transfer their performance to small students that are cheap to run. We ask which properties of a teacher help the student, how small a student can get and how close it can come to the state of the art. On a jet tagging benchmark, we distil six teachers that differ in architecture, symmetry and pretraining, including two foundation models, into five small students. Every teacher improves every student, and the gain depends mainly on the strength of the teacher. We then shrink our two strongest distilled students, Transformer and L-GATr-slim, in width and in depth. With 20 thousand parameters, a distilled L-GATr-slim outperforms every published jet tagger trained from scratch at a fraction of the inference cost of every teacher. Even with two thousand parameters, it matches ParT, a model with a thousand times as many parameters. We also study what the students learn from their teachers. Our benchmark of inference cost shows that students of the same size can differ by almost an order of magnitude in speed. We release the code and the weights of all trained models.

</details>


### [11] [Compositional neural networks for multimodal X-ray and neutron scattering inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29362)
*Valentin Munteanu,Alexander Hinderhofer,Dmitry Lapkin,Vladimir Starostin,Frank Schreiber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种基于先验摊销流匹配（prior-amortized flow matching）的模块化概率多测量分析框架，通过“晚期融合（late fusion）”组合预训练的单观测后验估计器，实现X射线与中子反射测量数据的联合反演与共精修。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散射实验通常只记录强度而丢失相位信息，导致反问题（inverse problems）非唯一；虽可通过互补测量缓解歧义，但将多测量整合进机器学习框架仍具挑战性。

Method: 为每种观测类型或物理探针分别训练单观测后验估计器（single-observation posterior estimator），仅在推断阶段通过组合采样器（compositional sampler）融合，无需专门的联合重训练；以反射测量（reflectometry）为案例，覆盖X射线、飞行时间中子与极化中子反射测量，并开展同质（多中子衬度）与异质（X射线+中子）共精修（co-refinement）研究。

Result: 单观测演示涵盖X射线、飞行时间中子与极化中子反射测量；分别的共精修研究表明，任务匹配的估计器可实现多中子衬度下的同质组合以及X射线与中子测量的异质多模态组合。

Conclusion: 结果确立了组合流匹配（compositional flow matching）作为不确定性感知反演与共精修的模块化方法，并可能推广至其他多观测约束同一物理结构、同时保留测量特异参数的散射问题。

Abstract: X-ray and neutron scattering are indispensable for structural characterization across condensed-matter physics and materials science. Yet, because scattering experiments generally record intensities without phase information, distinct structures can yield similar scattering patterns, rendering the corresponding inverse problems non-unique. This ambiguity can be reduced by combining complementary measurements, but their integration into machine-learning frameworks remains challenging. Here we introduce a modular framework for probabilistic multi-measurement analysis based on prior-amortized flow matching. Single-observation posterior estimators are trained separately for individual observation types or physical probes and combined only at inference time through a compositional sampler. This late fusion approach allows pretrained single-observation models to be reused without dedicated joint retraining. As a case in point, we consider reflectometry, which provides depth-resolved, nanometer-scale information about thin films and interfaces. Our single-observation demonstrations span X-ray, time-of-flight neutron and polarized neutron reflectometry. In separate co-refinement studies, task-matched estimators enable homogeneous composition across multiple neutron contrasts and heterogeneous multimodal composition of X-ray and neutron measurements. Together, these results establish compositional flow matching as a modular approach to uncertainty-aware inversion and co-refinement, with potential applicability to other scattering problems in which multiple observations constrain a common physical structure while retaining measurement-specific parameters.

</details>


### [12] [Phase Transitions in Learning Driven by Extremal Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29531)
*Ethan Levien,Trevor GrandPre*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多示例学习（multiple instance learning, MIL）中最大池化（max pooling）导致梯度消失（vanishing gradients）的问题，构建最小模型并在大样本 n、高维 d、α=ln n/d 固定的联合极限下推导梯度流（gradient flow）的低维投影，识别出两个相变（phase transition）并给出有限尺寸训练梯度的渐近收敛验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIL 中常用最大池化以汇聚实例（instance）隐含标签生成包级（bag-level）标签，但最大池化会引发梯度消失；现代流程以 softmax 近似最大值并将锐度 λ 设为可学习参数，当最大池化模型为真实数据生成过程时，需在梯度下降中增大 λ，从而进入梯度消失的参数区域。为理解梯度下降在何种条件下失效，有必要分析其动力学机制。

Method: 构建一个最小模型，在包规模 n 大与维度 d 高的联合极限下（固定 α=ln n/d），推导梯度流的低维投影，从而对梯度下降动力学进行解析刻画，并与数值模拟对照。

Result: 所推导的流存在两个不同相变：其一是与无序系统随机能量模型（random energy model）相关的内在冻结相变（freezing transition），其二是出现在现代 Hopfield 网络（modern Hopfield networks）中的检索相变（retrieval transition）。数值模拟同时证实了相图（phase diagram）以及有限尺寸训练梯度向其预测渐近形式的收敛。

Conclusion: MIL 中 softmax 近似最大池化的梯度下降失效可由低维梯度流的相变结构解释，冻结与检索两类相变构成其相图；有限尺寸模拟与渐近理论预测一致，支持该低维投影框架的有效性。

Abstract: We study an extremal regression problem, an example of multiple instance learning (MIL), where the predictors are given as multisets, called bags. The bag elements (called instances) possess hidden labels which are pooled to produce an observed bag-level label. Max pooling is common in MIL applications, but gives rise to vanishing gradients. To handle this, modern MIL pipelines approximate the max with a softmax, treating the sharpness as a learnable parameter, denoted $λ$. When the max pooling model is the true data generating process, learning will be successful when $λ$ is increased during gradient descent, thereby driving the dynamics into regions of parameter space with vanishing gradients. To understand the conditions under which gradient descent breaks down, we study a minimal model and derive a low dimensional projection of the gradient flow in the joint limit of large bags $n$ and high dimension $d$ with $α=\ln n/d$ fixed. The flow has two distinct phase transitions: an intrinsic freezing transition related to the random energy model of disordered systems, and a retrieval transition occurring in modern Hopfield networks. Numerical simulation confirms both the phase diagram and the convergence of the finite-size training gradient to its predicted asymptotic form.

</details>


### [13] [Mitigating Model Collapse through Dreaming Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30009)
*Matteo Benati,Alessandro Londei,Denise Lanzieri,Raffaello Mastromarino,Vittorio Loreto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文在受控马尔可夫（Markov）设定下从谱（spectral）角度研究合成数据训练导致的模型崩塌（model collapse），指出低温采样（T_s<1）使学习到的转移算子次主特征值趋近单位圆、谱隙收窄，而 Dreaming Learning（高温自采样正则）可抑制该漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在合成数据上训练生成模型会引起多样性丧失与性能下降，即模型崩塌，但其退化机制缺乏可解释的定量刻画。作者希望借助马尔可夫链转移算子的特征值谱，直接观察该退化过程。

Method: 在受控马尔可夫设定中分析学习到的转移算子特征值谱；将采样温度 T_s<1 解释为对算子的反复锐化，并分析 Dreaming Learning——在常规训练之外，对以更高温度 T_d>1 采样的模型自身样本追加损失项——对谱的影响。

Result: T_s<1 时次主特征值趋近单位圆、谱隙（spectral gap）收窄，且即使累积数据也无法阻止该漂移；高温样本使预测分布变平，避免学习算子元素趋近于零，从而使特征值远离单位圆。5000 代谱测量与该图景一致，缓解效果在 LSTM 语言模型与 124M 参数 GPT-2 上依然存在。

Conclusion: 模型崩塌可被刻画为转移算子谱的确定性化漂移，温度选择是关键因素；高温自采样正则（Dreaming Learning）在谱层面提供了一种有理论依据的缓解手段，并在复杂模型上保持有效。

Abstract: Training generative models on synthetic data causes loss of diversity and a decrease in performance, a phenomenon known as model collapse. We study this degeneration spectrally in a controlled Markov setting. The eigenvalue spectrum of the learned transition operator offers a direct view of the process, since the subdominant eigenvalues set how fast the chain mixes and therefore how much of the state space it continues to explore. We find that sampling at temperature $T_s < 1$ acts on the operator like repeated sharpening, pushing it toward a deterministic system: the subdominant eigenvalues approach the unit circle and the spectral gap narrows. When $T_s < 1$, even data accumulation does not appear to arrest this drift. We then analyze Dreaming Learning, a regulariser that, in parallel with regular training, adds a loss term on the model's own samples drawn at a raised temperature $T_d > 1$. Because high-temperature samples flatten the predicted distribution, training on them keeps the entries of the learned operator from becoming vanishingly small, which in turn keeps the eigenvalues bounded away from the unit circle. Measurements of the spectra over 5000 generations are consistent with this picture, and the mitigation effect persists in more complex settings such as LSTM language models and a 124M-parameter GPT-2.

</details>


### [14] [The role of locality and weight sharing in convolutional neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30118)
*Francesco Mori,Melanie Weber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文以教师-学生模型（teacher-student model）对比卷积学生与局部连接学生在在线随机梯度下降下的学习动态，在大特征维度极限下导出精确常微分方程，刻画局部性（locality）与权重共享（weight sharing）如何分别塑造学习，并揭示二者效果依赖自然图像空间相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积神经网络（convolutional neural networks）的成功常被归因于局部性与权重共享，但二者各自如何塑造学习、其效应如何依赖自然图像的空间相关性，仍缺乏清晰理解。该文旨在分别解析这两种性质的独立作用。

Method: 构建作用于 P 个输入位置、位置间相关性为 c 的卷积教师-学生模型（teacher-student model），比较经在线随机梯度下降（online stochastic gradient descent）训练的卷积学生与局部连接学生，并在大特征维度 D 极限下导出精确描述其动态的常微分方程（ordinary differential equations, ODEs）。

Result: 当 c=0 时，两类学生遵循相同学习轨迹，卷积学生学习速度快 P 倍；局部性影响样本复杂度（sample complexity）：核尺寸与教师匹配时需 O_D(D) 样本，核更大时需 O_D(D log D)。当 c>0 时，空间相关性削弱权重共享优势：卷积学生的渐近误差随 c 增大，而局部连接学生与 c 无关且在同一学习率下整体更低；若依训练时域调整学习率，则局部连接学生在早期占优、卷积学生在晚期占优，且该转变可解析预测。

Conclusion: 局部性与权重共享对学习的影响可分离且各有机制：权重共享主要带来速度增益，局部性主要决定样本复杂度，而空间相关性会削弱权重共享的相对优势并导致早晚期占优者的转换。

Abstract: The success of convolutional neural networks is often attributed to locality and weight sharing. However, how these two properties separately shape learning, and how their effects depend on the spatial correlations of natural images, remain poorly understood. We address these questions in a teacher-student model with a convolutional teacher acting on $P$ input positions with correlation $c$ between positions. We compare convolutional and locally connected students trained by online stochastic gradient descent, and derive ordinary differential equations that describe their dynamics exactly in the limit of large feature dimension $D$. For uncorrelated positions ($c = 0$), the two students follow the same learning trajectory, with the convolutional student learning exactly $P$ times faster. We also find that locality affects the sample complexity: a student that matches the kernel size of the teacher recovers it from $\mathcal{O}_{D}(D)$ samples, whereas $\mathcal{O}_{D}(D\log(D))$ samples are needed with a larger kernel. For $c > 0$, spatial correlations can reduce the advantage of weight sharing. The asymptotic error of the convolutional student grows with $c$, whereas that of the locally connected student is independent of $c$ and overall lower when evaluated at the same learning rate. When the learning rate is instead tuned to the training horizon, however, the locally connected student wins at early times and the convolutional student at late times, with a transition that we predict analytically.

</details>

### [15] [CRAFT-ε: A Conformer-Resolved Atom- and Fragment-based Theory for the Static Relative Permittivity of Pure Organic Liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28850)
*Simon Müller*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出CRAFT-ε模型，基于构象分辨的原子与片段描述符，结合Onsager-Kirkwood-Fröhlich关系预测中性纯液体的静态相对介电常数，在1393种液体上取得五折交叉验证AAD 2.40、R²=0.93。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态相对介电常数（static relative permittivity）在热力学建模、电化学与溶剂设计中十分重要，但因其同时受电子极化（electronic polarization）与分子偶极取向（dipole orientation）影响，直接从分子结构预测仍具挑战性。

Method: 采用GFN2-xTB/ALPB工作流获取构象自由能、分子偶极与原子分辨极化率；将分子偶极分解为化学定义的极性片段矢量以保留局部偶极贡献的大小与相对取向；并将电子极化与取向极化同基团贡献摩尔体积结合于Onsager-Kirkwood-Fröhlich关系，对非极性、极性与氢键化合物分阶段拟合41个参数。

Result: 对介电常数介于1.5至183的1393种液体，五折交叉验证平均绝对偏差为2.40（R²=0.93）；构象平均（conformational averaging）对含多个极性基团的液体至关重要；与含139个参数的既有片段模型相比，以约四分之一参数量取得相近交叉验证RMSE（3.65对3.41）且覆盖数据集全部液体。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了从分子结构到液体介电响应的低计算成本路径，并为后续推广至溶剂混合物与电解质体系奠定基础。

Abstract: The static relative permittivity is an important property in thermodynamic modeling, electrochemistry, and solvent design, but predicting it directly from molecular structure remains challenging because both electronic polarization and molecular dipole orientation contribute to the measured response. Here, we introduce CRAFT-ε (Conformer-Resolved Atom- and Fragment-based Theory for ε), a conformer-resolved molecular descriptor model for predicting the static relative permittivity of neutral pure liquids. Conformer free energies, molecular dipoles, and atom-resolved polarizabilities are obtained from a GFN2-xTB/ALPB 10 workflow. The molecular dipole is decomposed into chemically defined polar-fragment vectors, allowing fitted corrections to retain both the magnitude and relative orientation of local dipolar contributions. Electronic and orientational polarization are combined with a group-contribution molar volume in the Onsager-Kirkwood-Fröhlich relation. A staged parameterization with 41 parameters is applied to nonpolar, polar, and hydrogen-bonding compounds. For 1393 liquids with permittivities between 1.5 and 183, CRAFT-ε reaches a five-fold cross-validated average absolute deviation of 2.40 ({R^2} = 0.93). Conformational averaging 15 is essential for liquids with several polar groups. We then recover the light spectrum of BPS D3-branes wrapping cycles on the Type IIB side from the heterotic perspective, and finally match the duality groups governing the global structure of the vector multiplet moduli space. In the first, the heterotic string is compactified on a geometric K3 with a standard gauge bundle, demonstrating explicitly that type II$_0$ loci admit a consistent description as weakly coupled heterotic strings.   <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 当曲率与导数由单一能标Λ控制时，黑洞周围有效场论在ℓ∼(r_sΛ)^2内保持可预言性，而移动仅在此处达到量级一；在可预言区间内产生可分辨移动需要大数值系数或双尺度幂次计数（后者无已知紫外完备）。两种情形下四次曲率算符均占主导，该条件与平坦空间引力子振幅的正定性界（positivity bounds）在参数上重合；仅标量-张量理论中双尺度窗口可能超出正定性，且至多涉及一族十维算符。

Abstract: It has been proposed that consistency limits how slowly black holes can ring down in extensions of General Relativity: effective-field-theory (EFT) corrections may lengthen the decay time of a quasinormal mode by at most about one oscillation period, $\Re[ω_{\rm GR}]\,δτ\lesssim1$. We argue that, under standard power counting, this condition yields no new, model-independent constraint on gravitational EFTs. First, its motivation does not extend straightforwardly to black holes: the inverse oscillation frequency is not a universal, detector-independent uncertainty in the damping time of a classical waveform. Our main argument, however, rests on eikonal power counting alone. The decay-time shift grows with the angular momentum $\ell$ of the mode, but so does the weight of higher-derivative operators. When curvature and derivatives are controlled by a single scale $Λ$, as in every known ultraviolet completion, the EFT around a black hole of radius $r_s$ remains predictive up to $\ell\sim(r_sΛ)^2$, and the shift becomes of order one only there, where an infinite tower of operators contributes at the same order. A resolvable shift within the predictive regime requires either large numerical coefficients or a two-scale power counting, which has no known ultraviolet completion. In both cases the quartic-curvature operators dominate, and the condition coincides parametrically with the positivity bounds that flat-space graviton amplitudes impose under standard assumptions on the ultraviolet completion. </details>

This formalism establishes a refinement of the orbifold inheritance principle at supergraph level. As a direct application, we compute leading twist-dependent corrections to BPS two- and three-point functions, finding agreement with localisation results in extremal cases and generating new data for non-extremal correlators. We further map these corrections to finite-size effects in the twisted hexagon formalism. This opens a path towards an all-order integrability prescription of orbifold structure constants.

We show that these configurations cannot supply the chiral Standard Model through their perturbative D7 spectrum. Disjointness excludes massless fermions connecting different component sectors, so quark-doublet fermions are absent. Configurations containing symplectic factors require a separate argument: massless hypercharge, together with the same disjointness, forces the net quark-doublet chirality to vanish. The latter involves one untwisted BPS descendant, which contains physical hypermultiplet fields and can therefore connect directly to the twist-dependent hypermultiplet circle. This reduces the suppression in $g$, resulting in a contribution $4 g^{2(\ell+1)} W_{\ell+1}$ controlled by a bridge length $\ell$ rather than by an external operator length $L\geq\ell$. These examples demonstrate the feasibility of harmonic superspace calculations in orbifold theories and point to various future applications in the context of non-BPS correlators, integrability and marginal deformations.

Abstract: We develop the 1+1D $\mathcal{N}{=}2$ Wess--Zumino model with a central extension, starting from Wigner's theorem and the supertranslation group. Abstract: The unitarity and causality properties of asymptotically safe quantum gravity are still unsettled. Abstract: Carrollian geometry has recently emerged as the proper language to understand the geometry of null hypersurfaces from an intrinsic perspective. This result follows directly from the requirement that the gravitational phase space induced on a finite distance null hypersurface be anomaly-free, in a covariant phase space sense. We then show that a linearized perturbation of this dynamical entropy charge, sourced by an external matter perturbation falling across the hypersurface, satisfies a physical process first law. Moreover, upon imposing the classical null energy condition, a linearized second law holds for the dynamical entropy. We then turn to a semi-classical generalization of this construction, where we argue that a notion of dynamical generalized entropy follows from considering perturbations sourced by external quantum matter. Abstract: We propose a random Euclidean CFT description of three-dimensional de Sitter gravity, with fixed central charge and primary spectrum but fluctuating operator product coefficients. These relations connect the proposed OPE statistics to both the Euclidean boundary description and the Lorentzian bulk geometry. In this paper it is argued that the radius of the de Sitter space, i.e. the cosmological constant, can be computed using the principle of maximum entropy. The entropy reaches its maximal value for the 4-dimensional de Sitter space with the inflationary cosmological constant $Λ=3π\,\exp\{-ψ(3/2)\}$, where $ψ$ is the digamma function, $Λ\approx 9.087$ in Planck units.

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞信息问题（black hole information problem）关乎坍缩与蒸发过程中演化算符的幺正性（unitarity）；论文将同一问题推广至量子与暴胀宇宙学，追问两者是否均面临非幺正性。

Method: 论证不能采用固定的经典引力背景，须计入反作用（backreaction），因而使用半经典引力（semi-classical gravity），其中包含物质场能量-动量张量算符的期望值；对该张量重整化后得到含标量曲率与Ricci张量二次项的有效作用量，并讨论黑洞是否发射鬼场，以及$f(R)$引力下的黑洞熵。

Result: 二次曲率引力中的鬼场导致黑洞与宇宙学中幺正性被破坏；$f(R)$引力下黑洞熵不同于Bekenstein-Hawking熵与纠缠岛（entanglement island）熵，且在$R+R^2$引力中黑洞熵在蒸发过程中趋于常数。

Conclusion: 黑洞与宇宙学两情形下答案均为否定，即存在非幺正性；$R+R^2$引力中熵趋于常数可视为黑洞与宇宙学中可能出现非幺正性的另一佐证。

Abstract: Black hole information problem is the question about unitarity of the evolution operator during the collapse and evaporation of the black hole. One can ask the same question about unitarity of quantum and inflationary cosmology. In this paper we argue that in both cases, for black holes and for cosmology, the answer is negative and we face non-unitarity.
  Such a question can not be addressed by using the fixed classical gravitational background since one has to take into account the backreaction. To his end one uses the semi-classical gravity, which includes the expectation value of the energy - momentum tensor operator of the matter fields. One has to renormalize the energy-momentum tensor and one gets an effective action which contains quadratic terms in scalar curvature and Ricci tensor. Such quadratic gravity contains ghosts which in fact lead to violation of unitarity in black holes and cosmology. We discuss the question whether black holes will emit ghosts.
  One can try to restrict ourselves to the $f(R)$ gravity that seems is a good approximation to the semi-classical gravity and widely used in cosmology. The black hole entropy in $f(R)$ gravity is different from the Bekenstein-Hawking entropy and from entanglement island entropy. The black hole entropy in $R+R^2$ gravity goes to a constant during the evaporation process. This can be interpreted as another indication to the possible non-unitarity in black holes and cosmology

</details>

### [43] [Dynamical Tunneling Blockade in Layer-Pseudospin Ferromagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28760)
*Lingxian Kong,Yuta Murakami,Ryuichi Shindou,Zhiyuan Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究层赝自旋铁磁体在层间电压偏置与隧穿共同作用下的非平衡稳态，发现高偏压下层赝自旋动力学进动使谷赝自旋稳定于反平行构型，产生动力学隧穿阻塞，导致交流电流消失、直流隧穿电流骤降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代层赝自旋铁磁体实现中电子带有额外谷简并，使每层具有谷赝自旋自由度；其非平衡输运行为，尤其是隧穿与赝自旋序的耦合，尚待阐明。

Method: 基于包含层间隧穿的窄带或平坦带双层系统，施加静态层间电压偏置，分析层赝自旋铁磁序与谷赝自旋自由度的非平衡稳态动力学。

Conclusion: 动力学隧穿阻塞（dynamical tunneling blockade）构成与传统量子霍尔双层体系（quantum Hall double layers）截然不同的输运特征。

Abstract: Double-layer systems with narrow or flat electronic bands may exhibit spontaneous interlayer coherence, described by a layer-pseudospin ferromagnetic order parameter. In modern realizations of layer-pseudospin ferromagnets, the electrons carry additional valley degeneracies, endowing each layer with a valley-pseudospin degree of freedom. We study their nonequilibrium steady states driven by a static interlayer voltage bias in the presence of interlayer tunneling. While in the low-bias regime, the system is in a static state with parallel valley pseudospins between the two layers, we show that in the high-bias regime, the dynamical precession of the layer-pseudospin stabilizes the valley pseudospins in the antiparallel configuration, strongly suppressing the interlayer tunneling. This dynamical tunneling blockade leads to an absence of ac current and a sharp drop of the dc tunneling current to a tiny value, in stark contrast to conventional quantum Hall double layers.

</details>


### [44] [Characterizing universal topological phases beyond spatial embedding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29022)
*Sunkyu Yu,Xianji Piao,Namkyoo Park*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出一种仅依赖哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）的拓扑相（topological phases）表征框架，通过基于连通性构造哈密顿量驱动的空间嵌入（spatial embedding），将拓扑相概念拓展至抽象平台与复杂网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拓扑相表征依赖封闭倒空间流形（reciprocal-space manifolds）上的全局不变量（global invariants）或与位置算符（position operators）的局域对易关系，这种空间依赖性使得在无固有空间嵌入的抽象平台上无法唯一刻画拓扑相。

Method: 以拓扑特征在任意保持哈密顿量的形变（Hamiltonian-preserving deformations）下的不变性为基础，构建一种基于哈密顿量的空间嵌入，按系统组分之间的连通性（connectivity）对其进行分组。

Result: 该嵌入在已有系统中重现了常规拓扑诊断（topological diagnostics）的结果，并能仅凭哈密顿量将拓扑相一致地推广到复杂网络（complex networks），在小世界网络（small-world networks）中表现出显著的鲁棒性（robustness）。

Conclusion: 研究通过将拓扑表征与预设物理平台解耦，重塑了拓扑物理（topological physics）的概念基础。

Abstract: Although Hamiltonians govern wave-matter interactions, conventional characterizations of topological phases of matter require more than the Hamiltonian alone, relying on global invariants defined on closed reciprocal-space manifolds or local commutation relations with position operators. This spatial dependence precludes a unique characterization of topological phases on an abstract platform, whose Hamiltonian can be defined independently of its spatial embedding. Here we establish a framework for characterizing universal topological phases, grounded in the invariance of topological features under arbitrary Hamiltonian-preserving deformations. We construct a Hamiltonian-based spatial embedding that groups a system's constituents according to their connectivity. This embedding, which reproduces the results of conventional topological diagnostics in established systems, enables a coherent extension of topological phases to complex networks using the Hamiltonian alone, further highlighting exceptional robustness in small-world networks. Our results reshape the conceptual foundations of topological physics by decoupling topological characterization from prescribed physical platforms.

</details>


### [45] [Ubiquitous topological materials protected by hidden partial symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29028)
*Lumen Eek,Xuelong Chen,Malte Röntgen,Wenlong Gao,Cristiane Morais Smith*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出"部分潜在对称性（partial latent symmetries）"，仅要求有效哈密顿量在特定能标上满足不变性，从而以更弱的对称性约束实现拓扑效应，并在SSH与BBH模型中构造弱对称版本，且实验上实现了厄米与非厄米近似拓扑绝缘体及其边缘态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统拓扑绝缘体的表面态鲁棒性依赖于系统整体必须遵守对称性，这一约束过强。作者希望放宽该要求，探索能否用显著更弱的对称性条件仍保护拓扑效应。

Method: 引入"部分潜在对称性"概念：不变性仅需在有效哈密顿量的某些能标上成立，而非对整体对象成立；据此构建Su-Schrieffer-Heeger与Benalcazar-Bernevig-Hughes模型的弱对称版本。

Result: 弱对称模型在许多方面与传统模型行为一致，尤其是具有鲁棒的边缘态（edge states），但相应拓扑不变量（topological invariants）仅近似量子化（approximately quantized）。实验上实现了此类近似拓扑绝缘体的厄米（Hermitian）与非厄米（non-Hermitian）版本并探测其边缘态。

Conclusion: 鉴于对称性在物理与工程各领域的普遍性，该工作可作为基石，推动以弱对称条件替代传统强对称要求的相关探索。

Abstract: Symmetries play a crucial role in protecting the topological phases of insulators, ensuring the robustness of their surface states against perturbations. Traditionally, these symmetries have to be obeyed by the system as a whole, which is a rather strong constraint. In this work, we demonstrate that topological effects can also be realized through a substantially weaker form of symmetry that we coin \emph{partial latent symmetries}. For these, invariance is required only at certain energy scales of an effective Hamiltonian, rather than for the object as a whole. Applying this idea, we construct weak-symmetry versions of the Su-Schrieffer-Heeger and Benalcazar-Bernevig-Hughes models. These weak-symmetry models behave in many aspects just like their conventional brethren---in particular, they host robust edge states---, though the corresponding topological invariants are only \emph{approximately} quantized. We conclude this work by experimentally realizing Hermitian and non-Hermitian versions of such approximate topological insulators and probe their edge states. Overall, given the ubiquity of symmetry in all areas of physics and engineering, we foresee our work to serve as a cornerstone for exploring how conventional, strong symmetry requirements can be replaced by weak-symmetry ones.

</details>


### [46] [The Spectroscopic Atlas of Excitonic Complexes in hBN-Encapsulated S-TMD Monolayers: From Excitons to Oxcitons and Beyond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29081)
*Maciej R. Molas*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该综述为六方氮化硼（hexagonal boron nitride, hBN）封装半导体过渡金属硫族化合物单层（transition-metal dichalcogenide monolayers, MLs）中的激子（exciton）谱学提供了一套以光谱特征为线索的识别框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: hBN封装抑制了外在无序（extrinsic disorder），使激子态的复杂层级被逐级分辨，相关现象学快速扩张，亟需一种不依赖具体材料体系、以实验可观测指纹为核心的实用识别指南。

Method: 采用综述方式，系统梳理亮/暗激子、Rydberg态、带电激子、双激子（biexciton）、声子辅助复制态（phonon-assisted replicas）及高阶费米海多体态（如hexciton、oxciton），并整合光致发光与吸收类光谱、静电门控、偏振分析、激发功率与温度依赖、磁场及时间分辨测量等手段。

Result: 总结了区分各类激子复合物的实验特征，强调多手段联合使用以确立微观指认（microscopic assignment）；同时指出合金化（alloying）可作为调控激子层级、自旋-谷构型（spin-valley configuration）与磁光响应的额外自由度。

Conclusion: 该综述以实验可及的指纹而非单个材料体系来组织激子现象学，为高质量二维半导体中激子态的识别、比较与工程化设计提供实用且具普适性的指导。

Abstract: Hexagonal boron nitride (hBN) encapsulation has transformed the optical spectroscopy of semiconducting transition-metal dichalcogenide monolayers (MLs) by suppressing extrinsic disorder and enabling the resolution of an increasingly complex hierarchy of excitonic states. This Review provides a spectroscopy-oriented framework for understanding and identifying excitonic complexes in hBN-encapsulated Mo- and W-based MLs and their alloys. The discussion covers bright and dark excitons, Rydberg states, charged excitons, biexcitons, phonon-assisted replicas, and higher-order Fermi-sea many-body states, including hexcitons and oxcitons, with an emphasis on the experimental signatures that distinguish them. Particular attention is given to the combined use of photoluminescence and absorption-related spectroscopy, electrostatic gating, polarization analysis, excitation-power and temperature dependences, magnetic fields, and time-resolved measurements to establish microscopic assignments. The role of alloying as an additional degree of freedom for engineering the excitonic hierarchy, spin-valley configuration, and magneto-optical response is also highlighted. By organizing the rapidly expanding phenomenology around experimentally accessible fingerprints rather than individual material systems, this Review aims to provide a practical and broadly applicable guide for identifying, comparing, and engineering excitonic states in high-quality two-dimensional semiconductors.

</details>

*J. Alejandro de Sousa,Ilias Vandevenne,Jana Nikolić,Tuur Boonen,Etienne Goovaerts,Sofie Cambré*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出在(6,5)单壁碳纳米管（single wall carbon nanotubes, SWCNT）上引入对卤苯基（para-halophenyl）量子缺陷，可在室温常压条件下实现三重态激子（triplet excitons）的光学自旋初始化与读出，并初步演示对顺磁分子（paramagnetic molecules）的传感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室温常压下可光学寻址的电子自旋量子比特（spin qubits）对量子传感（quantum sensing）至关重要；固态缺陷平台缺乏精确定位与电学可调性，分子自旋量子比特则光稳定性（photostability）不足，需兼顾两者优势的新平台。

Method: 在(6,5)单壁碳纳米管表面引入对氯、对溴、对碘苯基缺陷，形成可束缚三重态激子的深光学陷阱（optical traps），构建自旋-光子接口（spin-photon interface），并开展室温光学初始化与读出实验。

Result: 缺陷在近红外（near infrared）生物透明窗口（biological transparency window）内发光，且位于纳米管表面而具备纳米级邻近性；深光学陷阱带来优异光稳定性；实现室温三重态光学初始化与读出，并完成顺磁分子传感的原理验证（proof of principle），显示片上检测（on chip detection）潜力。

Conclusion: 上述结果确立SWCNT缺陷作为未来量子传感的有前景新平台。

Abstract: Optically addressable electron spin qubits that function at room temperature and under ambient conditions are essential for advancing quantum sensing. Solid state defect platforms offer robust spin-photon interfaces but lack precise defect positioning and electronic tunability. Molecular spin qubits enable bottom up chemical design yet suffer from limited photostability. Here, we introduce para-halophenyl (-Cl, -Br and -I) quantum defects in (6,5) single wall carbon nanotubes that trap triplet excitons with a spin-photon interface that is operable under ambient, room temperature conditions. These defects were shown to emit in the near infrared within the biological transparency window, and their placement on the nanotube surface provides nanoscale proximity to target environments. The deep optical traps formed by the defects yield exceptional photostability. Here, we demonstrate optical initialization and readout of these triplet states at room temperature, provide proof of principle sensing of paramagnetic molecules, and highlight potential for on chip detection. Together, these results establish SWCNT defects as a promising new platform for future quantum sensing.

</details>


### [48] [Higher-order Angular Momentum Transport of Alteraxial Phonons in 2D Magnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29453)
*Linxin Liu,Fuyi Wang,Huaiqiang Wang*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维磁体中晶格振动与磁序耦合使携带声子角动量（phonon angular momentum, PAM）的轴声子产生磁效应而备受关注，但涵盖多极图案及其输运表现的轴声子系统分类仍基本空白。

Abstract: The coupling between lattice vibrations and magnetic order in two-dimensional (2D) magnets gives rise to a variety of physical phenomena, among which magnetic effects of axial phonons carrying phonon angular momentum (PAM) have attracted considerable interest. However, a systematic classification of axial phonons in 2D magnets that covers their multipolar patterns and the resulting transport manifestations remains largely unexplored. Here, based on symmetry analysis, we classify all 2D magnetic point groups by their long-wavelength PAM wave patterns. Beyond the s-wave axial phonon, we identify alteraxial phonons with p-, d-, f-, g-, and i-wave multipolar textures, characterized by 1, 2, 3, 4, and 6 zero-PAM nodal lines, respectively. We then develop a theory of nonlinear PAM transport driven by a temperature gradient and derive two selection rules: a parity selection rule that determines whether the response occurs at even or odd orders, and an angular-harmonic selection rule that fixes the lowest allowed order for each wave pattern. The multipolar PAM patterns and selection rules are exemplified by two effective lattice models realizing f-wave and d-wave PAM patterns, respectivley. The angular dependence of the nonlinear PAM current reflects the underlying multipolar PAM texture, offering a signature for experiments. Our results establish a symmetry-guided framework for detecting and utilizing higher-order PAM textures in 2D magnets.

</details>


### [49] [Quantum Interference Enhanced Rectification in Mesoscopic Nanowires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29484)
*T. J. Osborne,B. Braunecker,H. F. Legg*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文提出在具有栅极限定散射体的介观Rashba纳米线中，通过相干弹性散射（coherent elastic scattering）即可产生强非互易电流—电压关系，实现纳米尺度二极管响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可扩展量子技术需要纳米尺度的非互易元件（nonreciprocal elements）以控制信号流向，但现有非互易器件通常体积庞大，因此亟需在介观尺度上实现非互易输运。

Method: 作者先给出在相位相干导体（phase coherent conductor）中即使破缺空间反演与时间反演对称性仍强制互易透射的对称性约束，再说明电容耦合于引线的栅极配合磁性散射体或横向子带混合可解除该约束；并分别在弱散射与强散射区分析非互易电导的来源与共振增强机制。

Result: 弱散射区非互易电导源于不同背散射路径之间的干涉；强散射区通过透射通道与来自更高横向子带或自旋劈裂能带的束缚态（bound states）杂化实现共振增强，此时约百分之一的偏压诱导栅极电势变化即可产生量级为一的二极管响应，即一种偏压下近似洁净透射、另一偏压下电流被强烈抑制。

Conclusion: 相干弹性散射本身足以在介观Rashba纳米线中产生强非互易输运，为纳米尺度非互易量子器件提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Nanoscale nonreciprocal elements are needed to control signal flow in scalable quantum technologies, but existing nonreciprocal components are often bulky. Here, we show that a strongly nonreciprocal current-voltage relation can arise entirely from coherent elastic scattering in a mesoscopic Rashba nanowire with gate-defined scatterers. We first identify the symmetry constraints that enforce reciprocal transmission in a phase coherent conductor even when inversion and time-reversal symmetry are broken. We then show how gates that are capacitively coupled to the leads, together with a magnetic scatterer or transverse subband mixing, can remove these constraints. In the weak-scattering regime, nonreciprocal conductance originates from interference between distinct backscattering paths. In the strong-scattering regime, nonreciprocity can be resonantly enhanced by the hybridization of the transmitting channel with bound states that arise from a higher transverse subband or spin-split band. In this regime, we find that a bias-induced change in the gate potentials of about one percent can produce an order-unity diode response, i.e., electrons are transmitted almost as in a clean wire for one bias polarity, whereas for the other polarity the current is strongly suppressed.

</details>


### [50] [Interaction-Induced Bloch Geometry and Transverse Thermoelectric Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29486)
*Heng-Yu Di,YuanDong Wang,Zhen-Gang Zhu,Gang Su*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 论文在Hartree–Fock近似下，用热长度规范密度矩阵方法推导线性热电响应，将电子的相互作用重构Bloch波函数效应分解为Drude、量子度规与Berry曲率三类通道，并给出对称性约束与弱展宽标度规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子—电子相互作用会重构Bloch波函数，改变其几何性质及相关热电响应，但相互作用修正如何分配到不同输运通道尚缺乏系统刻画。

Method: 采用热长度规范密度矩阵方法（thermal length-gauge density-matrix approach），在Hartree–Fock近似下推导线性热电响应；在无相互作用Bloch本征基中将重构的Berry联络（Berry connection）分解为无相互作用部分与相互作用联络部分，并结合对称性分析与弛豫时间近似（relaxation-time approximation）。

Result: 分辨出Drude、对称量子度规（quantum metric）与反对称Berry曲率（Berry curvature）贡献，并将反常热电响应拆为裸项、相干项与相互作用联络项；给出空间反演、旋转、时间反演及组合对称性对三通道的约束，弱展宽下各通道分别按Γ^{-1}、Γ、Γ^0标度（带间标度适用于非简并情形）；三角晶格扩展Hubbard模型的自洽计算显示相互作用可产生Berry曲率与量子度规并改变体系对称性，从而贡献横向热电输运。

Conclusion: 相互作用重构的波函数几何可通过相互作用联络、对称性改变等途径对横向热电输运产生可分辨、可分类的贡献，为在关联体系中分析和调控热电响应提供框架。

Abstract: Electron--electron interactions can reconstruct Bloch wave functions, changing their geometry and the associated thermoelectric response. We derive the linear thermoelectric response within the Hartree--Fock (HF) approximation using the thermal length-gauge density-matrix approach, resolving Drude, symmetric quantum-metric, and antisymmetric Berry-curvature contributions. *Haozhe Yang,Zhendong Chi,Eoin Dolan,Josep Ingla-Aynés,Beatriz Martín-García,Witold Skowroński,Marco Gobbi,Andrey Chuvilin,Luis E. Hueso,Fèlix Casanova*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自旋-电荷相互转换是自旋信息与电信号之间的关键纽带，在自旋轨道耦合系统中可包含对应不同自旋极化方向的多重分量。调控自旋织构有望同时控制转换强度及各分量间的平衡，而石墨烯基范德华异质结构因界面自旋轨道耦合可承载多通道转换，成为理想平台。

Abstract: Spin-charge interconversion provides the essential link between spin information and electrical signals in spintronic devices. Tuning this spin texture therefore provides a way to control not only the magnitude of spin-charge interconversion, but also the balance among its different components. Increasing the vertical electric field enhances additional in-plane spin components with Rashba-Edelstein-like character, including both parallel and perpendicular to the charge current contributions. </details>


### [53] [From Traveling Waves to Synthetic Gauge Fields: A Field-Free Route to Superconducting Diode Effect](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29865)
*Latu Kalita,Koustav Roy,Bilal Tanatar,Saurabh Basu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种时空驱动的化学势 μ_j(t)=μ+V cos(ωt+k_d j)，可在螺旋 Shiba 链中同时破坏空间反演与时间反演对称性，从而在无需外磁场的情况下实现可调控的超导二极管效应（superconducting diode effect）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导二极管效应要求正反向偏置电流不同，需同时破坏空间反演（inversion）与时间反演（time-reversal）对称性，而后者通常必须依赖磁场实现；本文旨在寻找一种无需外磁场的替代机制。

Method: 对螺旋 Shiba 链施加行波形式的时空依赖化学势驱动，该驱动通过产生奇宇称动量项，等效于实空间中的复数次近邻 Haldane 型跃迁（Haldane-like hopping），并由此引入合成规范磁通（synthetic gauge flux）而非有效 Zeeman 场，且与自旋无关（spin-blind）。

Result: 整流效应（rectification）对驱动波矢 k_d 为奇函数，在 k_d=nπ 处精确为零，并在 k_d=2π/3 处存在解析最优值；其对交换耦合 J 为偶函数，在 J=0 时仍存在。整流幅度与破对能隙（pair-breaking gap）成反比，拓扑相中约达 50%，平庸相中约 10%。

Conclusion: 对称性破缺由驱动控制，可在无外磁场条件下实现开启、调节与反转，为超导二极管效应提供了一种全电学、可调的调控途径。

Abstract: The superconducting diode effect requires the forward and reverse bias currents to differ, a scenario achieved via inversion and time-reversal symmetries broken together. The latter, and the more crucial one, is inevitably achieved by a magnetic field. Here, we show that a spatio-temporally driven chemical potential of the form, $μ_j(t)=μ+V\cos(ωt+\kd j)$, achieves both at once. Applied to a helical Shiba chain, the drive transmutes a traveling Peierls phase via generating an odd-in-momentum term, equivalent to an imaginary next-nearest-neighbour Haldane-like hopping in real space. Remarkably, the protocol is spin-blind, which renders the time-reversal breaking via a synthetic gauge flux rather than an effective Zeeman field. The rectification is odd in the drive wavevector, with exact zeros at $\kd=nπ$ and an analytical optimum at $\kd=2π/3$. It is however, even in the exchange coupling, $J$ and persists even at $J=0$. Furthermore, the rectification scales inversely with the pair-breaking gap, yielding approximately $50\%$ rectification in the topological phase and $10\%$ in the trivial phase. Symmetry breaking is thus drive-controlled, allowing it to be switched on, tuned, and reversed without an external magnetic field.

</details>

### [54] [Polymer-Mediated Hotspot Evolution in Shock-Induced Pore Collapse in 1,3,5,7-Tetranitro-1,3,5,7-tetrazocane (HMX)-based Explosives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28812)
*Jalen Macatangay,Brenden W. Hamilton,Alejandro Strachan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过反应分子动力学（reactive MD）模拟研究冲击加载下HMX/聚苯乙烯界面孔隙塌缩中的聚合物化学行为，发现黏结剂发生主链断裂与芳环-主链交联，充当热沉并抑制热点增长与晶间合并。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高炸药（high explosives, HEs）冲击起爆受孔隙塌缩与热点（hotspot）形成强烈影响；此前MD模拟显示软聚合物（如聚苯乙烯）向孔洞膨胀可在再压缩时产生更大压力-体积（PV）功与更高局部温度，但孔隙塌缩、界面现象与黏结剂化学三者如何共同影响起爆仍认识不足。

Method: 采用反应分子动力学（reactive MD）模拟，构建晶体-间隙-晶体的HMX叠层结构，上游表面覆有聚苯乙烯，考察接近实验相关尺度的孔洞在冲击诱导塌缩过程中的化学行为，处于流体动力学塌缩（hydrodynamic collapse）区制。

Result: 热点在材料界面处形核，PV功、冲击温度与自持化学反应起始均随孔洞长度变化；在冲击变形与HMX分解驱动下，黏结剂先发生主链断裂（backbone chain scission），随后出现广泛的芳环-主链交联（cross-bonding），类似石墨到金刚石转变。

Conclusion: 聚合物反应充当热沉（heat sink），抑制爆燃（deflagration），最终阻碍热点生长及晶体间的热点合并（coalescence）；模拟将聚合物的化学响应与聚合物黏结炸药（polymer-bonded explosives）的冲击-起爆转变（shock-to-detonation transition）联系起来。

Abstract: The initiation of detonation in high explosives (HEs) under shock loading is strongly influenced by the collapse of porosity and the subsequent formation of hotspots. As these materials are widely integrated with polymer binders to form polymer-bonded explosives, the collapse of pores at explosive-binder interfaces has received significant interest. Recent molecular dynamics (MD) simulations revealed that the shock-driven expansion of soft polymers (e.g. polystyrene) into a void produces greater pressure-volume (PV) work during recompression and higher local temperatures compared to pore collapse in neat HEs, ultimately expediting the onset of chemical reactions. Yet how pore collapse, interfacial phenomena, and binder chemistry jointly affect initiation remains insufficiently understood. We provide deeper insights into the role of polymer in hotspot evolution through reactive MD simulations, specifically characterizing its chemical behavior during the shock-induced collapse of voids at sizes approaching experimentally relevant length scales. We consider the HE 1,3,5,7-Tetranitro-1,3,5,7-tetrazocane (HMX) in a crystal-gap-crystal stack with polystyrene along the upstream surface. In the hydrodynamic collapse regime, hotspots nucleate at the material interfaces, with void length-dependence in PV work, shock temperature, and the onset of self-sustaining chemistry. Driven by shock-induced deformation and HMX decomposition, the binder undergoes backbone chain scission followed by extensive aromatic-backbone cross-bonding, akin to a graphite to diamond transformation. These reactions act as heat sinks, suppressing deflagration and ultimately inhibiting hotspot growth and coalescence between crystals. The simulations further link the polymer's chemical response to the shock-to-detonation transition in polymer-bonded explosives.

</details>


### [55] [Paramagnetic phase of MnTe$_2$: complete one phonon Raman spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28844)
*Taras Palasyuk,Dominik Kurzydłowski,Ashutosh S. Wadge,Rajmund Bacewicz,Madhavi H. Dalsaniya,Michał Wierzbicki,Daniel Jastrzebski,Andrzej Wiśniewski,Wojciech Szuszkiewicz,Igor Antoniazzi,Bruno Camargo,Maciej R. Molas,Adam Babiński,Krzysztof Zberecki,Cezariusz Jastrzebski*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究完整报道了MnTe₂顺磁相（paramagnetic phase）的一阶拉曼散射（first-order Raman scattering）谱，通过偏振、激发波长与温度依赖测量确认了理论预测的全部五个拉曼活性声子模式（A_g、E_g及三个T_g），并用密度泛函理论（DFT）计算加以佐证；同时发现近红外激发下2A_g倍频峰及最高频T_g模式的反常角度依赖。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MnTe₂在奈尔温度（Néel temperature, T_N ≈ 90 K）以下由非共面反铁磁序（noncoplanar antiferromagnetic ordering）诱导的新奇自旋织构（spin texture）带来非常规磁性，而该材料顺磁相的完整一阶拉曼声子谱此前缺失，尤其T_g对称性模式长期未被观测到。

Method: 采用偏振、激发波长和温度依赖的拉曼散射测量，系统表征五个群论（group theory）预言的声子模式；并结合密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算声子谱与低能反铁磁自旋构型；同时使用633 nm与785 nm长波长激发获取顺磁相的非弹性散射信号。

Result: 在室温拉曼谱约107 cm⁻¹处明确分辨出此前缺失的T_g模式，其频率仅比E_g模式高约1.5 cm⁻¹；DFT计算复现了观测到的拉曼活性声子谱并支持全部五个模式的指认，包括E_g与某一T_g振动的近简并（near-degeneracy），还揭示出与基态仅相差数meV/化学式单元的低能反铁磁自旋构型密集流形（manifold）。长波长激发下观测到约360 cm⁻¹的2A_g倍频峰（overtone），近红外增强吸收可能是平行偏振片下最高频T_g模式（183 cm⁻¹）散射强度反常角度依赖的成因。

Conclusion: 降至T_N以下时，所测声子性质基本未受初生的长程反铁磁序（long-range antiferromagnetic ordering）扰动，表明晶格振动与磁有序的耦合在该温区表现较弱。

Abstract: In this study we present a complete first-order Raman scattering spectrum of paramagnetic phase of MnTe$_2$- a material, where new type of spin texture induced by noncoplanar antiferromagnetic ordering below the Néel temperature ($T_N \approx 90$ K) gives rise to unconventional scenario of magnetism. Basic properties of all five phonon modes ($A_g$, $E_g$ and three $T_g$) predicted by the group theory have been successfully approached through polarization, excitation and temperature dependent measurements. In particular, the ever-missing phonon mode of $T_g$ symmetry has been unambiguously resolved in the room temperature Raman spectrum at approximately 107 cm$^{-1}$, just 1.5 cm$^{-1}$ higher in frequency compared to the $E_g$ mode. Density functional theory calculations reproduce the observed Raman-active phonon spectrum and support the assignment of all five first-order Raman modes, including the near-degeneracy of the $E_g$ and one of the $T_g$ vibrations. The calculations further reveal a dense manifold of low-energy antiferromagnetic spin configurations, separated from the ground state by only a few meV per formula unit. Furthermore, strong signals of inelastically scattered light from the paramagnetic (PM) phase of MnTe$_2$ were acquired with long wavelength excitations, i.e. 633 nm and 785 nm, revealing the $2A_g$ overtone at approx. 360 cm$^{-1}$. The enhanced absorption of the light in the near infrared range could be a putative reason behind the anomalous angular dependence of scattered intensity of the highest frequency $T_g$ phonon mode (183 cm$^{-1}$) measured in PM MnTe$_2$ with parallelly aligned polarizers. Finally, upon cooling just below the $T_N$, the measured properties of phonons remained basically unperturbed by the incipient long-range antiferromagnetic ordering.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电层状半导体（ferroelectric layered semiconductors）为在低维体系中研究激子与电子耦合现象提供了独特平台，但NbOI₂中激子输运行为及环境稳定性尚待阐明。

Method: 采用瞬态吸收显微镜（transient absorption microscopy）研究层状NbOI₂的激子输运，并考察其环境稳定性及制备与封装（encapsulation）条件。

Result: 观察到超快且强各向异性的激子扩散，沿非极性晶轴（nonpolar crystal axis）的扩散系数约160 cm² s⁻¹，属范德华半导体（van der Waals semiconductors）中报道的最高值之一；极性（polar）与非极性方向间存在约两倍的面内各向异性（in-plane anisotropy），反映NbOI₂本征低对称晶体结构。

Conclusion: NbOI₂是研究各向异性激子输运的有前景平台，并凸显界面保护（interface protection）对空气敏感范德华材料的关键作用。

Abstract: Ferroelectric layered semiconductors provide a unique platform for exploring coupled excitonic and electronic phenomena in reduced dimensions. Here, exciton transport in layered NbOI$_2$ is investigated using transient absorption microscopy. Ultrafast and strongly anisotropic exciton diffusion is observed. The diffusion coefficient of about 160 cm$^2$ s$^{-1}$ along the nonpolar crystal axis is among the highest reported for van der Waals semiconductors. The transport exhibits an approximately twofold in-plane anisotropy between the polar and nonpolar directions, reflecting the intrinsic low-symmetry crystal structure of NbOI$_2$. The environmental stability of NbOI$_2$ is further investigated, and fabrication and encapsulation conditions required to achieve long-term sample stability are identified. These results establish NbOI$_2$ as a promising platform for studying anisotropic exciton transport and highlight the critical role of interface protection in air-sensitive van der Waals materials.

</details>


### [57] [Strain-Induced Interfacial Bond Evolution and Facet-dependent Strain Response of Electronic Properties in Oxidized Silicon Nanowires](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28967)
*Yasir Mahmood,Daniel Wines,Viviana Dovale-Farelo,Kamal Choudhary,Lucas M Hale*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合反应力场分子动力学与第一性原理计算，揭示氧化硅纳米线（Si NWs）中氧化诱导的界面结构与晶面取向如何决定应变下的键演化及电子性质的晶面依赖响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硅纳米线（silicon nanowires, Si NWs）是下一代纳米电子器件的候选材料，应变可提升其性能；但硅-二氧化硅（Si-SiO₂）界面处的应变重分布与低价氧化物键合（suboxide bonding）如何共同影响力学与电子响应尚需原子尺度认识。

Method: 采用反应力场分子动力学（reactive force field molecular dynamics）模拟干法热氧化以生成化学上真实的 Si-SiO₂ 界面，并结合密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）对晶面特化模型进行计算。

Result: 界面 Si-Si 键因部分氧化态而伸长、键序降低，导致局部应力集中与优先断键；DFT 计算表明取向依赖的界面键合调控电子性质的晶面依赖应变响应。

Conclusion: 结果建立了界面低价氧化物化学（suboxide chemistry）、机械变形与电子结构之间的直接耦合，为分面纳米线氧化硅界面的应变调控行为提供原子尺度机理见解。

Abstract: Silicon nanowires (Si NWs) have emerged as promising candidates for next-generation nanoelectronic devices due to their tunable electronic properties, and compatibility with aggressive scaling requirements. The integration of strain further enhances their performance by modulating carrier mobility, band structure, and transport behavior without altering material chemistry. Within the NW, the silicon-silicon dioxide (Si-SiO$_{2}$) interface serves as a critical region where strain redistribution and suboxide bonding collectively influence the mechanical and electronic response. Here, we combine reactive force field molecular dynamics and first-principles calculations to investigate how oxidation-induced interfacial structure and crystallographic facet orientation govern strain-dependent bond evolution and facet-dependent strain response of electronic properties in oxidized Si NWs. Simulated dry thermal oxidation generates chemically realistic Si-SiO$_{2}$ interfaces, enabling direct characterization of bond order redistribution and stress gradient development across the interface under tensile strain. Interfacial Si-Si bonds exhibit elongation and reduced bond order associated with partial oxidation states, leading to localized stress concentration and preferential bond rupture. Complementary density functional theory calculations on facet-specific models reveal that orientation-dependent interfacial bonding modulates facet-dependent strain response of electronic properties. Together, these results establish a direct coupling between interfacial suboxide chemistry, mechanical deformation, and electronic structure, providing atomistic insight into strain-modulated behavior at oxidized silicon interfaces in faceted NWs.

</details>


### [58] [Hereditary Effective Atom Theory for Large-Scale Atomic Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28996)

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: HEAT 将有效原子理论（Effective Atom Theory）嵌入遗传算法，借助可微的有效原子表示实现大体系的全局结构搜索，并在数百至上千原子的缺陷与扭转双层体系中显著优于传统遗传算法基线。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 提出遗传有效原子理论（Hereditary Effective Atom Theory, HEAT），把有效原子理论集成进遗传算法，通过可微的有效原子表示（differentiable effective-atom representation）进行全局搜索；核心是直接叠加两个父代结构并对其弛豫的交叉（crossover）策略。

Conclusion: 连续的有效原子弛豫可改善对大规模组合结构空间的探索，表明该框架适用于大体系复杂原子结构的全局搜索。

Abstract: Predicting the atomic structure of large systems requires simultaneous exploration of atomic positions, species, and, generally, the number of atoms. The combinatorial growth of possible arrangements can make this search difficult even for standard genetic algorithms. We introduce Hereditary Effective Atom Theory (HEAT), which integrates Effective Atom Theory into a genetic algorithm to enable efficient global search through a differentiable effective-atom representation. Central to the method is a crossover scheme that directly superimposes two parent structures and relaxes the combined structure. HEAT outperforms conventional genetic-algorithm baselines in recovering a missing 240-atom H sublattice, predicting a 364-atom edge-dislocation core, and reconstructing twisted-bilayer $\mathrm{WSe_2}$ containing 1150 atoms from ptychographic constraints. Conclusion: 该研究系统刻画了结构稳定性与电子性质随半径和手性的演化，确立了映射方法的适用边界，并为手性磷烯纳米管的电子结构调控提供依据。

This work presents the first systematic study of the geometric and electronic properties of chiral PNTs. Using the Chiraltube software, a comprehensive set of 214 PNTs was generated and analyzed via a 2D zone-folding mapping approach. While the mapping method overestimates band gaps by directly inheriting monolayer features, it serves as a qualitative tool for specific chiral geometries-particularly armchair configurations and larger-radius tubes-by effectively capturing their subband density and dispersion profiles. Finally, this work details the evolution of structural stability and electronic properties as a function of radius and chirality, identifying clear direct-to-indirect band gap transitions driven by curvature effects, alongside metallic behavior in specific zigzag configurations.

</details>


### [60] [Disorder-Mediated Magnetism in Transition-Metal Dichalcogenide Monolayers: XS$_2$ and XSe$_2$ (X = Ti, V, Co, Mo, and Ru)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29235)

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TMD单层的磁性尤其是单层1T-VSe₂中磁性的本质尚不明确，本文旨在阐明其磁性起源与调控机制。
