Result: 非磁性Ti、Co、Mo、Ru二硫/二硒化物混溶可诱导磁性，而磁性VS₂或VSe₂与其他化合物混溶可完全抑制磁性，两种情形均可降低总能量；少量Co替代V会破坏1T-V(S,Se)₂的铁磁性；本征1T-VSe₂中存在与铁磁态近简并的反向局域V磁矩亚铁磁构型，其竞争可能抑制长程铁磁序。

Conclusion: 磁性与结构无序的能量代价降低源于费米能级处态密度被抑制，机制类似Peierls相变（Peierls transition）；这种电子驱动的费米能级附近能量降低可能在多种TMD单层中普遍存在。

Abstract: Magnetism in transition-metal dichalcogenide monolayers has attracted considerable attention, yet its nature remains elusive, particularly in monolayer 1T-VSe$_2$. Our first-principles calculations show that Stoner instability can drive ferromagnetism in XS$_2$ and XSe$_2$ monolayers (X = Ti, V, Co, Mo, and Ru), associated with a reduced density of states at the Fermi level. We demonstrate that mixing nonmagnetic Ti, Co, Mo, and Ru disulfides or diselenides can induce magnetism. Conversely, mixing magnetic VS$_2$ or VSe$_2$ with other compounds can completely suppress magnetism. Both scenarios can lower the total energy. Moreover, substituting a small number of V atoms with Co defects can also disrupt ferromagnetism in 1T-V(S,Se)$_2$. Even in pristine 1T-VSe$_2$, we find ferrimagnetic configurations with reversed local V moments that are nearly degenerate with the ferromagnetic state. Their competition may contribute to the suppression of long-range ferromagnetic order. This reduced energy penalty for magnetic and structural disorder arises from a suppressed density of states at the Fermi level, resembling the mechanism underlying the Peierls transition. Electronically driven energy lowering near the Fermi level may therefore be widespread among transition-metal dichalcogenide monolayers in various forms.

</details>


### [61] [First-principles modeling of ordering in Fe-rich Fe-Si alloys](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29386)
*Daniil M. Khodachenko,Andrei V. Ruban,Franco Moitzi,Oleg E. Peil,Vsevolod I. Razumovskiy*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合密度泛函理论（DFT）得到的有效团簇相互作用（ECI）与统计热力学模拟，揭示bcc Fe-Si合金（≤20 at% Si）中磁性、成分与温度耦合如何决定原子有序化行为，并给出一套可预测的从头算（ab initio）描述框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 富铁Fe-Si合金的原子有序显著影响其磁性与力学行为，但既有实验相图仅提供长程有序的一般特征，具体原子构型及其驱动力仍属推测，因此需要从电子结构层面澄清有序化的驱动机制。

Method: 采用密度泛函理论（DFT）计算获得有效团簇相互作用（ECI），并据此开展统计热力学建模；结合基于磁性分辨ECI的蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）模拟，考察bcc Fe-Si合金在20 at% Si以下磁性与温度对有序化的影响。

Result: 分析表明有序化受磁有序、晶格膨胀与Si浓度之间的强耦合支配，特定ECI壳层随磁性状态改变而变号或改变量级；完全有序的铁磁态下以D0₃有序为主导，而顺磁态高温下则促进B2型有序。基于磁性分辨ECI的蒙特卡罗模拟再现了实验相平衡与短程有序特征。

Conclusion: 通过显式关联磁性状态、有效相互作用与宏观相行为，该工作建立了富铁Fe-Si合金有序化的从头算预测框架，并强调磁性在该体系有序现象中的决定性作用。

Abstract: The atomic ordering in Fe-rich Fe-Si alloys strongly influences their magnetic properties and mechanical behavior. While general features of long-range atomic ordering are known from the experimentally established phase diagram in this composition range, detailed atomic configurations and driving forces behind them remain speculative. Here, we address this gap by using effective cluster interactions (ECI) obtained from density functional theory (DFT) calculations and subsequent statistical thermodynamics modeling to unravel the interplay between magnetism, composition, and temperature in body-centered cubic (bcc) Fe-Si alloys up to 20 at% Si. Our analysis reveals that the ordering behavior is governed by a strong coupling between magnetic order, lattice expansion, and Si concentration, with specific ECI shells switching sign or magnitude as the magnetic state changes, which is reflected in the phase equilibria and ordering behavior of Fe-Si alloys. In particular, we demonstrate that D0$_3$ ordering is, in fact, primary in the totally ordered ferromagnetic state, while the B2 type of ordering is promoted in the paramagnetic state at elevated temperatures. The temperature-dependent evolution of these ordering patterns, obtained from Monte Carlo simulations based on the magnetically resolved ECIs, reproduces the experimentally observed phase equilibria and short-range order characteristics. By explicitly linking the magnetic state with the effective interactions and macroscopic phase behavior, this work establishes a predictive framework for an ab initio-based description of ordering in Fe-rich Fe-Si alloys and highlights the decisive role of magnetism in ordering phenomena in this system.

</details>


### [62] [Super-tetragonal ferroelectric barium titanate through anionic overstoichiometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29401)
*Jingtian Zhao,Johanna van Gent González,Iñigo Robredo-Magro,Ewout van der Veer,Daniel A. Chaney,Jorge Íñiguez-González,Beatriz Noheda,Martin F. Sarott*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过高非平衡脉冲激光沉积（pulsed laser deposition）在钛酸钡（BaTiO₃）中稳定出超四方相（super-tetragonal phase），其机理是过量氧原子引入形成过氧键（peroxide bond），使c/a比高达1.08，并带来更强的铁电极化与更优的器件性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电氧化物（ferroelectric oxides）薄膜的物性调控长期依赖衬底施加的外延应变（epitaxial strain），但该方法受限于双轴应变状态与可用衬底数量，因此需要超越传统外延生长（epitaxy）的调控途径。

Method: 采用高非平衡条件下的脉冲激光沉积（pulsed laser deposition）这一非常规生长路线，对铁电BaTiO₃薄膜进行缺陷工程（defect engineering），通过向晶胞中插入过量氧原子形成过氧键来稳定超四方相。

Result: 所稳定的超四方相长宽比（aspect ratio）最高达1.08；相较常规类块体（bulk-like）BaTiO₃薄膜，该相表现出增强的铁电极化（ferroelectric polarization）、降低的矫顽场（coercivity）、更低的漏电流（leakage current）以及提升的循环耐久性（cycling endurance）。

Conclusion: 结果表明，非平衡生长（non-equilibrium growth）可用于复杂氧化物（complex oxides）的缺陷工程，以获得涌现相（emergent phases）并调控薄膜物理性质，从而拓展传统外延的范围。

Abstract: Ferroelectric oxides, characterized by an electrical polarization that is switchable with an electric field, are an appealing class of materials for energy-efficient computing, electronics, and photonics. In thin films, the properties of ferroelectrics are commonly tuned using substrate-imposed epitaxial strain, an approach limited to biaxial strain states and a finite number of available substrates. Here, we demonstrate that is is possible to move beyond conventional epitaxial growth by using pulsed laser deposition under highly non-equilibrium conditions. Specifically, we show that this unconventional processing route allows us to stabilize a super-tetragonal phase of ferroelectric $\text{BaTiO}_3$. The key to this stabilization lies in the insertion of surplus oxygen atoms into the unit cell, resulting in peroxide bond formation that give rise to an aspect ratio of as high as 1.08. Notably, this super-tetragonal phase exhibits an enhanced ferroelectric polarization, reduced coercivity, lower leakage currents, and increased cycling endurance compared to conventional bulk-like $\text{BaTiO}_3$ films. These results, thus, showcase the potential of non-equilibrium growth for defect engineering to obtain emergent phases in complex oxides and tune their physical properties in thin films expanding the scope of traditional epitaxy.

</details>


### [63] [Microscopic origin of field-induced multiferroicity in Ni3TeO6](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29420)
*J. Lass,R. Toft-Petersen,A. A. Turrini,Ø. S. Fjellvåg,B. Decrausaz,K. M. L. Krighaar,R. Sibille,H. Jacobsen,M. E. Kamminga,C. R. Y. Andersen,D. Sheptyakov,J. Ollivier,U. B. Hansen,M. Enderle,A. M. Läuchli,H. M. Rønnow,K. Lefmann,D. G. Mazzone*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用中子散射（neutron scattering）技术揭示Ni3TeO6多铁性转变（multiferroic transition）的微观起源，指出有限Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya交换作用产生的非互易手性自旋波（non-reciprocal chiral spin waves）在磁场中软化并凝聚，从而同时改变磁结构、电极化与离子位移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续追求更小、更快、更节能的技术，推动凝聚态研究（condensed-matter research）转向在单一相中整合多种响应的材料；多铁性材料（multiferroic materials）中序参量间的交叉耦合尤具吸引力，而Ni3TeO6的磁场诱导无滞后转变同时影响磁结构、电极化（electric polarization）与声子频率，其微观机制尚待阐明。

Method: 结合多种中子散射（neutron scattering）技术对Ni3TeO6进行实验研究。

Result: 确认存在有限的Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya交换项，由此产生非互易手性自旋波（non-reciprocal chiral spin waves），且该自旋波在磁场中软化；其凝聚改变磁结构，进而调控电极化并驱动离子位移，使磁、电与晶格自由度相互耦合。

Conclusion: 中子散射是阐明多铁性材料中微观相互作用的有力手段，可为下一代电子器件（electronic devices）的设计提供依据。

Abstract: The continued demand for smaller, faster, and more energy-efficient technologies has driven condensed-matter research towards materials combining multiple responses within a single phase. Particularly interesting examples are multiferroic materials featuring cross-couplings between order parameters. Ni3TeO6 is a prominent member of this material class, exhibiting a hysteresis-free transition under magnetic field which simultaneously affects the magnetic structure, electric polarization, and phonon frequencies. Here, we uncover the microscopic origin of this multiferroic transition using a combination of neutron scattering techniques. We establish the presence of a finite Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya exchange term giving rise to non-reciprocal, chiral spin waves which soften in magnetic field. Their condensation alters the magnetic structure which controls the electric polarization and drives an ionic displacement, thereby coupling the magnetic, electric, and lattice degrees of freedom. Our study demonstrates that neutron scattering is a powerful technique to elucidate microscopic interactions in multiferroic materials, informing the design of next-generation electronic devices.

</details>


### [64] [Two-dimensional structures formed by pair potentials: An analytical and numerical study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29421)
*Shota Ono*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文通过解析与数值方法研究薄膜过剩能（excess energy）随层数 $N$ 的变化，发现在单层极限下、原子间相互作用为长程时，过剩能原有的 $1/N$ 标度律（scaling law）会失效，表明仅凭对势（pair potentials）即可形成非范德华（non-vdW）二维材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解薄膜过剩能的厚度依赖性并识别其偏离厚膜标度律的行为，对预测由非层状结构衍生的二维（2D）材料（即非范德华（non-vdW）二维材料）的形成至关重要。

Method: 在假定对势（pair potentials）的前提下，解析推导薄膜过剩能作为层数 $N$ 的函数，并采用 Lennard-Jones 势与 Morse 势进行数值模拟验证解析结果。

Result: 当原子间相互作用为长程时，过剩能的 $1/N$ 标度律（scaling law）在单层极限下发生失效；数值模拟结果与解析结论一致。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，非范德华（non-vdW）二维结构可以仅由对势（pair potentials）形成。

Abstract: Understanding the thickness dependence of the excess energy of thin films and identifying the deviation from the scaling law that holds for thicker films are crucial for predicting the formation of two-dimensional (2D) materials derived from nonlayered structures, commonly referred to as non-van der Waals (non-vdW) 2D materials. Here, we analytically study the excess energy of thin films as a function of the number of layers $N$, assuming pair potentials. We show that the $1/N$ scaling law of the excess energy breaks down in the monolayer limit when the interatomic interactions are long-ranged. The analytical results are confirmed by numerical simulations using the Lennard-Jones and Morse potentials. The present work suggests that non-vdW 2D structures can be formed by pair potentials alone.

</details>


### [65] [Large anisotropic magnetoresistance in paramagnetic Fe-doped Ta4SiTe4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29463)
*Keita Nagasawa,Ryutaro Okuma,Jun-ichi Yamaura,Yoshihiko Okamoto*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作表明，在顺磁（paramagnetic）Fe掺杂Ta4SiTe4中，稀磁矩的磁场诱导各向异性极化即可产生大各向异性磁阻（AMR），无需依赖长程磁有序（long-range magnetic order），8 K、10 T下AMR比达-33%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大各向异性磁阻（anisotropic magnetoresistance, AMR）通常依赖磁有序来确定易磁化方向并耦合电荷输运，材料设计空间受限于磁有序体系；本文旨在探索无序顺磁体系中实现大AMR的可能。

Method: 以准一维范德华窄带隙半导体（quasi-one-dimensional van der Waals narrow-gap semiconductor）Fe掺杂Ta4SiTe4为对象，开展磁化测量并用有效S = 1/2模型拟合各向异性g因子；同时在电流沿c轴条件下比较掺杂与未掺杂样品对不同磁场取向的磁阻响应，并用最小自旋无序模型（minimal spin-disorder model）解释温度依赖。

Result: 磁化测量显示直至2 K的居里型顺磁性（Curie-like paramagnetism），c轴为易磁化轴，各向异性g因子比g_c/g_ab = 1.43(1)。Fe掺杂样品在B//c时呈大负磁阻、B//ab时仅弱正响应，未掺杂样品两种取向磁阻均很小；AMR比在8 K、10 T达-33%，并呈非单调温度依赖，可被最小自旋无序模型定性重现。

Conclusion: 通过调控稀磁离子的局域环境，可在无长程磁有序条件下获得大顺磁AMR，将AMR的材料设计空间拓展至磁有序体系之外。

Abstract: Large anisotropic magnetoresistance (AMR) usually relies on magnetic ordering, which fixes a preferred moment orientation and couples it to charge transport. Here, we demonstrate that pronounced AMR can instead arise from the anisotropic magnetic-field-induced polarization of dilute local moments in paramagnetic Fe-doped Ta4SiTe4, a quasi-one-dimensional van der Waals narrow-gap semiconductor. Magnetization measurements reveal Curie-like paramagnetism down to 2 K, with the c axis as the magnetic easy axis. The magnetization is described by an effective S = 1/2 model with an anisotropic g-factor ratio of g_c/g_ab = 1.43(1). With the electrical current applied along the c axis, the Fe-doped sample exhibits a large negative magnetoresistance for B//c and only a weak positive response for B//ab, whereas the undoped sample shows little magnetoresistance for either field orientation. The resulting AMR ratio reaches -33% at 8 K and 10 T and exhibits a nonmonotonic temperature dependence qualitatively captured by a minimal spin-disorder model. These results show that engineering the local environment of dilute magnetic ions provides a route to large paramagnetic AMR without long-range magnetic order, extending the materials design space for AMR beyond magnetically ordered systems.

</details>


### [66] [Microstructural evolution during isothermal tempering of a highly supersaturated metastable austenitic state of a PM high-speed steel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29599)
*Stefan Müller,Santiago Benito,Hendric Schaefer,Yujiao Li,Leonhard Gertlowski,Oliver Schenk,Ulrich Hagemann,Christoph Broeckmann,Sebastian Weber*

Conclusion: 等温保温促进界面化学重组，支持外延、界面控制的碳化物长大与化学梯度碳化物边缘的形成；该结果深化了对碳化物球化机制的理解，并为通过热处理定制碳化物形貌及相应力学性能奠定基础。

Abstract: This study investigates the microstructural evolution of the powder metallurgical high-speed steel PM HS 3-3-4 during isothermal holding within the transformation gap between pearlite and bainite formation. Isothermal holding increased carbide circularity, carbide volume fraction, and hardness, while MatCalc calculations predict a strong thermodynamic driving force for additional carbide precipitation. The analytic techniques revealed a compositional gradient of approximately 15 nm at the carbide-matrix interface in the isothermally treated condition, compared with a sharp transition of only 2-3 nm in the directly quenched state. The associated solute depletion of the surrounding austenite provides a plausible explanation for its reduced stability, reflected by an increase in the martensite start temperature. The combined results demonstrate that isothermal holding promotes interfacial chemical reorganization, offering new insights into carbide spheroidization and providing a basis for tailoring carbide morphology and the resulting mechanical properties through heat treatment.

O'Mahoney,Rishav Khatiwada,Jing Zhao,Charis Horn,Evan Stocker,Benjamin Kostroun,Emilio Corrales,Christoph Klewe,Alpha T. N'Diaye,Yuri Suzuki,Jean J. Heremans,Frederick M. Abstract: Single-crystalline noncollinear antiferromagnets, such as Mn$_3$Sn, are actively explored as memory media, but they generally require high-temperature growth that is incompatible with industrial-scale device fabrication. ### [68] [Symmetry-Based Parameter Efficient Low-Rank Fine-Tuning for High-Throughput Calculation of Lattice Thermal Conductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29634)
*Rustam Arabov,Konstantin Sozykin,Nikita Rybin,Alexander Shapeev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 提出基于对称性的少样本微调策略（SBFS-FT），结合MatterSim 1M通用机器学习势与玻尔兹曼输运方程，实现同晶体对称性化合物数据集的高通量晶格热导率（LTC）精确计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热电、微电子与光伏材料研发需要准确且高效的晶格热导率（lattice thermal conductivity, LTC）预测，而大规模化合物数据集的高通量计算面临精度与效率的双重挑战。

Method: 对MatterSim 1M通用机器学习原子间势（machine-learned interatomic potential）采用基于低秩适配（low-rank adaptation）的对称性少样本微调策略（symmetry-based few-shot fine-tuning, SBFS-FT），在少量同晶体对称性化合物上微调后，结合玻尔兹曼输运方程（Boltzmann transport equation）求解计算LTC。

Result: 在半赫斯勒化合物（空间群216）、空间群225材料及尖晶石（空间群227）数据集上，微调后LTC计算与参考结果（矩张量势与polyMLP）的平均绝对相对偏差比未微调MatterSim 1M小2-3倍，精度与逐化合物单独微调相当；对100个化合物数据集，计算时间减少约5-7倍。

Conclusion: SBFS-FT可在共享晶体对称性的大规模化合物数据集上快速生成适用于精确LTC计算的势函数，在保持精度的同时显著提升高通量计算效率。

Abstract: Accurate and efficient prediction of lattice thermal conductivity (LTC) is essential for developing materials for thermoelectric, microelectronic, and photovoltaic applications. Here, we present a methodology for high-throughput calculation of LTC across large datasets of compounds sharing a common crystal symmetry. Our approach combines fine-tuning of the MatterSim 1M universal machine-learned interatomic potential with LTC calculations based on the solution of the Boltzmann transport equation. We introduce a symmetry-based few-shot fine-tuning strategy (SBFS-FT) based on low-rank adaptation. In this approach, MatterSim 1M is fine-tuned on a small, carefully selected set of compounds that share a common crystal symmetry. The resulting potential is then applicable for accurate LTC calculations for compounds with this crystal symmetry, even if they were not included in the fine-tuning set. We tested our methodology on datasets of half-Heusler compounds (space group 216), materials with space group 225, and spinels (space group 227). We demonstrated that LTC calculations with MatterSim 1M fine-tuned using SBFS-FT exhibited a mean absolute relative difference from the reference results (obtained using moment tensor potentials and polyMLPs) 2-3 times smaller than that achieved with the non-fine-tuned MatterSim 1M potential. Moreover, the accuracy of LTC calculations with MatterSim 1M after SBFS-FT was comparable to that achieved using the MatterSim 1M potentials individually fine-tuned for each compound. Importantly, SBFS-FT enabled generation of the potential suitable for accurate LTC calculations across large compound datasets substantially faster than fine-tuning of MatterSim 1M for each compound individually, reducing the computational time by approximately a factor of 5-7 for a dataset of 100 compounds.

</details>


### [69] [Zero-group-velocity modes in textured steel plates: experiments and elastic modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29693)
*C. Grünsteidl,C. Kerschbaummayr,G. Yan,G. Watzl,F. Rudinger,E. Scherleitner,M. Sonnleitner,C. Hoflehner*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究以零群速度（zero-group-velocity, ZGV）共振作为非接触超声探针，表征织构钢板中的弹性各向异性（elastic anisotropy）。方向性激光超声测量与基于XRD织构的有效刚度张量预测吻合良好，并揭示全硬态中位错对弹性响应的贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要一种非接触手段来探测织构金属板材的弹性各向异性，并检验织构与缺陷（如位错）对宏观弹性响应的相对作用。

Method: 对70%冷轧Ti稳定化无间隙原子钢（interstitial-free steel）在全硬态与再结晶态进行方向性激光超声（laser-ultrasonic）ZGV测量；将实验结果与由X射线衍射（X-ray diffraction, XRD）导出的有效弹性刚度张量预测对比，采用Voigt-Reuss-Hill估计与微结构分辨有限元均匀化（finite-element homogenization，含KUBC/SUBC界限）。

Result: 经典Voigt-Reuss-Hill估计与有限元均匀化均能较好重现两种状态下ZGV频率的角度依赖关系，且有限元KUBC/SUBC界限明显窄于经典Voigt-Reuss界限。全硬态仅在经过回复处理（recovery treatment）后才与基于织构的模型一致，表明全硬材料中的位错对弹性响应有贡献，该解释得到理论估计的支持。

Conclusion: 可辨识性分析（identifiability analysis）表明，方向性ZGV测量至少可获取刚度张量分量的三种独立可观测组合；据此提出了将该方法用于再结晶监测（recrystallization monitoring）的建议。

Abstract: Zero-group-velocity (ZGV) resonances are investigated as a non-contact ultrasonic probe of elastic anisotropy in textured steel plates. Directional laser-ultrasonic measurements were performed on 70% cold-rolled Ti-stabilized interstitial-free steel plates in the full-hard and recrystallized condition. Experimental results were compared with predictions based on effective elastic stiffness tensors derived from X-ray diffraction measurements. Classical Voigt-Reuss-Hill estimates and microstructure-resolved finite-element homogenization reproduce the measured angular dependence of the ZGV frequencies well for both states, with the finite-element KUBC/SUBC bounds considerably narrower than the classical Voigt-Reuss bounds. For the full-hard state, agreement with the texture-based model is obtained only after a recovery treatment. This indicates that dislocations present in the full-hard material contribute to its elastic response, an interpretation that is further supported by theoretical estimations. An identifiability analysis shows that the directional ZGV measurements provide access to at least three independently observable combinations of the stiffness tensor components, and concluding suggestions for application in recrystallization monitoring are provided.

</details>


### [70] [Framework to identify defect-insensitive electron transport in topological semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29848)
*Lily Jade Joyce,Michelle M. Kelley,Timothy Philicelli,Ching-tzu Chen,Ravishankar Sundararaman*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种基于非平衡格林函数（NEGF）的系统化方法，通过对所有可能的单原子空位缺陷集合的透射矩阵本征谱分析，快速评估拓扑半金属（topological semimetals）中电子输运的缺陷敏感性，并揭示CoSi(001)、PtAl(001)和NbAs中的高缺陷不敏感输运模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑半金属有望替代传统金属作为未来半导体芯片互连（interconnects）材料，因其电阻率可能随线宽缩小而降低且对缺陷处电子散射（electron scattering）敏感性较低。然而现有基于NEGF的第一性原理评估依赖特定缺陷几何结构，难以系统化并在不同材料间比较。

Method: 构建不依赖材料特定缺陷几何的NEGF评估框架，针对所有可能单原子空位缺陷的非相干集合（incoherent ensemble）分析透射矩阵（transmission matrix）的本征谱（eigen-spectrum），以本征值接近1识别缺陷不敏感输运模式。

Result: 缺陷散射鲁棒性对表面取向（surface orientation）和费米能级（Fermi level）高度敏感；仅在CoSi(001)与PtAl(001)特定费米能级范围内的单一输运模式波矢上发现近乎完全的缺陷不敏感性；NbAs中因自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）而出现的某些非孤立模式（non-isolated modes）也表现出高不敏感性，这些模式此前因淹没于总透射中而未被识别。

Conclusion: 该方法改进了现有NEGF技术的先进水平，通过缺陷构建的标准化实现材料间的无偏比较（unbiased evaluation），为未来纳米尺度互连用拓扑半金属候选材料的评估提供工具。

Abstract: Topological semimetals are of increasing interest to replace conventional metals for interconnects in future semiconductor chips, due to their potential to exhibit decreasing resistivity with reducing wire dimensions and a lower sensitivity to electron scattering at defects. First-principles evaluation of promising topological semimetals for interconnects typically employ non-equilibrium Green's functions (NEGF) techniques to estimate defect scattering, but such calculations for specific defect geometries are difficult to systematize and compare between disparate materials. Here, we develop an NEGF-based approach to systematically and rapidly evaluate the defect sensitivity of electron transport in materials without depending on material-specific defect geometries. Specifically, we analyze the eigen-spectrum of the transmission matrix for an incoherent ensemble of all-possible single-atom vacancy defects, which clearly reveals eigenvalues close to 1 for defect-insensitive transport modes. Applied to the leading candidates, we show that this robustness to defect scattering is extremely sensitive to surface orientation and Fermi level for each material. We find nearly-perfect insensitivity to defects only for wavevectors with single transport modes within specific Fermi level ranges in CoSi(001) and PtAl(001). Importantly, we also find high insensitivity for certain non-isolated modes in NbAs that emerge specifically due to spin-orbit coupling; these modes could not be identified previously as they were buried in the total transmission. These non-isolated modes have been buried in previous studies of total transmission, and can now be directly compared to other materials due to our standardization of defect-creation. This technique improves the state-of-the-art NEGF method and enables unbiased evaluation of future topological semimetal candidates for nanoscale interconnects.

</details>


### [71] [Robust Anomalous Hall Effect in Epitaxial Cubic D0$_3$-Fe$_3$Ge Nanoplates Grown by Chemical Vapor Deposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29858)
*Mingxuan Chen,Chao Chen,Siqi Jiang,Shuxiang Qiao,Hongyan Lu,Di Zhang,Jiabei Huang,Fuzhuo Lian,Zhaochen Qu,Siqin Wang,Ziyi Wang,Shuai Zhang,Yulu Liu,Ronghua Liu,Xiaoxiang Xi,Renjun Du,Lei Wang,Geliang Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作首次采用化学气相沉积（chemical vapor deposition, CVD）合成单晶立方 D0$_3$-Fe$_3$Ge 纳米片，揭示界面约 4.16% 的巨大压应变及其对能带结构与反常霍尔效应（anomalous Hall effect, AHE）的增强作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁基铁磁材料因高居里温度与由贝里曲率（Berry curvature）驱动的显著反常霍尔效应而成为自旋电子学（spintronics）的候选体系，但高质量单晶薄膜制备困难，晶界散射等问题限制了其表征与应用。

Method: 通过 CVD 合成单晶立方 D0$_3$-Fe$_3$Ge 纳米片，并借助结构表征、二次谐波产生（second-harmonic generation, SHG）、SQUID 磁测量、磁输运测量与密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）计算进行综合分析。

Result: 结构表征确认立方晶格与化学计量纯度，界面存在约 4.16% 的巨大压应变并向表面逐渐弛豫，SHG 信号提示界面应变破坏了立方相的中心对称性；样品表现出稳健的室温铁磁性与磁各向异性，AHE 在远高于室温下仍保持稳定，其标度关系表明由本征贝里曲率机制主导；DFT 显示应变诱导能带重构并在费米能级附近形成密集自旋轨道耦合能隙，使反常霍尔电导率加倍并与实验吻合。

Conclusion: 该研究实现了非层状铁基铁磁材料单晶立方相的可控 CVD 生长，并表明外延应变可作为调控贝里曲率相关输运性质的有效手段，为自旋电子学应用提供了新平台。

Abstract: The iron-based ferromagnetic materials have emerged as a promising system for spintronics due to their high Curie temperatures and significant anomalous Hall effect (AHE) driven by Berry curvature. While there have been investigations into the epitaxial growth of non-layered iron-based ferromagnetic materials, the realization of high-quality single crystal thin films continues to be a formidable obstacle. Meanwhile, the issues such as grain boundary scattering further hinder their characterization and application. Here, we report the first chemical vapor deposition (CVD) synthesis of single-crystalline cubic D0$_3$-Fe$_3$Ge nanoplates. This compound represents a substantial departure from the conventional hexagonal phases typically observed in the Fe-Ge system. Structural characterizations confirm the cubic lattice and stoichiometric purity of the crystals. Structural analysis identifies a strong epitaxial effect, resulting in a giant compressive strain of ~4.16% at the interface that relaxes gradually toward the surface. The second-harmonic generation (SHG) signals might further reveal that such interfacial strain disrupted the inherent centro-symmetry of the cubic phase. SQUID magnetometry shows robust room-temperature ferromagnetism and clear magnetic anisotropy. Magneto-transport measurements reveal a robust AHE that remains stable at temperatures far exceeding room temperature. The scaling observed between the anomalous Hall resistance and longitudinal resistance strongly suggests that this effect is dominated by the intrinsic Berry curvature mechanism. Furthermore, density functional theory (DFT) calculations reveal that the huge epitaxial strain induces band reconstructions and creates dense spin-orbit coupling gaps near the Fermi level, effectively doubling the anomalous Hall conductivity to match our experimental observations.

</details>


### [72] [Automated Materials Data and Knowledge Extraction using Machine Learning and Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29895)
*Aravindan Kamatchi Sundaram,B. Pabitramohan Prusty,Rohit Batra*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该综述梳理了从规则管线、自然语言处理（NLP）、Transformer编码器到大语言模型（LLM）与智能体系统（agentic systems）的材料信息抽取方法演进，并评估其数据集质量、抽取精度与计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年大量研究致力于从科学文献中直接抽取材料数据与材料知识，需要系统总结方法演进并评估其可靠性与可扩展性。

Method: 按方法谱系回顾启发式规则管线（rule-based pipelines）、NLP模型、Transformer编码器及LLM与智能体系统，覆盖文本、表格与图像多模态信息抽取。

Result: 这些进展支撑了可查询材料数据库与知识图谱（knowledge graphs）的构建，并催生材料科学问答、AI共同科学家（AI co-scientists）、科研辅助聊天机器人及与自主实验室（autonomous laboratories）结合的闭环优化与发现等新能力。

Conclusion: 综述批判性评估了抽取数据的准确性、计算成本与方法优劣，提出可扩展抽取管线的实践建议，并指出样本级“结构—性能—工艺—服役”四面体（structure-property-processing-performance tetrahedron）抽取、可靠基准数据集（benchmark datasets）稀缺及非标准材料术语消歧等开放挑战。

Abstract: Over the past decade, substantial efforts have been devoted to developing machine learning (ML) methods for extracting materials data and, where possible, materials knowledge directly from the scientific literature. This review traces the evolution of these methods from heuristic, rule-based pipelines through natural language processing (NLP) models and transformer-based encoders to, lastly, large language models (LLMs) and agentic systems capable of extracting materials information across multiple modalities, including text, tables, and figures. Beside construction of large, queryable materials databases and knowledge graphs that support downstream materials discovery, we discuss how these advances have enabled new capabilities, such as, generic question answering across materials science, AI co-scientists and chatbots for research assistance, and closed-loop optimization and discovery when integrated with autonomous laboratories. We critically assess the datasets generated using these approaches, their extraction accuracy and computational costs, and the respective strengths and limitations of different methodologies, and even provide practical guidelines for developing more reliable and scalable materials data extraction pipelines. Finally, we identify key open challenges, including the extraction of full materials structure-property-processing-performance tetrahedron at the sample level, the scarcity of reliable benchmark datasets for systematic evaluation, and the resolution of non-standard materials terminology across different document modalities.

</details>


### [73] [Electronic, structural and magnetic properties of goldene nanoribbons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30010)
*Fabrício M. de Vasconcelos,Kleuton A. L. Lima,Paloma V. Silva,Eduardo C. Girão,Alexandre C. Dias,Douglas S. Galvão,Luiz A. R. Junior*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）研究了金烯纳米带（goldene nanoribbons, GOLDNRs）的稳定性、电子与磁学性质，发现其电子结构强烈依赖于边缘手性（edge chirality）与带宽，可呈半导体性或金属性，且边缘区域为电子活性位点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维金烯（goldene）已被实验实现，被视为超越三维块体金的潜在下一代器件材料；而准一维（quasi-one-dimensional）衍生结构可能表现出与二维体系显著不同的性质，故需系统探究金烯纳米带的行为。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算方法，考察金烯基纳米带的稳定性、电子结构及磁学性质，分析边缘手性与带宽对电子结构的影响。

Result: 纳米带的电子结构强烈依赖边缘手性与带宽：依边界构型不同，体系可表现为带隙约0.77 eV与0.04 eV的半导体行为，或保持金属特性；边缘区域作为电子活性位点，其化学反应活性可能显著高于中心区域。随带宽增大，无限金烯片的金属特性逐渐恢复，量子限域（quantum confinement）与边缘效应对中心区域的影响减弱。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了通过横向限域（lateral confinement）调控金烯物理性质的途径，凸显其在纳米级场效应晶体管（field-effect transistors）、自旋电子器件（spintronic devices）与催化（catalysis）领域的应用潜力。

Abstract: Recently, two-dimensional goldene, a single-atomic-layer gold sheet, has been experimentally realized and is expected to be a promising material for next-generation devices, offering new possibilities beyond three-dimensional bulk gold. Because quasi-one-dimensional derivatives can exhibit properties markedly different from their two-dimensional counterparts, we investigate the stability, electronic, and magnetic properties of goldene-based nanoribbons (GOLDNRs) using first-principles computational methods. Our findings demonstrate that the electronic structure of these nanoribbons depends strongly on both edge chirality and ribbon width. Depending on the boundary configuration, the systems can exhibit semiconducting behavior with electronic band gaps of approximately 0.77 eV and 0.04 eV, or retain metallic characteristics. Additionally, the edge regions act as electronically active sites that may significantly enhance chemical reactivity relative to the central regions. As the ribbon width increases, the metallic characteristics of the infinite goldene sheet are progressively recovered, while the influence of quantum confinement and edge effects on the central region diminishes. These results provide important insights into tailoring the physical properties of goldene through lateral confinement, highlighting its potential for applications in nanoscale field-effect transistors, spintronic devices, and catalysis.

</details>


### [74] [Methane and ethane adsorption on calcite surfaces: effects of surface morphology from molecular dynamics and 3D-RISM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30030)
*Felipe Silva Carvalho,Tyler Luchko,Zhen-Gang Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作结合分子动力学（molecular dynamics, MD）模拟与三维参考相互作用位点模型（three-dimensional reference interaction site model, 3D-RISM），研究甲烷（methane）与乙烷（ethane）在含台阶缺陷（step defects）方解石（calcite）表面的吸附，并考察碳酸根重取向（carbonate reorientation）的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 烷烃（alkanes）在矿物表面的吸附对页岩储层（shale reservoirs）油气开采等工业过程具有重要意义，但真实表面普遍存在缺陷，其对吸附行为的影响尚待阐明。

Method: 采用MD模拟（分别施加与不施加位置约束（positional restraints）以考察碳酸根重取向效应）与3D-RISM计算（在模拟结构及弛豫晶体的能量最小化构型上进行），比较台阶缺陷区与平坦区的吸附行为。

Result: 两种方法均显示台阶缺陷处的吸附较平坦区增强，且甲烷的过量吸附（excess adsorption）高于乙烷；弛豫台阶边缘处的碳酸根重取向降低了特定台阶位点的局域密度峰，但MD给出的积分过量吸附与受约束表面相当。3D-RISM预测的峰最大值较低而过量吸附较大，但能以较低计算成本重现空间组织与总体吸附趋势。

Conclusion: 结果凸显表面非均匀性（surface heterogeneity）在流体-矿物相互作用（fluid-mineral interactions）中的重要性，并表明3D-RISM可作为复杂表面几何下吸附半定量筛选（semi-quantitative screening）的高效工具。

Abstract: Understanding the adsorption of alkanes on mineral surfaces is important for industrial processes such as oil and gas recovery from shale reservoirs. Previous studies have examined the adsorption of functionalized and non-functionalized alkanes on calcite, as well as adsorption under confinement. However, real surfaces commonly exhibit defects that can influence adsorption behavior. In this work, molecular dynamics (MD) simulations and the three-dimensional reference interaction site model (3D-RISM) are used to investigate the adsorption of methane and ethane on calcite surfaces with step defects. MD simulations were carried out with and without positional restraints to investigate the effects of carbonate reorientation. 3D-RISM calculations were performed on these structures and on energy-minimized configurations of the relaxed crystal. Both methods show adsorption enhanced at step defects relative to flat regions, with methane exhibiting higher excess adsorption than ethane. Carbonate reorientation at the relaxed step edges reduces the local density peaks at specific step sites in both methods, while the integrated excess adsorption from MD remains comparable to that of the restrained surface. 3D-RISM predicts lower maxima for the highest density peaks and larger excess adsorption than MD, but reproduces the spatial organization and overall adsorption trends at a fraction of the computational cost. These results highlight the importance of surface heterogeneity in fluid-mineral interactions and demonstrate that 3D-RISM is an efficient tool for semi-quantitative screening of adsorption across complex surface geometries

</details>

### [75] [The Octonionic Rational Wave Numbers: Non-Associative Sequence Algebras, Moufang Loops, and Exceptional $G_2$ Symmetries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28921)
*Terence R. Smith*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该文将离散有理波数推广至八元数（octonions，$\mathbb{O}$）上，构造性地由两个标量原语生成 $\mathbb{C}^\mathbb{Z},\mathbb{H}^\mathbb{Z},\mathbb{O}^\mathbb{Z}$，得到可除 Moufang 环路（Moufang loop），并以三项定理给出 $G_2$ 六重分岔、代数相位抖动与有理环路闭合的结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作（arXiv:2609.23233）中的离散有理波数限于较低维数域；若直接往更高维数推广需人为引入新的虚单位，缺乏统一构造基础。本文旨在以构造性方式统一复数、四元数与八元数上的有理波数空间，从而为非结合几何（non-associative geometry）与基本物理对称性提供自主的离散代数基础。

Method: 不假定更高虚单位，仅以坐标推进 $E_Z$ 与相位标定 $E_{\bar{1}}$ 两个标量原语出发，经逐点乘法、求逆与有理根的封闭性生成序列环 $\mathcal{S}_\mathbb{O}=\mathbb{O}^\mathbb{Z}$ 上的无限可除 Moufang 环路，并以十六元数零因子切除（sedenionic zero-divisor excision）使其终止于 $\mathbb{O}^\mathbb{Z}$。借助 Artin 定理刻画成对结合的成立性，以结合子 $[u,v,w]=(uv)w-u(vw)$ 度量三波相互作用产生的非结合曲率；同时定义实值序列内积以绕开 Jordan–von Neumann–Wigner 障碍，确立七个 Fano 坐标原语的规范正交性。

Result: 建立三项主要定理：(1) 不变六角网格定理（Invariant Hexagonal Mesh Theorem），证明累积积分在 $n=6$ 处出现尺度不变分岔，受 $G_2=\operatorname{Aut}(\mathbb{O})$ 的根系及其 $\mathrm{SU}(3)$ 稳定子子群支配；(2) 代数相位抖动定理（Algebraic Phase Jitter Theorem），表明非 Fano 三元组上的 Catalan 括号化树产生不可约零点相位涨落，为真空抖动提供代数起源；(3) 有理环路闭合定理（Rational Loop Closure Theorem），证明有理频率选取有限的闭合 Moufang 子环路，而无理频率给出拓扑稠密、不闭合的轨迹。

Conclusion: 该框架表明有理波数的离散代数结构能够统一承载复数、四元数与八元数情形，并将结合性破缺、六重对称分岔与频率选取的闭合性纳入同一形式体系，从而为非结合几何与基本物理对称性提供自主（autonomous）的离散代数基础。

Abstract: We generalize discrete rational wave numbers (arXiv:2609.23233) to the octonionic rational wave numbers $\mathbb{O}^\mathbb{Z}$ over Cayley's octonions $\mathbb{O}$. Rather than assuming higher imaginary units, all rational wave number spaces $\mathbb{C}^\mathbb{Z}, \mathbb{H}^\mathbb{Z}, \mathbb{O}^\mathbb{Z}$ are derived constructively from two scalar primitives: the coordinate advance $E_Z$ and phase calibration $E_{\bar{1}}$. Their closure under pointwise multiplication, inversion, and rational roots generates an infinite divisible Moufang loop on the sequence ring $\mathcal{S}_\mathbb{O} := \mathbb{O}^\mathbb{Z}$, terminating strictly at $\mathbb{O}^\mathbb{Z}$ via sedenionic zero-divisor excision. By Artin's Theorem, pairwise compositions remain strictly associative, whereas three-wave interactions generate an intrinsic non-associative curvature quantified by the associator $[u, v, w] = (uv)w - u(vw)$. We define a real-valued sequence inner product that bypasses the Jordan--von Neumann--Wigner obstruction, establishing the orthonormality of the seven Fano coordinate primitives. We establish three principal theorems: (1) The Invariant Hexagonal Mesh Theorem, proving that cumulative integration exhibits a scale-invariant bifurcation at $n=6$ governed by the root system of $G_2 = \operatorname{Aut}(\mathbb{O})$ and its $\mathrm{SU}(3)$ stabilizer subgroup; (2) The Algebraic Phase Jitter Theorem, showing that Catalan parenthesization trees across non-Fano triads produce an irreducible zero-point phase fluctuation, yielding an algebraic origin for vacuum jitter; and (3) The Rational Loop Closure Theorem, proving that rational frequencies select closed finite Moufang sub-loops, while irrational frequencies yield topologically dense, non-closing trajectories. This provides an autonomous, discrete algebraic foundation for non-associative geometry and fundamental physical symmetries.

</details>

### [76] [Phonon-assisted free-electron recoil control and mechanical sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30124)
*Sunjun Hwang,Lee Wei Wesley Wong,Liang Jie Wong,Leong Chuan Kwek*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该理论工作表明，光机械薄膜（optomechanical membrane）的振动可散射腔光形成高动量倏逝边带（evanescent sidebands），从而相位匹配地调控自由电子（free electron）的反冲（recoil）与感知局域力，为机械可重构的自由电子光学（free-electron optics）与模式选择性力学传感提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机械振动在众多物理现象与技术中具有基础地位，但其主动塑造自由电子动力学的潜力仍 largely unexplored（摘要原文措辞）。研究由此出发，探索利用机械振动作为自由电子与光相互作用的调控接口。

Method: 理论研究：横向薄膜运动将腔光散射为高动量倏逝边带，与真空中传播的电子实现相位匹配（phase matching）相互作用；振动振幅同时调控电子—光相互作用强度并重新分配电子在反冲阶（recoil orders）上的布居。

Result: 受外力扰动的振动信息被编码进电子反冲谱（electron recoil spectrum）；在建模条件下，以相同探测速率进行比较时，电子读出局域随机力可获得高于高斯光学零拍探测（Gaussian optical homodyne）基准的信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio）。

Conclusion: 结果可为机械可重构的自由电子光学（mechanically reconfigurable free-electron optics）与模式选择性力学传感（mode-selective mechanical sensing）提供理论依据。

Abstract: Mechanical vibrations are fundamental to a wide range of physical phenomena and technologies, yet their ability to actively shape free-electron dynamics remains largely unexplored. Here we theoretically show how an optomechanical membrane provides a phonon-assisted optical interface for controlling free-electron recoil and sensing localized forces. Transverse membrane motion scatters cavity light into high-momentum evanescent sidebands that enable phase-matched interaction with electrons traveling in vacuum. Beyond supplying momentum for phase matching, the vibration amplitude controls the electron-light interaction strength and redistributes electron populations among recoil orders. External forces that perturb the vibration are thereby encoded in the electron recoil spectrum. Under the modeled conditions, electron readout of localized stochastic forces can yield a higher signal-to-noise ratio than a Gaussian optical homodyne benchmark at equal detected probe rates. These results provide a theoretical basis for mechanically reconfigurable free-electron optics and mode-selective mechanical sensing.

</details>


### [77] [Higher-Order Flatband-Touching Points Mediate Unconventional Bulk-Boundary Correspondence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29251)
*Limin Song,Jianqi Xiong,Liqin Tang,Daohong Song,Zhigang Chen*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作在光子交叉层状类Kagome晶格中实验实现由高阶平带接触点（higher-order FBTP）主导的非传统体-边对应（bulk-boundary correspondence），并观测到对输入场扰动保持边界局域的鲁棒边界模（RBMs）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统体-边对应由动量空间体能带拓扑不变量描述，而一类奇异平带系统（singular flatband systems）的鲁棒边界模源于平带接触点（FBTPs）及与不完备紧致局域态（CLSs）集合相关的实空间拓扑。已有实验仅限于低阶FBTP系统，高阶FBTP情形尚未得到实验验证。

Method: 采用连续波激光写入（continuous-wave laser writing）技术在光子交叉层状类Kagome晶格（cross-bedded Kagome-like lattice）中构建高阶FBTP，该接触点由两条简并平带与一条色散带相接触形成；并测量体态CLSs与边界RBMs的传播行为。

Result: 观测到体态CLSs与边界RBMs的保形传播（shape-preserving propagation）；与以往报道的平带系统不同，在涉及奇异与非奇异CLSs的输入场扰动下，RBMs仍保持边界局域性，呈现与高阶FBTP相关的非传统边界响应。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了类Kagome晶格作为探索平带物理与非传统体-边对应的新平台，并指向在波局域、平带激光器（flatband lasers）及新兴拓扑光子器件中的潜在应用。

Abstract: The bulk-boundary correspondence establishes a fundamental connection between bulk band topology and the emergence of boundary states, underpinning topological phenomena across electronic, acoustic, photonic, and many other wave systems. A broad class of singular flatband systems, however, exhibits an unconventional form of bulk-boundary correspondence, in which robust boundary modes (RBMs) originate from flatband-touching points (FBTPs) and the real-space topology associated with incomplete sets of compact localized states (CLSs), rather than conventional momentum-space bulk topological invariants. To date, experimental studies of such boundary modes have been limited to systems hosting lower-order FBTPs. Here, we experimentally demonstrate unconventional bulk-boundary correspondence in a photonic cross-bedded Kagome-like lattice featuring a higher-order FBTP formed by the touching of two degenerate flatbands and a dispersive band. Using continuous-wave laser writing, we observe shape-preserving propagation of both bulk CLSs and boundary RBMs. Remarkably, unlike previously reported flatband systems, the RBMs retain their boundary localization under input-field perturbations involving both singular and nonsingular CLSs, revealing an unconventional boundary response associated with the higher-order FBTP in this lattice. Our results establish the Kagome-like lattice as a new platform for exploring flatband physics and unconventional bulk-boundary correspondence, with potential applications in wave localization, flatband lasers, and other emerging topological photonic devices.

</details>

### [78] [Geometry-controlled zeros of resonant Kelvin-wave conversion in four spatial dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29218)
*Wei-can Yang,Xiang-dong Xu,Cheng Li*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该文在四个空间维度中构造一种涡旋面（vortex surface）几何，用以揭示Kelvin波共振相互作用中被线性谱（linear spectrum）遮蔽的信息，并预测在每一条足够小的非零波数处存在完全耦合（full coupling）的零点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kelvin波沿涡旋输运能量，但其共振相互作用（resonant interactions）所携带的信息不在线性谱中，局部张力（local tension）描述会遗漏周围流动（surrounding flow）引起的非线性效应。

Method: 通过选取特定几何，使垂直入射波抵消完整的局域面积四波耦合（local-area four-wave coupling），仅保留由周围流体介导的有限非局域相互作用（nonlocal interaction）；再与Gross-Pitaevskii（GP）理论匹配，纳入微观涡核响应（microscopic core response），并结合相干混合（coherent-mixing）计算与弱探针谱（weak-probe spectra）分析。

Result: 非局域相互作用在一般波数比下恢复波转换，但在某一选定波数比处改变符号并消失，同时动量与频率严格匹配；与GP理论匹配表明在每条足够小的非零波数处完全耦合存在零点，比值趋于 λ_*≃1.469，有限网格GP系数对同一共振四元组（resonant quartets）出现符号反转。

Conclusion: 耦合符号与生成波相位及反射偶/反射奇模式（reflection-even/reflection-odd modes）的排序相关；该抑制去除了在保持配对波长不变的情况下改变传播方向的主要通道，隔离出局部张力无法捕捉的非线性效应，为涡旋波理论提供微观基准，并为研究Kelvin谱之外的角度重分布（angular redistribution）提供起点。

Abstract: Kelvin waves carry energy along vortices, but their resonant interactions contain information that is absent from the linear spectrum. We identify a geometry that exposes this information on a vortex surface in four spatial dimensions. Perpendicular incoming waves cancel the complete local-area four-wave coupling, leaving the finite nonlocal interaction mediated by the surrounding fluid. This interaction restores conversion at generic wave-number ratios, yet changes sign and vanishes at a selected ratio while momentum and frequency remain exactly matched. Matching to Gross-Pitaevskii (GP) theory, including the microscopic core response, establishes a zero of the full coupling at every sufficiently small nonzero wave number, with ratio tending to $λ_*\simeq1.469$. Finite-grid GP coefficients exhibit a sign reversal for the same resonant quartets. Separate coherent-mixing calculations and weak-probe spectra connect the coupling sign to the generated-wave phase and the ordering of reflection-even and reflection-odd modes. The resulting suppression removes a leading channel for changing propagation directions without changing the paired wavelengths. These results isolate a nonlinear effect of the surrounding flow that local tension misses, providing a microscopic benchmark for vortex-wave theories and a starting point for studying angular redistribution beyond the Kelvin spectrum.

</details>


### [79] [From hard-core to soft-core bosons in a shell trap](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29440)
*G. Costa,S. Prestipino,F. Cinti,M. Ciardi*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作在宽球形壳层中利用路径积分蒙特卡洛（path-integral Monte Carlo）模拟无自旋玻色子，通过调节粒子间势核从硬到软以及粒子质量，系统扫描出一系列有限尺寸物相，包括多层相、团簇相、超流相和单层超固相，并确认超固相（supersolid）仅在存在团簇（cluster）时出现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在弥合两类玻色子行为的差距：自组装为普通晶体的类氦原子（Helium-like atoms）与在模拟中形成团簇（超）固体（cluster (super)solids）的里德堡缀饰原子（Rydberg-dressed atoms），并考察量子性（粒子质量）如何改变固相与流体相的相对稳定性。

Method: 采用虚拟的路径积分蒙特卡洛（path-integral Monte Carlo）实验，在宽球形壳层中改变势核的软硬程度与粒子质量（即量子性），并选取60至180个粒子的三个系统尺寸，同时按密度固定对陷阱半径（trap radius）进行标度变换。

Result: 发现一整套具有弯曲结构的有限尺寸（finite-size）相谱，含多层相、团簇相、超流相与单层超固相；超固相仅与团簇共存，其存在对应确定的粒子质量区间，且该区间通过不同尺寸的密度固定标度予以验证。

Conclusion: 该模型可作为检验平台，用于研究球形几何（spherical geometry）下从原子向缀饰原子（dressed atoms）过渡时涌现性质（emergent properties）如何被改变。

Abstract: We study the low-temperature states of a system of spinless bosons trapped in a broad spherical shell. In our virtual (path-integral Monte Carlo) experiments, we specifically examine how the equilibrium structure and overall superfluidity of the system are affected by a change of the interparticle-potential core from hard to increasingly soft. In this manner we bridge the gap between the behaviors of two species of bosons: Helium-like atoms, which self-assemble into ordinary crystalline solids, and Rydberg-dressed atoms, which in simulation form cluster (super)solids. We also vary the particle mass, i.e., the quantumness of the system, so as to modify the relative stability of ``solid'' and fluid states. We thus find a whole spectrum of (finite-size) phases with a curved structure, including multilayer and cluster phases, as well as superfluids and single layer supersolids. In particular, we confirm that supersolids only occur in the presence of clusters. By considering three system sizes (from 60 to 180 particles), but rescaling the trap radius so that the density stays fixed, we verify that supersolid states only exist in the same range of particle masses. Our model serves as a platform to test how emergent properties are modified, in spherical geometry, in the crossover from atoms to dressed atoms.

</details>


<div id='math.RT'></div>

# math.RT [[Back]](#toc)

### [80] [On unitary vertex operator algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29878)
*Victor G. Kac,Pierluigi Möseneder Frajria,Paolo Papi*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 该文证明：具非零不变厄米形式的共形顶点代数，若存在正定不变厄米形式的正能量模则即为酉的；由此借助自由场实现给出主$W$-代数酉性的中心荷下界，并证明除可容许情形外简单$W$-代数仅在有限个水平$k$处酉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究共形顶点代数与$W$-代数何时为酉（unitary），即其不变厄米形式正定；此前对主$W$-代数及一般$W$-代数的酉性判定缺乏统一判据与不存在的刻画。

Method: 先建立从“单一正能量模的正定性”推出整个顶点代数酉性的判据；再用自由场实现（free field realization）估计主简单$W$-代数$W_k(\mathfrak g)$及$osp(1|2n)$、$osp(2|2n)$型的酉性；最后用$sl_2$子代数（even $sl_2$-subalgebra）及其中心化子性质分析$W_k(\mathfrak g,\mathfrak s)$的酉性水平数。

Result: 主$W$-代数$W_k(\mathfrak g)$在$c\ge \mathrm{rank}(\mathfrak g)+24((\rho|\rho^\vee)+\|\rho\|\|\rho^\vee\|)$时酉，单纯李代数且单连通（simply laced）情形为所有$c\ge \mathrm{rank}(\mathfrak g)$；$W_k(osp(1|2n))$在$c\ge n+\frac12$时酉，$osp(2|2n)$给出显式界；若$\mathfrak s$的中心化子非交换，则$W_k(\mathfrak g,\mathfrak s)$至多对有限个$k$酉，$sl_{qm}$的Jordan型$q^m$幂零元对应情形仅两个水平可能（$q=2$时两者均实现，如$sl_4$的$2+2$分划仅在$k=-2,-\frac32$酉）；李超代数情形失效但更强假设下成立；若$\mathfrak s$非可区分$sl_2$子代数，则$W_k(\mathfrak g,\mathfrak s)$仅对有限个$k$酉。

Conclusion: 酉性可由单个正定正能量模判定；自由场实现给出主$W$-代数及部分正交辛超代数的酉性下界；对非可区分$sl_2$子代数情形，$W$-代数的酉性被限制在有限个水平，超代数情形需额外假设。

Abstract: We prove that a conformal vertex algebra with a non-zero invariant Hermitian form is unitary as soon as it admits a non-zero positive energy module with a positive definite invariant Hermitian form. Using free field realizations, we deduce that the principal simple $W$-algebra $W_k(\mathfrak g)$ of a simple Lie algebra $\mathfrak g$ is unitary for central charge $c\ge rank(\mathfrak g)+24\bigl((ρ|ρ^\vee)+\|ρ\|\,\|ρ^\vee\|\bigr)$, and, if $\mathfrak g$ is simply laced, for all $c\ge rank(\mathfrak g)$. Among basic Lie superalgebras which are not Lie algebras, the free field argument applies to $osp(1|2n)$ and $osp(2|2n)$. It shows that $W_k(osp(1|2n))$ is unitary for $c\ge n+\frac12$ and gives an explicit bound for $osp(2|2n)$. Next, we prove that if $\mathfrak s$ is an even $sl_2$-subalgebra of a simple Lie algebra $\mathfrak g$ whose centralizer is not abelian, then the simple $W$-algebra $W_k(\mathfrak g,\mathfrak s)$ is unitary for at most finitely many $k$. We illustrate this on the $W$-algebras of $sl_{qm}$ attached to the nilpotents of Jordan type $q^m$: unitarity is possible only at two levels, and for $q=2$ it does occur at both. For instance, the $W$-algebra of $sl_4$ attached to the partition $2+2$ is unitary only for $k=-2$ and $k=-\frac32$. This fails for Lie superalgebras, as minimal $W$-algebras show, but still holds under stronger assumptions. Finally, we prove that a simple $W$-algebra $W_k(\mathfrak g,\mathfrak s)$ can be unitary for only finitely many $k$ if $\mathfrak g$ is a simple Lie algebra and $\mathfrak s$ is not a distinguished $sl_2$ subalgebra.

</details>

### [81] [AnyonWiki.org](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30081)
*Colleen Delaney,Ralph Razzouk,Joost Slingerland,Gert Vercleyen*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: AnyonWiki 是开源项目，提供融合范畴（fusion category）与酉模融合范畴（unitary modular fusion category）的完整代数与数值数据，配套数据库 AnyonWikiDatabase 已收录秩 7 以内的无重数主轴（含编织）融合范畴分类及秩 10 以内无重数融合环（fusion ring）分类（超过 25000 个），并通过 AnyonWiki.com 网站提供检索、下载与逐条引用功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该工作的动机是为科学界提供可直接访问、可独立验证、可细粒度引用且易于使用的融合范畴数据资源，以支撑相关研究与社区协作。

Method: 构建开源数据库 AnyonWikiDatabase 并配套网站 AnyonWiki.com；数据库设计围绕数据可访问性、独立可验证性、细粒度引用（granularly citable）与易用性等目标展开，网站为每个融合环与范畴提供独立网页并支持搜索与下载。

Result: 数据库目前已包含秩 7 以内的无重数主轴（编织）融合范畴分类，以及超过 25000 个融合环，其中包括秩 10 以内的无重数融合环分类，且扩充工作仍在进行。

Conclusion: 该短文介绍了 AnyonWiki.com 上可用的数据及其底层数据库设计的科学与社区动机，强调以可访问、可验证、可细粒度引用和易用为核心的数据资源建设方向。

Abstract: The AnyonWiki is an open-source project that provides full algebraic and numerical data of fusion and unitary modular fusion categories as a resource for the scientific community. It comes with a database, the AnyonWikiDatabase, that currently includes the classification of multiplicity-free pivotal (braided) fusion categories through rank 7 and more than 25000 fusion rings including the classification of multiplicity-free fusion rings through rank 10, with work in progress to expand. This data is available through AnyonWiki.org, a website that provides robust search and download functionality and presents each fusion ring and category via a dedicated webpage. In this short article we discuss the data available at AnyonWiki.org as well as the scientific and communal motivations for the design of its underlying AnyonWikiDatabase, which are centered around making the data accessible, independently verifiable, granularly citable, useful, and easy to work with.

</details>

### [82] [Resonant Inverse Photoelectron Spectroscopy of Ce and U Oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28857)
*JG Tobin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作讨论氧化铈（cerium oxide）M5跃迁与二氧化铀（uranium dioxide）N5跃迁的软X射线共振逆光电子能谱（soft x-ray Resonant Inverse Photoelectron Spectroscopy, RIPES）及相关仪器，并以进一步实验证据和分析支持“Ce 5d态为该共振过程内禀组成”的假说。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确陈述研究动机，仅指出需以更多实验证据与分析检验关于氧化铈中共振过程内禀含Ce 5d态（Ce 5d states）的假说。

Method: 采用软X射线共振逆光电子能谱（RIPES）测量氧化铈的M5跃迁与二氧化铀的N5跃迁，并结合支撑性仪器（supporting instrumentation）与实验数据分析。

Result: 提供了进一步实验证据与分析，支持Ce 5d态是氧化铈共振过程（resonance process）内禀组成部分的假说；同时强调与二氧化铈（cerium dioxide）之间的强关联。

Conclusion: RIPES结果支持Ce 5d态内禀参与氧化铈M5共振过程，且该现象与二氧化铈性质密切相关。

Abstract: The soft x-ray Resonant Inverse Photoelectron Spectroscopy (RIPES) of the M5 transition in cerium oxide and the N5 transition in uranium dioxide and supporting instrumentation is discussed. Further experimental evidence and analysis is presented to support the hypothesis that the Ce 5d states are intrinsic to the resonance process in cerium oxide. The strong connection with cerium dioxide is emphasized.

</details>


### [83] [Valence-Driven Structural Transformation and Phase Coexistence in Chemically Substituted EuPd2Si2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29180)
*Masoud Mardani,Shivani Sharma,Saul H. Lapidus,Theo Siegrist*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过Ge化学取代调控EuPd2Si2的铕价态转变温度（Tv），证实价态转变伴随Eu2+/Eu3+相共存，并揭示晶格参数与Tv之间的逆线性关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EuPd2Si2因其对温度、压力和磁场敏感的价态转变（valence transition），是研究晶格动力学（lattice dynamics）与电子不稳定性（electronic instabilities）相互作用的理想体系；但温度诱导价态不稳定性的起源及其与结构响应的关联尚不明确。

Method: 对母体EuPd2Si2及Ge取代样品EuPd2Si1.95Ge0.05、EuPd2Si1.9Ge0.1进行磁化率（magnetic susceptibility）测量以确定Tv，并结合变温同步辐射X射线衍射（synchrotron X-ray diffraction）分析相组成与峰宽变化。

Result: Ge取代使Tv从母体的143 K系统性下移至EuPd2Si1.95Ge0.05的87 K；所有样品在价态转变区均呈现Eu2+Pd2Si2-xGex与Eu3+Pd2Si2-xGex两相共存，半高全宽（full width at half maximum, FWHM）在Tv以上显著增大，反映转变的渐进性及结构应变（structural strain）对共存相的稳定作用。

Conclusion: 结果支持相分离基态（phase-segregated ground state），而非均匀的中间价态（intermediate-valence）或混合价态（mixed-valence）图像；晶格参数与Tv呈逆线性关系，为预测该体系其他化学取代变体的转变温度提供可能的框架。

Abstract: EuPd2Si2 serves as an ideal system to probe the interplay between lattice dynamics and electronic instabilities through its tunable valence transition, responsive to temperature, pressure, and magnetic field. Here, we focus on investigating the origin of the temperature-induced europium valence instability in pristine EuPd2Si2, alloyed EuPd2Si1.95Ge0.05, and EuPd2Si1.9Ge0.1 and its correlation with structural responses. Compared with the parent compound EuPd2Si2 which exhibits the europium valence transition at Tv = 143 K, magnetic susceptibility measurements reveal a systematic shift in Tv with Ge substitution, decreasing to 87 K for EuPd2Si1.95Ge0.05. Temperature-dependent synchrotron X-ray diffraction confirms phase coexistence between Eu2+Pd2Si2-xGex and Eu3+Pd2Si2-xGex phases across the valence transition for all samples measured (x = 0, 0.05, 0.10). The pronounced increase in the full width at half maximum (FWHM) well above Tv reflects the gradual nature of the transformation and highlights the role of structural strain in stabilizing the coexisting phases, supporting the presence of a phase-segregated ground state, in contrast to a homogeneous intermediate-valence or mixed-valence state. The variation in Tv across different compositions correlates with changes in lattice parameters, highlighting the robust coupling between the structural and electronic degrees of freedom. The phase diagram suggests an inverse linear relationship between the lattice parameter and Tv, suggesting a possible predictive framework for estimating transition temperatures in other chemically substituted variants of this family.

</details>


### [84] [Interplay of disorder and phonons in two-dimensional transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29260)
*Aryaman Babbar,Sankar Das Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 基于玻尔兹曼输运理论（Boltzmann transport theory）与随机相近似（random phase approximation）屏蔽，计算双层 MoSe$_2$ 在带电杂质、短程缺陷和纵声学声子共同作用下的电阻率，并与近期输运实验对比；结果表明声学声子修正很小，且电阻率极小值可出现在金属-绝缘体交叉两侧，其本身不能用于判别相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解释双层 MoSe$_2$ 输运测量中电阻率的温度依赖来源，并澄清电阻率极小值（resistivity minimum）是否能作为判别金属-绝缘体交叉（metal-insulator crossover）所处相的依据。

Method: 采用玻尔兹曼输运理论，以随机相近似处理屏蔽效应，同时考虑带电杂质、短程缺陷与纵声学声子（longitudinal acoustic phonons）三种散射机制；无序参数取自实验本身，声子参数取自 MoSe$_2$ 的文献估计值。

Result: 在整个实验温度范围内，经屏蔽的声学声子对无序限制电阻率仅构成小幅修正；金属侧的电阻率温度依赖主要由带电无序的温度依赖屏蔽和中性缺陷控制。

Conclusion: 电阻率极小值可通过两种不同机制分别出现在金属-绝缘体交叉的两侧：绝缘侧源于激活载流子（activated carriers）与电阻性散射的共同作用，金属侧源于无序与声子散射的共同作用；因此电阻率极小值本身并不能用于判别体系所处相。

Abstract: We calculate the temperature- and density-dependent resistivity of bilayer MoSe$_2$ in the presence of charged impurities, short-range defects, and longitudinal acoustic phonons, using the Boltzmann transport theory with screening treated in the random phase approximation, and compare the results with recent transport measurements. The disorder parameters are taken from the experiment itself and the phonon parameters are taken from literature estimates for MoSe$_2$. We find that screened acoustic phonons are a small correction to the disorder-limited resistivity over the entire experimental temperature range, so that the temperature dependence of the measured resistivity on the metallic side is controlled by the temperature-dependent screening of charged disorder and by neutral defects. We also show that a resistivity minimum as a function of temperature can arise on both sides of the metal-insulator crossover through two different mechanisms: on the insulating side from the interplay between activated carriers and resistive scattering, and on the metallic side from the interplay between disorder and phonon scattering. The existence of a resistivity minimum by itself therefore does not identify the phase.

</details>


### [85] [Chirality, Klein duality, and the stability of Kitaev spin liquids on the star lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29278)
*Harsh Raj,Yasir Iqbal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文借助费米子部分子（fermionic partons）的投影对称群（projective symmetry groups, PSGs）证明，星格（star lattice）上时间反演对称的Kitaev型自旋液体不存在，任何该格子的Kitaev自旋液体必然自发破坏时间反演，从而说明手性并非可解点的偶然性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 此前DMRG研究发现，星格Yao-Kivelson手性自旋液体在加入Heisenberg交换后，铁磁Kitaev耦合有较宽稳定窗口而反铁磁耦合窗口很窄，但这种符号不对称及手性的来源缺乏对称性层面的解释。

Method: 采用费米子部分子的投影对称群分析，在自旋-规范锁定（spin-gauge locking）条件下考察时间反演对称的平均场态；并对352个含自旋轨道耦合振幅的Z2 PSG进行分类，辅以Kitaev点附近的严格二阶微扰论。

Result: 只要π自旋旋转在部分子上如精确解那样作用，时间反演对称的平均场态在任何范围内在三角形上振幅为零；Abel与非Abel液体共享同一手性PSG，其转变为带反转（band inversion）；二阶微扰显示三角形响应两种符号相同，而三角形间键上两个非对角Heisenberg项对反铁磁耦合相长干涉、对铁磁近乎相消，四visons低能态混合强度相差82倍。

Conclusion: 星格Kitaev型自旋液体的手性由PSG结构必然要求，Klein对偶置换PSG而保持锁定PSG不变；该分类同时识别出已知Schwinger-玻色子自旋液体的费米子对应物。

Abstract: The star lattice hosts the Yao-Kivelson chiral spin liquid, an exactly solvable Kitaev spin liquid that breaks time reversal spontaneously and is non-Abelian or Abelian depending on the ratio of its two couplings. A density-matrix renormalization group study found that an added Heisenberg exchange leaves it a wide stability window for ferromagnetic but only a narrow one for antiferromagnetic Kitaev coupling. Using projective symmetry groups (PSGs) of fermionic partons, we show that the chirality is not a peculiarity of the solvable point: whenever the $π$ spin rotations act on the partons as in the exact solution (spin-gauge locking), a time-reversal-symmetric mean-field state has no amplitude on the triangles, at any range, so a Kitaev-type spin liquid on this lattice must break time reversal. The Abelian and non-Abelian liquids of both signs share a single chiral PSG, within which their transition is a band inversion. The Klein duality, which reverses the Heisenberg coupling, permutes the PSGs and leaves exactly the locked ones unchanged; a $Z_2$ spin-liquid phase mapped onto itself is therefore locked and, if it spans a connected range of couplings, contains a Kitaev point. Unprojected mean-field theory in Kitaev's Majorana representation treats both signs alike. Exact second-order perturbation theory about the Kitaev points locates the asymmetry: the triangles respond identically for both signs, whereas on the bonds between triangles the two off-diagonal Heisenberg terms create the same four visons and interfere constructively for antiferromagnetic and almost destructively for ferromagnetic coupling, admixing a low-energy four-vison state $82$ times more strongly on the antiferromagnetic side. The PSG results rest on a classification of $352$ $Z_2$ PSGs with spin-orbit-coupled amplitudes, which also identifies the fermionic partners of the known Schwinger-boson spin liquids.

</details>


### [86] [Ab Initio Approach for Quantum Magnets with Coupled Cluster and Transcorrelation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29324)
*Kristoffer Simula,Nikolay A. Bogdanov,Olle Gunnarsson,Daniel Kats,Ali Alavi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文用嵌入团簇（embedded-cluster）方法结合双行列式耦合簇（2-determinant coupled cluster）与转相关（transcorrelated）哈密顿量，计算了五种氧桥自旋-1/2量子磁体的最近邻交换耦合 $J_1$，发现基组误差显著，而转相关方法以小基组即可高精度复现完备基组极限（CBS）结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最近邻交换耦合 $J_1$ 虽是理解真实材料磁性的最简单模型参数，但其从头算（ab initio）确定需要高水平的关联处理和大型波函数展开基组，计算代价高昂。该工作旨在寻求兼顾精度与成本的计算途径。

Method: 采用嵌入团簇方法研究 Sr$_2$CuO$_3$、La$_2$CuO$_4$、NdNiO$_2$、ZnCu$_3$(OH)$_6$Cl$_2$、Li$_2$CuO$_2$ 五种桥角从 180° 到 94° 的氧桥自旋-1/2 磁体；用双行列式耦合簇单双激发处理开壳单重态（open-shell singlets），并引入转相关哈密顿量（transcorrelated Hamiltonians）。

Result: 基组误差较大：由双-$ζ$ 质量基组外推至完备基组极限（complete basis-set, CBS）使 $J_1$ 变动可达 50 meV；双行列式耦合簇从极小参考态即可再现超交换（superexchange）的轨道呼吸增强（orbital-breathing enhancement）；转相关哈密顿量以适中的基组规模复现 CBS 估计值，与实验吻合良好且成本低廉。

Conclusion: 该工作是转相关方法在强关联固态系统中的首次应用，确立了转相关（transcorrelation）作为材料计算研究中一种强有力理论的地位。

Abstract: The nearest-neighbour exchange coupling $J_1$ is the simplest ingredient of model Hamiltonians used to understand magnetism in real materials. Nevertheless, its ab initio determination requires a high-level correlation treatment and a large basis for wave function expansion. Here we compute $J_1$ for five oxygen-bridged spin-1/2 quantum magnets --- Sr$_2$CuO$_3$, La$_2$CuO$_4$, NdNiO$_2$, ZnCu$_3$(OH)$_6$Cl$_2$, and Li$_2$CuO$_2$ --- spanning bridging angles from 180$^\circ$ to 94$^\circ$ and the crossover from antiferromagnetic to ferromagnetic coupling, using embedded-cluster methodology. To describe the open-shell singlets we use 2-determinant coupled cluster with single and double excitations, which recovers the orbital-breathing enhancement of superexchange from a minimal reference. Basis-set errors are found to be large: extrapolation to the complete basis-set (CBS) limit shifts $J_1$ by up to 50 meV from bases of double-$ζ$ quality. Transcorrelated Hamiltonians reproduce the CBS estimates with modest basis set sizes, yielding an excellent accuracy against experiment at highly affordable cost. These results are the first application of transcorrelated methods in strongly correlated solid-state systems, and establish transcorrelation as a powerful theory in computational research of materials.

</details>


### [87] [Quantics tensor trains for multiloop functional renormalization group in multiorbital impurity models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29341)
*Hyun-Yong Lee,Naoki Kawashima*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作将多圈泛函重整化群（multiloop functional renormalization group）直接构建于 quantics 张量列（quantics tensor train）表示中，在保留全部三个独立顶点频率且无需高频显式参数化的前提下，实现多轨道体系的动力学关联计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子相互作用的完整频率依赖是动力学关联的核心，但其数值成本严重制约多轨道计算；现有方法需借助高频参数化或稠密顶点中间量，难以在宽频率范围内同时保持多圈精度。

Method: 在 quantics 张量列表示下构造多圈泛函重整化群，保留三个独立顶点频率的宽范围依赖；通过解析张量算子完成频率变换与内部求和，避免稠密顶点中间量，并在多轨道杂质模型（含简并双轨道杂质、补偿性交错磁环境）中进行低温柔性计算。

Result: 多圈修正使顶点与响应函数和独立参考结果的一致性提升，并复现数值重整化群（numerical renormalization group）的低能自能；更低温度下解析延拓散射率呈现二次频率贡献。简并双轨道杂质在固定 Hund 耦合下表现出可区分的自旋、轨道与电荷响应；补偿性交错磁（altermagnetic）环境中轨道能级劈裂诱发局域自旋响应，且增大 Hund 耦合抑制轨道响应、增强自旋极化，而仅用重整化单粒子传播无法再现该强极化依赖。

Conclusion: 该方案将压缩式多圈计算拓展至多轨道环境中的动力学关联问题，表明完整的频率依赖与多圈修正对描述自旋—轨道—电荷响应及其对 Hund 耦合的敏感性不可或缺。

Abstract: The full frequency dependence of electronic interactions is central to dynamical correlations, but its numerical cost restricts multiorbital calculations. We formulate the multiloop functional renormalization group directly in the quantics tensor train representation, retaining all three independent vertex frequencies over broad ranges without an explicit high-frequency parametrization. Analytical tensor operators perform frequency transformations and internal summations without dense vertex intermediates. Multiloop corrections improve agreement of vertices and responses with independent references and reproduce the low-energy numerical renormalization group self-energy. Lower-temperature calculations resolve a quadratic frequency contribution to the analytically continued scattering rate. A degenerate two-orbital impurity exhibits distinct spin, orbital, and charge responses at fixed Hund coupling. In a compensated altermagnetic environment, an orbital-level splitting induces a local spin response despite zero magnetization of the unperturbed impurity. Increasing Hund coupling suppresses the orbital response while increasing its spin polarization. Renormalized one-particle propagation alone does not reproduce this strong polarization dependence. These results extend compressed multiloop calculations to dynamical correlations in multiorbital environments.

</details>

Conclusion: 结果揭示了杂化诱导的热电响应增强，并确立CsCr$_6$Sb$_6$为通过热电输运研究平带衍生Kondo物理的kagome平台。

Abstract: Flat bands in kagome lattices provide a prominent route to strongly correlated electronic states. Here we report charge and thermal transport measurements on single crystals of CsCr$_6$Sb$_6$, a kagome Kondo system arising from the hybridization between flat and dispersive bands. The Seebeck coefficient and Nernst signal increase rapidly below $T_\mathrm{F}\sim70$ K, concomitant with the emergence of short-range antiferromagnetic correlations and flat-band resonance. The Nernst coefficient in the intermediate-temperature range of $\sim30-80$ K further follows the universal scaling reported for prototypical heavy-fermion compounds, consistent with the development of strongly renormalized quasiparticles associated with hybridization. Both the Seebeck and Nernst responses reach extrema at a lower temperature $T_\mathrm{p}\sim20-25$ K, where phonon drag and correlated quasiparticles may both contribute. These results reveal a hybridization-induced enhancement of the thermoelectric response and establish CsCr$_6$Sb$_6$ as a kagome platform for probing flat-band-derived Kondo physics through thermoelectric transport.

</details>


### [89] [Chiral-Nematic Lattice Instability in Charge Density Wave of 1T-TiSe2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29448)
*Abdurrafi Ahsan Alghifari,Euihwan Do,Jae Whan Park,Han Woong Yeom*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性电荷密度波（chiral CDW）是一类少见且具有手征性的电子有序态，但其微观起源即便在原型体系1T-TiSe2中仍不清楚，作者旨在解释其结构稳定性与手性来源。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（ab initio calculations），考察1T-TiSe2中相互竞争的CDW模型的结构稳定性与电子结构，并分析围绕能量极小结构方向的势能景观。

Result: 手性单斜（C2）结构由多声子模的协同凝聚（cooperative condensation）所稳定，并能重现实验观测的手性形貌与能隙；同时，该能量极小结构附近沿破坏剩余旋转对称的方向存在异常柔软的势能景观，使晶格可在完全不对称构型间涨落而平均结构仍保持手性。

Conclusion: 动态手性CDW（dynamical chiral CDW）可自然统一时间平均形貌探测所见的~手性畴与体相谱学所观测的完全对称性破缺，并厘清手性CDW源于晶格不稳定性与涨落的相互作用。

Abstract: Chiral charge-density waves (CDWs) are a rare form of electronic order with handedness, enabling novel couplings among light, lattice, and topology in quantum materials. Yet the microscopic origin of chirality has remained elusive, even in the prototypical chiral CDW system 1T-TiSe2. Using ab initio calculations, we investigate the structural stability and electronic structure of competing CDW models in 1T-TiSe2. We show that a chiral monoclinic (C2) structure is stabilized by the cooperative condensation of multiple phonon modes and reproduces the chiral topography and energy gap observed experimentally. We further find an exceptionally soft potential landscape around this energy-minimized structure, along the direction that removes the remaining rotational symmetry, allowing the lattice to fluctuate through fully asymmetric configurations while its average structure remains chiral. This dynamical chiral CDWnaturally reconciles the chiral domains seen by time-averaged topographic probes with the complete loss of symmetry detected by bulk spectroscopy, and clarifies how chiral CDWs emerge from the interplay of lattice instability and fluctuation.

</details>


### [90] [On thermal Hall effect in boson systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29482)
*Yu. V. Baramygina,D. N. Aristov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 论文从理论上讨论玻色子体系的热霍尔效应（thermal Hall effect, THE），面向具有斯格明子晶格（skyrmion lattice）基态的磁性有序绝缘体，重新推导相关公式并指出热霍尔电导在低温下存在此前未报道的残余 1/T 贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性有序绝缘体中磁振子（magnon）谱在磁场变化下可能发生拓扑相变（topological transition），热霍尔效应有望成为确认该现象的实验工具，因此需要适用于该目的的可靠理论公式。

Method: 以玻色子体系为对象进行理论分析，针对斯格明子晶格基态的磁性绝缘体，重新推导适合研究目的的热霍尔效应公式，并考察磁振子哈密顿量中出现反常项（anomalous terms）的情形。

Result: 所得表达式的热霍尔电导温度依赖性与既有报道结果不同；当磁振子哈密顿量含反常项时，热霍尔电导具有一个小而有限的残余 1/T 贡献，该贡献在极低温下可变得显著。

Conclusion: 热霍尔效应可作为探测磁振子谱拓扑相变的手段，且反常项导致的 1/T 残余项在低温区不可忽略，其行为类比于超导金属中的迈斯纳效应（Meissner effect）。

Abstract: We theoretically discuss the thermal Hall effect (THE) in boson systems keeping in mind possible applications to magnetically ordered insulators with skyrmion lattice ground state. The magnon spectrum of such systems may undergo a topological transition upon the variation of magnetic field, and THE could be a tool to confirm this phenomenon. We rederive formulas, suitable for our purposes, and find expressions which have a different temperature dependence of THE as compared to previously reported results. Particularly, in presence of anomalous terms in magnon Hamiltonian the thermal Hall conductivity has a small but finite residual 1/T contribution. Such contribution may become sizable at very low temperatures and resembles Meissner effect in superconducting metals.

</details>


### [91] [Confirmation of complex non-collinear Fe(II) spin ordering in the iron oxychalcogenide La$_2$O$_2$Fe$_2$OSe$_2$ by broad range $^{139}$La-NQR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29635)
*Tillmann Weinhold,Jerin Abraham George,Cornelius Herrmann,Domenic Nowak,Rajib Sarkar,Felix Seewald,Rowena Wachtel,Sabine Wurmehl,Bernd Büchner,Hans-Henning Klauss*
