<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该体系的非共线自旋冰型反铁磁序（spin-ice like antiferromagnetic ordering）此前由 $^{57}$Fe 穆斯堡尔谱（Mössbauer spectroscopy）与 $^{139}$La-NMR 联合推断，需独立的零场核四极共振（nuclear quadrupole resonance, NQR）手段予以验证。

Method: 对多晶 La$_2$O$_2$Fe$_2$OSe$_2$ 开展零场 $^{139}$La-NQR 测量，频率范围 2.8–20 MHz、温度范围 4.2–100 K。

Result: 在 $ν_Q$、$2ν_Q$、$3ν_Q$ 处观测到共振信号，与既有 NMR 研究及所提出的磁结构相符；未发现 $T_N$（约 90 K）以下 La 位电场梯度（electric field gradient）存在实质性改变。

Conclusion: 零场 NQR 结果支持此前的磁结构图像，并对 $T_N$ 以下电场梯度显著变化的说法提出质疑。

Abstract: The transition metal oxychalcogenide La$_2$O$_2$Fe$_2$OSe$_2$ exhibits a non-collinear spin-ice like antiferromagnetic ordering below $T_N \approx $ 90 K due to complex magnetic exchange interactions and axial single-ion anisotropy of Fe$^{2+}$ spins. The magnetic structure was deduced/determined from combined $^{57}$Fe Mössbauer spectroscopy and $^{139}$La-NMR on polycrystalline specimen. In this work, we present zero-field $^{139}$La-NQR investigations on polycrystalline La$_2$O$_2$Fe$_2$OSe$_2$ in a broad frequency and temperature range between 2.8 - 20 MHz and 4.2 - 100 K. The measured resonances at $ν_Q$, $2 \, ν_Q$, and $3 \, ν_Q$ are fully consistent with the previous NMR studies and also with the magnetic structure. A substantial modification of the electric field gradient at the La-position below $T_N$ is inconsistent with the current investigations.

</details>


### [92] [Spin order and excitations in the collinear kagome antiferromagnet Y$_{0.5}$Fe$_3$Sn$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29708)
*Jin Ding,Yang Liu,Feihao Pan,Yinghao Zhu,Yixi Su,Stanislav E. Nikitin,Ursula B. Hansen,Mechthild Enderle,Zhilun Lu,Linxuan Song,Yong-Chang Lau,Xiaoyan Ma,Enke Liu,Huiqian Luo*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究通过系统的中子散射（neutron scattering）研究了kagome金属Y0.5Fe3Sn3：其在TN=551 K以下形成共线c轴反铁磁（antiferromagnetic, AF）序，并在远低于TN的in-plane磁场下出现超越常规磁化标度行为的反常霍尔效应（anomalous Hall effect, AHE）；弹性/非弹性/极化中子测量揭示自旋激发具有Y型色散、双能隙结构及强各向异性，作者将其归因于无隙激发下由场致自旋倾斜（spin canting）产生的标量自旋手性（scalar spin chirality）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kagome晶格因几何阻挫（geometric frustration）与强自旋关联而富含磁、拓扑与输运现象，但缺乏对共线反铁磁kagome金属中自旋激发与非常规输运响应关联的系统微观表征。

Method: 对kagome金属Y0.5Fe3Sn3开展系统的中子散射研究：弹性中子散射（elastic neutron scattering）确定零场下的磁结构，非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering）测量自旋激发色散，极化中子（polarized neutron）测量分离面内与c轴自旋激发分量，并结合in-plane磁场下的输运测量。

Result: Fe磁矩在零场下直至1.5 K仍保持共线c轴AF排列；非弹性中子散射显示特征的Y型自旋激发色散，在10 meV以下呈双能隙（two-gap）结构，可能源于两支非简并自旋波分支（spin-wave branches）；低温自旋激发高度各向异性，面内分量M_ab占主导而c轴分量M_c几乎缺失，升温后M_c明显增长但在300 K仍远弱于M_ab；在B∥ab下观察到超出常规磁化标度响应的额外AHE贡献。

Conclusion: 作者提出该非常规AHE源于场致自旋倾斜（无论静态或动态构型、与无隙激发相关）所产生的非零标量自旋手性，从而将共线AF kagome金属Y0.5Fe3Sn3确立为无需自发自旋重取向（spin reorientation）即可实现手性驱动AHE的平台。

Abstract: Kagome lattices host rich magnetic, topological, and transport phenomena owing to their geometric frustration and strong spin correlations. Here we present a systematic neutron scattering study of the kagome metal Y$_{0.5}$Fe$_3$Sn$_3$, which orders into a collinear $c$-axis antiferromagnetic (AF) state below $T_N =$ 551 K. An additional anomalous Hall effect (AHE) contribution beyond the conventional magnetization-scaling response is observed well below $T_N$ under an in-plane magnetic field $\mathbf{B} \parallel ab$. Elastic neutron scattering demonstrates that the Fe moments preserve their collinear $c$-axis AF alignment down to 1.5 K at zero field. Inelastic neutron scattering reveals a distinctive Y-shape spin excitation dispersion exhibiting a two-gap structure below 10 meV, likely arising from two nondegenerate spin-wave branches. Polarized neutron measurements further show that the low-temperature spin excitations are strongly anisotropic: the in-plane component $M_{ab}$ dominates while the $c$-axis component $M_c$ is nearly absent. Upon warming, $M_c$ gradually grows markedly but remains much weaker than $M_{ab}$ even at 300 K. We propose that the unconventional AHE originates from a nonzero scalar spin chirality induced by field-driven spin canting either in the static or dynamic configurations due to gapless excitations. These findings establish the collinear AF kagome metal Y$_{0.5}$Fe$_3$Sn$_3$ as a platform for chirality-driven AHE without spontaneous spin reorientation.

</details>


### [93] [Intertwined complex orbital order and ferrimagnetism in honeycomb molybdate Fe$_2$Mo$_3$O$_8$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29729)
*Hong-Hao Song,Gang v. Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文借鉴蜂巢钼酸盐 A$_2$Mo$_3$O$_8$ 的实验研究兴趣，重建并发展用于描述 Fe$_2$Mo$_3$O$_8$ 基本物理的最小模型，指出该体系磁性源于四面体亚晶格自旋轨道耦合与八面体亚晶格超交换的相互诱导，并探讨杂化磁振子-声子激发及其 Berry 曲率与热霍尔效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期关于蜂巢钼酸盐（honeycomb molybdates）A$_2$Mo$_3$O$_8$ 实验研究的推动，需要对 Fe$_2$Mo$_3$O$_8$ 的磁性机制建立能刻画其核心物理的最小模型（minimal models）。

Method: 构建最小模型，描写四面体亚晶格（tetrahedral sublattice）上活性轨道的自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）与八面体亚晶格（octahedral sublattice）自旋超交换（superexchange）之间的相互诱导；并进一步计算杂化磁振子-声子（magnon-phonon）激发及其 Berry 曲率（Berry curvature）与热霍尔效应（thermal Hall effect）。

Result: 模型指出，四面体与八面体亚晶格间的超交换直接驱动亚铁磁性（ferrimagnetism），而四面体亚晶格上残余的自旋轨道耦合诱导出复杂的轨道序（orbital order）。杂化磁振子-声子激发及其相应的 Berry 曲率与热霍尔效应得到进一步探讨。

Conclusion: 讨论了与 Fe$_2$Mo$_3$O$_8$ 的实验关联，并指出该框架可推广至 FeNiMo$_3$O$_8$、Ni$_2$Mo$_3$O$_8$ 及其他同构磁体（isostructural magnets）。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent experimental interest in honeycomb molybdates A$_2$Mo$_3$O$_8$, we return and develop the minimal models to capture the essential physics for Fe$_2$Mo$_3$O$_8$. It is pointed out that, the magnetism in these materials arises from the mutual induction between the spin-orbit coupling of the tetrahedral sublattice with active orbitals and the superexchange with the spins from the octahedral sublattice. Essentially, the superexchange between the tetrahedral and octahedral sublattices directly drives the ferrimagnetism, and the residual spin-orbit coupling on the tetrahedral sublattice induces the complex orbital order. The hybrid magnon-phonon excitations are further explored together with their resulting Berry curvatures and thermal Hall effect. We discuss the experimental relevance with Fe$_2$Mo$_3$O$_8$, and the framework can be extended to FeNiMo$_3$O$_8$, Ni$_2$Mo$_3$O$_8$ and other isostructural magnets.

</details>


### [94] [Quantum criticality in a two-component two-dimensional nematic metal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29745)
*Yuhang Xiao,Ning Hao*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究含双分量内部自由度的二维向列金属（nematic metal），指出纵向向列涨落给出非费米液体（non-Fermi-liquid）自能∝|ω|^{2/3}，而横向涨落虽仅产生次领头的对数性边缘费米液体（marginal-Fermi-liquid）修正，却通过量子有序化无序机制（quantum order-by-disorder）主导相图修正，使相变变为一级并遮蔽裸量子临界点（QCP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 向列涨落会改变低能电子行为并重塑平均场相图，但纵向与横向涨落对自能与相图修正的相对重要性、以及量子临界点是否可观测，尚需澄清。

Method: 在二维向列金属模型中引入双分量内部自由度，分别计算量子临界点附近纵向与横向向列涨落对自能的贡献，并借助量子有序化无序机制分析向列背景对电子谱、向列玻色子衰减及自由能奇异修正的影响，用标度函数刻画自由能修正。

Result: 纵向涨落产生∝|ω|^{2/3}的非费米液体自能；横向涨落给出次领头的对数性边缘费米液体修正，但通过量子有序化无序产生负的奇异自由能修正，其标度函数的两个渐近极限对应自由能修正分别按R^{8/3}与R^3标度（R为序参量幅度），幂次源于正常态向列涨落特征动量与有序态背景特征动量的竞争。

Conclusion: 该自由能修正重构相图并将相变驱动为一级；扩大的节点α相（nodal α phase）遮蔽了原本的裸量子临界点使其不可及，因而对数自能修正在低能下绝不可忽略；两个临界区间可通过向列磁化率（nematic susceptibility）的滞回测量实验区分。

Abstract: Nematic fluctuations alter the low-energy electronic behavior and reshape the mean-field phase diagram. We study a two-dimensional nematic metal with a two-component internal degree of freedom. Near the quantum critical point (QCP), longitudinal nematic fluctuations generate the non-Fermi-liquid self-energy $\propto|ω|^{2/3}$, whereas transverse fluctuations give a subleading logarithmic, marginal-Fermi-liquid correction. Nevertheless, the transverse sector dominates the correction to the phase diagram through a quantum order-by-disorder mechanism: the emergent nematic background reshapes the electronic spectrum, alters the decay of nematic bosons, and produces a negative singular free-energy correction. This correction reconstructs the phase diagram, drives the transition first order, and is described by a scaling function whose two asymptotic limits define two critical regimes with free-energy corrections scaling as $R^{8/3}$ and $R^3$, respectively, where $R$ is the order-parameter magnitude; their powers follow from the competition between the characteristic momentum of normal-state nematic fluctuations and that of the ordered-state background. These two regimes are experimentally distinguishable through nematic-susceptibility hysteresis measurements. The corrected phase diagram shows that the enlarged nodal $α$ phase masks the otherwise bare QCP, making it inaccessible; consequently, the logarithmic self-energy correction is by no means negligible at low energies.

</details>


### [95] [Charge Density Wave transient states induced by ultrashort hard X-ray pulses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29860)
*D. Ghoneim,E. Bellec,L. Ortega,V. L. R. Jacques,A. A Sinchenko,P. Wzietek,U. Boesenberg,K. Kazarian,J. Moeller,J. D. Moore,A. Rodriguez-Fernandez,R. Shayduk,A. Zozulya,A. Madsen,D. Le Bolloc'h*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究揭示超短X射线脉冲泵浦可在电荷密度波（Charge Density Wave, CDW）体系中驱动一种新型可逆非平衡态，其演化沿非热动力学路径，并伴随波矢、关联长度、电子能隙与维度的多重变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子体系与超短激光脉冲相互作用可产生远离平衡的瞬态态，其中CDW体系在红外超短光激发下具有丰富的非平衡物理；而X射线泵浦与激光泵浦的作用机制可能截然不同，值得探究。

Method: 以NbSe$_3$体系为对象，采用能量比激光脉冲高约6000倍的超短X射线脉冲同时对整个体相（bulk）进行泵浦与探测，追踪CDW态随X射线脉冲到达的连续演化。

Result: 观测到连续的CDW态沿非热动力学路径演化，表现为2k$_F$波矢、关联长度（correlation length）与电子能隙（electronic gap）的变化以及维度的改变，整个过程处于450 ns至0.01 s的极长恢复时间（recovery time）范围内。

Conclusion: X射线泵浦可将CDW体系驱动至一种新颖的可逆非平衡态；作者提出包括带电拓扑对象（charged topological objects）的瞬态成核（transient nucleation）与复合（recombination）在内的若干解释情景，但尚未给出确定结论。

Abstract: The interaction of quantum systems with ultrashort laser pulses may lead to transient states far from equilibrium. Among these, Charge Density Wave (CDW) systems offer very rich non-equilibrium physics when submitted to ultrashort optical excitations in the infrared regime. In this Letter, we show that CDW systems pumped by ultrashort x-ray pulses excite the system in very different way. The x-ray pump, 6000 times more energetic than a laser pulse, pumping and probing the whole bulk system simultaneously, drives CDW systems in a novel reversible out of equilibrium state. The successive CDW states observed in the NbSe$_3$ system with respect to the arrival of x-ray pulses follow a non-thermal kinetic path. It is characterized by a change in 2k$_F$ wavevector, correlation lengths and electronic gap, as well as a change of dimensionality, all of this takes place within a very long recovery time ranging from 450$ns$ to 0.01$s$. Several scenarios are proposed, including transient nucleation and recombination of charged topological objects.

</details>


### [96] [Stabilizing and detecting electron fractionalization in the Hofstadter-Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29916)
*Jiahao Yang,Zeyu Han,Gang v. Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 受莫尔体系关联陈绝缘体进展启发，研究半填充三角晶格 Hofstadter–Hubbard 模型与电子分数化；轨道磁通增强并拓宽 U(1)_2 手性自旋液体相，并产生可分辨的谱学特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期莫尔（moiré）体系中关联陈绝缘体（correlated Chern insulator）物理取得进展，促使研究半填充三角晶格 Hofstadter–Hubbard 模型中的电子分数化（electron fractionalization）及其非磁竞争态。

Method: 在 Mott 区研究多种竞争性非磁态，通过调节轨道磁通（orbital flux）考察 U(1)_2 手性自旋液体（chiral spin liquid）相的稳定性与范围，并将电子分数化为有能隙的自旋子（spinon）与荷子（chargon）。

Result: 增大轨道磁通稳定并拓宽 U(1)_2 手性自旋液体相；自旋子与荷子均具能隙，其有效能带与轨道磁通相关，窄自旋子能与荷子激发的卷积在电子谱函数（spectral function）与态密度中产生丰富且可分辨的结构。

Conclusion: 轨道磁通可作为调控电子分数化模式与 Mott 转变（Mott transition）性质的参数；所得谱学特征为角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）与扫描隧道显微（STM）研究提供判据，将自由粒子能带拓扑与关联区本征拓扑序（topological order）联系起来。

Abstract: Motivated by the recent progress in correlated Chern insulator physics in moiré systems, we investigate the half-filled triangular-lattice Hofstadter--Hubbard model and its electron fractionalization. Among various competing non-magnetic states, increasing the orbital flux stabilizes and broadens the $\mathrm{U}(1)_2$ chiral spin liquid phase in the Mott regime. In this exotic regime, the electron is fractionalized into gapped spinons and gapped chargons, whose effective bands are linked to the orbital flux. The convolution of the narrow spinon bands with the gapped chargon excitations produces rich and discernible structures in the electron spectral function and the density of states. These results not only identify the orbital flux as a control parameter for the detailed patterns of electron fractionalization and the nature of the Mott transition, but also provide the key spectral characteristics for the angle-resolved photoemission and scanning tunneling studies of the relevant systems, linking the free-particle band topology to intrinsic topological order in the correlated regime.

</details>


### [97] [Projective symmetry groups beyond spin-$1/2$: Classifying integer-spin quantum spin liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30048)
*Atanu Maity,Chunxiao Liu,Rhine Samajdar,Yasir Iqbal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文将 Wen 的投影对称群（PSG）框架推广到任意自旋 $S$ 的费米子部分子（fermionic partons），指出整数与半奇数自旋在规范结构上本质不同，并给出整数自旋下三条与晶格无关的结论，最后将其应用于自旋-1 金刚石晶格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 PSG 仅适用于自旋-$1/2$ 体系，而当前众多候选材料与量子模拟器具有自旋-$1$ 或更大磁矩，缺乏可比的分类框架。

Method: 为任意自旋 $S$ 的费米子部分子构建 PSG，比较整数与半奇数自旋的不变规范群（invariant gauge group）层级，并推导整数自旋下的三条晶格无关结果；随后应用于阻挫 A 位尖晶石中实现的自旋-1 金刚石晶格。

Result: 半奇数自旋给出熟悉的 $\mathrm{SU}(2)$、$\mathrm{U}(1)$、$\mathbb{Z}_2$ 规范群；整数自旋给出包含 $\mathrm{U}(1)\rtimes\mathbb{Z}_2$ 与 $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$ 的不同层级，后者在自旋-$1/2$ 中无对应物。自旋-1 金刚石晶格在至第二近邻范围内得到十个全对称 Ansatz；平均场层面基态依赖于约束施加方式，粒子-空穴表示下最低态为完全有能隙的 $\mathrm{U}(1)$ 态，其两子晶格处于相反的约束扇区，其中一个与配对 Ansatz 规范等价。

Conclusion: 该分类为其他晶格与材料中的整数自旋量子自旋液体（quantum spin liquids）研究提供了出发点。

Abstract: Wen's projective symmetry group (PSG), introduced a quarter of a century ago, reduced the classification of symmetric quantum spin liquids to an algebraic problem and has guided the study of spin-$1/2$ models and materials ever since. Many current candidate materials and quantum simulators, however, have spin-$1$ or larger moments, and no comparable classification exists for them. We formulate the PSG for fermionic partons of arbitrary spin $S$. Integer and half-odd-integer spins already differ in their gauge structure. Half-odd-integer spins give the familiar $\mathrm{SU}(2)$, $\mathrm{U}(1)$, and $\mathbb{Z}_2$ invariant gauge groups, while integer spins give a different hierarchy that includes $\mathrm{U}(1)\rtimes\mathbb{Z}_2$ and $\mathbb{Z}_2\times\mathbb{Z}_2$; the latter has no spin-$1/2$ counterpart. Three lattice-independent results follow for integer spin. Singlet pairing of the partons is odd under site exchange. A lattice symmetry can exchange the particle and hole representations of the local constraint, which forces symmetry-related sites into opposite constraint sectors. And a time-reversal-symmetric $\mathrm{U}(1)$ state with the same constraint on every site can be gapped only if the number of sites per unit cell is a multiple of $2S+1$. We apply the formalism to the spin-$1$ diamond lattice, realized in frustrated A-site spinels, and find ten fully symmetric Ansätze up to second neighbors. In the $J_1$-$J_2$ Heisenberg model at the mean-field level, the ground state depends on how the constraint is imposed. With the particle and hole representations, the lowest states are fully gapped $\mathrm{U}(1)$ states whose two sublattices sit in opposite constraint sectors, and one of them is gauge equivalent to a paired Ansatz. The classification provides a starting point for integer-spin quantum spin liquids on other lattices and in other materials.

</details>


<div id='physics.ins-det'></div>

# physics.ins-det [[Back]](#toc)

### [98] [remage: a Geant4-based simulation framework for low-background experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29655)
*Toby Dixon,Eric Esch,Reyco Henning,Manuel Huber,Moritz Neuberger,Luigi Pertoldi,Zichen Wang*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: remage 是一个开源蒙特卡罗（Monte Carlo）模拟框架，旨在标准化低本底物理（low-background physics）中的模拟工作，将几何构建与编译应用分离，并已针对放射成因与宇宙成因本底完成验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低本底物理的模拟通常需要各合作组自行开发并维护复杂的 Geant4 应用，投入成本高且重复劳动多，因此需要统一的标准化方案以显著降低工作量。

Method: 该框架将几何建模（基于专用 Python 工具构建）与编译后的应用程序解耦，并提供针对放射成因（radiogenic）与宇宙成因（cosmogenic）本底调校的物理列表（physics list）和事件产生器（event generators），输出适配常见探测器类型，同时以现代软件开发实践支撑验证。

Result: 摘要报告了 remage 的设计与验证工作：软件以开箱即用形式发布，可随大规模模拟所需的计算资源高效扩展，并具备稳健的验证体系。

Conclusion: remage 主要为 LEGEND 实验开发，但适用于多种类型的实验，有望成为低本底物理模拟的通用开源框架。

Abstract: Monte Carlo simulations are an essential tool for data modeling and experimental design in low-background physics. These simulations typically demand a considerable effort, with each collaboration developing and maintaining its own complex Geant4 application. In this paper we present the design and validation of remage, which is an open-source framework designed to standardize these simulations and significantly reduce this effort. Its design separates the geometry, built with dedicated Python tools, from the compiled application. It provides a physics list and event generators tuned to radiogenic and cosmogenic backgrounds, output tailored to the most common detector types, and robust validation backed by modern software development practices. The software is distributed ready to use and scales efficiently with the computing resources that demanding simulations require. remage has been primarily developed for the LEGEND experiment, but is applicable to a wide variety of experiments.

</details>


### [99] [NTL amplification in a SuperCDMS-style detector fabricated from a University of South Dakota-grown germanium crystal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29973)
*Dongming Mei,Gerardo Gonzalez,William Baker,Mark Platt,Nader Mirabolfathi*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该研究评估了南达科他大学（USD）生长的锗晶体在约10 mK下作为SuperCDMS型无热声子探测器时的Neganov-Trofimov-Luke（NTL）放大性能。无接触偏压未产生可分辨放大，但沉积600 nm铝偏压接触后，探测器2出现明显的电压依赖NTL增益，能量从0 V的约3.9 keV增至40 V的34.9 keV（8.9倍增强）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NTL放大可为低质量暗物质（low-mass dark matter）与相干弹性中微子-核散射（coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering, CEAνS）实验提供eV至亚keV的探测阈值，因此需验证USD生长锗晶体能否实现该放大。

Method: 对两块USD生长锗晶体制作成SuperCDMS风格四通道无热声子探测器（athermal-phonon detector）并在约10 mK运行；晶体电活性杂质浓度约10^10 cm^-3、蚀刻坑密度300–7000 cm^-2；先采用无接触偏压（contact-free biasing），再沉积600 nm铝偏压接触进行对比测试。

Result: 无接触偏压无可分辨放大；加铝接触后探测器2呈现清晰电压依赖NTL增益，通道B重建能量由0 V约3.9 keV升至40 V的34.9 keV（8.9倍）。统计质心不确定度低于0.3%，低偏压拟合得有效瞬发声子与NTL声子响应系数分别为(6.77±0.82)%与(4.77±0.20)%（不含系统不确定度）。

Conclusion: 结果证明USD生长锗可用于SuperCDMS型高压声子探测器，并表明晶体质量与接触制备对实现NTL放大均至关重要。

Abstract: Neganov-Trofimov-Luke (NTL) amplification enables eV-sub-keV detection thresholds for low-mass dark-matter and coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering experiments. We evaluated two University of South Dakota (USD)-grown germanium crystals fabricated as SuperCDMS-style four-channel athermal-phonon detectors and operated near 10 mK. The crystals had electrically active impurity concentrations near $10^10~\mathrm^-3$ and etch-pit densities of $300$-$7000~\mathrm^-2$. Contact-free biasing produced no resolvable amplification. However, after depositing a 600 nm aluminum bias contact, Detector~2 exhibited clear voltage-dependent NTL gain. The reconstructed channel-B energy increased from approximately 3.9 keV at 0 V to 34.9 keV at 40 V, corresponding to an 8.9-fold enhancement. Statistical centroid uncertainties were below 0.3%, while low-bias fits yielded effective prompt- and NTL-phonon response coefficients of $(6.77\pm0.82)%$ and $(4.77\pm0.20)%$, respectively, excluding systematic uncertainties. These results demonstrate the feasibility of USD-grown germanium for SuperCDMS-style high-voltage phonon detectors and highlight the importance of both crystal quality and contact fabrication in achieving NTL amplification.

</details>

# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [100] [Prior volume effect inflates the gravitational-wave background in pulsar timing arrays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28739)
*Biwei Dai,Matias Zaldarriaga,Yacine Ali-Haïmoud*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文指出 NANOGrav 15 年数据中超出超大质量黑洞双星（SMBHBs）预期的纳赫兹引力波背景（GWB）幅度，主要源于先验体积效应（prior volume effect），修正先验后幅度可回落至与 SMBHBs 一致的区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉冲星计时阵列（PTAs）探测到的纳赫兹引力波背景幅度高于多数 SMBHBs 理论预测，需厘清该超出是物理信号还是推断方法（先验）带来的偏差。

Method: 基于 NANOGrav 15 年数据，对 GWB 后验（posterior）进行分解，考察标准对数均匀红噪先验（log-uniform red-noise priors）经边缘化（marginalization）引入的先验体积项；并对比剖面似然（profile likelihood）、仅用互相关（cross-correlation-only）的估计量，以及功率均匀先验或由脉冲星族群给出的先验。

Result: GWB 幅度主要由自相关（auto-correlations）约束，与脉冲星内禀红噪（intrinsic red noise）简并；先验体积项偏好更强 GWB，将推断幅度拉高至最大似然估计（maximum-likelihood estimate）之上逾 2σ，而 Hellings-Downs（HD）互相关不足以抵消该效应。

Conclusion: 修正先验可消除或削弱该效应，使幅度与 SMBHBs 一致，同时给出更接近 SMBHB 预测的陡峭谱（steeper spectrum）、增强 HD 互相关证据，并削弱对新物理源（new-physics sources）相对 SMBHBs 的偏好。

Abstract: Pulsar timing arrays (PTAs) have found evidence for a nanohertz gravitational-wave background (GWB) whose amplitude exceeds most predictions based on supermassive black hole binaries (SMBHBs). Using NANOGrav 15-year data, we show that a substantial part of this excess arises from a prior volume effect. The GWB amplitude is constrained mainly by the pulsars' auto-correlations, where the GWB is degenerate with each pulsar's intrinsic red noise. By decomposing the GWB posterior, we find that marginalizing over the standard log-uniform red-noise priors introduces a prior volume term that favors a stronger GWB, pulling the inferred amplitude more than $2σ$ above the maximum-likelihood estimate. The Hellings-Downs (HD) cross-correlations, which measure the GWB alone, are not strong enough to counter the volume term. The profile likelihood, a cross-correlation-only estimator, and priors that are uniform in power or informed by the pulsar population all avoid or reduce this effect and lower the amplitude into agreement with SMBHBs. The revised priors also yield a steeper GWB spectrum closer to the SMBHB predictions, strengthen the evidence for HD correlations, and weaken the preference for new-physics sources over SMBHBs.

</details>


### [101] [Can we constrain the binary neutron star merger time from kilonova light curves?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28775)
*Noah Jamsin,Marion Pillas,Marko Ristić,Laurence E. H. Datrier,Daniel Brethauer,Stefano Ascenzi,Mattia Bulla,Barbara Patricelli,Silvia Piranomonte,Henrik Rose,Nikhil Sarin,Thibeau Wouters,Khun Sang Phukon,Alex Jacquesson,Samuele Ronchini,Alberto Revilla-Peña,Tyler Barna,Michael W. Coughlin,Tim Dietrich,Richard O'Shaughnessy,Fabio Ragosta*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究探索利用千新星（kilonova, KN）光变曲线反演双中子星（binary neutron star, BNS）并合时刻的可行性，重点评估Vera C. Rubin天文台在不同观测条件下的约束能力。结果显示，真实观测条件下并合时刻可恢复至约5.5天精度（90%置信区间），理想情形可达亚天量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多信使天文学（multimessenger astronomy）随GW170817等事件兴起，未来BNS并合与KN探测将增多，但KN探测未必对应电磁辐射（EM emission）起始，难以界定与引力波（gravitational wave, GW）事件符合的时间窗口，因此需要从KN光变曲线独立约束并合时刻。

Method: 采用多种KN模型与不同推断框架（inference frameworks）对模拟及真实KN光变曲线进行拟合，考察不同观测节奏（cadence）与波段覆盖（wavelength coverage）的影响；测试涵盖AT2017gfo真实数据及若干与KN候选体相关的伽马射线暴（gamma-ray burst, GRB）。

Result: 在Vera C. Rubin天文台的真实观测条件下，并合时刻可恢复精度为5.5天（90% CI），最佳情形下准确度0.18天、精度0.74天（90% CI）；若已知源红移（redshift）结果可改善约5%，并合时刻附近的上限（upper limits）可使后验分布（posterior）宽度缩小约60%。

Conclusion: BNS并合时刻可从KN光变曲线中获得有效约束，且其他望远镜的电磁随访观测（EM follow-up observations）有望进一步改进估计，为GW与KN的时间符合判据提供依据。

Abstract: The joint observation of gravitational waves (GWs) from a binary neutron star (BNS) merger (GW170817), together with a short Gamma-Ray Burst (GRB 170817A) and a kilonova (KN, AT2017gfo), marked the birth of multimessenger astronomy with GWs, and demonstrated the informative power of multimessenger observations. More BNS merger and KN detections are expected , thanks to the increasing sensitivity of GW detectors and the advent of new electromagnetic facilities such as the Vera C. Rubin Observatory. However, the association between a BNS merger and a KN might not be trivial: the detection of a KN might not coincide with the onset of the EM emission, making it difficult to define a temporal window to assess the coincidence with a GW event. In this work we investigate the possibility to constrain the BNS merger time from KN light curves, exploring different observing conditions and with focus on the Vera C. Rubin Observatory. We used different KN models and different inference frameworks to simulate and fit KN light curves. We explored different cadences and wavelength coverages for observations, to investigate how the different observing conditions can affect the capability to recover the BNS merger time. The tests performed also include real data related to AT2017gfo, plus a few GRBs that have been associated to possible KN candidates. We find that, under realistic observing conditions with the Vera C. Rubin Observatory, the BNS merger time can be recovered with a precision of 5.5 days (90\% CI) with a best-case scenario with an accuracy of 0.18 day and a precision of 0.74 day (90 \% CI). Results can slightly improve (by $\sim$ 5 \%) if the source redshift is available. Upper limits near the merger time can also improve the results, reducing the width of the posterior by $\sim60$ \%. The BNS merger time estimate could also benefit from EM follow-up observations with other telescopes.

</details>


### [102] [General Relativistic Simulations of the Nonlinear Evolution of r-modes in Rapidly Rotating Neutron Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29259)
*Justin K. Yu,Andrew Melatos*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该文通过广义相对论数值模拟研究快速旋转中子星中 $l=|m|=2$ 的非线性 r 模，放弃 Cowling 近似与小振幅近似，测量了 r 模频率、应变及非线性现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旋转中子星中的 r 模（r-modes）是连续引力波（continuous gravitational waves）的潜在来源，其振幅可增长或被激发至 $α\lesssim1$，此时线性近似与 Cowling 近似可能失效，故需开展非线性、完全相对论性的模拟研究。

Method: 采用广义相对论（general relativistic）数值模拟，将星体理想化为无磁、单组分、理想流体（perfect fluid），状态方程为多方方程（polytropic equation of state）；自转频率约 792 Hz，初始振幅 $α_0$ 在 0.2–1.2 间变化，并基于 Newman-Penrose 标量计算引力波应变。

Result: $α_0=0.2$ 时 r 模频率为 1014 Hz，无量纲频率 $κ\approx0.7197$，与线性微扰理论估计相差在 1.7% 以内；频率随振幅平方增长，$κ$ 在 $α_0$ 由 0.2 增至 1.2 范围内下降 3.4%。$α_0=0.2$、距离 10 kpc 时应变约 $9.7\times10^{-22}$，与爱因斯坦四极公式（quadrupole formula）吻合在 35% 以内。

Conclusion: 模拟中观测到差异转动（differential rotation）、振幅衰减（amplitude decay）以及时空振荡中非线性模式耦合（nonlinear mode coupling）的特征，表明非线性效应在较大振幅 r 模演化中不可忽略。

Abstract: The r-modes in rotating neutron stars are a promising source of continuous gravitational waves, and may grow or be excited to amplitudes $α\lesssim1$. We perform general relativistic simulations of nonlinear $l=|m|=2$ r-modes in a rapidly rotating neutron star with spin frequency $f_{spin}\approx792$ Hz, relaxing the Cowling and small-$α$ approximations. The star is idealised as an unmagnetised, one-component, perfect fluid with a polytropic equation of state. The r-mode frequency is measured to be $f_{r-mode}=1014$ Hz for initial amplitude $α_0=0.2$. The dimensionless frequency $κ\approx0.7197$ agrees with an estimate from linear perturbation theory to within 1.7%. $f_{r-mode}$ increases quadratically with amplitude, and $κ$ decreases by 3.4% across simulations where $α_0$ is varied through the range $0.2\leqα_0\leq1.2$. The gravitational wave strain calculated from the Newman-Penrose scalar implies $h_{r-mode}\approx9.7\times10^{-22}$ at $10$ kpc for $α_0=0.2$, which agrees with Einstein's quadrupole formula to within 35%. We also report on nonlinear phenomena observed in our simulations, e.g. differential rotation, amplitude decay, and signatures of nonlinear mode coupling in the spacetime oscillations.

</details>


### [103] [Lasting mEMRIs: Signatures of Multiple Black Hole Extreme Mass Ratio Inspirals from AGN discs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29376)
*Jordan W. N. Moncrieff,Evgeni Grishin,Fiona H. Panther,Alessandro A. Trani*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 活动星系核盘中的恒星级黑洞对可形成平运动共振；本文给出其保持完整的判据，并首次展示共迁移共振对的引力波应变特征（mEMRI），估计其发生率约为每年10例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 活动星系核（active galactic nuclei, AGN）盘中的恒星级黑洞（stellar-mass black hole, sBH）相遇会自然形成平运动共振（mean-motion resonance）对，但这类系统能否在靠近中心超大质量黑洞处被引力波探测、以及其信号形态尚不清楚。

Method: 推导了仅依赖sBH质量比与共振指数（resonance index）的简单判据，用于判断共振对在引力波辐射主导气体力矩（gas torques）时是否保持完整；并计算了一对共迁移（co-migrating）共振sBH的引力波应变（gravitational-wave strain）信号，包含轨道会合（orbital conjunctions）引起的额外谐波。

Result: 频率演化与啁啾质量（chirp mass）与不同质量的孤立EMRI存在简并（degenerate）；但mEMRI的质量比被共振阶数量子化且大于真实质量比，若能从单个应变振幅推断真实质量比则可破除简并。局部宇宙（z ≲ 0.2）中预计约每年10例mEMRI。

Conclusion: 若盘驱动的EMRI能被LISA探测，mEMRI应较为普遍，因此未来LISA数据分析中需要对mEMRI系统进行建模。

Abstract: Encounters of stellar-mass black holes (sBH) in active galactic nuclei discs naturally form mean-motion resonances of sBH pairs. These mean-motion resonances can be broken close to the central supermassive black hole when gravitational-wave emission dominates over gas torques. We derive a simple condition that depends only on the sBH mass ratio and the resonance index for which a pair of sBH in a mean-motion resonance will remain intact and can be detectable as a multiple Extreme-Mass-Ratio-Inspirals (mEMRIs) in the LISA band. We present for the first time the gravitational-wave strain signatures of a pair of co-migrating sBH in mean-motion resonance, including additional gravitational-wave harmonics associated with orbital conjunctions of the individual EMRIs. The frequency evolution and the associated chirp mass is degenerate with isolated EMRIs of different masses. However, the mEMRI mass ratio is quantised by the resonance order and is larger than the true mass ratio. The degeneracy can be broken if the true sBH mass ratio can be inferred from individual strain amplitudes. We argue that mEMRIs should be common if disc driven EMRIs are to be detected by LISA, with $\sim 10$ mEMRIs per year in the local universe ($z \lesssim 0.2$), highlighting the need to model mEMRI systems in future LISA data analysis.

</details>

### [104] [Experimental determination of the double $K$-shell vacancy creation probability in the electron-capture decay of $^{55}$Fe with a microcalorimeter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29715)
*Shuo Zhang,Karol Kozioł,Caojie Shao,Ruitian Zhang,Cheng Qian,Kebao Shu,JingKai Xia,Guocheng Wei,Yingli Xue,Huan Jia,Neng Pu,Hanjie Cai,Deyang Yu,Hongqiang Zhang,Liangting Sun,Yuan He,Reinhold Schuch,Xinwen Ma*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 利用高分辨率微热量计（microcalorimeter）测量了 \(^{55}\)Fe 电子俘获衰变（electron-capture decay）后双 K 壳层空穴（double K-shell vacancy）产生的概率，并直接分辨出 Mn 的 K 超卫星线（K-hypersatellite lines）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双 K 壳层空穴产生概率 \(P_{KK}\) 是原子物理与衰变过程的重要参数，需借助高能量分辨手段在主图表线（diagram lines）主导的谱中将其微弱超卫星成分分离出来，并与既有实验与理论结果对照。

Method: 采用高分辨率微热量计测量 \(^{55}\)Fe 的 X 射线发射谱，直接分辨由双 K 壳层空穴填充产生的 K 超卫星线，并测定其能量与相对强度；再由相对强度结合已有荧光产额（fluorescence yield）值推算 \(P_{KK}\)。

Result: 测得 \(Kα_2^h\)、\(Kα_1^h\)、\(Kβ_{1,3}^h\) 能量分别为 (6143.5±0.5) eV、(6162.1±1.5) eV、(6811.9±2.6) eV；超卫星线与图表线 X 射线的相对强度为 (1.30±0.07)×10⁻⁴，由此得到每次 K 壳层俘获的双空穴产生概率 \(P_{KK}\) = (1.22±0.09)×10⁻⁴。

Conclusion: 摘要将所得 \(P_{KK}\) 与先前实验结果及理论计算进行了比较讨论，未在摘要中给出具体比较结论；其核心贡献在于以高分辨谱学直接分辨超卫星线并给出精确的 \(P_{KK}\) 值。

Abstract: The probability of double \(K\)-shell vacancy production following electron-capture decay is determined for $^{55}$Fe using a high-resolution microcalorimeter. The \(K\)-hypersatellite lines arising from double \(K\)-shell-vacancy filling are directly resolved within the measured spectra of X-ray emissions which are dominated by Mn \(K\)-X-ray diagram lines. The energies of the Mn \textit{K}-hypersatellite lines, \(Kα_2^h\), \(Kα_1^h\), and \(Kβ_{1,3}^h\), are measured as $(6143.5\pm0.5)$ eV, $(6162.1\pm1.5)$ eV and $(6811.9\pm2.6)$ eV, respectively. The relative intensities of \textit{K}-hypersatellite to diagram X-rays are measured to be $(1.30 \pm 0.07) \times 10^{-4}$. From the relative intensities together with established fluorescence yield values, the probability of double-vacancy creation per \(K\)-shell capture ($P_{KK}$) is determined as $(1.22 \pm 0.09) \times 10^{-4}$. Comparisons with previous experimental results and theoretical calculations are discussed.

</details>


### [105] [Heavy-Quark Dynamics from $D^{0}$-Meson Anisotropy-Response Scaling Violations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30085)
*Roy A. Lacey*

Main category: nucl-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出物种分辨的 anisotropy-response 标度框架，利用 $D^0$ 介子的椭圆流 $v_2$ 与三角流 $v_3$ 研究重夸克动力学，通过粲夸克特异衰减重标定参数 $k_c$ 与小低-$p_T$ 强子散射修正 $\zeta_{\rm hs}\simeq0.06$ 恢复共同响应，揭示粲夸克标度破缺（charm scaling violation）并为重夸克输运与介质演化提供差分基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻味已独立约束的响应为检验重夸克是否遵循共同标度提供了直接零检验（null test）；若 $D^0$ 谐波偏离以 kaon 为锚点的标度函数，则表明粲夸克存在特有的标度破缺，需要量化其来源。

Method: 采用物种分辨的 anisotropy-response 标度（species-resolved anisotropy-response scaling）框架，检验变换后的 $D^0$ 谐波在谐波间闭合性（inter-harmonic closure）及与 kaon-anchored 标度函数的偏离；以粲特异衰减重标定 $k_c$ 与强子散射修正 $\zeta_{\rm hs}$ 恢复共同响应，并在 Pb+Pb、p+Pb 与 Au+Au 不同碰撞系统中拟合 $k_c$ 与转变动量 $p_{T,\rm tr}$。

Result: 变换后的 $D^0$ 谐波呈近似谐波间闭合，但系统性偏离 kaon 锚定标度函数，确认粲标度破缺。Pb+Pb（$\sqrt{s_{NN}}=5.02$ TeV）中 $k_c$ 由中心/半中心碰撞的约 2.0 降至 30–50\% 区间的 1.27；超中心 p+Pb（8.16 TeV）的 $D^0$ $v_2$ 需 $k_c\simeq1.5$，Au+Au（200 GeV）与 $k_c\simeq1$ 一致。$p_{T,\rm tr}$ 呈系统依赖：Pb+Pb 中 $D^0$ 与轻介子约 4.5–5.0 GeV/$c$，p+Pb 中 $D^0$ 与约 1.6 GeV/$c$ 的轻介子标度一致。

Conclusion: 衰减强度（$k_c$）与转变动量（$p_{T,\rm tr}$）的互补约束构成重夸克输运与介质演化的差分基准（differential benchmark），同时表明大系统与小系统采用不同的标度处方。

Abstract: A species-resolved anisotropy-response scaling framework is used to investigate heavy-quark dynamics through the elliptic ($v_2$) and triangular ($v_3$) azimuthal anisotropies of $D^0$ mesons. The independently constrained light-flavor response provides a direct null test: the transformed $D^0$ harmonics exhibit approximate inter-harmonic closure but remain systematically displaced from the kaon-anchored scaling function, revealing a charm scaling violation. The common response is recovered through a charm-specific attenuation rescaling, $k_c$, together with a small low-$p_T$ hadronic-rescattering correction, $ζ_{\rm hs}\simeq0.06$, while retaining the established light-flavor prescription. In Pb+Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}=5.02$~TeV, $k_c$ decreases from approximately $2.0$ in central and mid-central collisions to $1.27$ in the 30--50\% interval. For $D^0$ $v_2$, ultra-central p+Pb collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}=8.16$~TeV require $k_c\simeq1.5$, whereas Au+Au collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}=200$~GeV are consistent with $k_c\simeq1$. The transition momentum exhibits a distinct system dependence: $D^0$ and light mesons show comparable $p_{T,\rm tr}\simeq4.5$--$5.0$~GeV/$c$ in Pb+Pb, consistent with the common large-system prescription also used in Au+Au, whereas p+Pb $D^0$ measurements are consistent with the lower light-meson scale of approximately $1.6$~GeV/$c$. These complementary constraints on attenuation strength and transition momentum provide a differential benchmark for heavy-quark transport and medium evolution.

</details>

### [106] [Binder migration across film consolidation and pore emptying in electrode drying: a continuous model validated against drying-rate, thickness, and schedule experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29286)
*Younghwan Joo,Sanghyun Oh,Sung-il Kim*

Main category: cs.CE

TL;DR: 该研究提出跨干燥两阶段的溶解黏结剂（binder）连续模拟，用单一参数集复现多类已发表测量，并可用于优化电极干燥的能效与质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 快速干燥可节能，但会使溶解黏结剂向膜表面迁移，耗尽集流体（current collector）界面并削弱黏附；现有迁移模型在膜固结（consolidation）结束时即停止黏结剂对流，而孔排空模型忽略黏结剂或假定其分布，故尚无模拟能贯穿干燥规程所作用的两阶段。

Method: 方法是对溶解黏结剂场在固结与孔排空两阶段进行连续模拟，以已发表的干燥速率为驱动，并以有效描述补充基础输运物理未解决的两个问题：固结膜中液体携运黏结剂的位置，以及孔排空时黏结剂停止移动的时机。

Result: 单一参数集下，模拟复现三类已发表测量：干燥速率变化5.6倍时表/界面黏结剂比的变化及厚6倍膜的表/界面水平、膜内部浓度分布、以及中途切换干燥速率时刻对界面黏结剂的影响；对已发表三段式规程，模型无需额外调整即再现早期降速带来的干燥缩短与界面完全恢复，并显示末段高速率阶段始于黏结剂停止移动之后。

Conclusion: 该模型可衔接涂布机尺度（coater-scale）过程模型，用于识别同时提升能效与产品质量的电极生产操作点。

Abstract: Drying battery electrodes faster saves energy but drives dissolved binder toward the film surface, depleting the current-collector interface and weakening adhesion. Models of this migration stop convecting the binder when film consolidation ends, and models of the subsequent pore emptying omit the binder or start from an assumed binder field, so no simulation has followed binder convection through both stages that a drying schedule acts on. This study presents a continuous simulation of the dissolved binder field across both stages, driven by the published drying rates and completed by effective descriptions of the two questions the base transport physics leaves open: where the liquid of the consolidating film carries the binder, and when the binder stops moving as the pores empty. With a single parameter set, the simulation reproduces three types of published measurements: the change of the surface-to-interface binder ratio with drying rate over a factor of 5.6 and the surface and interface binder levels of films six times thicker; concentration profiles through the film interior; and the response of the interface binder to the time at which the drying rate is switched mid-process. Applied without further adjustment to a published three-stage schedule, the model reproduces the shortened drying and the full interface recovery when the rate is lowered early, and shows that its final high-rate stage begins after the binder has stopped. Combined with a coater-scale process model, the model can be used to identify operating points for electrode production that improve energy efficiency and product quality together.

</details>

### [107] [Mellin transformed exponential volumes and probabilistic moduli spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.26580)
*Alexander B. Goncharov,Zhe Sun*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究装饰曲面（decorated surface）上理想双曲结构（ideal hyperbolic structures）模空间的指数体积（exponential volumes）E(S,K,L)，证明其 Mellin–Laplace 变换具有 Barnes 积分表示并满足递归关系，并将该理论推广为开弦形式的 Schwarzian 场论（Schwarzian field theory）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为延续作者前期工作中引入的指数体积 E(S,K,L) 的研究，并寻求将其从圆盘情形推广到任意装饰曲面，即 Schwarzian 场论的开弦（open string）形式。

Method: 证明 E(S,K,L) 的 Mellin–Laplace 变换（Mellin–Laplace transform）具有 Barnes 积分表示（Barnes integral presentations）并满足递归；对蓝色边界区间（blue boundary intervals）赋权，沿 K 变量取 Mellin 变换（Mellin transform）；并构造携带典范 Wiener 型测度（Wiener type measures）的概率 Teichmüller 空间（probabilistic Teichmüller spaces）。

Result: 所得递归推广了 Mirzakhani 递归；加权 Mellin 变换可计算各类模空间的加权指数体积，当所有区间为蓝色且权重取 1/2 时恢复 Chekhov 的带冠（crowns）双曲曲面模空间体积并离散化 Schwarzian 作用量；概率模空间的体积有限，并给出测度与体积的递归公式。

Conclusion: 所建立的理论将 Stanford–Witten 的 Schwarzian 场论从圆盘推广至任意装饰曲面（开弦情形），并由此获得装饰曲面上 Schwarzian 场论 Feynman 积分（Feynman integrals）的递归关系。

Abstract: A decorated surface is a surface with boundary and a finite set of marked boundary points called cusps. We continue to study the exponential volumes E(S,K,L) of moduli spaces of ideal hyperbolic structures on a decorated surface S, with the given values of lengths of boundary geodesic circles L and boundary geodesic intervals K, introduced in our previous work:
  1. We prove that the Mellin--Laplace transform of E(S,K,L) admits Barnes integral presentations, and satisfies recursions, generalising Mirzakhani's recursions.
  2. We specify a subset I of the blue boundary intervals, and assign to them any collection of weights. We show that the Mellin transform of the function E(S,K,L) along the variables K at the blue intervals, evaluated at the weights values, calculates weighted exponential volumes of various moduli spaces. If all boundary intervals are blue and all weights are 1/2, we recover Chekhov's volumes of moduli spaces of hyperbolic surfaces with crowns, discretizing the Schwarzian action. We derive recursions for the weighted exponential volumes.
  3. We introduce probabilistic Teichmuller spaces related to decorated surfaces S. They are infinite dimensional and carry canonical Wiener type measures. The definition of the measures builds on the works of Bauerschmidt - Losev - Wildemann and Losev. Their quotients by the action of the modular group of S are probabilistic moduli spaces. We show that their volumes are finite. We prove recursion formulas for these measures, and hence for the volumes.
  The emerging theory generalizes the Schwarzian field theory of Stanford - Witten from the disc to any decorated surfaces, i.e. to open string format. In particular, we get recursions for the Feynman integrals in Schwarzian field theory on decorated surfaces.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.soft'></div>

# cond-mat.soft [[Back]](#toc)

### [108] [Influence of embedding depth on the pullout of a plate from a confined granular medium](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29016)
*Dominik Krengel,Jian Chen,Shun Nomura,Hidenori Takahashi*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 采用二维多边形离散元方法（Discrete Element Method）研究致密颗粒介质中板状侵入体的垂直拔出过程，揭示峰值阻力与稳态阻力随埋深及摩擦系数变化的差异，并指出域壁相互作用与颗粒间摩擦强弱会改变聚集体的破坏模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受限颗粒介质（confined granular media）中运动物体的行为在诸多工程应用中具有重要意义，但板状侵入体从启动到稳态绕流的全过程、以及埋深与摩擦条件如何影响阻力与破坏机制，仍需系统研究。

Method: 采用二维多边形离散元方法（two-dimensional polygonal Discrete Element Method），模拟埋置于致密颗粒聚集体（dense granular aggregate）不同深度的板状侵入体（plate-like intruder），追踪其从运动起始到稳态绕流全过程的阻力演化。

Result: 峰值阻力（peak drag force）与稳态阻力（steady-state drag force）对埋深的依赖关系随摩擦系数不同而不同；摩擦较强时板可能与域壁（domain walls）相互作用，使聚集体破坏前阻力增大，但稳态下因侵入体周围的剪胀（dilation）使力链（force chains）向上并远离壁面而不受影响；颗粒间摩擦较弱且埋深超过一定值时，上覆压力（overhead pressure）可克服摩擦阻力。

Conclusion: 弱颗粒间摩擦会阻止侵入体上方剪胀破坏区（dilated failure zones）的形成并改变聚集体的破坏模式（failure mode），导致稳态阻力随拔出距离衰减更慢。

Abstract: Understanding the behaviour of moving objects within confined granular media is important in many technical applications. In this work we study the vertical pullout of a plate-like intruder buried at different depths within a dense granular aggregate from the onset of motion to flow around the intruder in the steady state by means of a two-dimensional polygonal Discrete Element Method. We find that the peak and steady-state drag forces depend differently on their embedding depth for different friction coefficients. Depending on the strength of friction the plate may interact with the domain walls, which enhances the drag force until failure of the aggregate, but not in the steady-state as dilation around intruder directs the force chains emanating from the plate upwards and away from the walls. If the inter-particle friction is weak, it is possible for the overhead pressure to overcome frictional resistance beyond a certain embedding depth. This prevents the formation of dilated failure zones above the intruder and changes the failure mode of the aggregate, leading to a slower decay of the steady-state drag forces with pullout distance.

</details>


### [109] [Numerical Prediction of NaCl Solubility in Water Using the TIP4P/05 Water Model NaCl/Madrid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29163)
*Raúl Fuentes-Azcatl*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 采用NaCl/Madrid力场与TIP4P/05水模型的分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulation）研究NaCl在水中的固液平衡与溶解度；以径向分布函数（radial distribution function）确定区分晶相与水相的合理截断半径（cut-off radius）范围，并据此估计溶解度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 识别晶相离子所依赖的截断半径r_cut具有任意性，缺乏物理依据；研究旨在为NaCl溶解度（solubility）的分子动力学测定建立结构判据，避免截断距离的随意选取。

Method: 从浸没于NaCl水溶液中的NaCl晶体出发（沿用Manzanilla-Granados等提出的构型），平衡后进行长轨迹分析；利用g(r)中主Na-Cl相关峰后的第一个极小值刻画离子配位环境并约束有意义的r_cut范围，再统计晶相残留离子数随r_cut的变化以估计溶解度。

Result: 晶相残留离子数随r_cut变化的曲线呈现明确拐点（inflection point），可作为区分以晶态为主与已溶解离子群体的判据；由此可直接从固液体系演化中确定NaCl溶解度。

Conclusion: 径向分布函数与截断半径依赖分析相结合，为表征固液行为、通过分子动力学模拟估计离子晶体溶解度提供了有效的结构框架。

Abstract: The solid-liquid equilibrium and solubility of NaCl in water were investigated using molecular dynamics simulations with the NaCl/Madrid force field and the TIP4P/05 water model. Particular attention was given to the influence of the intermolecular cut-off radius, rcut, used to identify ions belonging to the crystalline phase. Simulations were initiated from a NaCl crystal immersed in an aqueous NaCl solution, following the configuration proposed by Manzanilla-Granados et al. After equilibration, the system was analysed over an extended simulation trajectory. The radial distribution function, g(r), was used to establish a physically motivated criterion for distinguishing the crystalline and aqueous phases. Specifically, the first minimum following the principal Na-Cl correlation peak was used to characterise the ionic coordination environment and constrain the range of meaningful cut-off radii. The number of ions remaining in the crystalline phase was then evaluated as a function of rcut, allowing the corresponding solubility to be estimated. The resulting curve exhibits a well-defined inflection point, providing a criterion for identifying the transition between predominantly crystalline and dissolved ionic populations. This approach offers a structural basis for determining NaCl solubility directly from the evolution of the solid-liquid system, avoiding the arbitrary selection of the cut-off distance. The results demonstrate that combining radial distribution functions with cut-off-dependent analysis provides a useful framework for characterising solid-liquid behaviour and estimating the solubility of ionic crystals through molecular dynamics simulations.

</details>


### [110] [Stochastic model of elongation-shortening-switching kinetics of a polymeric filament: Critical slowing down of first-passage and finite-size regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29270)
*Manvendra Singh,Ashok Garai,Debashish Chowdhury*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本文构建一个最小随机模型（minimal stochastic model），刻画丝状生物聚合物（filamentous biopolymers）在活性/非活性状态（active/inactive state）间随机切换并伴随伸长/缩短的动力学，将长度命中临界值视为首达时间（first-passage time, FPT）问题并解析求解其分布与前两阶矩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多种丝状生物聚合物表现出非常规的聚合—解聚动力学（polymerization-depolymerization kinetics）：仅在某状态下可持续伸长或缩短，且状态间发生偶发随机切换（random switching）。既有针对具体生物丝的模型较复杂，缺乏可供检验近似理论精度的基准结果，因此需要抓住关键特征的简化模型。

Method: 提出仅保留随机伸长—缩短—切换核心特征的极小随机模型；将缩短过程中长度达到临界值视为终止结构/功能的事件，并以该临界长度为吸收边界（absorbing boundary）计算首达时间；由于实际丝长受限于有限最大值 N，另设反射边界（reflecting boundary）限制生长；据此推导 FPT 的分布及其前两阶矩。

Result: 获得 FPT 分布与前两阶矩的解析结果；识别出一个临界点（critical point），在该点丝端漂移（drift）消失、运动变为纯扩散（purely diffusive）；当 N → ∞ 时，该临界点处 FPT 的涨落发散且平均首达时间（MFPT）趋于无穷，表现为临界慢化（critical slowing down）。

Conclusion: 该解析结果可作为基准（testing ground），用于检验更贴近实际的特定生物丝聚合—解聚动力学模型的近似理论精度。

Abstract: Several filamentous biopolymers exhibit unusual polymerization-depolymerization kinetics where continued elongation (shortening) is possible only in an `active' (`inactive') state while occasional random transition (switching) between the active and inactive states takes place. Here, we present a minimal stochastic model capturing only the key features of random elongation-shortening-switching kinetics of a generic filament. The structure and/or function of the filament ends when its length hits a critical value during its shortening; the time to reach this critical length can be formulated as a first-passage time (FPT) and for its calculation the critical length is treated as an absorbing boundary. Moreover, the lengths of the filaments that inspire our model are constrained to a finite maximum value $\mathcal{N}$; therefore, in our model we impose a reflecting boundary beyond which the filament cannot grow. We determined the distribution of the FPT and derived its first two moments. We identify a critical point where the drift of the tip of the filament vanishes making its movement purely diffusive; at this critical point, in the limit $\mathcal{N} \to \infty$, the fluctuations in the FPT diverges and MFPT becomes infinite indicating `critical slowing down'. Our analytical results will serve as the testing ground for the accuracy of the approximate theoretical results of more realistic models for polymerization-depolymerization kinetics of specific bio-filaments.

</details>


### [111] [Tuning Thermal Memory and Dissipation in Colloidal Glasses via Microgel Particle Stiffness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29389)
*Sonali Vasant Kawale,Ranjini Bandyopadhyay*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 通过调节交联剂浓度合成不同刚度的PNIPAM微凝胶，发现中等刚度微凝胶可最大化Kovacs不对称性，并将该不对称性确立为非平衡玻璃态动力学的可靠探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kovacs效应（Kovacs effect）是玻璃态体系非线性、路径依赖结构回复的标志，而软胶体微凝胶玻璃中颗粒刚度如何影响这一路径依赖回复尚不明确。

Method: 调整极性交联剂MBA浓度合成不同刚度微凝胶，采用振荡流变学（oscillatory rheology）在不同升温速率下测量储能与损耗模量弛豫，并用冷冻场发射扫描电镜（cryo-FESEM）直接观测颗粒堆积结构。

Result: 软颗粒的快速适应性与刚性颗粒的动力学阻滞均抑制Kovacs不对称性，而中等刚度颗粒具适度热响应性，在升降温过程中穿越不同能量景观，使不对称性与耗散最大化；cryo-FESEM图像与流变结果一致。

Conclusion: 中等刚度PNIPAM微凝胶优化了结构回复，是形成胶体玻璃的潜力材料；Kovacs不对称性可作为玻璃态体系非平衡动力学的稳健探针。

Abstract: The Kovacs effect, characterized by nonlinear, path-dependent structural recovery, is a hallmark of glassy systems. In soft colloidal microgel glasses, the stiffness of the constituent particles governs their deformation, swelling, rearrangement, and response to external stimuli, thereby dictating the path-dependent structural recovery of these jammed non-equilibrium systems. To investigate this influence, microgels of different stiffnesses were synthesized by adjusting the concentration of the polar crosslinker, N, N'-methylenebisacrylamide (MBA). The relaxation of the storage and loss moduli was measured using oscillatory rheology under diverse temperature ramp rates, and cryo-FESEM imaging provided direct visualization of the underlying particle packing. While the rapid adaptability of soft particles and the kinetic arrest of stiff ones suppresses Kovacs asymmetry, intermediate-stiffness particles exhibit moderate thermoresponsivity, traversing distinct energy landscapes on heating and cooling to maximize asymmetry and dissipation. Cryo-FESEM micrographs, displaying gel-like networks in soft particle suspensions, dense packing in stiff particle suspensions, and disordered, compact structures in intermediate-stiffness systems, corroborate these findings. Our results demonstrate that intermediate-stiffness PNIPAM microgels optimize structural recovery, rendering them promising candidates for forming colloidal glasses and establishing Kovacs asymmetry as a robust probe of non-equilibrium dynamics in glassy systems.

</details>


### [112] [Mechanism of delayed elastic state absorption versus fatigue failure in cyclic shear of amorphous materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29615)
*Samuel B. Walker,Suzanne M. Fielding*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究在非晶材料（amorphous materials）的格点弹塑性模型（lattice elastoplastic model）中，将循环剪切下的"吸收态弛豫"映射为有限区间上带两个吸收边界的单元素扩散问题，并据此统一解释了阈值上下的疲劳与失效行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环剪切下非晶材料的阈值行为（缓慢趋于弹性态 vs. 疲劳失效）缺乏机制层面的理解，本文旨在给出该行为的微观力学解释。

Method: 基于格点弹塑性模型的循环剪切模拟，识别出随退火（annealing）增大的阈值剪切幅值 γ_c；通过将活跃元（active elements）比例的衰减映射为有限区间双吸收边界上的单元素扩散，推导衰减率与存活分布（survival distribution），并结合应力传播（stress propagation）协同性分析与人为放大应变幅值的数值实验进行验证。

Result: 扩散变量为局域元应变，每周期频闪式跳跃两次；当其扩散至屈服阈值以下，元素转为弹性，导致阈值下方应变的逐渐累积。γ_0≲γ_c 时，扩散行为之前存在大量由应力传播协同性主导的周期；γ_0>γ_c 时应力传播主导并促发失效。疲劳过程中 dσ/dγ 在 |γ|=γ_0^- 趋于 1（循环内完美弹性），在 |γ|=γ_0^+ 趋于 0（预示失效）。

Conclusion: 同一种"阈值下方应变累积"机制同时解释了 γ_0≲γ_c 的吸收弹性态与 γ_0≳γ_c 的疲劳失效，将宏观阈值行为归结为局域应变的扩散动力学与应力传播协同性的竞争。

Abstract: We study cyclic shear in a lattice elastoplastic model of amorphous materials. The model displays a threshold shear amplitude $γ_{\rm c}$, which increases with sample annealing. For imposed amplitudes $γ_0\lesssimγ_{\rm c}$, it shows slow progression to an absorbing elastic state. For $γ_0\gtrsim γ_{\rm c}$, it shows slow fatigue and delayed material failure. Our key contribution is to elucidate a mechanistic understanding of this behaviour. We show that progression to an absorbing state maps to single element diffusion on a finite interval with two absorbing boundaries. We derive expressions for the decay rate of the fraction of active elements, and the survival distribution. The diffusing variable is the local elemental strain, which takes two jumps stroboscopically per cycle. Once the value at any site diffuses below its yielding threshold, the element behaves elastically. This leads to a gradual accumulation of strains just below threshold. For $γ_0\lesssim γ_{\rm c}$ this diffusive behaviour is preceded by many cycles in which cooperativity between elements via stress propagation is also important. For $γ_0 > γ_{\rm c}$, stress propagation dominates and precipitates failure. For $γ_0\gtrsim γ_{\rm c}$, the same accumulation of elements just below local threshold that led to an elastic state for $γ_0\lesssimγ_{\rm c}$ accounts for the fatigue that brings the system to the brink of failure. We evidence this via simulations in which the strain amplitude artificially exceeds $γ_0 $ in each half cycle, before reverting to the system's actual state at $\pmγ_0$ and resuming the next half cycle. During fatigue, the stress-strain slope $dσ/dγ$ evolves towards unity at $|γ|=γ_0^-$, signifying perfect elasticity within cycle, yet towards zero at $|γ|=γ_0^+$, precipitating failure.

</details>


### [113] [Construction of highly coarse-grained molecular models for aqueous electrolytes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29653)
*Michael A. Seaton,Benjamin T. Speake,Tristan Rimmer,Ilian T. Todorov*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该文提出将耗散粒子动力学（DPD）与新型高粗粒化可极化水模型结合，用于构建钒氧化还原液流电池（VRFB）水相电解质的介观尺度模型，并参数化离子扩散性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超越原子尺度的电化学体系分子建模需同时兼顾正确的热力学、流体力学与介电性质及大规模计算可行性；对含高价离子氧化还原反应的水相电解质（如钒电池），既需要能响应局域电场的背景介质，又需可控的离子传输性质，二者一致嵌入模型具有挑战性。

Method: 采用耗散粒子动力学（Dissipative Particle Dynamics, DPD）方法，结合新设计的、基于成对相互作用（pairwise interactions）的高粗粒化可极化水模型，以再现真实热力学行为；并探讨如何将水模型改造以纳入VRFB正极液（catholyte）与负极液（anolyte）中的离子组分，以及如何参数化DPD成对恒温器力（thermostat forces）以获得真实扩散系数。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果；所述工作为模型构建与参数化方案，包括核心水模型的离子物种扩展方式与通过DPD恒温器力调节扩散率（diffusivities）的途径。

Conclusion: 将DPD与高粗粒化可极化水模型相结合，有望作为VRFB水相电解质的介观建模基础，在保持热力学真实性的同时实现对离子输运性质的调控。

Abstract: Molecular modelling of electrochemical systems at larger than atomic scales poses particular challenges, not least the ability to represent correct thermodynamic, hydrodynamic and dielectric properties while still being computationally viable for large scale applications. In the case of aqueous electrolytes such as those used in vanadium-based batteries with oxidation-reduction (redox) reactions, both a background medium that can respond to localised electric fields due to high-valence ions and control over ion transport properties are crucial to embed consistently in the model. To this end, we propose the use of Dissipative Particle Dynamics (DPD) combined with a newly-devised highly coarse-grained polarisable water model with pairwise interactions designed to produce realistic thermodynamic behaviour as the basis for the aqueous electrolytes found in vanadium redox flow batteries (VRFB). We consider how the core water model can be modified to incorporate the ionic species found in VRFB catholytes and anolytes, as well as how the DPD pairwise thermostat forces can be parameterised to attain realistic diffusivities for those species.

</details>


### [114] [Hydrodynamics of microalgae in sea spray](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29719)
*Elettra Figà Talamanca,Juliette Pierre,Eva Ternon,Paula Cohen,Maarten De Rijcke,Hélène de Maleprade*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究证明微藻（5–50 μm）可被稳定捕获于海盐喷射液滴（jet drops，100–800 μm）中，并基于射流流体动力学（hydrodynamics of the jet）提出新的颗粒捕获与富集模型，进而揭示有毒底栖甲藻 Ostreopsis cf. ovata 可经海喷（sea spray）气溶胶化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有害藻华（harmful algal blooms）期间海岸人群呼吸道不适频发，溶解态藻毒素可气溶胶化已获确认，但颗粒结合态毒素（particle-associated toxins）的贡献尚不清楚；大气中微藻的观测提示细胞或细胞碎片可能作为载体，而现有颗粒捕获模型仅考虑表面效应（surface effects），无法解释大尺寸微藻细胞如何进入射流液滴。

Method: 以多种微藻物种为对象，验证其在气泡破裂（bursting of air bubbles）产生的射流液滴中的捕获现象；引入富集因子（enrichment factor，即首个射流液滴相对本体浓度的增量）刻画捕获规律，并依据射流流体动力学而非表面效应推导颗粒捕获模型与富集模型。

Result: 微藻被一致性地捕获于射流液滴中，富集行为呈现两个区制（two regimes），其转变强烈依赖细胞尺寸；新模型可推广既有关于细菌与微塑料（microplastics）捕获的结果，并解释文献中的不一致之处；同时证实海喷可将有毒 Ostreopsis cf. ovata 细胞气溶胶化。

Conclusion: 海喷构成有害藻华期间空中毒性的潜在颗粒途径（particulate pathway），并提出需进一步厘清颗粒态与溶解态毒素相对贡献的问题。

Abstract: Airborne transmission of biological matter is likely to cause numerous respiratory distress in coastal populations during certain harmful algal blooms. While we know that dissolved algal toxins aerosolize, the contribution of particle-associated toxins is still unknown. The observation of microalgae in the atmosphere suggests that cells or cell debris could be a carrier. Exchange of particulate matter between ocean and atmosphere is driven by sea spray: microplastics and bacteria aerosolize in jet droplets, following the bursting of air bubbles. However, current models for particle capture only consider surface effects, which fail to explain how large microalgal cells (5-50 um) can be entrapped in jet drops (100-800 um). Here, we demonstrate with a variety of species that microalgae are consistently captured in jet drops, and derive a new particle capture model based on the hydrodynamics of the jet. Using the enrichment factor, which measures the increase in concentration in the first jet drop relative to the bulk, we uncover two regimes. The transition between regimes strongly depends on cell size. In addition, we highlight the dynamics of particles within the jet and derive an enrichment model based on hydrodynamics rather than surface considerations. This work widens and generalizes established results on bacterial and microplastic entrapment in jet drops, resolving discrepancies found in the literature. Finally, we demonstrate that sea spray can aerosolize toxic Ostreopsis cf. ovata cells. This reveals a potential particulate pathway for airborne toxicity during harmful algal blooms and raises the question of the relative contribution of particles to that of dissolved toxins.

</details>


### [115] [Closing of density wake in concentrated colloidal suspensions: two regimes governed by compressibility, friction, and self-propulsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29801)
*Guillaume Duprez,Gianmarco Spera,Benjamin Buhl,François Graner,Vincent Démery,Cécile Cottin-Bizonne,Hélène Delanoë-Ayari,Mathieu Leocmach*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究结合实验与数值模拟，考察致密二维热浴及自驱动浴中圆形障碍物后方密度尾迹（density wake）的闭合行为，揭示尾迹特征长度随密度非单调变化，并指出活性浴可等价为"有效更大的障碍物"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有关于布朗悬浮液中密度尾迹形状的理论工作，或假设无限稀释（infinite dilution），或仅给出远场描述，缺乏对致密体系中紧邻障碍物后方的尾迹闭合过程的定量理解。

Method: 采用实验与数值模拟相结合的方法，研究热（thermal）与自驱动（self-propelled）粒子组成的致密二维悬浮液中圆形障碍物后的密度扰动；并用解析论证解释标度关系。

Result: 热性情形下，尾迹特征长度（characteristic length）是密度的非单调函数，先减小后增大；两个区间的标度可借助悬浮液的压缩性因子（compressibility factor）加以解释。活性情形下，自驱动的影响可理解为障碍物在活性浴（active bath）中"有效变大"，且自驱动速度与持续时长（persistence time）发挥不同且非平凡的作用。

Conclusion: 研究为致密悬浮液中密度尾迹提供了新的定量图像，将被动情形的理解推广至活性情形，并以有效障碍尺寸与持续性参数刻画自驱动效应。

Abstract: When an obstacle moves through a compressible medium, it creates a density perturbation. Theoretical arguments regarding the shape of this density wake in Brownian suspensions either suppose infinite dilution or describe only the far field. Here, we study via experiments and numerical simulations the closing of the density perturbation just behind a circular obstacle in dense 2D suspensions of particles that are thermal or self-propelled. In the thermal case, we show that the characteristic length of the wake is a non-monotonic function of density, decreasing then increasing. We rationalise the scalings of both regimes by analytical arguments that highlight the role of the compressibility factor of the suspension. Based on this new understanding of the passive case, we show that the influence of self-propulsion observed in experiments and simulations can be understood as if the obstacle is effectively larger in an active bath, with non-trivial and distinct roles of the self-propulsion velocity and of the persistence time.

</details>


### [116] [Quenched disorder in polymerized membranes: from anomalous elasticity to a glassy phase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29862)
*D. Mouhanna*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该综述以场论方法（微扰与非微扰重整化群）梳理聚合膜（polymerized membranes）从纯净到淬火无序体系的固定点结构，提出热主导平整相、无序主导玻璃相及二者之间的连续起皱转变，并与脂质膜实验的三段标度区对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 聚合膜中几何、弹性与统计涨落耦合产生反常长程行为（anomalous long-distance behavior），而无序（quenched random metric and curvature）会引入与热涨落相互竞争的自由度，需要系统厘清其固定点与相变图像并与实验对照。

Method: 采用微扰场论（单圈及高阶计算）与非微扰重整化群（nonperturbative renormalization-group）分析，研究对象由纯净膜扩展至含淬火随机度规与曲率的无序膜。

Result: 早期单圈研究给出无无序固定点 P4 与零温无序主导固定点 P5；非微扰重整化群揭示有限温度与无序下的第三个固定点 Pc，支配平整相与玻璃相（glassy phase）间的连续起皱转变（wrinkling transition）；高阶微扰计算亦重现类 Pc 固定点，其反常维度与非微扰预言重展开结果高度接近。

Conclusion: 该图像可自然联系部分聚合脂质膜中的起皱转变，高度涨落的三个实验标度区可分别对应 P4、Pc、P5，且在临界点定量吻合较好；文中还讨论玻璃态情景的现状、微扰与非微扰固定点稳定性预言间的未解分歧，以及向缺陷石墨烯（defective graphene）等二维晶体材料的推广。

Abstract: Polymerized membranes are paradigmatic fluctuating systems in which geometry, elasticity, and statistical fluctuations combine to produce anomalous long-distance behavior. In the disorder-free flat phase, bending rigidity increases at long wavelengths while in-plane elastic moduli soften. Quenched random metric and curvature introduce competing thermal and disorder-induced fluctuations. We review perturbative and nonperturbative field-theoretical approaches to these systems, from pure membranes to their disordered counterparts. Early one-loop studies identified a disorder-free fixed point, $P_4$, and a zero-temperature disorder-dominated fixed point, $P_5$. A subsequent nonperturbative renormalization-group analysis revealed a third fixed point, $P_c$, at finite temperature and disorder, governing a continuous wrinkling transition between the thermally dominated flat phase and a disorder-dominated glassy phase. Higher-order perturbative calculations also recover a $P_c$-like fixed point, with anomalous dimensions remarkably close to the reexpanded nonperturbative predictions. This scenario connects naturally with the wrinkling transition observed in partially polymerized lipid membranes. The three experimental scaling regimes of height fluctuations can be associated with $P_4$, $P_c$, and $P_5$, with theoretical exponents showing good quantitative agreement with measurements, particularly at criticality. Finally, we discuss the status of the glassy scenario, the unresolved discrepancy between perturbative and nonperturbative fixed-point stability predictions, and possible extensions to defective graphene and related two-dimensional crystalline materials.

</details>


### [117] [Clutter Noise Induced Phase Segregation in Binary Active Polar Flocks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30008)
*Pradeep Kumar Yadav,Ignacio Pagonabarraga*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 在二元活性混合物（binary active mixtures）中，仅由依赖局域组分（composition-dependent）的杂乱噪声（clutter noise）即可驱动宏观相分离（phase segregation），无需排斥或反取向力矩。基于智能体 Vicsek 模型（agent-based Vicsek model）与流体力学理论（hydrodynamic theory）均支持该结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上宏观相分离常归因于排斥相互作用或反取向力矩（anti-alignment torques），本文旨在检验局域随机的杂乱噪声本身是否足以破坏混合态并诱发相分离。

Method: 采用智能体 Vicsek 模型（agent-based Vicsek model）模拟二元活性混合物，并构建相应的流体力学理论（hydrodynamic theory）加以对照分析。

Result: 仅杂乱噪声即可使混合态失稳，产生多条纹（multi-stripe）、共动带（co-moving band）、亚稳反向传播（metastable counter-propagating）等多种形貌，以及稳定的相分离极性流体（phase-segregated polar fluid）。理论上，杂乱噪声抑制局域极性序（local polar order）与输运效率（transport efficiency），与模拟中的去混合（demixing）一致。

Conclusion: 杂乱噪声（clutter noise）是二元活性混合物中相分离的独立驱动机制；理论与数值结果一致表明其通过抑制局域极性序和输运效率引发去混合。

Abstract: Macroscopic phase segregation in binary active mixtures can emerge solely from clutter noise: local, composition-dependent stochasticity, without repulsion or anti-alignment torques. Using an agent-based Vicsek model, we show that clutter noise alone destabilizes the mixed state, producing multi-stripe, co-moving band, and metastable counter-propagating morphologies, as well as a stable phase-segregated polar fluid. Our hydrodynamic theory shows that clutter noise suppresses local polar order and transport efficiency, consistent with the demixing observed in the agent-based model.

</details>


### [118] [Spatial Control of Compressible Active Fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30092)
*Yu-Chuan Cheng,Dimitris Krommydas,Seungwoo Shin,Paarth Gulati,Michael F. Hagan,Aparna Baskaran,Cristina M. Marchetti,Zvonimir Dogic*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究结合实验与理论，考察可压缩微管基活性流体（compressible microtubule-based active fluids）对正弦活动模式的响应，发现微管在低活性区聚集、高活性区耗竭，密度差由活动模式波长决定，并可由包含正比于向列序张量的主动应力与各向同性主动压力的连续介质模型解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在理解可压缩活性流体（active fluids）在外加活动模式控制下的响应规律，并厘清控制协议（control protocols）驯服其混沌动力学（chaotic dynamics）所面临的基本约束。

Method: 采用实验与理论相结合的方法：实验上施加正弦变化的活动模式（sinusoidally varying activity patterns）并观测微管密度分布；理论上构建连续介质模型（continuum model），同时纳入正比于向列序张量（nematic order tensor）的主动应力（active stresses）与各向同性主动压力（isotropic active pressure）。

Result: 微管在低活性区累积、在高活性区耗竭，密度差受活动模式波长的调控；短波长下跨越相邻高、低活性区的活性涡旋（active vortices）抑制密度调制，较长波长下涡流局限于单一活性区，从而允许密度反差出现。

Conclusion: 一个最小化的可压缩活性流体理论模型即可准确再现实验结果，同时揭示了对该类混沌动力学实施控制时控制协议所受的基本约束。

Abstract: We study the response of compressible microtubule-based active fluids to sinusoidally varying activity patterns, using experiments and theory. Microtubules accumulate in low-activity regions and are depleted from high activity ones, with the density difference controlled by the wavelength of the activity pattern. To explain these observations the continuum model needs to account for both active stresses that are proportional to nematic order tensor and the isotropic active pressure. At short wavelengths, active vortices spanning adjacent high and low activity regions suppress density modulation. At larger wavelengths, vortical flows confined to the low or high activity region allow for the emergence of the density contrast. Our work shows that a minimal theoretical model of active compressible fluids accurately reproduces experiments, while elucidating the fundamental constraints on control protocols that seek to tame their chaotic dynamics.

</details>

# cond-mat.other [[Back]](#toc)

### [119] [Two quantum skyrmions form a magnon Josephson junction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28854)
*R. L. Silva*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 在受阻挫的自旋-1/2磁体中，两个量子斯格明子（quantum skyrmion）可束缚成“分子”，该分子等效为一个磁振子约瑟夫森结（magnon Josephson junction），其相对螺旋度（helicity）θ与转移磁振子数n满足哈密顿量H=E_C n^2−W cosθ。DMRG计算给出E_C与W≃0.02J_1，表明相邻两个螺旋度量子比特（helicity qubit）通过自身相互作用实现强耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索量子斯格明子之间能否形成束缚态，并借助该束缚态实现磁振子（magnon）层面的约瑟夫森型相位相干器件，从而为基于螺旋度的量子比特耦合提供新机制。

Method: 采用密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）方法研究J_1–J_2三角晶格（triangular lattice）上的受挫自旋-1/2磁体，将斯格明子对映射为满足H=E_C n^2−W cosθ的类约瑟夫森结模型；E_C由天然尺寸（七个翻转自旋）处斯格明子能量的曲率确定，W由测量的螺旋度相干性确定。

Result: DMRG给出E_C为斯格明子在其天然尺寸（七个翻转自旋）处能量的曲率；螺旋度相干性测量给出W≃0.02J_1，该值等于斯格明子—反斯格明子（skyrmion–antiskyrmion）隧穿劈裂的一半，且与各向异性（anisotropy）无关。

Conclusion: 两个量子斯格明子束缚成的分子可作为磁振子约瑟夫森结，其耦合强度W由斯格明子—反斯格明子隧穿劈裂决定而非各向异性；由此，并排的两个螺旋度量子比特可凭借斯格明子间自身相互作用实现强耦合。

Abstract: Two quantum skyrmions in a frustrated spin-$1/2$ magnet bind into a molecule, and the molecule acts as a magnon Josephson junction: the relative helicity $θ$ and the number $n$ of magnons transferred between them obey $H=E_Cn^2-W\cosθ$. DMRG on the $J_1$--$J_2$ triangular lattice gives $E_C$ as the curvature of the skyrmion energy at its natural size of seven flipped spins, and the measured helicity coherence fixes $W\simeq0.02J_1$, half the skyrmion--antiskyrmion tunnel splitting and independent of the anisotropy. Two helicity qubits side by side are thus strongly coupled by their own interaction.

</details>


### [120] [Chiral near-fields at achiral plasmonic tips enable nanoscale valley polarimetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29972)
*Jonggeun Hwang,Kijeong Park,Hyeongwoo Lee,Yeonjeong Koo,SeokJae Yoo,Long Ju,Kyoung-Duck Park*

Main category: cond-mat.other

TL;DR: 该研究证明使用非手性金针尖（achiral Au tip）可通过入射角调控圆偏振光，在纳米尺度产生强局域且保持螺旋度（helicity-preserving）的近场，从而实现纳米尺度谷偏振测量（valley polarimetry）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光学螺旋度（optical helicity）在手性光-物质相互作用中居核心地位，但等离激元局域（plasmonic confinement）通常在纳米尺度使其发生畸变，限制了纳米分辨的手性/谷分辨探测。

Method: 采用非手性金针尖，通过入射角控制圆偏振光，构建强局域、保持螺旋度的近场；并结合手性针尖增强光致发光（chiral tip-enhanced photoluminescence, c-TEPL）进行成像测量。

Result: c-TEPL 在单层 WSe2 中直接分辨并实现局域谷偏振的反转；在受应变 MoS2 中揭示出被衍射极限测量所掩盖的纳米尺度偏振变化。

Conclusion: 非手性针尖结合入射角调控可在纳米尺度保持光学螺旋度，为二维材料中谷偏振与手性光学响应的纳米分辨表征提供可行途径。

Abstract: Optical helicity is central to chiral light-matter interactions, yet plasmonic confinement typically distorts it at the nanoscale. Here, we demonstrate nanoscale valley polarimetry using an achiral Au tip, where incidence-angle control of circularly polarized light creates a strongly localized, helicity-preserving near-field. Chiral tip-enhanced photoluminescence (c-TEPL) directly resolves and reverses local valley polarization in monolayer WSe2 and reveals nanoscale polarization variations in strained MoS2 hidden by diffraction-limited measurements.

</details>

### [121] [Percolation on Bidirected Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28762)
*Yucheng Liu,Leonardo T. Rolla,Yuliang Shi*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 在双向渗流（bidirected percolation）模型中证明了传递可均图（transitive amenable graph）上强连通阈值与普通伯努利渗流阈值相等，并构造了非可均图上的分离情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究将无向边替换为两条独立开边的有向结构后，强连通（strongly connected）无限簇阈值的普适性，厘清其与经典伯努利渗流阈值（Bernoulli percolation threshold）的关系取决于图的可均性（amenability）。

Method: 对每条无向边放置两条方向相反、各自以概率 p 独立开放的边；在传递可均图上作一般性证明，并对传递非可均图（transitive non-amenable graph）构造充分展布（spread-out）的修改，另结合高维 Z^d 的分析。

Result: 所有传递可均图满足 p_sc = p_c；相反，每个传递非可均图的充分展布修改均满足 p_sc > p_c。此外，在 d ≥ 11 的 Z^d 上，p = p_sc 时强连通簇的期望规模有限。

Conclusion: 可均性是决定两阈值是否重合的关键因素：可均图上二者相同，非可均图上可被展布修改分离；高维整数格点上的期望簇规模有限性补充了临界点的定量刻画。

Abstract: We study bidirected percolation on an undirected graph $G$: each edge is replaced by two oppositely directed edges, each of which is open independently with probability $p$. Two vertices are strongly connected if they are mutually reachable by open directed paths. Let $p_\mathrm{sc}$ denote the threshold for the existence of an infinite strongly connected cluster, and let $p_c$ denote the usual Bernoulli percolation threshold on $G$.
  On every transitive amenable graph, we prove that $p_\mathrm{sc}=p_c$. In contrast, sufficiently spread-out modifications of every transitive non-amenable graph satisfy $p_\mathrm{sc}>p_c$. We also show that, on $\mathbb Z^d$ with $d\ge11$, the expected size of the strongly connected cluster is finite at $p=p_\mathrm{sc}$.

</details>


### [122] [Painlevé representations for joint moments of characteristic polynomials from the orthogonal and unitary symplectic groups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29947)
*Theodoros Assiotis,Thomas Bothner,Jonathan P. Keating,Nicholas Simm,Fei Wei*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 研究 Haar 分布的辛群与正交群随机矩阵特征多项式及其二阶导数的联合矩，在全部可允许参数范围内给出大 N 渐近，并用 Bessel 点过程的首个逆幂和与 σ-Painlevé III' 方程的特解刻画极限系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有结果（Assiotis、Gunes、Keating、Wei）仅覆盖部分矩参数范围，本文旨在将该类联合矩的大 N 渐近推广到完整可允许参数范围。

Method: 将问题化归为 Jacobi 酉系综（Jacobi unitary ensemble）的奇异逆幂统计量，结合其有限 N 的 σ-Painlevé V 表示与精细的 Riemann-Hilbert 分析，得到精确的小 x 渐近与有限 N 修正项的一致导数估计；并以基于核的证明说明 Jacobi 系综重标度后的首个逆幂和依分布收敛到 Bessel 点过程的对应量。

Result: 在完整可允许参数范围内建立联合矩的大 N 渐近，极限系数可由 Bessel 点过程（Bessel point process）的首个逆幂和概率地刻画，并可由 σ-Painlevé III' 方程的一个特定解表示。

Conclusion: 结果把前述工作推广至全部可允许参数范围，并揭示了随机矩阵特征多项式矩、Bessel 点过程逆幂统计与 σ-Painlevé 方程之间的对应关系。

Abstract: We study joint moments of characteristic polynomials and their second derivatives for Haar-distributed matrices from the unitary symplectic and orthogonal groups. We establish their large-$N$ asymptotics throughout the full admissible range of the moment parameters and identify the limiting coefficients probabilistically in terms of the first inverse power sum of the Bessel point process. This extends the corresponding results of Assiotis, Gunes, Keating, and Wei [Proc. Lond. Math. Soc., 132 (2026), e70136] to the full admissible parameter range. Throughout this full range, we also express these limits in terms of a distinguished solution of the $σ$-Painlevé III' equation. Our approach reduces the problem to singular inverse-power statistics of the Jacobi unitary ensemble and combines their finite-$N$ $σ$-Painlevé V representation with a refined Riemann-Hilbert analysis yielding precise small-$x$ asymptotics and uniform derivative estimates for the finite-$N$ correction term. A key ingredient in the probabilistic identification is a kernel-based proof that the rescaled first inverse power sum of the Jacobi ensemble converges in distribution to its Bessel point process counterpart.

</details>
