# cs.DL [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [An Open-Source Data Infrastructure for Alloy Synthesis Research: Stage 1 --- Composition Screening and Literature Cross-Referencing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28647)
*Iliya Angelov Radulov*

Main category: cs.DL

TL;DR: 该文报告了一个面向单一合金合成实验室的开源数据基础设施第一阶段（Stage 1）：关系型数据库模式、成分/质量计算器、三项经验合金设计筛选指标与合成可行性检查、对三个公共材料数据库的自动交叉比对层，以及统一工作流的桌面图形界面（GUI）。作者以系统/方法学贡献定位，重点总结构建过程中遇到的四类工程缺陷及其诊断与修复经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验材料科学实验室（materials-science lab）的合成与表征数据分散于电子表格、仪器导出文件和临时文件夹，样品的配方、实测性质与已有类似成分报道之间缺乏持久关联，数据难以追溯与复用。

Method: 构建关系型模式（relational schema）记录成分与合成数据；实现严格的成分/质量计算器、VEC（valence electron concentration）、原子尺寸错配（atomic-size mismatch）与混合焓（mixing enthalpy）三项筛选指标及熔/沸点合成可行性检查；搭建对 Materials Project、OQMD、Alexandria 三个公共数据库的自动交叉引用层（含去重与可调相关性过滤）；并以桌面 GUI（graphical user interface）将流程整合为单一“新建条目”操作。

Result: 系统由一名实践研究者（practicing researcher）而非软件团队构建并投入使用；报告四类可推广的工程缺陷：导致静默错误答案的解析器歧义（parser ambiguity）、隐蔽破坏依赖链的部分代码修改、可追溯至未类型转换数值的数据库元数据错误，以及 .gitignore 无法完全覆盖的凭证卫生（credential-hygiene）缺口。

Conclusion: 上述缺陷可推广至任何由缺乏正式软件工程训练的领域专家构建的小型科学软件项目；系统本身与构建经验共同构成该阶段成果，后续两篇论文分别涵盖表征流水线（Stage 2）与基于检索增强的合成顾问及 CALPHAD 热力学模块（Stage 3）。

Abstract: Experimental materials-science labs routinely accumulate synthesis and characterization data across spreadsheets, instrument export files, and ad-hoc folders, with no durable link between a sample's recipe, its measured properties, and whether a similar composition has been reported elsewhere. We present Stage 1 of an open-source data infrastructure closing this loop for a single working alloy-synthesis lab: a relational schema for composition and synthesis records, a strict composition/mass calculator, three empirical alloy-design screening metrics (VEC, atomic-size mismatch, mixing enthalpy) plus a melt/boil-point synthesis-feasibility check, an automated cross-referencing layer against three public materials databases (Materials Project, OQMD, Alexandria) with deduplication and adjustable relevance filtering, and a desktop GUI unifying the workflow behind a single "new entry" action. Built and used by a practicing researcher rather than a software team, we report it as a systems/methodology contribution - the architecture, the concrete engineering failures encountered while building it, and how each was diagnosed and fixed - rather than new materials-science results. We argue the specific defects found - a parser ambiguity that produced silent wrong answers, a partial code edit that broke a dependency chain invisibly, a database-metadata bug traceable to an un-cast numeric type, and a credential-hygiene gap that .gitignore alone did not close - generalize to any small scientific-software project built by a domain expert without formal software-engineering training. Both the system and the lessons from building it are reported here; two follow-on papers cover the validated characterization pipelines (Stage 2) and the retrieval-augmented synthesis advisor and CALPHAD thermodynamics module (Stage 3) built on this foundation.

</details>


<div id='hep-ph'></div>

# hep-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Unfolding the low-energy reactor neutrino flux from CE$ν$NS data with a finite Dirac sum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.16107)
*Muping Chen,Graciela Gelmini,Danny Marfatia,Koichiro Yasuda*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出一种基于凸几何（convex geometry）的无模型方法，从相干弹性中微子—原子核散射（CEνNS）数据中提取低于逆β衰变阈值1.8 MeV的反应堆反中微子能谱，并与Tikhonov正则化解谱进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1.8 MeV以下的反应堆反中微子能谱因低于逆β衰变（inverse beta decay）阈值而无法测量，CEνNS数据提供了探测该能区的可能途径。现有解谱方法通常依赖参数化形式或平滑先验，可能引入模型偏差。

Method: 借鉴暗物质直接探测中的halo-independent分析技术，基于凸几何论证，将微分中微子反应率写成Dirac delta函数的有限线性组合（finite Dirac sum, FDS），项数上限由数据点数量决定，从而得到积分中微子通量的最佳拟合与逐点置信带（pointwise confidence band），无需假设谱的参数形式或平滑先验。

Result: 将“FDS方法”应用于低阈值锗（Ge）探测器的模拟CEνNS数据，并与Tikhonov正则化解谱（Tikhonov-regularized unfolding）进行比较，评估其表现。

Conclusion: 该方法为在未测量能区无模型地重构反应堆反中微子能谱提供了可行途径，其相对Tikhonov正则化解谱的优势需依据模拟数据对比结果加以判断。

Abstract: We present a novel method to analyze coherent elastic neutrino--nucleus scattering (CE$ν$NS) data to extract the reactor antineutrino spectrum below the inverse beta decay threshold of 1.8 MeV, where it remains unmeasured. Adapting halo-independent analysis techniques developed for direct dark matter detection, we show how to obtain a best-fit and a pointwise confidence band for the integrated neutrino flux, without assuming a parametric form or smoothness prior for the spectrum. In our approach, which follows from convex geometry arguments, the differential neutrino rate is written as a linear combination of Dirac delta functions -- a finite Dirac sum (FDS) -- with a maximum number of terms determined by the number of data points. We apply our "FDS method" to mock CE$ν$NS data for a low-threshold Ge detector and compare it with Tikhonov-regularized unfolding.

</details>


### [125] [Measurement of the neutrino cross section across the TeV scale using IceCube data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.24068)
*Rasmi Hajjar,Mauricio Bustamante,Sergio Palomares-Ruiz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该研究利用 IceCube 中微子天文台（IceCube Neutrino Observatory）八年穿透式缪中微子数据，在 150 GeV–40 TeV 能区给出缪中微子–核子带电流截面（charged-current cross section）的测定，全区间不确定度低于 20%，为迄今 TeV 能标最精确的中微子–核子相互作用刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子仅通过弱力（weak force）与物质作用，截面极小且难以测量；TeV 能区的中微子–核子相互作用探测的是核子结构（nucleon structure）知识较不完整的区域，并可能显现超出标准模型（beyond the Standard Model）的物理迹象。此前对撞机、大气和天体物理中微子测量尚无单一结果能覆盖亚 TeV 至数十 TeV，且不确定度仍约 20–40%。

Method: 使用 IceCube 中微子天文台八年穿透式缪中微子（through-going muon neutrino）数据，测定 150 GeV–40 TeV 能区缪中微子–核子带电流截面随能量的变化；同时测量中微子与反中微子的平均截面以便与既有结果直接比较，并尝试将两者分别测量。

Result: 测量结果与标准模型（Standard Model）预言一致，整个能区不确定度低于 20%；在 1 TeV 以上首次出现中微子与反中微子截面可分别测量的轻微迹象（mild indications），实现了迄今跨 TeV 能标最精确的中微子–核子相互作用刻画。

Conclusion: 该工作在该能区提供了统一的中微子–核子截面测量，将加速器中微子观测与既往更高能中微子观测衔接起来，推动高能中微子物理（high-energy neutrino physics）进入精确测量时代（precision era）。

Abstract: Neutrinos interact with matter exclusively through the weak force, resulting in extremely small cross sections that are difficult to measure. At TeV energies, neutrino-nucleon interactions probe the structure of the nucleon in regimes where our knowledge of it is less complete, and signatures of physics beyond the Standard Model could manifest. Recent measurements using collider, atmospheric, and astrophysical neutrinos have begun to explore this regime, but no single measurement has provided precise coverage from sub-TeV to tens of TeV, and uncertainties remain at the level of about 20-40%. Here we present a determination of the muon-neutrino-nucleon charged-current cross sections as a function of energy across 150 GeV-40 TeV using eight years of through-going muon neutrino data collected by the IceCube Neutrino Observatory. We measure the average neutrino-antineutrino cross section, enabling direct comparison with previous results, and find, for the first time above 1 TeV, mild indications that the neutrino and antineutrino cross sections can be measured separately. The measured cross sections are consistent with Standard Model predictions and are determined with uncertainties below 20% throughout the explored energy range, achieving the most precise characterization of neutrino-nucleon interactions across the TeV scale to date. These results provide a unified measurement of neutrino-nucleon cross sections in this energy regime, connecting accelerator-based and previous higher-energy neutrino observations - thereby advancing high-energy neutrino physics into the precision era.

</details>


### [126] [Testing the statistical compatibility of data sets with a common prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28681)
*Pilar Coloma,Michele Maltoni,Thomas Schwetz*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文将参数拟合优度检验（Parameter Goodness-of-fit，PG）扩展到存在先验数据约束的情形，证明其在高斯近似下服从广义χ²分布，并可高效计算p值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始PG检验假定各数据集统计独立，但实际分析中模型部分参数常受辅助先验数据集约束，导致数据集不再独立，原有检验不再适用。

Method: 提出先验约束下的PG检验统计量：在所有数据集共享同一先验约束的前提下，于高斯近似（Gaussian approximation）框架中推导该统计量的分布性质。

Result: 证明先验约束的PG统计量服从广义χ²分布（generalised χ² distribution），据此可直接获得p值；在强先验约束近似下，该检验收敛为无约束模型的标准PG检验。

Conclusion: 扩展后的PG检验为先验约束条件下评估多数据集兼容性提供了简便的统计工具，并与原PG检验在极限情形下保持一致。

Abstract: The Parameter Goodness-of-fit (PG) test [hep-ph/0304176] offers a simple test statistic to study the compatibility of different data sets within a given theoretical model. In its original formulation it assumes that the data sets are statistically independent. We propose an extension of the PG test for the situation when some parameters of the model are constrained by an auxiliary data set called prior here. As the prior is imposed for all data sets, they are no longer statistically independent. We show that under the Gaussian approximation, the prior-constrained PG test statistic follows a generalised $χ^2$ distribution, which can be used to obtain $p$-values in a straight-forward way. In the approximation of a strong prior constraint, the test converges towards the standard PG test for the unconstrained model parameters.

</details>


### [127] [Radiative corrections to double-$β$ decay and their impact on experimental analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28742)
*Jordy de Vries,Emanuele Mereghetti,Saad el Morabit,Stefan Sandner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文在重核有效场论框架下系统计算双β衰变（double-β decay）的O(α)辐射修正（radiative corrections），给出大激发能下因子化为树级衰变率与普适双弱Sirlin函数相乘的闭式结果，并指出实光子发射（real-photon emission）对实验谱分析影响显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有文献中处理辐射修正时，通常将其近似为两个单β衰变Sirlin函数之和，该近似在双β衰变情形下的适用性缺乏严格检验；此外，此前分析普遍忽略了实光子发射的贡献，可能影响核矩阵元比值（nuclear matrix elements ratios，如ξ31）的提取。

Method: 基于重核有效场论（heavy-nucleus effective field theory），计算双中微子与无中微子双β衰变（two-neutrino and neutrinoless double-β decay）的全部虚修正与实发射修正，涵盖任意激发能的中间核态，并给出含重核传播子的单圈积分（one-loop integrals）的新闭式表达式。

Result: 当激发能远大于轻子能量时，修正因子化为树级衰变率乘以一个普适的双弱Sirlin函数（universal double-weak Sirlin function），其与两个单β Sirlin函数之和差异显著；忽略辐射修正会使ξ31的提取产生显著偏差，而实光子发射起核心作用且其影响强烈依赖探测器配置；对0νββ衰变，辐射修正使包含性衰变率（inclusive rate）提高约5%，且与核矩阵元和中微子Majorana质量无关。

Conclusion: 研究提供了光子能谱（对光子能量与方向完全微分），建议实验显式模拟光子发射，并表明必须采用正确的双弱Sirlin函数而非单β函数之和来计入辐射修正，以保证谱分析与核矩阵元提取的可靠性。

Abstract: We present a comprehensive study of the $\mathcal{O}(α)$ radiative corrections to two-neutrino and neutrinoless double-$β$ decay within a heavy-nucleus effective field theory. We compute all virtual and real-emission corrections for intermediate nuclear states of arbitrary excitation energy, including new closed-form expressions for the required one-loop integrals with heavy-nucleus propagators. For excitation energies large compared with the lepton energies, the corrections factorize into the tree-level rate times a universal double-weak Sirlin function, which differs substantially from the sum of two single-$β$ Sirlin functions used so far in the literature. We discuss the impact of the radiative corrections on the analysis of measured spectra in terms of ratios of nuclear matrix elements, $ξ_{31}$, and show that neglecting radiative corrections can significantly bias the extraction. Real-photon emission, not considered in previous analyses, plays a central role and the impact of radiative corrections depends crucially on the detector setup. We provide the photon spectrum, fully differential in the photon energy and direction, and recommend that experiments simulate the photon explicitly. For $0νββ$ decay, the radiative corrections increase the inclusive rate by about $5\%$, independently of the nuclear matrix elements or the neutrino Majorana mass.

</details>


### [128] [Probing New Physics with $b \to u \ell ν_{\ell}$ induced decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29707)
*Maria Letizia Di Cuia,Davide Milillo*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 基于Belle II最新分bin分支比数据，对三个半轻子B衰变道做SMEFT框架下的联合分析，以约束b→uℓν跃迁的新物理耦合；结果显示标准模型整体仍与数据相符，但部分耦合存在轻微偏离零的偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Belle II对半轻子B衰变提供了新的分bin分支比数据，而b→uℓν跃迁对新物理敏感；不同衰变道对不同的非标准结构敏感，故需联合分析以系统约束新物理耦合。

Method: 将描述b→uℓν跃迁的低能哈密顿量（low-energy Hamiltonian）扩展至包含左手中微子的全部六维洛伦兹不变算符（dimension-six, Lorentz-invariant operators），符合SMEFT框架；Wilson系数（Wilson coefficients）视为复数、依赖轻子味的有效耦合，并用蒙特卡洛方法（Monte Carlo methods）对三个衰变道进行联合拟合，取三个|V_ub|基准值。

Result: 标准模型（Standard Model）总体仍与数据大范围兼容，但部分新物理耦合显示出对非零值的轻微偏好（mild preference），且结果依赖于所选取的|V_ub|基准值。

Conclusion: 在SMEFT框架下对多个半轻子衰变道的联合分析可有效约束b→uℓν新物理耦合，当前数据尚不足以确立新物理信号，但为后续更精确检验提供了约束基础。

Abstract: We analyze $B^- \to μ^- \barν_μ$, $\bar{B}^0 \to π^+ μ^-\barν_μ$, and $B^- \to ρ^0 μ^- \barν_μ$ decays using the data on binned branching fractions recently measured by Belle II Collaboration for the semileptonic modes. We extend the low-energy Hamiltonian governing $b \to u \ell \barν_\ell$ transitions to include the full set of dimension-six, Lorentz-invariant operators with left-handed neutrinos, consistent with the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) framework. The Wilson coefficients are treated as complex, lepton-flavor-dependent effective couplings. Since the three selected channels are sensitive to different non-standard structures, we perform a combined analysis using Monte Carlo methods to constrain the New Physics couplings for three benchmark values of $|V_{ub}|$. We find that, while the Standard Model remains broadly compatible with the data, some of the couplings show a mild preference for non-zero values.

</details>


### [129] [Gauge Field Fluctuations Protect the Higgs Vacuum When a Small Universe Begins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28704)
*Alan Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在反周期费米子的小周期盒中，常数规范模的零点涨落使λ<0时的希格斯零模变为共振态，其宽度随|λ|^{-1/2}指数衰减；由此给出宇宙从Casimir转折点起始时的真空存活判据，当前数据下存活概率约0.7。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型希格斯四次耦合（quartic coupling）λ在高能标变为负值，威胁电弱真空（electroweak vacuum）的稳定性，需澄清负λ情形下真空是否仍可存活。

Method: 考虑含反周期费米子（antiperiodic fermions）的小周期盒（periodic box），分析常数规范模（constant gauge modes）零点涨落的效应；以盒持续N个光穿越时间（light-crossing times）定义存活概率，并考察宇宙始于Casimir转折点（Casimir turning point）的情形。

Result: λ<0时希格斯零模（Higgs zero mode）成为共振（resonance），宽度按|λ|^{-1/2}指数衰减；存活概率超过1/2要求λ>λ_c(N)，且λ_c仅对数依赖N。Casimir起始情形下λ_c≈-0.012低于标准模型值-0.0098；当ξ_H≳0.08时，m_t<172.96 GeV对应存活概率>1/2，当前数据约为0.7。

Conclusion: 负λ下真空仍可在该有限盒框架中存活；FCC-ee若测得m_t≳173.7 GeV即可排除这一宇宙起始假设，从而将顶夸克质量测量与真空稳定性及早期宇宙起源联系起来。

Abstract: The Standard Model Higgs quartic coupling $λ$ turns negative at high energies. We show that in a small periodic box with antiperiodic fermions, zero-point fluctuations of the constant gauge modes turn the Higgs zero mode into a resonance for $λ<0$, whose width falls exponentially in $|λ|^{-1/2}$. A box lasting $N$ light-crossing times keeps its vacuum with probability above $1/2$ for $λ>λ_c(N)$, which depends on $N$ only logarithmically. For a universe that begins at a Casimir turning point, $λ_c\approx-0.012$ lies below the Standard Model value $-0.0098$; given a curvature coupling $ξ_H\gtrsim0.08$, survival has probability above $1/2$ for $m_t<172.96$ GeV and $\approx0.7$ for current data, and FCC-ee can exclude this beginning if $m_t\gtrsim173.7$ GeV.

</details>


### [130] [Quark TMDPDFs from Large to Small $x$: A Unified Description of CSS, DGLAP, and Small-$x$ Double Logarithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28778)
*Swagato Mukherjee,Vladimir V. Skokov,Andrey Tarasov,Shaswat Tiwari*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文将胶子横动量依赖部分子分布函数（TMDPDFs）的全运动学背景场构造推广至夸克情形，提出统一的MSTT方案，使大$x$与小$x$动力学可在同一框架下描述，并给出大$x$ DGLAP对数与小$x$双对数的统一复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作仅为胶子TMDPDF建立了全运动学背景场构造，夸克情形尚缺统一框架；而大$x$的DGLAP演化与小$x$双对数动力学通常分属不同描述体系，亟需一个共同框架加以统一。

Method: 采用背景场方法（background-field method），在保留纵向与横向动量、不施加共线或程函排序的条件下，计算次领头阶（next-to-leading order）领头幂次夸克TMD束流函数，并区分定义TMD算符的重整化尺度与分离微扰/非微扰模式的因子化尺度。

Result: 标准TMD重整化下夸克TMDPDF服从Collins-Soper-Sterman（CSS）演化，低尺度分离由背景场TMD算符组织；MSTT方案中算符展开的领头项在相应极限下同时复现DGLAP对数与小$x$双对数，并保留部分高扭度（higher-twist）与次程函（sub-eikonal）修正。另提出仅含快度劈裂的rapidity-only方案，其演化方程在共线极限退化为DGLAP、在小$x$给出双对数演化，并建立与MSTT及标准TMD方案的匹配。

Conclusion: 该工作为夸克TMDPDF提供了统一描述大$x$与小$x$动力学的全运动学框架（MSTT方案）及其rapidity-only简化方案，并建立了不同方案间的匹配关系，有助于TMD与共线因子化的系统衔接。

Abstract: We extend the full-kinematic background-field construction developed for gluon transverse-momentum dependent parton distribution functions (TMDPDFs) in Mukherjee et al. [Phys. Rev. D 109, 034035 (2024)] to quark TMDPDFs, providing a common framework for their large- and small-$x$ dynamics. We compute the leading-power quark TMD beam function at next-to-leading order using the background-field method. The background fields retain both longitudinal and transverse momenta, with neither collinear nor eikonal ordering imposed. We distinguish the renormalization scales that define the TMD operator from the factorization scales that separate its perturbative and non-perturbative modes. With standard TMD renormalization, the quark TMDPDF obeys Collins-Soper-Sterman (CSS) evolution, while the lower-scale separation is organized directly in terms of background-field TMD operators. We refer to this full-kinematic construction as the MSTT scheme. In the MSTT scheme, the leading term of the resulting operator expansion reproduces both the Dokshitzer-Gribov-Lipatov-Altarelli-Parisi (DGLAP) logarithm and the small-$x$ double logarithm in the appropriate limits, while retaining parts of the higher-twist and sub-eikonal corrections. We also formulate a rapidity-only scheme for quark TMDPDFs with distinct renormalization and factorization cutoffs. We derive its evolution equation, which reduces to DGLAP evolution in the collinear limit and to double-logarithmic quark evolution at small $x$, and establish its matching to the MSTT and standard TMD schemes.

</details>


### [131] [New CP-violating sources beyond the reach of EDMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28790)
*Venus Keus*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 摘要提出一类无法直接产生电偶极矩（electric dipole moments, EDMs）的新型CP破坏源：其构造为C破坏、P守恒，从而绕过EDM实验的严格限制，并给出具体模型实现及其对重子生成（baryogenesis）的意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型（Standard Model）所含CP破坏不足以解释观测到的宇宙重子不对称（baryon asymmetry）。超出标准模型（Beyond Standard Model）的新CP破坏源通常会馈入EDM，而EDM受实验严格约束，构成主要限制。

Method: 通过区分对称性结构识别新一类CP破坏源：EDM算符为C守恒、P破坏，而所引入的CP破坏按构造为C破坏、P守恒，故不能直接生成EDM；并给出可容纳此类源的具体模型实现。

Result: 确认存在一类不直接产生EDM的CP破坏源，并给出包含该机制的显式模型构造。

Conclusion: 此类C破坏、P守恒的CP破坏源可规避EDM实验限制，并与重子生成相关，为解释宇宙重子不对称提供可行方向。

Abstract: The Standard Model does not contain sufficient amount of CP violation to account for the observed baryon asymmetry of the universe. Beyond Standard Model scenarios that aim to explain this asymmetry introduce new sources of CP violation, but these generically feed into electric dipole moments (EDMs), which are severely constrained by experiment. We indentify a new class of CP-violating sources that cannot generate EDMs directly: EDM operators are C conserving and P violating, whereas CP violation introduced here is, by construction, C violating and P conserving. We give a concrete model realisation accommodating such sources and discuss their relevance for baryogenesis.

</details>


### [132] [$B_{sJ}(6114)$ as a Higher-Partial-Wave $\bar{K}^{*}B^{(*)}$ Molecular Candidate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29354)
*Shiquan Zhang,Dan Jiang,Yin Huang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在单玻色子交换模型（one-boson-exchange model）框架下，采用高斯展开法（Gaussian expansion method）求解薛定谔方程，研究B_sJ(6114)作为B(*)K*分子态的可能性；仅凭质量谱无法确定自旋宇称，但衰变宽度分析排除J^P=0^+、0^-、1^+、2^+、3^-，支持1^-、2^-、3^+，其中1^-与实验最吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LHCb合作组于2021年在B±K∓不变质量谱中观测到B_sJ(6114)，这是粒子数据组（Particle Data Group, PDG）收录的最高质量底—奇（bottom-strange）态，但其自旋宇称（spin-parity）量子数尚未由实验确定，内部结构仍存争议。已有夸克模型（quark model）解释（如1^3D_1态及1D(2^-,3^-)双重态成员）被提出，但该态作为强子分子态（hadronic molecular state）的可能性尚未被系统研究。

Method: 在单玻色子交换模型（one-boson-exchange model）下将B_sJ(6114)解释为B(*)K*分子态，利用高斯展开法（Gaussian expansion method）求解薛定谔方程（Schrödinger equation），在不同自旋宇称指派下计算束缚态性质，并计入分波混合（partial-wave mixing）与耦合道效应（coupled-channel effects）；进一步计算到允许的B(*)K*道的强衰变宽度（strong decay width）并与实验数据比较。

Result: 质量谱单独无法唯一确定B_sJ(6114)的自旋宇称；衰变宽度分析不利于J^P=0^+、0^-、1^+、2^+、3^-的指派，而J^P=1^-、2^-、3^+构型仍与实验约束相容。其中J^P=1^-指派与测量衰变宽度的符合程度最好。

Conclusion: 结果表明B_sJ(6114)可能是一个较高分波（higher-partial-wave）的B(*)K*分子态。

Abstract: In 2021, the LHCb Collaboration observed the $B_{sJ}(6114)$ state in the $B^\pm K^\mp$ invariant mass spectrum. It is currently the highest-mass bottom-strange state listed by the Particle Data Group (PDG). However, its spin-parity quantum numbers have not yet been experimentally determined, and its internal structure remains under debate. Although several quark-model interpretations have been proposed, including the $1^3D_1$ state and a member of the $1D(2^-,3^-)$ doublet, the possibility of a hadronic molecular structure for this state has not yet been systematically investigated. We investigate the possibility of interpreting \(B_{sJ}(6114)\) as a \(B^{(*)}\bar{K}^{*}\) molecular state within the one-boson-exchange model. By solving the Schrödinger equation using the Gaussian expansion method, we study the bound-state properties with different spin-parity assignments, taking into account partial-wave mixing and coupled-channel effects. We further calculate the strong decay widths into the allowed \(B^{(*)}\bar{K}\) channels and compare them with the experimental data. Our results show that the mass spectrum alone cannot uniquely determine the spin-parity of \(B_{sJ}(6114)\). However, the decay-width analysis disfavors the \(J^P=0^+\), \(0^-\), \(1^+\), \(2^+\), and \(3^-\) assignments, while the \(J^P=1^-\), \(2^-\), and \(3^+\) configurations remain compatible with the experimental constraints. Among these possibilities, the \(J^P=1^-\) assignment provides the best agreement with the measured decay width. These findings suggest that \(B_{sJ}(6114)\) may be a higher-partial-wave \(B^{(*)}\bar{K}^{*}\) molecular state.

</details>


### [133] [Fermion Hierarchies from Fricke Modular Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29754)
*Takafumi Kai,Justin Kaidi,Katsumi Kina,Hajime Otsuka*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该文展示Fricke模对称性（Fricke modular symmetry）可在超对称有效理论中由单一模量（modulus）生成现实的费米子层级与夸克CP破坏。通过在Γ₀⁺(N)的一维特征标下标记物质场并使用完整的尖点正则全纯模形式空间，得到夸克质量幂次(7,3,0)、(5,4,2)与CKM幂次(1,2,3)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是解决费米子质量层级与夸克CP破坏能否由单一模量统一生成的问题，并探索Fricke模对称性在此类建构中的作用。

Method: 方法上，物质场按Γ₀⁺(N)中在Γ₀(N)上平凡的两个一维特征标标记，相互作用使用完整的尖点正则全纯模形式空间；在level 3，Fricke扩张将内部稳定子（stabilizer）从三阶扩大到六阶，其宇称性（parity）强制位移t的额外三个幂次。

Result: 结果给出显式赋值下的夸克质量幂次(7,3,0)和(5,4,2)、CKM幂次(1,2,3)；领头结构保持CP守恒，但模生成元间的精确关系在有限位移处产生可观的CKM相位。在|t|≈0.306处拟合，以固定归一化下的order-one系数再现夸克与轻子参考值。

Conclusion: 结论指出Fricke双陪集代数（double-coset algebra）可与陪集非可逆对称性（coset non-invertible symmetry）相联系，且在此所用系数空间上其作用约化为普通Fricke宇称。

Abstract: We show how Fricke modular symmetry can generate realistic fermion hierarchies and quark CP violation from a single modulus in a supersymmetric effective theory. Matter fields are labelled by the two one-dimensional characters of $Γ_0^+(N)$ that are trivial on $Γ_0(N)$, and interactions use complete spaces of cusp-regular holomorphic modular forms. At level three, the Fricke extension enlarges an interior stabilizer from order three to order six, allowing its parity to enforce three additional powers of the displacement $t$. An explicit assignment gives quark mass powers $(7,3,0)$ and $(5,4,2)$ and CKM powers $(1,2,3)$. The leading textures conserve CP, but an exact relation among the modular generators produces an appreciable CKM phase at finite displacement. A fit at $|t|\simeq0.306$ reproduces the quark and lepton reference values with order one coefficients in a fixed normalization. We also relate the Fricke double-coset algebra to coset non-invertible symmetry; on the coefficient spaces used here its action reduces to ordinary Fricke parity.

</details>


### [134] [Gravitational Waves from New Inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29967)
*Naoya Kitajima,Kamil Mudrunka,Kazunori Nakayama*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次给出新暴胀场景（new inflation）完整引力波（gravitational wave）频谱的综合预测，纳入经典与量子两类已知产生机制，用以刻画暴胀子（inflaton）性质与暴胀后动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波是区分不同暴胀模型（inflationary models）的有力观测探针，且高频引力波谱被认识到蕴含暴胀子属性与暴胀后动力学的丰富信息；但缺乏对小场暴胀典型实现——新暴胀场景的完整谱预测。

Method: 在新暴胀场景（small-field inflation 的典型实现）框架下，综合纳入所有已知引力波产生机制，同时考虑经典源与量子源，计算完整的引力波频谱。

Result: 给出了新暴胀场景下完整引力波频谱的首次综合预测，涵盖经典与量子产生机制的贡献。

Conclusion: 该完整频谱预测为利用引力波观测检验小场暴胀模型、并提取暴胀子及暴胀后动力学信息提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Gravitational waves provide one of the most powerful observational probes for discriminating among inflationary models. In particular, it has recently become clear that the high-frequency gravitational-wave spectrum encodes a wealth of information about the properties of the inflaton and the post-inflationary dynamics. In this paper, we present the first comprehensive prediction of the complete gravitational-wave spectrum in the new inflation scenario, a prototypical realization of small-field inflation, incorporating all known production mechanisms including both the classical and quantum sources.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>

# math-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [135] [Methods for Deriving Nonlinear Kinetic Equations from the Dynamics of Quantum Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28846)
*V. I. Gerasimenko*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 论文综述了量子动理学方程（quantum kinetic equations）的推导问题，重点讨论其数学起源、从量子系统演化方程层级（hierarchies of evolution equations）导出的新方法，以及在可观测量（observables）演化框架下和不可分离初始态（non-separable initial states）情形下的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清量子多粒子系统（quantum many-particle systems）各演化方程之间的关系，并说明量子动理学方程的数学来源，以及现有推导方法在可观测量演化与不可分离初始态方面的不足。

Method: 采用综述与理论分析的方式，考察从演化方程层级（hierarchies of evolution equations）推导动理学方程的新方法，并分别在可观测量演化框架与不可分离初始态（non-separable initial states）条件下对量子动理学方程进行推广。

Result: 摘要未给出具体定量结果或数值验证，仅指出所讨论的主题涵盖动理学方程的数学起源、基于方程层级的新推导方法，以及由不可分离初始态所刻画的量子动理学方程推广形式。

Conclusion: 论文将量子动理学方程的推导置于量子多粒子系统演化方程关系的整体图景中，强调可观测量演化视角与不可分离初始态对动理学方程形式的推广意义。

Abstract: The article outlines key topics related to deriving kinetic equations and, in general, to the relationship between the evolution equations governing quantum many-particle systems. The main issues being considered are the mathematical origin of quantum kinetic equations, novel methods for deriving kinetic equations from hierarchies of evolution equations for quantum systems, specifically focusing on kinetics within the framework of the evolution of observables, and the generalization of the quantum kinetic equation specified by non-separable initial states.

</details>


### [136] [Lieb-Robinson bounds via the Combes-Thomas estimate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28927)
*Tali Braester,Haochen Luo,George Peykanu,Sergii Solomakha*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文针对由局部相互作用耦合的自旋网络构成的量子自旋系统（quantum spin systems），给出有限信息传播速度的 Lieb-Robinson 界（Lieb-Robinson bound）的一个新证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lieb-Robinson 界预言量子自旋系统中信息以本质上有限的速度传播，是理解此类系统动力学与因果结构的关键结果，而既有证明依赖路径计数（path-counting）论证，作者希望提供一条不同的证明途径。

Method: 新证明不采用标准的路径计数论证，而是基于带状矩阵（band matrices）预解式（resolvent）元素的一个谱界，即 Combes-Thomas 估计（Combes-Thomas estimate）。

Result: 作者获得了 Lieb-Robinson 界的一个新证明，其核心工具为预解式元素的谱界（Combes-Thomas 估计）而非路径计数。

Conclusion: 研究表明，借助带状矩阵预解式的谱界可替代路径计数方法导出 Lieb-Robinson 界，从而为该结果提供了新的、基于谱分析的证明路线。

Abstract: This paper studies quantum spin systems: networks of spins coupled by local interactions. The Lieb-Robinson bound predicts that in these systems, information propagates at an essentially finite speed. We produce a new proof of this result. Our approach relies on a spectral bound on entries of resolvents of band matrices, the Combes-Thomas estimate, rather than on the standard path-counting argument.

</details>


### [137] [On the Road Again: How the $N$-Lane TASEP and Local Reservoirs Drive Jamming in Axonal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28952)
*Gustav Jennetten,Bhargav R. Karamched*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文构建了分子马达在 \(N\) 条微管轨道上可逆脱离/返回的 TASEP 模型，通过平均场与快切换近似得到总密度满足的有效非线性输运方程；当货物生产速率超过网络输运能力 \(N/4\) 时，入口密度发生鞍点分岔，导致持续货物堆积并向整个区域传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴突内由分子马达沿微管进行的胞内输运对细胞功能至关重要，而轨道（微管）破坏与神经退行性疾病相关；因此需要理解轨道逐步退化如何使高效输运转变为拥堵的病理状态。既有单轨道 TASEP 描述难以刻画多轨道与轨道外脱离态的情形。

Method: 建立 \(N\) 轨道全不对称简单排他过程（totally asymmetric simple exclusion process, TASEP）模型，允许马达可逆地脱离进入局域轨道外（off-track）状态；采用平均场（mean-field）、连续介质（continuum）与快切换（fast-switching）近似，推导关于总马达密度的有效非线性输运方程。

Result: 约化模型保留经典 TASEP 的相结构（低密度相、高密度相与最大流相），但增加轨道数 \(N\) 会移动这三相之间的边界。在反映恒定货物生产的源驱动（source-driven）边界条件下，当生产速率超过网络输运能力 \(N/4\) 时，入口密度发生鞍点分岔（saddle-node bifurcation），触发持续的货物积累并在整个区域内传播。

Conclusion: 研究给出了一种物理机制：微管的渐进退化可驱动系统从高效输运跨越到拥堵的病理状态，阈值由网络输运能力 \(N/4\) 与生产速率的关系决定。

Abstract: Intracellular transport by molecular motors along microtubules is essential for cellular function, particularly in neuronal axons where track disruption drives neurodegenerative disease. We develop an \(N\)-track totally asymmetric simple exclusion process (TASEP) model where motors reversibly detach into local off-track states. Using mean-field, continuum, and fast-switching approximations, we derive an effective nonlinear transport equation for total motor density. This reduced model retains the classical TASEP phase structure but reveals that increasing \(N\) shifts the boundaries between low-density, high-density, and maximal-current regimes. Crucially, under source-driven boundary conditions reflecting constant cargo production, the entrance density undergoes a saddle-node bifurcation when the production rate exceeds the network's transport capacity, \(N/4\). This triggers persistent cargo accumulation that propagates throughout the domain. Our results establish a physical mechanism by which gradual microtubule degradation drives a transition from efficient transport to jammed pathological states.

</details>


### [138] [Widom asymptotics for spatial compressions of Schrödinger spectral projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29076)
*Pan Ma,Daoyuan Zheng*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文为半经典薛定谔算子（semiclassical Schrödinger operators）精确谱投影的空间压缩建立 Widom 迹公式（trace formula），在 \(h^{1-n}\log h^{-1}\) 尺度上把系数分解为边界积分与普适谱泛函两部分，并据此给出固定正阶 von Neumann 与 Rényi 纠缠熵的精确渐近及其与粒子数方差的普适比值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典可容许区域内部观测区域的谱投影压缩缺乏同尺度下的迹刻画，纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）的精确渐近与普适比值此前未被系统给出；同时需明确连续性等弱正则条件是否足以支撑迹公式成立。

Method: 证明结合任意固定正 Schatten 类（Schatten class）中的交换子估计（commutator estimates）与局部迹公式；局部迹公式由边界适配正规形（boundary-adapted normal form）及 Hardy 模型得到。\(n\ge2\) 时用正分割与已知方差渐近控制掠射贡献（grazing contribution），一维情形下所有边界费米点（Fermi points）均为横向。

Result: 对每个在两端具有正幂衰减的连续谱函数，得到 \(h^{1-n}\log h^{-1}\) 尺度的迹公式，其系数分离为边界积分与普适谱泛函；由此得到每个固定正阶 von Neumann 与 Rényi 纠缠熵的精确渐近，并证明其与粒子数方差的普适比值。调和振子（harmonic oscillator）反例表明仅凭连续性与加权可积性不足以推出该迹公式。

Conclusion: 在半经典框架下，精确谱投影空间压缩的迹渐近具有边界项加普适谱泛函的分离结构，并可统一导出纠缠熵的精确渐近与普适比值；正则性假设不可进一步放松至仅连续加加权可积。

Abstract: We establish a Widom trace formula for spatial compressions of exact spectral projections of semiclassical Schrödinger operators. The potential is smooth near a compact classically allowed region, and the observation domain lies strictly inside that region. For every continuous spectral function with positive power decay at both endpoints, we obtain a trace formula at scale $h^{1-n}\log h^{-1}$ whose coefficient separates into a boundary integral and a universal spectral functional. This yields the exact von Neumann and Rényi entanglement asymptotics for every fixed positive order, and proves their universal ratios to the particle-number variance. The proof combines commutator estimates in every fixed positive Schatten class with a local trace formula obtained from a boundary-adapted normal form and a Hardy model. In dimensions $n\ge2$, a positive partition and the known variance asymptotic control the grazing contribution; in one dimension all boundary Fermi points are transverse. A harmonic-oscillator counterexample shows that continuity and weighted integrability alone are insufficient for the trace formula.

</details>


### [139] [The Lévy wave packet and fractional uncertainty relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29397)
*Zi-Xuan Li,Xiao-Dong Li,Towe Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该工作重新审视十年前关于分数阶不确定性关系（fractional uncertainty relations）的争论并尝试给出结论，通过对Lévy波包（Lévy wave packet）位置-动量不确定性乘积的研究，提出分数阶量子力学（fractional quantum mechanics）中不确定性关系的一个新下界，该下界与Lévy指数近似呈线性，并可由文献中精确可解结果满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 十年前出现的关于分数阶量子力学中不确定性关系形式的争论尚未定论，本文旨在厘清该争论并给出新的界。

Method: 仔细考察Lévy波包的位置-动量不确定性乘积（position-momentum uncertainty product），据此构造分数阶量子力学中不确定性关系的新下界（lower bound）。

Result: 所提出的下界与Lévy指数（Lévy index）近似呈线性关系，且与文献中已有的精确、可计算解（exact, calculable solutions）相容，被这些解所满足。

Conclusion: 该新下界为分数阶量子力学的不确定性关系提供了可行表述，并可能为持续十年的相关争论提供解决思路；其有效性由现有精确解的支持所体现。

Abstract: This work revisits the debate on fractional uncertainty relations that arose a decade ago and tries to settle it. After a careful study of the position-momentum uncertainty product of the Lévy wave packet, a novel lower bound is proposed for uncertainty relations in fractional quantum mechanics. This bound is nearly linear in the Lévy index and is satisfied by exact, calculable solutions available in the literature.

</details>


### [140] [Geometrization of continuum mechanics and nonequilibrium thermodynamics: Heat conduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29644)
*Ilya Peshkov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出非平衡热传导的几何表述，将SHTC方程的变分（哈密顿）结构与微分形式的外微分运算相结合，以四维物质-时间流形上"热冲量一型"（thermal impulse one-form）为核心对象，推导出与麦克斯韦方程同构的非平衡双温度模型，并在松弛极限下约化为Guyer-Krumhansl型热传导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非平衡热传导理论缺乏将变分/哈密顿结构与几何（微分形式）语言统一起来的表述，难以同时刻画弹道输运与非局部、耗散效应。本文旨在为热传导提供一种兼具变分结构与外微分几何的解释框架。

Method: 在四维物质-时间流形（matter-time manifold）上引入热冲量一型；可逆（弹道）热输运时其时间分量为温度且为标量势的恰当微分（holonomic）。非平衡效应由第二支"小尺度热冲量"一型描述，其外微分为四维热涡度（four-thermal vorticity），满足比安基型运动学恒等式（Bianchi-type identity）。变分原理与该恒等式联合导出双温度场方程；通过守恒总能量并产生熵的松弛项引入耗散，并作形式渐近分析。

Result: 所得场方程与介质中麦克斯韦方程（Maxwell's equations）结构相同，热冲量场、热涡度场与热坡印廷矢量（thermal Poynting vector）共同贡献热流。形式渐近分析表明，在松弛极限下模型约化为具有独立横向与纵向长度尺度的Guyer-Krumhansl型热传导：横向非局部项源于小尺度热冲量的空间非完整性（non-holonomy），纵向项源于其在时间上的非完整性（即涨落温度的松弛）。

Conclusion: 该模型在运动学上属于阿贝尔规范理论（abelian gauge theory），但因存在两个温度而显式破缺局域规范对称性。文中还讨论了该几何结构对保结构数值方法（structure-preserving numerical methods）的启示。

Abstract: We propose a geometric formulation of non-equilibrium heat conduction. It combines the variational (Hamiltonian) structure of symmetric hyperbolic thermodynamically compatible (SHTC) equations with the exterior calculus of differential forms. The central object is the thermal impulse one-form on the four-dimensional matter-time manifold. Its time component is the temperature, and for reversible (ballistic) heat transport it is the exact differential of a scalar potential (i.e. it is holonomic). Non-equilibrium effects are described by a second one-form, the small-scale thermal impulse, which is not required to be exact. Its time component is a fluctuating temperature, and its exterior derivative, the four-thermal vorticity, satisfies a Bianchi-type kinematic identity. The variational principle together with this identity yields a non-equilibrium two-temperature model. Its field equations have the same structure as Maxwell's equations in a medium, with the thermal impulse field, the thermal vorticity field and a thermal Poynting vector contributing to the heat flux. Dissipation is introduced through relaxation terms that conserve the total energy and produce entropy. A formal asymptotic analysis shows that, in the relaxation limit, the model reduces to a Guyer-Krumhansl-type heat conduction with independent transverse and longitudinal length scales. The transverse nonlocal term comes from the spatial non-holonomy of the small-scale thermal impulse, while the longitudinal term comes from its non-holonomy in time, i.e. from the relaxation of the fluctuating temperature. We also discuss the gauge-theoretic interpretation of the model. Its kinematics is that of an abelian gauge theory, while the local gauge symmetry is explicitly broken by the existence of two temperatures. Finally, we discuss the consequences of the geometric structure for structure-preserving numerical methods.

</details>


### [141] [On the planar Neumann--Poincaré inverse spectral problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29661)
*Habib Ammari,Matthew J. Colbrook*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文用反例否定了 Neumann–Poincaré 谱决定平面区域（至扩展 Möbius 变换）的猜想，并对规定有限维族给出 Lipschitz 稳定的正结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是 Neumann–Poincaré 谱（Neumann–Poincaré spectrum）能否将平面区域确定到扩展 Möbius 变换（extended Möbius transformation）为止；摘要表明即使在解析、严格凸、代数的边界下也需检验此猜想。

Method: 构造一对区域（其算子相似）给出反例；并对规定的有限维法向外部共形映照（normalised exterior conformal maps）族，结合 Grunsky 表示（Grunsky representation）、加权 Hankel 矩阵（weighted Hankel matrices）的非退化论证与微分扰动估计证明正结果。

Result: 反例说明答案为否，即使在解析、严格凸、代数边界下两算子仍相似。正结果：对每个 m≥1，前 m 个正特征值在小截断凸锥上确定 m 个实 Laurent 系数（Laurent coefficients），前提是更高阶系数为规定的二阶解析余项；恢复具 Lipschitz 稳定性且在趋于圆盘时一致。

Conclusion: 谱不能唯一决定区域，但在受约束的有限维映照族中特征值可稳定恢复有限个 Laurent 系数；双参数、三参数及单模族、Möbius 等价类等例子说明该结果，并借完全极化数据（complete polarization data）的直接证明对比谱权重的额外作用。

Abstract: Does the Neumann--Poincaré spectrum determine a planar domain up to an extended Möbius transformation? We construct a pair showing that the answer is no, even for analytic, strictly convex, algebraic boundaries. Their operators are similar. We then prove a positive result for prescribed finite-dimensional families of normalised exterior conformal maps. For every $m\ge1$, the first $m$ positive eigenvalues determine $m$ real Laurent coefficients on a small truncated convex cone, provided the higher coefficients are a prescribed analytic remainder of quadratic order. Recovery is Lipschitz stable, uniformly down to the disk. The proof combines the Grunsky representation with a nondegeneracy argument for weighted Hankel matrices and differentiated perturbation estimates. Explicit two- and three-parameter examples, single-mode families with repeated leading eigenvalues, and Möbius equivalence classes illustrate the result. Direct proofs for complete polarization data show what spectral weights add to the eigenvalues.

</details>


### [142] [BCC optimality for the three-dimensional Coulomb lattice energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29732)
*C. B. Muratov,M. Novaga*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明：在固定协体积的三维布拉菲格子（Bravais lattice）中，体心立方格子（body-centered cubic lattice）在正交变换意义下唯一使重整化库仑能（renormalized Coulomb energy）取最小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定何种三维周期结构使库仑能最小是固体物理与数学中的经典问题；此前对三维布拉菲格子缺乏全局最优性的严格证明，本文旨在给出该最小化问题的完整刻画。

Method: 采用 Ewald 求和（Ewald summation）将能量（差一个可加常数）表示为实空间与倒空间两个正项快速收敛级数之和，利用其正性给出所有竞争格子的有限下界；再通过过渡到对偶格子（dual lattice）将问题化为面心立方（face-centered cubic）比较，并结合短基向量估计、经验证的局部 Hessian 与五维紧致区域上的区间界完成计算机辅助证明。

Result: 体心立方格子在固定协体积的三维布拉菲格子中唯一极小化重整化库仑能（至正交变换）。

Conclusion: 该工作以可验证的计算机辅助方式建立了三维库仑能最小化的全局最优性，将实/倒空间级数的正性方法与对偶化归约相结合，为相关格点能量比较问题提供了严格框架。

Abstract: We prove that the body-centered cubic lattice uniquely minimizes the
  renormalized Coulomb energy among three-dimensional Bravais lattices
  of fixed covolume, up to orthogonal transformations. Using Ewald
  summation, we write the energy, to within an additive constant, as a
  sum of two positive, rapidly convergent series in real and
  reciprocal space. Their positivity provides finite lower bounds for
  all competitors. A passage to the dual lattice reduces the problem
  to a face-centered cubic comparison. A short basis vector estimate,
  a certified local Hessian, and interval bounds on a compact
  five-dimensional domain complete the computer-assisted proof.

</details>


### [143] [Quantum Charge as a Particle/Antiparticle Label](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29791)
*Alexander D. Popov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出粒子与反粒子的区分源于外尔-海森堡群（Weyl-Heisenberg group）$W\!H_3$ 的中心元 $Q_{\sf v}$ 所定义的 U(1)$_{\sf v}$ 量子荷：粒子波函数荷为 $q_+=1/\hbar$，反粒子为 $q_-=-1/\hbar$；恢复 U(1)$_{\sf v}$ 不变性可消除负概率、负能量与克莱因悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统克莱因-戈尔登（Klein-Gordon）与狄拉克（Dirac）理论中存在负概率/负能量及克莱因悖论（Klein paradox），作者认为其根源在于这些理论破坏了 U(1)$_{\sf v}$ 不变性，因而需要从群论角度重新界定粒子与反粒子的本质区别。

Method: 以 $W\!H_3$ 的位置算符 $\hat x^a$、动量算符 $\hat p_a$ 与中心元 $Q_{\sf v}$ 为出发点，把波函数按 $Q_{\sf v}$ 本征值分为 $\psi_+$（$q_+=1/\hbar$）与 $\psi_-$（$q_-=-1/\hbar$），分别置于希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）$\mathcal{H}_+$、$\mathcal{H}_-$ 上，对应两个酉不等价表示（unitarily inequivalent representations）$\pi_\hbar$ 与 $\pi_{-\hbar}$，并由反线性电荷共轭算符（charge conjugation operator）$\hat C$ 相联系。在此基础上分析上述理论中 U(1)$_{\sf v}$ 不变性的破坏。

Result: 量子荷 $q_\pm$ 被视为波函数的内禀属性（类似自旋与电荷），在指定任何方程之前即已确定；负概率/负能量与克莱因悖论被解释为 U(1)$_{\sf v}$ 不变性被破坏的结果。

Conclusion: 恢复 U(1)$_{\sf v}$ 不变性可实现粒子与反粒子的分离，并在第一量子化相对论理论（first-quantized relativistic theories）层面消除所有相关悖论。

Abstract: We show that the concept of particles and antiparticles is related to the Weyl-Heisenberg group $W\!H_3$. Its generators are the position operators $\hat x^a$, the momentum operators $\hat p_a$, and the central element $Q_{\sf{v}}$, which defines a subgroup U(1)$_{\sf v}$ of $W\!H_3$. We show that the wave functions have a charge relative to the group U(1)$_{\sf{v}}$ equal to either $q_+=1/\hbar$ (particle $ψ_+$) or $q_-=-1/\hbar$ (antiparticle $ψ_-$). We call these eigenvalues of the operator $Q_{\sf{v}}$ quantum charges: $Q_{\sf{v}}ψ_\pm^{}=q_\pm^{}ψ_\pm^{}$. The wave functions $ψ_+$ and $ψ_-$ belong to the Hilbert spaces $\mathcal{H}_+$ and $\mathcal{H}_-$, on which unitarily inequivalent representations $π_\hbar$ and $π_{-\hbar}$ of the Weyl-Heisenberg group $W\!H_3$ are defined. These representations are related to each other by the antilinear charge conjugation operator $\hat C$, which maps $\mathcal{H}_+, q_+$ to $\mathcal{H}_-, q_-$. Charges $q_\pm^{}$ are intrinsic characteristics of the wave functions $ψ_\pm^{}$, similar to spin and electric charge. The wave function possesses one of these charges, $q_+$ or $q_-$, prior to the specification of any equations. We show that negative probabilities/energies and the Klein paradox in Klein-Gordon and Dirac theories are consequences of the violation of U(1)$_{\sf v}$-invariance in these theories. Restoring this invariance separates particle and antiparticles and eliminates all paradoxes at the level of first-quantized relativistic theories.

</details>


### [144] [Generalized Coordinate Bethe Ansatz and Quantum Integrability of the Time-Dependent Massive Thirring Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29998)
*Parameshwar R. Pasnoori*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文为具有显式时间依赖费米子质量的大质量Thirring模型（massive Thirring model）构建了广义坐标Bethe拟设（coordinate Bethe ansatz）框架，其核心新结构是“快度格点（rapidity lattice）”。通过Ruijsenaars型标量解析差分方程（analytic difference equations）求解振幅，得到完整的时间依赖多体波函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者指出，手征量子场论（chiral quantum field theory）中的广义Bethe拟设作用于内部自旋空间并导向量子Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov方程，与处理粒子坐标与物理快度的思路不同；本文旨在构建直接作用于粒子坐标和物理快度的坐标Bethe拟设，以处理时间依赖质量带来的新结构。此外，该工作也希望给出时间依赖可积性-重整化群对应（integrability-renormalization group correspondence）的新实现。

Method: 由于时间依赖质量在单粒子扇区（one-particle sector）内即耦合右行与左行分量，固定快度块（fixed-rapidity block）在空间缠绕下不封闭，需引入由有限平移β→β−p生成的全部扇区以满足周期性。粒子交换通过Ruijsenaars型标量解析差分方程关联快度格点上的振幅；坐标构造的自洽性进一步筛选出裸质量（bare mass）的指数演化。

Result: 该快度格点构造给出了完整的时间依赖多体波函数，并选择出裸质量的指数演化形式，从而提供时间依赖可积性-重整化群对应的新实现。在静态极限（static limit）下，该构造退化为常规的大质量Thirring Bethe方程（Bethe equations）。借助大质量Thirring/sine-Gordon等价性，结果还将该精确量子构造与广义时空依赖Lax形式（Lax formalism）联系起来。

Conclusion: 研究确立了以快度格点为核心的广义坐标Bethe拟设，作为描述时间依赖质量可积场论的有效框架，并统一了静态极限、重整化群对应与Lax表述等多个层面；但其适用范围与普适性仅由摘要所述内容界定。

Abstract: We develop a generalized coordinate Bethe-ansatz framework for the massive Thirring model with an explicitly time-dependent fermion mass. Unlike the generalized Bethe ansatz for chiral quantum field theories, where particle transport acts in internal spin spaces and leads to quantum Knizhnik-Zamolodchikov equations, the present construction acts directly on particle coordinates and physical rapidities. Its central new feature is a rapidity lattice. Because the time-dependent mass couples right- and left-moving components already in the one-particle sector, a fixed-rapidity block is not closed under spatial winding. Periodicity therefore requires all sectors generated by the finite shift $β\toβ-p$. Particle exchange relates amplitudes on this lattice through scalar analytic difference equations of Ruijsenaars type, whose solutions determine the complete time-dependent many-body wavefunction. Consistency of the coordinate construction also selects an exponential evolution of the bare mass, providing a new realization of the time dependent integrability-renormalization group correspondence. In the static limit, the rapidity-lattice construction reduces to the conventional massive-Thirring Bethe equations. Through the massive-Thirring/sine Gordon equivalence, the result also connects this exact quantum construction to the generalized space-time dependent Lax formalism.

</details>


### [145] [Microscopic Parametric Correlations and Spectral Rigidity in the Non-Hermitian Threefold Way](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13434)
*Yan V Fyodorov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究三个非厄米高斯体类（$A$、$AI^\dagger$、$AII^\dagger$）中相邻参数值之间的微观谱关联，利用平稳矩阵 Ornstein–Uhlenbeck 演化与副本方法导出有限 $N$ 的辅助场积分表示及其体渐近双时刻核，并给出数方差、跨时刻协方差与短时谱扩散机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非厄米随机矩阵的微观谱关联已有较成熟结果，但两个相近参数值之间的“双时刻”谱关联核在 $AI^\dagger$ 与 $AII^\dagger$ 类中尚属空缺，需要建立刻画参数依赖下谱涨落的微观理论。

Method: 以平稳矩阵 Ornstein–Uhlenbeck 过程建模参数依赖，微观时间尺度为 $N^{-1}$；对每个正整数副本数 $n$ 推导联合特征多项式矩的精确有限 $N$ 辅助场积分表示，取 $N\to\infty$（$n$ 固定）的体渐近，并结合静态厄米/非厄米副本延拓得到双时刻核与精确微扰论分析。

Result: 谱归一化到单位圆后，关联仅依赖标度变量 $s=N|z_1-z_2|^2+N(1-|z_0|^2)|t_1-t_2|$；由此得到显式双时刻核（$AI^\dagger$、$AII^\dagger$ 为副本猜想），可确定密度关联、跨时刻本征值计数协方差与等时数方差：$\mathrm{Var}\mathcal N_X(D_y)=\kappa_X\sqrt y+\beta_X/\sqrt y+o(y^{-1/2})$，各类常数显式给出。微扰论指出本征向量非正交性是短时谱扩散机制，并给出 $AII^\dagger$ 类二维 Kramers 本征空间与基无关的条件数，其逆伽马律猜想与直接模拟定量吻合。

Conclusion: 该工作为三类非厄米高斯体类提供了统一的微观双时刻谱关联框架，首次提出 $AI^\dagger$ 与 $AII^\dagger$ 的双时刻核（以副本猜想形式），并揭示本征向量非正交性驱动短时谱扩散；模拟支持其逆伽马律预测。

Abstract: We study microscopic spectral correlations between two nearby parameter values in the three non-Hermitian Gaussian bulk classes: $A$ (unconstrained), $AI^\dagger$ (complex symmetric), and $AII^\dagger$ (complex self-dual, with each degenerate doublet counted once). Parameter dependence is modelled by stationary matrix Ornstein--Uhlenbeck evolution, whose microscopic time scale is $N^{-1}$. For every positive integer replica number $n$, we derive exact finite-$N$ auxiliary-field integral representations for joint characteristic-polynomial moments and obtain their bulk asymptotics as $N\to\infty$ with $n$ fixed. With the spectrum normalized to the unit disk, correlations near a bulk point $z_0$, between spectral positions $z_1,z_2$ at times $t_1,t_2$, depend only on $s=N|z_1-z_2|^2+N(1-|z_0|^2)|t_1-t_2|$. Adopting the static Hermitian/non-Hermitian replica continuation yields explicit two-time kernels; for $AI^\dagger$ and $AII^\dagger$ these are replica conjectures and, to our knowledge, the first microscopic two-time kernels proposed for these classes. They determine density correlations, cross-time eigenvalue-count covariances, and equal-time number variances. For a disk of mean eigenvalue count $y$, the latter obey $\mbox{Var}\mathcal N_X(D_y)=κ_X\sqrt y+ β_X/\sqrt y+o(y^{-1/2})$, with explicitly given constants for each class. Exact perturbation theory further identifies eigenvector nonorthogonality as the mechanism of short-time spectral diffusion and yields a basis-independent condition number for a two-dimensional Kramers eigenspace in class $AII^\dagger$. Direct simulations agree quantitatively with its conjectured inverse-gamma law.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.supr-con'></div>

### [146] [Theory of the intrinsic optical anomalous Hall responses in time-reversal symmetry breaking superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28786)
*Jia-Long Zhang,Wen Huang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作建立了反演对称破缺时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry breaking, TRSB）多带超导体的统一理论，论证其本征光学霍尔响应（optical anomalous Hall effect）本质上是量子几何（quantum geometry）现象，并据此给出区分不同TRSB配对态的可观测判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 确定超导体库珀配对（Cooper pairing）的对称性是凝聚态物理的核心难题，TRSB配对态（如拓扑 $p_x+ip_y$ 态）预期表现出光学反常霍尔效应，但在洁净超导体中促成霍尔响应的微观要素及其组织原则尚未阐明。

Method: 构建面向反演对称TRSB多带超导体的统一理论，区分酉（unitary）与非酉（nonunitary）态；通过图论方法识别单电子隧穿与库珀对隧穿两类过程，二者均涉及由带间速度（与带间Berry连接（interband Berry connection）紧密相关）驱动的带间隧穿；并借助积分掉空穴自由度得到的有效理论及对称性分析加以刻画。

Result: 严格证明带间配对（interband pairing）是酉态产生本征霍尔响应的前提，而非酉态并不绝对需要；在仅有带内配对（intraband pairing）的非酉态中，若存在自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling），霍尔效应可经单电子隧穿产生。有效理论重现上述结果，并显示配对可使电子获得有效Berry曲率（Berry curvature）；对称性分析表明，超导能隙边缘附近的本征共振在非酉态允许、在酉态被禁止。

Conclusion: 该研究确立量子几何作为超导体本征霍尔响应的统一原则，并为区分不同候选TRSB配对态提供理论指导。

Abstract: Determining the symmetry of the Cooper pairing in superconductors is a central challenge in condensed matter physics. Time-reversal symmetry breaking (TRSB) is among the most distinctive symmetry-breaking pattern of a superconductor. An important subset of TRSB pairing states, including the topological $p_x+ip_y$ pairing, is expected to exhibit the optical anomalous Hall effect. However, the microscopic ingredients that enable the Hall response and their underlying organizing principle in clean superconductors remain unelucidated. Here, we develop a unified theory for TRSB multiband superconductors possessing inversion symmetry, and establish their intrinsic optical Hall response as fundamentally a quantum-geometric phenomenon. We distinguish unitary and nonunitary TRSB states. Diagrammatically, we identify two classes of processes: single-electron tunneling and Cooper-pair tunneling. Both involve at least one interband tunneling event driven by the interband velocity-which is closely tied to the interband Berry connection. We rigorously prove that interband pairing is a prerequisite for unitary states to generate intrinsic Hall response, whereas it is not absolutely needed for nonunitary states. In nonunitary states with intraband pairing alone, the Hall effect can arise through single-electron tunneling, provided spin-orbit coupling is present. An effective theory obtained by integrating out the hole degrees of freedom reproduces these results and shows how pairing may endow electrons with effective Berry curvature. Finally, through symmetry analysis, we show that intrinsic resonances near the superconducting gap edge are allowed in nonunitary states but forbidden in unitary states. Our work thus establishes quantum geometry as a unifying principle for the intrinsic Hall response in superconductors and provides a guide for distinguishing different candidate TRSB pairings.

</details>


### [147] [Josephson effect in symmetry-mismatched van der Waals hybrid junctions of NbSe2-Bi2Sr2CaCu2O8+x and NbSe2-Bi2Sr2-xLaxCuO6+y](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29021)
*Yuying Zhu,Ziye Liu,Hongtao Yan,Lin Zhao,Xingjiang Zhou,Qi-Kun Xue,Ding Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作通过范德华堆叠（van der Waals stacking）将NbSe2薄片与Bi2Sr2CaCu2O8+x或Bi2Sr2-xLaxCuO6+y新鲜解理面结合，制备出跨不同对称性超导体的约瑟夫森结（Josephson junction），在7 K以下观测到显著约瑟夫森电流，I_cR_n可达约0.3 mV，并观察到Shapiro台阶与Fraunhofer衍射图样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同对称性超导体耦合可能产生涌现量子现象（emergent quantum phenomena），但针对晶格失配（lattice-mismatched）晶体的异质结实验研究仍很缺乏，需要可行的材料平台加以探索。

Method: 采用范德华堆叠在Bi2Sr2CaCu2O8+x或Bi2Sr2-xLaxCuO6+y的新鲜解理表面上制备NbSe2薄片约瑟夫森结，并进行微波辐照（microwave irradiation）与面内磁场（in-plane magnetic field）输运测量，同时与考虑相干隧穿（coherent tunneling）和序参量混合（order parameter mixing）的理论计算进行对比。

Result: 在低于7 K时测得明显的约瑟夫森电流，临界电流与正常态电阻乘积I_cR_n可达约0.3 mV，远高于此前报道的Pb-Bi2Sr2CaCu2O8+x或Nb-Bi2Sr2CaCu2O8+x结；微波辐照诱导出整数Shapiro台阶（integer Shapiro steps），面内磁场下观察到Fraunhofer衍射图样。

Conclusion: 该混合结（hybrid junction）可作为一种独特平台，用于研究配对对称性（pairing symmetry）及相关涌现量子现象，实验结果与包含相干隧穿与序参量混合的理论计算相符。

Abstract: Coupling superconductors of different symmetries may lead to emergent quantum phenomena but experimental studies on heterojunctions made of lattice-mismatched crystals are scarce. Here, we fabricate Josephson junction by van der Waals stacking NbSe2 flakes on freshly cleaved surfaces of Bi2Sr2CaCu2O8+x or Bi2Sr2-xLaxCuO6+y. We obtain pronounced Josephson currents across the heterojunctions at temperatures below 7 K. The Josephson coupling strength, characterized by the product of the critical Josephson current I_c and the normal state resistance R_n, can reach about 0.3 mV, much exceeding the values previously reported in Pb-Bi2Sr2CaCu2O8+x or Nb-Bi2Sr2CaCu2O8+x junctions. Microwave irradiation induces integer Shapiro steps. We also observe the Fraunhofer diffraction pattern by applying an in-plane magnetic field. We compare our results with the theoretical calculations that take into account the coherent tunneling and the order parameter mixing. Our work demonstrates the hybrid junctions as a distinctive platform for addressing pairing symmetry and emergent quantum phenomena.

</details>


### [148] [Exotic vortex states and enhancement of orbital upper critical fields in an $s$-wave quasicrystalline superconductor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29064)
*Takumi Fukushima,Yuki Yamamoto,Nayuta Takemori,Shiro Sakai,Masanori Ichioka,Anuradha Jagannathan*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该论文利用Bogoliubov-de Gennes平均场理论研究了Ammann-Beenker拼砌上的s波准晶超导体中的涡旋态，发现涡旋构型可由Abrikosov三角格子转变为受局域几何调控的非均匀分布，并伴随上临界场的增强与类平台型行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准晶（quasicrystal）具有长程有序但无平移对称性，由此产生内禀非均匀的超导态；然而其高场涡旋态及上临界场（upper critical field）行为尚需微观层面澄清。

Method: 采用Bogoliubov-de Gennes平均场理论（mean-field theory），在Ammann-Beenker拼砌（tiling）上求解s波（s-wave）准晶超导体的涡旋态，重点关注高场区域。

Result: 在填充度ν=0.8时，涡旋优先穿透以配位数（coordination number）为8的格点为中心的弱配对区域，其空间组织强烈受局域几何影响；同时观察到上临界场增强，且该填充度下空间平均序参量（order parameter）呈类平台型场依赖，并伴随涡旋团簇（vortex clusters）形成，这些团簇在局域弱超导区容纳额外磁通量。

Conclusion: 涡旋团簇在局域弱超导区容纳额外磁通量为平台型行为提供了微观解释；此外，上临界场在降温至零温过程中持续增大且无明显饱和迹象。

Abstract: Quasicrystals possess long-range order without translational symmetry, giving rise to intrinsically inhomogeneous superconducting states. Using the Bogoliubov-de Gennes mean-field theory, we investigate vortex states in an $s$-wave quasicrystalline superconductor on the Ammann-Beenker tiling, with particular emphasis on the high-field regime. We find that the vortex configuration can differ qualitatively from the Abrikosov triangular lattice. In particular, at filling $ν=0.8$, vortices preferentially penetrate weak-pairing regions centered on sites with coordination number $8$, indicating that their spatial organization is strongly influenced by the local geometry. Furthermore, we observe an enhanced upper critical field. At $ν=0.8$, where the enhancement is particularly pronounced, the spatially averaged order parameter exhibits a plateau-like field dependence accompanied by the formation of vortex clusters. These clusters accommodate additional flux in locally weak superconducting regions, providing a microscopic interpretation of the plateau-like behavior. Moreover, the upper critical field increases upon cooling toward zero temperature without a clear sign of saturation.

</details>


### [149] [Magnetization and Josephson current coupling drives the Josephson diode effect in FeTe0.55Se0.45 van der Waals junctions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29124)
*Seung-Hyun Shin,Cheong-Eung Ahn,Chang-Won Choi,Taekoo Oh,Si-Young Choi,Genda Gu,Gil Young Cho,Gil-Ho Lee*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 基于FeTe0.55Se0.45范德华约瑟夫森结，通过磁场、微波与数值模拟证明约瑟夫森二极管效应（JDE）源于界面磁性与约瑟夫森电流的耦合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约瑟夫森二极管效应（Josephson diode effect, JDE）反映电流-相位关系（current-phase relation, CPR）中的对称性破缺，但其微观起源往往难以确定，因此需要在合适平台上厘清机制。

Method: 制备基于FeTe0.55Se0.45的范德华约瑟夫森结（van der Waals Josephson junctions），结合磁场下的Fraunhofer图样与磁共振电压特征、微波下半整数Shapiro台阶（half-integer Shapiro steps）测量，并采用磁化动力学与约瑟夫森相位动力学联合的数值模拟。

Result: 器件在零场下表现出稳健的二极管响应与非对称切换电流；磁场测量显示迟滞Fraunhofer图样与磁共振诱导的电压特征；微波响应呈现非对称演化的半整数Shapiro台阶，表明CPR中同时存在二次谐波与磁化诱导的相位移动，数值模拟可复现上述特征。

Conclusion: JDE源自磁化与约瑟夫森电流之间的界面耦合；该工作提供了从输运角度识别JDE微观机制的途径，为研究磁活性界面超导（magnetically active interfacial superconductivity）及设计非互易超导器件（nonreciprocal superconducting devices）建立平台。

Abstract: The Josephson diode effect (JDE) reflects symmetry breaking in the current-phase relation (CPR) of Josephson junctions, yet establishing its microscopic origin is often challenging. We investigate the mechanism of the JDE in van der Waals Josephson junctions based on FeTe0.55Se0.45, a platform where superconductivity, topology, and magnetism may coexist at the interface. Our devices exhibit a robust zero-field diode response with asymmetric switching currents. Magnetic-field measurements reveal a hysteretic Fraunhofer pattern and magnetic-resonance-induced voltage feature, evidencing interfacial magnetism coupled to the Josephson current. Microwave response further shows half-integer Shapiro steps with asymmetric evolution, demonstrating contributions from both a second harmonic and a magnetization-induced phase shift in CPR. Numerical simulations combining magnetization dynamics and Josephson phase dynamics reproduce these features, confirming that the JDE originates from interfacial coupling between magnetization and Josephson current. This work provides a transport-based route to pinpoint the microscopic mechanism of the JDE, establishing a platform for exploring magnetically active interfacial superconductivity, and enables the design of nonreciprocal superconducting devices in clean, interface-engineered van der Waals systems.

</details>


### [150] [Suppressing Majorana Hybridization in Atomically Assembled Cr Chains on Bi2Pd](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29446)
*Jinkyung Kim,Piotr Kot,Ryo Ogawa,Atustaka Maeda,Andreas J. Heinrich,Nicolas Lorente,Deung-Jang Choi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 在Bi2Pd的Bi终止面上逐个原子组装Cr链，可利用短局域长度的Shiba链机制抑制有限尺寸杂化，从而在实验可及的链长下获得稳定的Majorana零能模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性原子链置于超导体上可承载Majorana零能模（Majorana zero modes），但有限长链中两端模式通常发生杂化（hybridization）而偏离零能，需寻找能抑制该有限尺寸效应的体系。

Method: 采用原子逐个组装（atom-by-atom）方式在Bi2Pd的Bi终止面制备Cr链，并用扫描隧道谱（scanning tunneling spectroscopy, STS）测量随链长演化的带隙内谱，辅以格林函数（Green's function）计算进行对照。

Result: 实验观察到链长驱动的带隙内谱（in-gap spectrum）闭合与再打开，随后出现局域于链端的零偏压模式（zero-bias modes）；计算重现该演化并揭示跨越转变时电子-空穴特征的交换。由残余劈裂（residual splitting）提取的局域长度约三个原子位点。

Conclusion: 短局域长度的Shiba链区间能强烈抑制有限尺寸杂化，约三个原子位点的局域长度解释了零能模在实际链长下的稳定性。

Abstract: Magnetic-atom chains on superconductors can host Majorana zero modes, but in finite chains the two end modes generally hibridize and split away from zero energy. We show that Cr chains assembled atom-by-atom on the Bi-terminated surface of Bi2Pd enter a short-localization-length Shiba chain regime wthat strongly suppreesses this finite-size hybridization. Scanning tunneling spectroscopy reveals a chaing-leght-driven closing and reopening of the in-gap spectrum, followed by zero-bias modes localized at the chains ends. Green-function calculations reproduce the evolution and show an exchange of electorn-holec haracter across the transition. From the residual splitting we extract a localization length of about three atomic sites, explaining the stability of the zero-energy modes in experimentally accessible chains.

</details>


### [151] [Superconductivity via repulsive interaction in the model with electrons and holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29583)
*A. O. Sboychakov*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文研究一个包含电子型与空穴型载流子相互杂化的二维电子气（2D electron gas）玩具模型，指出轻微掺杂引入的高态密度载流子可使屏蔽库仑相互作用稳定自旋三重态 p 波超导，转变温度可达 150 K。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索二维电子气体系中是否存在由屏蔽库仑相互作用（screened Coulomb interaction）本身驱动的高温超导机制，并考察电子—空穴杂化与轻微掺杂的作用。

Method: 构建二维电子气玩具模型（toy model），其中电子型与空穴型电荷载流子（charge carriers）相互杂化，二者可位于不同层或同一层；未掺杂时为绝缘体，轻微掺杂引入大态密度的额外载流子，并在该框架下分析屏蔽库仑相互作用对超导稳定性的影响及序参量对称性。

Result: 屏蔽库仑相互作用足以在轻微掺杂体系中稳定超导性；序参量（order parameter）对称性对应于自旋三重态 p 波（spin-triplet p-wave）序；在现实模型参数取值下，超导转变温度（superconducting transition temperature）可达 150 K。

Conclusion: 电子—空穴杂化的二维电子气在轻微掺杂下可仅凭屏蔽库仑相互作用实现自旋三重态 p 波超导，且转变温度可达百开尔文量级，提示该机制可能是一条高温超导路径。

Abstract: We study a toy model of the 2D electron gas including electron-like and hole-like charge carriers hybridized between each other. Electrons and holes can move either in different layers or in the same layer. Undoped system is an insulator. Slight doping brings extra charge carriers having large density of states. We show that the screened Coulomb interaction is enough to stabilize the superconductivity in the slightly doped system. The symmetry of the order parameter corresponds to the spin-triplet $p$-wave order. For realistic values of the model parameters the superconducting transition temperature can reach the value of $150$ kelvin.

</details>


### [152] [Unambiguous determination of the THz conductivity in driven YBa$_2$Cu$_3$O$_{6+x}$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29892)
*K. Kaj,K. H. Martens,D. Choi,M. Buzzi,D. Nicoletti,Y. Liu,B. Keimer,A. Cavalleri*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该研究通过实验测量泵浦频率（15-20 THz）处的注量依赖复光学常数，澄清了YBa₂Cu₃O₆₊ₓ中太赫兹响应随泵浦注量次线性增长（sublinear growth）的来源，确认其源于高注量下泵浦场反射增强与穿透深度减小，而非响应饱和，从而在定性与定量上支持光诱导超导态的原始解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中红外泵浦-太赫兹探测（mid-infrared pump, THz probe）实验提示YBa₂Cu₃O₆₊ₓ存在具有超导体光学性质的高温瞬态，但提取的c轴太赫兹瞬态光学性质存在歧义：信号随泵浦注量次线性增长，可能源于响应饱和（saturation），也可能源于反射率或泵浦沉积深度变化等无关效应。

Method: 通过实验测量泵浦光所在15-20 THz频段的注量依赖复光学常数（complex optical constants），以区分次线性增长的不同来源。

Result: 观测到的次线性增长在很大程度上归因于高注量下泵浦场反射增强与穿透深度（penetration depth）减小；计入该效应后，原先关于光诱导超导态的解释在定性和定量上均得到确认。

Conclusion: 次线性增长主要不是响应饱和所致，而是泵浦场空间分布变化的结果；该工作消除了瞬态光学性质提取中的歧义，支持YBa₂Cu₃O₆₊ₓ中光诱导超导态（light-induced superconducting state）的原始结论。

Abstract: Mid-infrared pump, THz probe experiments in YBa$_2$Cu$_3$O$_{6+x}$ yield evidence of a high-temperature transient state of the material with the optical properties of a superconductor. However, there are ambiguities in the extracted transient c-axis THz optical properties, primarily resulting from the sublinear growth of the THz response with increasing mid-infrared pump fluence. It was proposed that this effect may be related to saturation of the material's response, which would then perturb its spatial profile and lead one to overestimate the induced change in the transient optical properties. Yet, sublinear growth of the signal could also arise from other effects unrelated to saturation, for example from a change in reflectivity or deposition depth for the pump pulses. Here, we remove this ambiguity by experimentally measuring the fluence-dependent complex optical constants at the 15-20 THz frequencies of the pump. We show that the observed sublinear growth is to be attributed in large part to the enhanced reflection and reduced penetration depth of the pump field at high fluences. When taking into account these findings, we confirm the original interpretation of a light-induced superconducting state both qualitatively and quantitatively.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>

# math.NA [[Back]](#toc)

### [153] [Scaling H2 Factorization to Billion-Unknown Three-Dimensional Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30131)
*Tianyu Liang,Chao Chen,James Demmel,Yang Liu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向对称矩阵的分布式内存 $\mathcal{H}^2$ 分解求解器（distributed-memory $\mathcal{H}^2$ factorization solver），首次在十亿未知量规模的三维体积积分方程上实现强递归骨架化（strong recursive skeletonization）分解，将 Laplace 与 Helmholtz 分解时间分别缩短 6.9 倍和 8.5 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强递归骨架化虽能为积分方程与核方法产生的稠密矩阵提供近似直接求解器，但其分布式实现在通信开销与并行度方面存在显著瓶颈，三维情形尤为突出。

Method: 方法结合空间盒着色（spatial box coloring）、延迟 Schur 补更新（delayed Schur complement updates）、流式草图（streamed sketching）驱动的插值分解（interpolative decomposition），以及 BLAS-3 与共享内存内核（shared-memory kernels）。空间着色使每个进程参与各边界阶段，延迟更新与流式草图则降低稠密存储与通信。

Result: 在最多 512 个计算节点上完成 36.2 亿未知量三维 Laplace 系统的分解，并给出三维 Helmholtz 及 2.68 亿未知量二维 Matérn 系统测试。相较未草图化、即时更新（unsketched, eager）配置，流式草图与完全延迟更新的组合使分解时间分别缩短 6.9 倍（Laplace）与 8.5 倍（Helmholtz），单进程最大常驻内存分别降低 57% 与 54%。

Conclusion: 该工作据作者所知为首个在十亿未知量规模的三维体积积分方程上展示的分布式内存强递归骨架化分解，验证了所提通信与存储优化策略的可扩展性。

Abstract: Strong recursive skeletonization provides approximate direct solvers for dense matrices from integral equations and kernel methods, but distributed implementations face substantial communication costs and limited parallelism, especially in three dimensions. We present a distributed-memory $\mathcal{H}^2$ factorization solver for symmetric matrices that combines spatial box coloring, delayed Schur complement updates, streamed sketching-based interpolative decomposition, and BLAS-3 and shared-memory kernels. Spatial coloring enables every process to participate in each boundary stage, while delayed updates and streamed sketching reduce dense storage and communication. To our knowledge, this is the first distributed-memory strong recursive skeletonization factorization demonstrated for three-dimensional volume integral equations at billion-unknown scale.
  Experiments on up to 512 compute nodes demonstrate factorization of a three-dimensional Laplace system with 3.62 billion unknowns, alongside three-dimensional Helmholtz tests and a two-dimensional Matérn system with 268 million unknowns. Combining streamed sketching and fully delayed updates reduces factorization time by $6.9\times$ for Laplace and $8.5\times$ for Helmholtz relative to the unsketched, eager configuration, while reducing maximum per-process resident memory by 57\% and 54\%, respectively.

</details>


### [154] [A linear, second-order, unconditionally energy-stable scalar-auxiliary-variable scheme for the CHQ viscoelastic phase-separation model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30077)
*Ritesh Bhola,Michael te Vrugt*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 该文针对CHQ粘弹性相分离模型提出一种标量辅助变量（SAV）时间离散格式，结合BDF2与相依赖迁移率的二阶外推，实现每步线性求解、精确质量守恒与无条件能量稳定，并在Fourier–Galerkin实现中验证二阶精度与相对Newton–BDF2的效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 参考的Newton–BDF2格式每步需完全非线性求解，计算代价高。作者希望给出一个线性、计算更廉价的替代方案，同时保持可解性、质量守恒与能量稳定性。

Method: 对双势阱采用SAV重构，时间上使用二阶向后差分公式（BDF2），并对所有相依赖迁移率作二阶外推；对所得耦合结构证明每步存在唯一解。

Result: 格式每步线性；质量守恒为离散方程的精确代数恒等式（数值漂移仅源于线性求解器有限容差）；对修正离散能量无条件稳定，并具一步能量耗散恒等式，且该恒等式可推广至滤波变体并附加两个显式非负项。Fourier–Galerkin实现验证能量恒等式在浮点舍入误差内逐步成立、时间二阶精度（含陡界面区）并与独立Newton–BDF2解一致。

Conclusion: 在相同网格分辨率下，SAV方法相对独立Newton–BDF2求解器减少2.5–3.0倍线性求解迭代次数、节省1.7–2.0倍实际耗时，是兼具理论保证与效率的线性替代方案。

Abstract: The CHQ model describes viscoelastic phase separation in a polymer--solvent mixture: a Cahn--Hilliard equation for the polymer volume fraction $φ$ is two-way coupled, through a phase-dependent cross-diffusion flux, to a damped relaxation equation for a scalar bulk elastic stress $q$. We propose a scalar-auxiliary-variable (SAV) time discretisation of this system, combining the SAV reformulation of the double-well potential with a second-order backward differentiation formula (BDF2) and a second-order extrapolation of all phase-dependent mobilities: a linear, computationally cheaper complement to the reference scheme's fully nonlinear Newton solve. The resulting method is (i) linear at every time step, with a coupling structure we prove has exactly one solution at every step, for every choice of time-step size; (ii) mass conserving, exactly, as an algebraic identity of the discrete equations themselves (the only drift ever seen numerically traces to the finite tolerance of the iterative linear solver, not a failure of the conservation property); and (iii) unconditionally stable for a modified discrete energy, via an exact one-step energy--dissipation identity. We show the energy identity extends to this filtered variant as well, with two explicit extra non-negative terms. Hence, the scheme actually producing the physics results is itself provably stable for the same energy. A Fourier--Galerkin implementation verifies that the energy identity holds at every time step to within floating-point round-off. It also confirms second-order temporal accuracy, including in the steep-interface regime, and agreement with an independently implemented nonlinear Newton--BDF2 solver. Compared with that independent nonlinear Newton--BDF2 solver, at equal grid resolution, the SAV method uses $2.5$--$3.0\times$ fewer linear-solver iterations and $1.7$--$2.0\times$ less wall-clock time.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Regular magnetic black holes with anti-de Sitter cores in scalar-tensor gravity with nonlinear electrodynamics: ghost-free construction and axial stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28670)
*Rustam Ibadov,Najibullokhon Shukurullokhon*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文在爱因斯坦框架的标量-张量引力中，結合磁性非线性电动力学，构造了静态、渐近平直的正则黑洞解，并证明其在轴向微扰下模式稳定，同时说明无需事件视界解被角向不稳定性排除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究正则黑洞（regular black holes）在标量-张量引力框架下的可实现性及其稳定性，是检验此类替代引力方案自洽性的关键问题。

Method: 采用重构（reconstruction）方法：预先给定密度与标量轮廓，反解出度规、标量-光子耦合（scalar--photon coupling）与势（potential）；并通过轴向微扰（axial perturbations）化为对称双通道主方程系统作稳定性分析。

Result: 正则性要求中心处质量函数为零从而固定ADM质量，密度中心 r² 增长须由标量梯度能平衡；解具反德西特核（anti-de Sitter core）与近Reissner–Nordström外部；无鬼条件（no-ghost condition）将磁荷限制于一窗口；微扰势正定保证模式稳定，谱方法与时间域准正规模（quasinormal frequencies）一致。

Conclusion: 中心附近的角向拉普拉斯不稳定性（angular Laplacian instability）可被内视界遮蔽，因此排除无视界解；所构造的黑洞解在物理上是自洽且稳定的。

Abstract: We construct static, asymptotically flat regular black holes in Einstein-frame scalar--tensor gravity, sourced by a canonical scalar coupled to magnetic nonlinear electrodynamics. Prescribing the density and scalar profiles, we reconstruct the metric, the scalar--photon coupling and the potential. Regularity requires the mass function to vanish at the centre, which fixes the ADM mass, and the central $r^2$ growth of the density to be balanced by the scalar gradient energy. The solutions have an anti-de Sitter core supported by a negative potential and a nearly Reissner--Nordström exterior; the no-ghost condition confines the magnetic charge to a window that closes at a finite core depth. Axial perturbations reduce to a symmetric two-channel master system whose positive-definite potential guarantees mode stability; spectral and time-domain quasinormal frequencies agree. An angular Laplacian instability near the centre can be hidden behind the inner horizon, which excludes horizonless solutions.

</details>


### [156] [Learning Parameters of dRGT Black Holes in Nonlinear Electrodynamics with Neural Network Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28684)
*Adil Belhaj,Salah Eddine Ennadifi,Maryem Jemri*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文利用机器学习方法，对dRGT型大质量引力（massive gravity）耦合非线性电动力学（nonlinear electrodynamics）的AdS黑洞参数情景进行约束，并以EHT观测数据检验其光学特征的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于通过黑洞阴影（black hole shadow）等光学观测量，对dRGT型大质量引力与非线性电动力学耦合下的AdS黑洞参数空间施加观测约束。

Method: 首先借助高性能并行计算（high performance parallel computations）研究光子球（photon sphere）、黑洞阴影及能量发射率（energy emission rate）；随后构建数值框架，结合事件视界望远镜（Event Horizon Telescope, EHT）的阴影观测约束电荷与非线性电动力学参数；并采用神经网络（neural networks）在1-σ与2-σ置信区间（confidence intervals）内考察参数区域。

Result: 基于与M87*、SgrA*_VLTI及SgrA*_Keck的EHT观测数据的一致性，得到相应的参数约束，结果显示所得约束与SgrA*_VLTI的经验数据（empirical data）相符。

Conclusion: 机器学习结合EHT阴影观测可为dRGT型大质量引力耦合非线性电动力学的AdS黑洞提供有效的参数约束手段，且约束结果与SgrA*_VLTI数据保持一致。

Abstract: Using machine learning methods, we present constraining parameter scenarios of AdS black holes in dRGT-like massive gravity coupled to nonlinear electrodynamics. We first approach the photon sphere, the black hole shadow, and the energy emission rate with the help of high performance parallel computations. To corroborate such optical results, we develop a numerical framework to constrain the electric charge and the nonlinear electrodynamic parameter, for certain gravity regimes, via the shadow observations reported by the Even Horizon Telescope collaboration. Employing neural networks, we then examine the parameter regions of the black holes based on their consistency with EHT observations of M87$^*$, SgrA$^*_{\mathrm{VLTI}}$, and SgrA$^*_{\mathrm{Keck}}$, within the $1-σ$ and $2-σ$ confidence intervals. As a result, we find that the obtained constraints are consistent with the $SgrA^*_{\mathrm{VLTI}}$ empirical data.

</details>


### [157] [Noisy Pipelines I: An end-to-end pipeline to perform multivariate noise inference for LISA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28726)
*Ollie Burke,Joseph Bayley,Charlie Hoy,Laura Nuttall*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 提出基于 JAX 的 LISA 噪声推断管线 lisa-noise，在考虑数据后处理（滤波、降采样、分段、加窗）与不等臂、非恒定臂长等非平稳效应的情况下，给出经后处理修正的 3×3 协方差矩阵谱估计，可直接供全局拟合（global-fit）管线使用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LISA 仪器数据在全局拟合分析前必须经滤波、降采样、分段与加窗等后处理，每一步都会改变噪声过程，因而需要相应修正描述数据流的噪声协方差矩阵（noise covariance matrix），以支持频域中信号与噪声参数的联合推断。

Method: 构建基于 JAX 的噪声推断管线：对底层次级噪声（secondary noises）拟合样条（splines），并拟合 Michelson 通道完整的多元 3×3 协方差矩阵；全程假设臂长不等且非恒定；通过在每个分段内对多个时段（epochs）的传递函数（transfer functions）取平均，刻画其零点漂移这一非平稳效应。

Result: 在 30 天条件化模拟数据上，7 天分段的单次似然评估在当前笔记本上耗时约 3 ms；从紧先验出发的梯度拟合可在不到一秒内恢复注入谱；在以模拟真值为中心、范围 ±10（log10）的大先验上进行多起点搜索，可由贝叶斯方法验证得到与数据一致的噪声估计；拟合协方差可在各频段及传递函数所有零点处白化（whiten）条件化数据。

Conclusion: lisa-noise 能够给出经后处理效应修正的噪声谱估计，其拟合协方差以全局拟合管线可直接使用的形式实现数据白化，为 LISA 数据分析中的噪声参数推断提供高效可行的方案。

Abstract: Instrumental data from the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA) must be post-processed prior to any global-fit based analysis. For example, it is critical to filter, downsample, segment and window the data in order to perform inference of both the signal and noise parameters in the frequency domain. Each step alters the noise process, requiring a correction to the noise covariance matrix that describes the underlying data stream. We present $\texttt{lisa-noise}$, a JAX-based noise inference pipeline that returns a spectral estimate of the noise process subject to post-processing effects. We fit splines to the underlying secondary noises and fit the full multivariate $3 \times 3$ covariance matrix of the Michelson channels. We assume unequal and non-constant arm-lengths throughout. The drift of the zeros of the transfer functions, a non-stationary effect, is captured by averaging the transfer functions over a number of epochs within each segment. On 30 days of conditioned simulated data, a likelihood evaluation costs $3\,$ms on a current laptop for a 7 day segment. A gradient-based fit from a tight prior recovers the injected spectra in a fraction of a second. A multi-start search over large priors $\pm 10$ in $\log_{10}$ centred on the simulated truth obtains a noise estimate consistent with the data, verified by Bayesian techniques. The fitted covariance whitens the conditioned data in every band and through every zero of the transfer functions, in a form that global-fit pipelines can use directly.

</details>


### [158] [Lamb shift of rotational energy levels in quantum gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28754)
*Oliver Benz,Marius Krumm,Wolfgang Wieland*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 论文提出将量子化度规微扰与各向异性量子刚体耦合，得到类似凝聚态中angulon准粒子激发的引力类比模型，并导出引力Lamb位移，作为检验引力是否量子化的潜在实验判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子引力（quantum gravity）直接检验所需能标过高，研究重心转向近期可行的有效或唯象模型预测，如引力诱导纠缠（gravitationally induced entanglement）及线性化引力与非相对论测量装置的相互作用。作者直觉上认为，足够重的刚体由极多组分构成，可在单原子尺度可忽略的量子引力效应经由组分数目被放大。

Method: 将量子化度规微扰（quantized metric perturbation）与各向异性量子刚体（anisotropic quantum rigid rotor）耦合，构建有效模型；该模型对应于凝聚态物理中angulon准粒子激发的引力类比，并据此计算转子能量本征态被虚引力子云（virtual gravitons） dress 后的引力Lamb位移。

Result: 推导出转子能量本征态的引力Lamb位移（gravitational Lamb shift），该效应源自本征态被虚引力子云修饰。效应量级极小，但作者认为其可为引力是否真正量子化提供具体检验途径。

Conclusion: 该工作给出引力类比angulon的有效框架并指路未来类比实验；引力Lamb位移虽微弱，仍被视为判别引力量子性的潜在实验判据。

Abstract: One of the major challenges of modern foundational physics is the combination of quantum physics and general relativity into a theory of quantum gravity. A severe obstacle are the required extremely high energy scales for direct tests of quantum gravity effects. In recent years, attention has shifted towards near-future experimental predictions of effective or phenomenological models, such as gravitationally induced entanglement or interactions of linearized gravity with non-relativistic measurement devices. These developments motivate us to study the coupling between the quantized metric perturbation and an anisotropic quantum rigid rotor. The intuitive motivation for our study is that a sufficiently heavy rotor consists of a very large number of component systems. Although quantum gravitational effects are completely negligible at the scale of a single atom or molecule, the effects can get amplified by the sheer number of constituents. The resulting effective model describes the gravitational analogue of the angulon quasiparticle excitations discussed in condensed matter physics, suggesting a pathway for future analogue experiments. As a first concrete test of our model, we derive the gravitational Lamb shift for the energy eigenstates of the rotor, which become dressed by a cloud of virtual gravitons. Although the effect is very small, it could provide a concrete test for whether gravity is truly quantum.

</details>


### [159] [Fields on accelerating and super accelerating black holes: stability, quasinormal modes, greybody factors and strong cosmic censorship](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28796)
*Francisco Silva*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文研究C-度规描述的加速与超加速黑洞时空中无质量标量场、狄拉克场与麦克斯韦场的传播，推导准正规模谱与灰体因子，并检验强宇宙监督猜想。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超加速黑洞（super accelerating black hole）时空为近期才被识别、尚缺乏探索的体系，其场传播性质与线性稳定性、模式稳定性（mode stability）尚不清楚。

Method: 在C-度规（C-metric）背景下，于多种时空极限中解析推导准正规模（quasinormal mode）谱与灰体因子（greybody factor），对准正规模族进行分类，并通过Christodoulou β判据检验强宇宙监督（strong cosmic censorship）。

Result: 获得准正规模谱与灰体因子的解析表达式，并识别出超加速区域中出现的新准正规模族；发现参数空间中强宇宙监督可能被违反的区域，包括超加速情形。

Conclusion: 强宇宙监督违反可能发生于无质量标量场与麦克斯韦场，而狄拉克场（Dirac field）保持该猜想成立。

Abstract: We investigate the propagation of massless scalar, Dirac, and Maxwell fields on accelerating and super accelerating black hole spacetimes described by the C-metric. The latter regime has only recently been identified and remains largely unexplored. We study the linear and mode stability of these backgrounds and derive analytical expressions for the quasinormal mode spectra and greybody factors in various spacetime limits. We classify the quasinormal mode families, paying particular attention to new families arising in the super accelerating regime. We also investigate strong cosmic censorship through Christodoulou's $β$ criterion, finding regions of parameter space where it may be violated, including in the super accelerating regime. These violations are possible for massless scalar and Maxwell fields, whereas Dirac fields preserve the conjecture.

</details>


### [160] [Harmonic branches of Einstein--Maxwell--scalar field gravity: exact solutions and regularity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29089)
*Leonel Bixano,Tonatiuh Matos*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在 Einstein–Maxwell–dilaton 引力的调和分支几何框架下，统一重构了黑洞视界与虫洞喉部解。通过先求解调和方程再联合重构度规、Maxwell 四势与标量场，再现了 Schwarzschild、Reissner–Nordström（含 NUT 延拓）、Gibbons–Maeda、双子弦及 Kaluza–Klein 解；当标量部门取 phantom 符号时得到 Ellis–Bronnikov 虫洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于将黑洞视界（black-hole horizons）与虫洞喉部（wormhole throats）纳入同一几何描述，为电荷关系与正则性约束提供统一的几何解读。

Method: 采用统一的 Ernst 框架（Ernst framework）：先求解调和方程（harmonic equations）再固定空间调和函数，联合重构度规、Maxwell 四势与标量场；以守恒测地线能量固定子午 Weyl 求积（Weyl quadrature），初始测地线数据给出渐近荷。选取 Schwarzschild 调和函数，由正则非极端视界确定归一化 H=1 及物质场正则性要求。

Result: 该构造再现了 Schwarzschild 与 Reissner–Nordström 族（含 NUT 延拓）、Gibbons–Maeda 解、双子弦（dyonic string）与 Kaluza–Klein 解，并保留其更一般的分支；标量部门取 phantom 符号时，光滑延拓选取 H=-1，得到大质量与超静态（ultrastatic）Ellis–Bronnikov 虫洞。

Conclusion: 通过与真空环几何（vacuum ring geometry）比较，强调了整体延拓（global extension）的进一步重要性；整体给出重构解之电荷关系与正则性约束的统一黑洞–虫洞几何解读。

Abstract: We relate black-hole horizons and wormhole throats to the geometry of the harmonic branches of Einstein--Maxwell--dilaton gravity. In the unified Ernst framework, we first solve the harmonic equations prior to fixing the spatial harmonic, and then jointly reconstruct the metric, Maxwell four-potential, and scalar field. The conserved geodesic energy fixes the meridional Weyl quadrature, whereas the initial geodesic data capture the asymptotic charges. Choosing the Schwarzschild harmonic function, regular nonextremal horizons fix the normalization \(H=1\) along with the associated matter-field regularity requirements.
