This reproduces the Schwarzschild and Reissner-Nordstrom families (including the NUT extension), the Gibbons-Maeda solution, and the dyonic string and Kaluza--Klein solutions, while also preserving their more general branches. If the scalar sector is continued to the phantom sign, one obtains a complementary oblate construction: smooth continuation selects \(H=-1\) and produces the massive and ultrastatic Ellis-Bronnikov wormholes. By comparing with the vacuum ring geometry, we make clear the further importance of the global extension. Altogether, these findings offer a unified black-hole--wormhole geometric reading of the charge relations and regularity constraints for the reconstructed solutions.

</details>


### [161] [Wormholes in Vegh--dRGT massive gravity: a null-energy transparency theorem, horizon obstruction, and exact isotropic solutions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29179)
*Sudan Hansraj,Ayan Banerjee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在 de Rham–Gabadadze–Tolley 大质量引力（massive gravity）中采用 Vegh 简并参考度规时，大质量引力子（massive graviton）提供的有效流体对径向光线的零能量条件（null energy condition）不可见，因此喉部仍须由物质本身违反零能量条件，纠正了文献中的相关论断；并给出两类各向同性压强虫洞（wormhole）的精确解及其薄壳（thin shell）不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大质量引力虫洞文献中广泛存在一个论断：引力子项可替代奇异物质以维持喉部（throat）。本文旨在检验该论断，并厘清在 Vegh 参考度规下喉部可以出现的位置。

Method: 考虑静态球对称（static spherically symmetric）几何，考察空间简并的 Vegh 参考度规类，对全部四种相互作用势逐项分析大质量引力子所携带有效流体在密度与径向压强组合中的贡献，并结合 flare-out 条件与径向零能量条件进行判定；随后以各向同性压强的理想流体（perfect fluid）求精确解。

Result: 该不可见性对全部简并参考度规、四种相互作用势与任意静态球对称几何均成立，并唯一地把 Vegh 选择标定为唯一自洽选项。各向同性压强虫洞分为两族：一族位于该理论自身真空的宇宙学视界（cosmological horizon）之外，无静态外部且在大引力下为空、由引力子耦合开启；另一族位于视界之内，需要陡降的红移。第二族中引力子项可将喉部张力转为普通压强；薄壳可闭合构型，其表面应力与奇异物质含量有闭式表达，且壳层径向不稳定，喉部附近光线呈不稳定圆轨道。

Conclusion: 在这一设定下，引力子项无法免除对零能量条件违反的需求，物质本身必须在每个喉部违反零能量条件，与广义相对论（general relativity）情形一致；所构造的两族解均存在径向不稳定性。

Abstract: We study static, spherically symmetric wormholes in deRham--Gabadadze--Tolley massive gravity with the spatially degenerate Vegh reference metric. The effective fluid carried by the massive graviton contributes nothing to the combination of density and radial pressure that the flare-out condition constrains, so it is invisible to the null energy condition along radial light rays. This holds for the whole degenerate class of reference metrics, for all four interaction potentials, and for any static spherically symmetric geometry; the same property singles out the Vegh choice as the only consistent one. The matter alone must therefore violate the null energy condition at every throat, exactly as in general relativity. This corrects a claim repeated across the massive-gravity wormhole literature. We then determine where throats may sit. Wormholes with isotropic pressure fall into two families. One lies beyond the cosmological horizon of the theory's own vacuum and has no static exterior; it is empty in general relativity and is switched on by the graviton coupling. The other lies inside that horizon and needs a steeply falling redshift. We give exact perfect-fluid solutions for both. In the second family the graviton term can turn the tension at the throat into ordinary pressure. A thin shell closes the configuration, and its surface stresses and its exotic-matter content follow in closed form. The shells are radially unstable, and light rays near the throat trace an unstable circular orbit.

</details>


### [162] [How general relativity stacks up: Testing gravity by coherently combining gravitational-wave observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29247)
*Lachlan Passenger,Nir Guttman,Paul D. Lasky,Eric Thrane*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一个群体层级（population-level）框架，将多个双星并合事件的数据相干结合，用于检验广义相对论（general relativity, GR）之外的物理；应用于LVK第五星表（Fifth Gravitational-Wave Transient Catalog）28个高信噪比双黑洞并合事件，未发现偏离GR的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波天文学（gravitational-wave astronomy）的核心任务之一是在高曲率、高速度区域检验GR，但如何联合一族双星并合（binary merger）的数据仍是待解难题；联合分析既可提升潜在GR偏离的显著性，也可刻画偏离在不同并合事件中的现象学表现。

Method: 构建将引力波测量相干组合的群体层级检验框架，基于三条假设：(i) 偏离在并合（merger）附近最强，(ii) 随双星总质量平滑变化，(iii) 在 ℓ=|m|=2 模式中最显著；该框架可展示偏离如何随系统总质量变化。

Result: 对LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA第五引力波瞬变星表中28个高信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio）双黑洞并合事件的分析未发现偏离GR的证据；框架能够给出偏离随总质量变化的可能形式。

Conclusion: 作者认为，这种质量依赖的重构可用于令人信服地区分波形系统误差（systematic waveform errors）与真实的GR违反，为更普适的框架提供合理起点。

Abstract: A core mission of gravitational-wave astronomy is to test general relativity (GR) in the high-curvature, high-velocity regime. The field has already seen significant progress in this space, though a remaining challenge is how to combine data from a population of binary mergers. This can both boost the significance of potential GR violations, and develop a phenomenological picture of how deviations manifest in different mergers. We propose a framework to coherently combine gravitational-wave measurements to build a population-level test of physics beyond GR. We assume that deviations from GR are (i) strongest near the merger, (ii) vary smoothly with the total mass of the binary, and (iii) are most prominent in the $\ell=|m|=2$ mode. While these assumptions do not describe every possible deviation of GR, they provide us with a plausible starting point upon which a more general framework can be built. We apply our framework to 28 high signal-to-noise ratio binary black hole mergers from LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA's Fifth Gravitational-Wave Transient Catalog, finding no evidence for deviations from GR. Our framework allows one to show how deviations could vary as a function of total mass of the system. We argue that such a reconstruction can be used to convincingly distinguish between systematic waveform errors and genuine GR violations.

</details>


### [163] [Tomimatsu--Sato $δ=2$ Solution in Kaluza--Klein Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29277)
*Haryanto M. Siahaan,Masoud Ghezelbash*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 通过五维提升—沿紧致方向加速—约化，构造了耦合常数 \(a=\sqrt3\) 的 Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton 理论中 Tomimatsu-Sato \(\delta=2\) 真空时空的 Kaluza-Klein 电修饰解，给出其多极矩精确律、近视界几何与电荷分布特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已知的 Einstein-Maxwell 带电与磁化 Tomimatsu-Sato 解不含膨胀子（dilaton）；本文旨在构造带有非平凡膨胀子的 Kaluza-Klein 电修饰解，并考察 \(\delta=2\) 四极矩超出在加速下的行为及其几何与因果结构后果。

Method: 将 Tomimatsu-Sato \(\delta=2\) 真空时空提升到五维，沿紧致方向作加速（boost），再约化到耦合 \(a=\sqrt3\) 的 Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton 理论；采用 Mayerson 的非真空 Geroch-Hansen 多极矩框架计算四极矩，并在 Kodama-Hikida 图像下分析 Weyl 段、视界、电荷通量与遍历面（ergosurface）。

Result: 领头阶上该解与加速 Kerr 黑洞共享质量—电荷—膨胀子关系及旋磁比 \(g=2-\tanh^2\alpha\)，种子差异首次出现在四极矩；得到精确关系 \(M_2(\alpha)=\tfrac12(1+\cosh^2\alpha)M_2^{(0)}\)，故 \(\delta=2\) 的四极矩超出 \(\Delta M_2=-\tfrac14p^2m^2M\) 在任何加速下都保留。视界保持退化且非旋转，其面积与 Komar 角动量按 \(\cosh\alpha\) 缩放，电势为 \(\tfrac12\tanh\alpha\)；视界与环奇点为不带电，电荷全部由杆（rod）承载，并满足 \(M-\Phi_eQ=m\)。在 \(f=\coth^2\alpha\) 处出现新的曲率奇异边界（该处 Kaluza-Klein 圆变为类光），且对任意非零加速均存在，因此 TS\(_2\) 的任何 Kaluza-Klein 加速都不是处处类空的约化。

Conclusion: 该 Kaluza-Klein 电修饰 TS\(_2\) 解保留了种子的四极矩超出，其近视界几何为种子几何的常数重标度，电荷集中于杆而非视界或环奇点；加速对环附近的因果性破坏具有点wise增强效应，并必然引入紧致切除区域。

Abstract: We construct the Kaluza-Klein electric dressing of the Tomimatsu-Sato $δ=2$ vacuum spacetime by uplifting to five dimensions, boosting along the compact direction and reducing to Einstein-Maxwell-dilaton theory with coupling $a=\sqrt3$. It carries a nontrivial dilaton, unlike the known Einstein-Maxwell charged and magnetized Tomimatsu-Sato solutions. At leading order it shares the mass-charge-dilaton relations and gyromagnetic ratio $g=2-\tanh^2α$ of the boosted Kerr black hole; the seed first shows up in the quadrupole. Using Mayerson's non-vacuum Geroch-Hansen multipoles, we derive the exact law $M_2(α)=\frac12(1+\cosh^2α)M_2^{(0)}$, so the $δ=2$ quadrupole excess $ΔM_2=-\frac14p^2m^2M$ survives every boost. In the Kodama-Hikida picture the Weyl segment $ρ=0$, $|z|<κ$ is a singular rod with degenerate Killing horizons at its endpoints. There the boost function equals $\coshα$, so the near-horizon geometry is a constant rescaling of the seed's: the horizons stay degenerate and non-rotating, their areas and Komar angular momenta scale by $\coshα$, and the electric potential is $\frac12\tanhα$. The electric flux through any closed surface is $\sinhα\coshα$ times the enclosed seed Komar mass, so the horizons and the ring singularity are uncharged and the rod carries all the charge; the boost-invariant identity $M-Φ_eQ=m$ follows. The ergosurface stays at $f=0$; a new curvature-singular boundary appears at $f=\coth^2α$, where the Kaluza-Klein circle becomes null. As the seed potential $f$ is unbounded above near the ring, it exists for every nonzero boost: unlike for Kerr, no Kaluza-Klein boost of TS$_2$ is a spacelike reduction everywhere. The excised region is compact, has the ring on its boundary and never reaches the horizons. Charging pointwise strengthens the causality violation near the rod.

</details>


### [164] [Orientation-dependent sensitivity of the all-sky all-frequency gravitational-wave radiometer search to continuous-wave sources](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29384)
*Stavros Venikoudis,Giacomo Bruno*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文分析了全天全天候（all-sky all-frequency, ASAF）引力波辐射计搜索在探测连续波（continuous-wave, CW）源时的偏振投影效应，证明投影因子 μ² 对各向同性平均恰为 2/5，并量化了该效应对信噪比与贝叶斯上限的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ASAF 搜索对 +、× 两种张量偏振（tensor polarization）等权交叉相关，仅对无偏振随机背景（unpolarized stochastic background）最优；而连续波源的偏振取决于倾角 ι 与偏振角 ψ，非正对（face-on）观测时会低估源强度，需系统刻画该偏差。

Method: 推导投影因子 μ²(ι,ψ) 进入 ASAF 统计量的方式及其对 ψ 的精确依赖，并证明其对取向的平均恒为 2/5；随后将形式应用于注入 O2 运行期 LIGO 探测器的四个模拟脉冲星信号（偏振从圆偏振到近线偏振，分布于四个天区位置）。

Result: 近侧视（nearly edge-on）注入的信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio, SNR）为 5.20，低于阈值 6.19，而同源正对观测的 SNR 可达 31.1 ± 6.0；对 h₀ 的贝叶斯上限为正对情形的 2.7–2.9 倍。对 O4a 的 505 个亚阈候选体，上限提高 2.5–2.9 倍，同强度源仅在偏离正对 34.7°–50.3° 以内才可达阈值。

Conclusion: 偏振投影显著降低 ASAF 对连续波源的探测灵敏度并抬高振幅上限，但各候选体的实测 SNR 与显著性均不受影响；结果提示需在解释上限时纳入取向边缘化（marginalization）与投影修正。

Abstract: The all-sky all-frequency (ASAF) gravitational-wave radiometer search cross-correlates the data of two detectors with equal weight on the $+$ and $\times$ tensor polarizations. This is optimal for an unpolarized stochastic background, but not for continuous-wave (CW) sources, whose polarization depends on the inclination angle $ι$ and the polarization angle $ψ$. Unless the source is seen face-on, the search measures less power than a face-on source with the same intrinsic amplitude, and underestimates its strength. The ratio of the two powers, the projection factor $μ^2(ι,ψ)$, is known from earlier radiometer studies. We show how $μ^2$ enters the ASAF statistic, derive its exact dependence on $ψ$, and prove that its average over orientations is exactly $2/5$, independent of sky position, baseline geometry, noise weighting, and frequency. We apply the formalism to four simulated pulsar signals at four sky positions, with polarization from circular to nearly linear, injected into the LIGO detectors during the second observing run (O2). A nearly edge-on injection is measured with a signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) of $5.20$, below the threshold of $6.19$, while the same signal seen face-on would have an SNR of $31.1 \pm 6.0$. Bayesian upper limits on the strain amplitude $h_0$, marginalized over orientation and calibration uncertainty, are $2.7$ to $2.9$ times the face-on values. For the $505$ sub-threshold candidates of the first part of the fourth LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA observing run (O4a), the upper limits increase by a factor of $2.5$ to $2.9$, and a source of the same strength would reach threshold only when inclined by less than $34.7^{\circ}$ to $50.3^{\circ}$ from face-on. The measured SNR and the significance of every candidate are unchanged.

</details>


### [165] [An end-to-end function for the photograph of a Schwarzschild black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29437)
*Ping Li,Jun Cheng,Jiang-he Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文给出史瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild black hole）照片的闭式（closed-form）表达，将像平面取为与以黑洞为中心之球面相切的平面，并把源点与像点之间的映射视为解析对象（称为“照片函数”），从而在不求解中间零测地线（null geodesics）的情况下得到显式结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统黑洞成像需追踪零测地线（null geodesics），计算繁复且不易直接读出像的结构性质；本文旨在以解析映射的形式直接刻画黑洞照片，使多重分支结构等特征可读。

Method: 把像平面嵌入为与球面相切的平面，将源与其像之间的对应关系抽象为“照片函数（photograph function）”并作为解析对象处理；分别对无穷远处天球（celestial sphere）上的源与赤道面（equatorial plane）内的源给出显式表达式，且不需求解中间零测地线。

Result: 获得精确（exact）构造与显式表达式，使多值分支结构（multivalued branch structure）、与光子球（photon sphere）及阴影边界（shadow boundary）的关系、以及自相似的高阶像（self-similar higher-order images）均可直接读出。

Conclusion: 黑洞照片可由闭式解析映射完整描述；该构造为精确结果，为理解光子球、阴影边界与高阶像的自相似层级提供直观且可解析的框架。

Abstract: We show that the photograph of a Schwarzschild black hole can be written in closed form. The image plane is embedded as a plane tangent to a sphere centred on the black hole, and the map between a point source and its image point, which we call the photograph function, is treated as the analytic object. Explicit expressions are obtained for sources on the celestial sphere at infinity and for sources in the equatorial plane, without solving for the intermediate null geodesics. The construction is exact, and it makes the multivalued branch structure, the relation to the photon sphere and to the shadow boundary, and the self-similar higher-order images directly readable.

</details>


### [166] [SpinOctopus: A Versatile, User-Friendly, and Extensible Public Code for General-Relativistic Ray-Tracing in axisymmetric spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29800)
*Shiyang Hu,Dan Li,Chen Deng,Lina Zhang,Wenfu Cao*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: SpinOctopus 是一个面向任意轴对称时空的相对论性光线追踪（relativistic ray-tracing）代码，采用 Fortran/Python 混合架构与 OpenMP 多线程加速，可模拟类时轨道、零测地线及多种吸积源照明下的黑洞图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论性光线追踪算法是研究引力理论与高能天体物理（high-energy astrophysics）不可或缺的数值工具，论文旨在提供一个适用于任意轴对称时空的多功能、高效且易用的通用代码。

Method: 开发并发布 SpinOctopus，基于 Fortran/Python 混合架构与 OpenMP 多线程加速；用户仅需在配置文件中指定目标时空的协变度规张量（covariant metric tensor）与模拟参数，并采用定制的测地线积分自适应步长策略（adaptive step-size strategy）。

Result: 该工具可模拟类时轨道（timelike orbits）及其光变曲线、零测地线（null geodesics），以及由几何薄/厚吸积盘、倾斜薄盘、相对论喷流（relativistic jets）、球对称吸积流和点源等多种现象学源照明的黑洞图像；在 Intel Core i7-14700 平台上模拟几何厚吸积盘图像时，效率约为每线程每秒 800 条光线。

Conclusion: SpinOctopus 提供透明接口以便用户修改光源的几何与运动学性质，后续版本将进一步支持 Fe Kα 荧光谱线轮廓及几何厚倾斜吸积盘图像等能力。

Abstract: Relativistic ray-tracing algorithms serve as indispensable numerical tools for investigating gravitational theories and high-energy astrophysics. In this paper, we develop and release SpinOctopus, a versatile relativistic ray-tracing code tailored for arbitrary axisymmetric spacetimes, powered by a Fortran/Python hybrid architecture and OpenMP multi-threading acceleration. This numerical toolkit is capable of simulating timelike orbits and their corresponding light curves, null geodesics, as well as black hole images illuminated by a wide array of phenomenological sources, including geometrically thin and thick accretion disks, geometrically thin tilted disks, relativistic jets, spherically symmetric accretion flows, and point sources. To execute these tasks, users are solely required to specify the covariant metric tensor of the target spacetime and the simulation parameters within a dedicated configuration file. Benefiting from our customized adaptive step-size strategy for geodesic integration, SpinOctopus achieves an impressive processing efficiency of $\sim 800$ rays per second per thread on an Intel Core i7-14700 platform when simulating geometrically thick accretion disk images. Furthermore, the code provides transparent interfaces, allowing users to seamlessly modify the geometric and kinematic properties of the light sources. Additional capabilities, such as simulating Fe K$α$ fluorescent line profiles and geometrically thick tilted accretion disk images, will be introduced in future releases of the framework.

</details>


### [167] [Kinematic and Dynamic Parameters of Anisotropic Models with Inflaton, Without A Priori Relation Between the Scale Factors and with A Posteriori Isotropization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29827)
*Maressa Perovano,Wesley Spalanza,Breno Barreto da Silva,Sergio Vitorino de Borba Gonçalves,Humberto Belich*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统综述均匀且各向异性宇宙学模型，重点研究Kantowski-Sachs模型，给出以一般物质形式表达的场方程精确解，并考察标量场情形及模型的运动学与演化特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究常预设标度因子间的固定关系，可能掩盖各向异性模型的真实演化行为；本文旨在不设先验条件的情况下检验Kantowski-Sachs模型的动力学及其各向同性化过程。

Method: 对均匀各向异性宇宙学模型进行详细综述；推导以宇宙物质一般形式表示的场方程精确解，得到能量密度表达式，并研究含标量场（scalar field）的具体情形。在Raychaudhuri方程（Raychaudhuri equation）框架下计算标量膨胀Θ、剪切标量σ、旋转参数ω及Raychaudhuri标量等运动学参数，同时分析Hubble、密度与减速参数；标度因子（scale factors）自由演化，不施加先验约束。

Result: 获得了一般物质情形下的能量密度表达式及标量场情景的解；模型演化动力学由Hubble、密度和减速参数刻画；在所研究的设定中，各向同性性质迅速显现，最终趋于传统的各向同性模型。

Conclusion: Kantowski-Sachs模型在标度因子自由演化条件下会快速实现各向同性化，表明无需预设标度因子关系即可自然回归标准各向同性宇宙学图景。

Abstract: In this work, we present a detailed review of the calculations concerning homogeneous and anisotropic cosmological models, with particular emphasis on the Kantowski-Sachs cosmological model. As an innovative contribution, we derive an exact solution for the field equations, expressed in terms of a general form for the material content of the universe. We obtain the expressions for the energy density in this general case, as well as investigate the specific scenario involving the presence of a scalar field. We examine the evolutionary dynamics of the model that encompasses Hubble, density, and deceleration parameters. Additionally, we calculate the kinematic parameters associated with the model, namely: the scalar expansion Θ, the shear scalar σ, the rotation parameter ω, and the parameter Rα\b{eta}UαU\b{eta}, also known as the Raychaudhuri scalar or convergence condition, all of these parameters expressed within the framework of the Raychaudhuri equation. Instead of employing a preestablished relation between the scale factors of the model, we allow them to evolve freely, without a priori conditions. Thus, we can verify that the isotropic nature of the studied scenario manifests itself rapidly, leading to the traditional isotropic model.

</details>


### [168] [Efficient Eccentric and Quasicircular Effective-One-Body Waveforms via Near-Identity Averaging Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29856)
*Philip Lynch,Alessandra Buonanno,Aldo Gamboa,Maarten van de Meent*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将偏心有效单体（effective-one-body, EOB）动力学改用以偏心率矢量笛卡尔分量表述的演化方程，消除圆轨道极限的奇异性，并通过径向谐波查表快速合成波形，在中后绝热阶实现∼10^-8量级失配与3–300倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代引力波探测器（LISA、爱因斯坦望远镜、宇宙探路者）需要长时、偏心黑洞双星信号的高精度高效模型；既有基于偏心率与相对论近点角的振动轨道根数（osculating orbital elements）平均化方案在圆轨道极限奇异，排除准圆双星与偏心旋进的晚期阶段，且波形生成成本将总加速限制在1.5–8倍。

Method: 以偏心率矢量的笛卡尔分量替代偏心率与相对论近点角，使平均化方程在0 ≤ e < 1全范围正则；并将波形模式分解为径向谐波（radial harmonics），其系数作为平均变量的函数预先制表，从而在输出时间网格上以每采样固定成本直接组装波形。

Result: 在地面探测器可观测的双星参数空间（现含圆轨道）上与完整EOB演化对比验证；二阶后绝热阶（second postadiabatic order）下失配中位数约10^-8、最大1.1×10^-5，相对串行EOB波形单核加速3–100倍、八核最高300倍。

Conclusion: 所提表述消除了圆轨道奇异性并显著提升波形生成效率，验证了平均化加查表的有效性；后续计划将该技术推广至最先进的SEOBNR模型。

Abstract: Next-generation gravitational-wave detectors, such as LISA, the Einstein Telescope, and Cosmic Explorer, will require accurate and efficient models of long-lived, eccentric black-hole binary signals. In previous work, we reduced the cost of evolving nonspinning eccentric effective-one-body dynamics by recasting the equations of motion in terms of osculating orbital elements and applying near-identity averaging transformations to remove the fast orbital-timescale structure during the inspiral. However, the parametrization in terms of the eccentricity and relativistic anomaly becomes singular in the circular-orbit limit, excluding quasicircular binaries and the late stages of eccentric inspirals. Moreover, the cost of generating the waveform from the averaged dynamics limited the overall speed-up to a factor of $1.5-8$. In this work, we replace the eccentricity and relativistic anomaly by the Cartesian components of the eccentricity vector, in which the averaged equations are regular for all eccentricities $0 \leq e < 1$. We also decompose the waveform modes into radial harmonics whose coefficients are tabulated as functions of the averaged variables, so that the waveform is assembled directly on the output time grid at a fixed cost per sample. We validate the method against the full effective-one-body evolution across the parameter space of binaries observable by ground-based detectors, now including circular orbits. At second postadiabatic order, the waveforms reach median mismatches of $\sim 10^{-8}$ with a maximum of $1.1 \times 10^{-5}$, while achieving speed-ups of $3-100 \times$ on a single core or up to $300 \times$ on eight cores against the serial EOB waveform. Given the speed-ups demonstrated here, we plan to apply these techniques to state-of-the-art SEOBNR models in future work.

</details>


### [169] [Semiclassical effects on ultramassive spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29857)
*Valentin Boyanov,Luis J. Garay,Alberto García Martín-Caro,Gerardo García-Moreno*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在既有“超质量时空（ultramassive spacetimes）”结果基础上，首次探讨半经典引力（semiclassical gravity）效应对该几何的影响，考察霍金辐射（Hawking radiation）的命运及Polyakov近似下重整化应力能量张量（renormalized stress-energy tensor）的行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明，史瓦西-德西特黑洞（Schwarzschild-de Sitter black hole）吸积物质可越过临界极限，使事件视界与宇宙视界合并，转变为类似Kantowski-Sachs宇宙的坍缩宇宙；此类“超质量时空”中未来渐近无穷与黑洞事件视界均不复存在，其半经典层面的性质尚待研究。

Method: 在超质量时空背景下分析霍金辐射的演化，并在Polyakov近似（Polyakov approximation）框架内研究重整化应力能量张量的行为，同时考察半经典反作用（semiclassical backreaction）对视界演化的影响，重点关注接近极端极限（extremal limit）的情形及随后的普适坍缩（universal collapse）。

Result: 摘要未给出具体定量结果，仅表明研究围绕霍金辐射在该类背景中的命运、重整化应力能量张量的行为，以及半经典反作用驱动下的视界演化与普适坍缩展开。

Conclusion: 该工作是对超质量时空半经典引力效应的初步探索，为理解视界合并后几何的量子修正与坍缩演化提供了起点，但摘要未给出明确结论性论断。

Abstract: A recent work [Senovilla, Port. Math. 80 (2023)] has shown that accretion of matter by a Schwarzschild-de Sitter black hole can take this object beyond a critical limit where the event and cosmological horizons coalesce, transforming it into a collapsing universe resembling a Kantowski-Sachs cosmology. In these configurations, dubbed ``ultramassive spacetimes'', future asymptotic infinity and the event horizon associated with the black hole cease to exist. In this work, we take the first steps toward exploring semiclassical gravity effects on such geometries. In particular, we examine the fate of Hawking radiation in these backgrounds and analyze the behavior of the renormalized stress-energy tensor within the Polyakov approximation. We also explore the effect of semiclassical backreaction on the evolution of the horizons, focusing on cases in the vicinity of the extremal limit, and on the subsequent universal collapse.

</details>


### [170] [A Broadband Room-Temperature Design for the Einstein Telescope](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29863)
*Teng Zhang,Amit Singh Ubhi,Huan Yang,Alan V. Cumming,Mark Barton,Angus Bell,Fabio Bergamin,Giacomo Bruno,Katherine L. Dooley,Johannes Eichholz,Mariana Fazio,Aaron Goodwin-Jones,James Hough,Keiko Kokeyama,Haixing Miao,Xingrui Peng,Geraint Pratten,Stuart Reid,Sheila Rowan,Patricia Schmidt,Alberto Vecchio,Giles Hammond,Hartmut Grote,Denis Martynov*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出一种全新的全室温、全共振、单干涉仪架构，用于爱因斯坦望远镜（Einstein Telescope, ET），在无需低温与异频（xylophone）分频配置的前提下，实现优于传统ET设计方案的宽频段灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ET的地下选址虽能抑制地震与重力梯度噪声，但其基准设计依赖低温与室温干涉仪组合的异频配置以覆盖宽频段；该方案受垂直悬挂热噪声、制冷机振动及失谐光学配置影响，技术复杂度高。

Method: 采用成熟的熔融石英（fused-silica）悬挂技术、新型光学镀膜与惯性隔振（inertial isolation）方案，论证4米长熔融石英光纤可满足悬挂热噪声要求；并通过干涉仪、测试质量悬挂、光学地震仪（optical seismometer）与量子态的设计，控制振动、光学刚度（optical rigidity）及量子反作用噪声（quantum back-action noise）。

Result: 该全室温、全共振、单干涉仪架构可应对低频灵敏度的各项技术挑战，并获得优于传统ET异频配置的宽频段灵敏度。

Conclusion: 无需低温与多干涉仪组合，仅凭成熟的室温技术与合理的光学与悬挂设计，即可实现ET目标级别的宽频段引力波探测灵敏度。

Abstract: The unique underground location of the Einstein Telescope (ET) is expected to substantially suppress seismic and gravity-gradient noise, providing a favorable environment for gravitational-wave detection at low frequencies compared with surface-based observatories. The baseline ET design aims to achieve broadband sensitivity through a xylophone configuration, in which separate cryogenic and room-temperature interferometers are optimised for low- and high-frequency gravitational-wave detection. However, achieving the target low-frequency sensitivity introduces substantial technical complexity caused by vertical suspension thermal noise, vibrations of the cryocooler, and detuned optical configuration. In this paper, we demonstrate an alternative, entirely room-temperature, fully resonant, single-interferometer architecture that addresses these challenges and achieves broadband sensitivity superior to that of the conventional ET xylophone configuration. Using mature fused-silica suspension technology, recent advances in optical coatings, and novel inertial isolation, we show that 4-m-long fused-silica fibres can meet the required suspension thermal-noise performance. We further demonstrate that vibrations, optical rigidity, and quantum back-action noise can be controlled through appropriate design of the interferometer, test mass suspensions, optical seismometers, and quantum states.

</details>


### [171] [Rotation slows the instability of Ellis-Bronnikov wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29941)
*Nikita M. Shirokov,Ilya Nachevsky*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文对旋转的Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞（wormhole）进行了非线性3+1数值演化，构建旋转"排水孔"（drainhole）稳态解并研究其稳定性。结果表明旋转会减缓静态虫洞的径向不稳定性（radial instability），在高速自旋下演化时间内未发现不稳定性证据，塌缩喉部会辐射轴对称四极引力波（quadrupolar gravitational-wave burst）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞已知存在径向不稳定性，但旋转对稳定性的影响尚不明确；二阶微扰理论（second-order perturbation theory）预言不稳定性会消失，需用完整非线性演化加以检验。

Method: 采用谱求解器（spectral solver）构造旋转drainhole稳态解，赤道旋转速度最高达光速的99%，并将求解器与已发表的对称解（symmetric solutions）对照验证，同时修正了某已发表场方程中的不一致之处。随后仅以截断误差（truncation error）作为扰动进行3+1非线性演化。

Result: 旋转使静态虫洞的径向不稳定性变慢；在接近30%极端角动量（extremal angular momentum）处，未扰动的喉部（throat）反而膨胀而非塌缩，这与二阶微扰理论预言的不稳定性消失不符；自58%起至演化结束未发现不稳定性证据，最快自旋情形在三种分辨率下均成立。塌缩的旋转喉部发出能量随自旋增长的轴对称四极引力波暴。

Conclusion: 旋转显著改变Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞的非线性稳定性图像，且与二阶微扰预言存在定性差异；二阶微扰初始数据（perturbative initial data）因自身截断误差而触发不稳定性，不适用于稳定性研究。

Abstract: We present nonlinear 3+1 evolutions of rotating Ellis-Bronnikov wormholes. We construct stationary rotating drainhole solutions with a spectral solver up to an equatorial rotation velocity of 99% of the speed of light, validate the solver against published symmetric solutions, and correct an inconsistency in a published field equation. Perturbed only by truncation error, the evolutions show that rotation slows the radial instability of the static wormhole. Near 30% of the extremal angular momentum the unperturbed throat expands instead of collapsing, rather than the instability disappearing as second-order perturbation theory suggests. From 58% upward we find no evidence of instability over the evolved times; at the fastest spin this holds at three resolutions. Collapsing rotating throats emit an axisymmetric quadrupolar gravitational-wave burst whose energy grows with spin. Second-order perturbative initial data trigger the instability through their own truncation error, so they are unsuitable for stability studies.

</details>


### [172] [Local tests of Schwarzschild-Bertotti-Robinson spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29990)
*Fatma Kutlu,Livanur Çelik,Özgür Delice*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究嵌入外磁场的静态黑洞（Schwarzschild-Bertotti-Robinson 时空）在弱场局域检验中的表现，考察近日点进动、光线偏折与雷达回波时延三种效应，并同时给出弱场解析结果与直接积分测地线方程的精确处理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Schwarzschild-Bertotti-Robinson 时空描述处于外部磁场中的静态黑洞，但外磁场对这些经典观测检验（近日点进动（perihelion precession）、光线偏折（deflection of light rays）、雷达回波时延（time delay of radar echoes））的可能影响尚需系统澄清。

Method: 采用适当方法推导弱场（weak field）解析结果，并对相关测地线方程（geodesic equations）进行直接积分的精确（exact）处理，用以验证弱场解析结果并给出超越弱场近似的结果。

Result: 摘要未给出具体的数值或解析表达式；所述结果包括弱场解析结论，以及由直接积分测地线方程得到的、与之一致并超出弱场近似的精确结果。

Conclusion: 外磁场对该时空三类观测检验的影响可通过弱场解析与精确积分两条路径共同刻画，精确方法可确认弱场结果并拓展至弱场近似之外。

Abstract: We study weak field local tests of Schwarzschild-Bertotti-Robinson spacetime, which describes a static black hole embedded in an external magnetic field. Perihelion precession, deflection of light rays and time delay of radar echoes are the phenomena considered in this work to understand the possible effects of this external magnetic field on these observationally important tests. We not only derive weak field analytical results by employing appropriate methods, but also consider an exact approach to directly integrate the relevant geodesic equations, to both confirm those weak field analytical results and to obtain results beyond the weak field approximation.

</details>


### [173] [Modeling QNMs-Filtered Gravitational Waveforms: Approximation Limits and Filtering Effects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30031)
*Jun-Xi Shi,Zhen-Tao He,Tingyuan Jiang,Yu Tian,Dan-Dan Yuan,Hongbao Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文系统考察双星引力波（binary gravitational waveforms）经准正规模（quasinormal modes）滤波后残余波形（direct wave）解析模型中存在的近似误差、适用范围及滤波本身的作用。结果表明点粒子模型与滤波后的双黑洞波形存在明显偏差，且即使是全通滤波器（all-pass filter）也会显著影响建模结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准正规模滤波后的残余波形（即直接波，direct wave）近期引发广泛争论；其解析模型包含多层近似，而滤波对建模的影响尚不明确，因此需要进一步研究这些模型的近似误差与适用边界。

Method: 检验所考察模型的近似误差、适用范围与滤波作用：将点粒子模型（point-particle models）与滤波后的双黑洞（binary-black-hole）波形对比，并分析基于驻相近似（stationary-phase approximation）的解析粒子模板在不同多极（multipole）上的表现；同时在史瓦西背景（Schwarzschild background）上进行解析计算，考察高斯初始数据（Gaussian initial data）的真空扰动（vacuum perturbations）经滤波后的波形形态。

Result: 点粒子模型与滤波后双黑洞波形存在显著差异；基于驻相近似的解析粒子模板对 (ℓ,m)=(2,2) 多极表现尚可，但在 (4,4) 多极存在明显局限。尽管滤波器为全通（all-pass），其对建模结果仍有实质性影响。史瓦西背景上的解析计算显示，高斯初始数据的真空扰动经滤波后亦可呈现类直接波波形，其形态取决于初始数据相对于光球（light ring）的位置。

Conclusion: 滤波后的直接波建模需谨慎对待：点粒子与驻相近似模板的适用性有限且依赖多极，滤波过程本身不可忽略；类直接波形态并非双星系统独有，真空扰动亦可产生，其形态由初始数据相对光球的位置决定。

Abstract: The waveform left after quasinormal modes filtering of binary gravitational waveforms, often called the direct wave, has recently attracted considerable debate. Analytic models of this residual waveform involve several levels of approximation, while the effects of filtering on the modeling remain unclear. These issues require further investigation. In this paper, we examine the approximation errors, limits of applicability, and role of filtering in these models. We find that the point-particle models examined here show notable discrepancies with the filtered Binary-black-holes waveforms. Analytic particle templates based on the stationary-phase approximation perform reasonably well for the \((\ell,m)=(2,2)\) multipole, but exhibit substantial limitations for the \((4,4)\). Although the filter is all-pass, it substantially affects the modeling results. Our analytic calculations on a Schwarzschild background further show that vacuum perturbations with Gaussian initial data can also exhibit direct-wave-like waveforms after filtering, with their morphology depending on the location of the initial data relative to the light ring.

</details>


### [174] [Exceptional points and hysteresis in Kerr-Newman quasi-normal modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30049)
*Mario Misas Arcos,Alberto T. Pérez,Carlos F. Sopuerta*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文指出，奇异点（exceptional points, EPs）与本征态迟滞（eigenstate hysteresis）在Kerr-Newman黑洞的准正规模（quasinormal-mode）谱中内禀出现，无需此前Kerr情形中所需的辅助标量场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往仅在带有辅助标量场的Kerr背景下发现奇异点与本征态迟滞，本文旨在检验这些现象是否能在纯粹的Kerr-Newman黑洞中内生地出现。

Method: 采用耦合引力-电磁Teukolsky方程的双曲型表述（hyperboloidal formulation）与Chebyshev伪谱方法（pseudospectral method），计算ℓ=m=2的准正规模谱并定位奇异点。

Result: 在接近极端性（extremality）最高达99.974%处定位到四个奇异点，其中一个源于新的排斥（repulsion）；能隙以平方根分支点（square root branch point）形式闭合，绕一个或多个奇异点的准静态回路（quasistatic loops）会交换合并的模式，从而证实迟滞。

Conclusion: 奇异点与本征态迟滞是Kerr-Newman黑洞准正规模谱的内禀特征，而非依赖辅助标量场的外部效应。

Abstract: Exceptional points (EPs) and eigenstate hysteresis, previously found in Kerr only with an auxiliary scalar field, arise intrinsically in the quasinormal-mode spectrum of Kerr-Newman black holes. Using a hyperboloidal formulation of the coupled gravito-electromagnetic Teukolsky equations and a Chebyshev pseudospectral method, we compute the $\ell=m=2$ spectrum and locate four EPs at up to $99.974\%$ of extremality, one from a new repulsion. The gaps close as a square root branch point, and quasistatic loops around one or more EPs exchange the coalescing modes, confirming hysteresis.

</details>


### [175] [Post-adiabatic effective-one-body waveforms for coalescing compact binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30064)
*Rossella Gamba*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文将 Damour 等（2004）与 Lynch 等（2026）的密切轨道（osculating orbits）形式推广至偏心有效单体（EOB）模型，以等分点轨道根数（equinoctial orbital elements）重写以规避零偏心率奇点，从而高效描述潮汐、自旋及进动致密双星的旋近至坠落（inspiral-to-plunge）动力学，并显著加速波形生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偏心、含潮汐与自旋的致密双星波形生成在计算成本上较高，需要一种既高效又保持精度的旋近—坠落动力学描述方式；同时零偏心率极限下的奇点问题需被规避。

Method: 采用并扩展密切轨道形式，将其以等分点轨道根数重新表述，并通过对原平面运动进行“扭曲”（twisting）处理来纳入进动效应；具体以 TEOBResumS-Dalí 模型族为对象实现，并进一步通过多谐波驻定相位近似（multi-harmonic stationary phase approximation）构造频域波形。

Result: 在初始偏心率至 0.6（20 Hz 处）的逾 $10^4$ 个双中子星（BNS）与同等数量双黑洞（BBH）上，相对优化版模型的中位加速约为 BNS 2.6 倍、BBH 1.5 倍，与完整数值解的失配（mismatch）始终低于 $5\times10^{-7}$；个别系统最高约 7 倍（轻质量 BBH，$M\lesssim 8M_\odot$、$e_0\lesssim0.1$）。BNS 波形生成中位时间约 0.5 s，99.7% 系统低于 1 s，长信号情形较前版本最高快约 26 倍。

Conclusion: 该形式具有通用性，可应用于任意 EOB 模型；在 $e\rightarrow0$ 极限下计入辐射反作用（radiation reaction）后，模型输入偏心率与规范不变的波形偏心率可一致至约 $7\times10^{-8}$，并可用于频域偏心波形构建。

Abstract: I apply and extend the formalism of osculating orbits of Damour et al. (2004) and Lynch et al. (2026) to eccentric effective one body (EOB) models, in order to obtain an efficient yet accurate description of the inspiral-to-plunge dynamics of tidal, spinning compact binaries, including precessing ones by twisting the otherwise planar motion. The method is recast in terms of equinoctial orbital elements, to circumvent singularities associated with the zero-eccentricity limit. While the formalism is general and can be applied to any EOB model, I focus here on the TEOBResumS-Dalí family and demonstrate how this technique speeds up waveform generation without significantly compromising the accuracy of the model. Over $10^4$ binary neutron stars (BNSs) and as many binary black holes (BBHs) with initial eccentricities up to $0.6$ at $20$ Hz, the median speedup against an optimized version of the model is $\sim 2.6$ for BNSs and $\sim 1.5$ for BBHs, with mismatches against the full numerical solution always below $5 \times 10^{-7}$. The largest speedups, up to $\sim 7$ for individual systems, are obtained for light BBHs ($M \lesssim 8 M_\odot$) with low eccentricities $(e_0 \lesssim 0.1)$. In absolute terms, the median time needed to generate a BNS waveform is $\sim 0.5$ s, with $99.7\%$ of the systems below one second, and for long signals the model is up to $\sim 26$ times faster than a previous version. Applied to the calculation of initial conditions in the $e \rightarrow 0$ limit, the inclusion of radiation-reaction effects allows the model's input eccentricity to agree with the gauge-invariant waveform eccentricity down to initial values of $\sim 7 \times 10^{-8}$. Beyond time-domain applications, the same construction also gives frequency-domain EOB waveforms for eccentric compact binaries via a multi-harmonic stationary phase approximation.

</details>


### [176] [Inaccessibility of the Cosmological Singularity to Quantum Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30071)
*Vishnulal Cheriyodathillathu,Saurya Das,Soumen Basak,Tanmay Patil*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文提出以曲率耦合的量子探针在操作层面消解FLRW大爆炸奇点：通过Madelung-Bohm分解，诱导的共形几何使曲率不变量有限且过去指向因果测地线完备，但底层FLRW时空仍保持奇异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典FLRW（Friedmann-Lemaître-Robertson-Walker）时空存在大爆炸奇点（big bang singularity），经典描述在该处失效；本文旨在从可操作（operational）角度探讨量子探针能否规避该奇点。

Method: 采用满足曲率耦合Klein-Gordon方程（curvature coupled Klein-Gordon equation）的量子探针，在经典近奇点FLRW背景上对Klein-Gordon场实施Madelung-Bohm分解（Madelung-Bohm decomposition）。

Result: 诱导出的共形几何（conformal geometry）使曲率不变量（curvature invariants）保持有限，并使过去指向的因果测地线（past directed causal geodesics）完备。

Conclusion: 在有限有效固有时（effective proper time）内，该类量子探针无法抵达经典大爆炸面，但底层FLRW时空本身仍然奇异；即奇点消解是操作层面的而非几何层面的。

Abstract: We propose an operational resolution of the FLRW big bang singularity using quantum probes satisfying a curvature coupled Klein-Gordon equation. Applying the Madelung-Bohm decomposition of a Klein-Gordon field on a classical near-singularity FLRW background, we show that the induced conformal geometry renders curvature invariants finite and makes past directed causal geodesics complete. The classical big bang surface is therefore inaccessible to this class of quantum probes in finite effective proper time, while the underlying FLRW spacetime remains singular.

</details>


### [177] [Dyonic black holes supporting nearly-black self-gravitating thin shells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14410)
*Shahar Hod*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文证明准拓扑非线性电动力学（quasitopological non-linear electrodynamic）理论的磁荷-电荷黑洞（dyonic black hole）时空中存在满足 d[r·g_tt(r)]/dr→0⁺ 的径向区域，可支撑处于静态平衡、接近黑洞临界状态的自引力薄壳，且其离散半径与中心致密天体质量无关，具有普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作表明该类时空中存在 dg_tt/dr=0 的离散径向区域，可支撑忽略自引力的检验壳层（test shells，即自引力可忽略的 Dyson 壳层）；本文旨在将这一结论推广到自引力不可忽略的薄壳情形。

Method: 在准拓扑非线性电动力学理论（quasitopological non-linear electrodynamic field theory）的磁荷-电荷时空（dyonic spacetimes）中，通过解析证明识别满足无量纲条件 d[r·g_tt(r)]/dr→0⁺ 的径向区域，并据此构造处于静态平衡的自引力薄壳（self-gravitating thin shells）。

Result: 证明该类时空确实存在上述径向区域，可支撑处于向黑洞演化临界状态（on the verge of becoming black holes）的静态平衡自引力薄壳；并进一步证明这些“近黑洞 Dyson 壳层”的离散半径与中心支撑磁荷-电荷致密天体（dyonic compact objects）的质量无关，即具有普适性（universal）。

Conclusion: 自引力近黑洞 Dyson 壳层的静态平衡可由时空度规的特定径向条件保证，且其位置具有质量无关的普适特征，从而将此前关于检验壳层的结论推广至自引力情形。

Abstract: It has recently been revealed that dyonic black-hole spacetimes of a quasitopological non-linear electrodynamic field theory may be characterized by discrete radial regions with the property $dg_{tt}(r)/dr=0$ in which spherically symmetric massive {\it test} shells (Dyson shells with negligible self-gravity) can be supported in static equilibrium states. In the present paper we prove that the dyonic spacetimes of the non-linear electrodynamic field theory may also be characterized by the presence of radial regions with the dimensionless property $d[r\cdot g_{tt}(r)]/dr\to0^+$ in which massive {\it self-gravitating} thin shells that are on the verge of becoming black holes can be supported in static equilibrium states. Intriguingly, it is proved that the discrete radii of these self-gravitating nearly-black Dyson shells are universal in the sense that they are independent of the masses of the central supporting dyonic compact objects.

</details>


<div id='quant-ph'></div>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [178] [The Prevalence of Straight Shots in Control Space for Reaching Optimal Quantum Performance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28852)
*Yiyou Chen,Gabriele Herr,Herschel Rabitz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 论文提出一种非随机直射搜索算法（STSA），用于在控制景观（control landscape）中识别大量直射路径（straight shots），并通过上万条数值路径的统计分布分析论证其在控制空间中的普遍性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于梯度（gradient-based）的算法通常沿最陡上升路径寻找量子目标泛函的最优控制，而沿直射路径移动可视为一种代价更低、单调完整的景观爬升方式；已有研究虽发现直射存在，但其识别依赖计算开销大的随机（stochastic）方法。

Method: 引入非随机的直射搜索算法（non-stochastic straight-shot search algorithm, STSA），并将其应用于三类目标：(i) 态-态跃迁（state-to-state transitions）、(ii) 一般可观测量的优化（general observables）、(iii) 幺正变换生成（unitary transformation generation），数值生成并统计分析超过10,000条不同的直射优化路径。

Result: 随样本量增大，直射路径之间的最近邻距离（nearest-neighbor distances）减小，提示直射在控制空间中具有普遍性；进一步推测景观等值面（level set）上直射起点的位置可能构成连通邻域（connected neighborhoods）。

Conclusion: STSA可用于定位海量直射解，但其当前形式并非高效算法；直射解普遍存在这一发现为未来加速最优控制中直射路径的搜寻留下空间，并引出对其拓扑结构的后续研究。

Abstract: Gradient-based algorithms are often applied to identify an optimal control for maximizing a quantum objective functional by following a steepest ascent path to the top of the control landscape. In contrast, this paper explores straight shots through control space where movement along such a path corresponds to a less demanding monotonic complete climb up the landscape for the optimal objective value. Existing studies of control landscapes have revealed the presence of straight shots, but identifying them required computationally intensive stochastic methods. In this paper, we introduce a non-stochastic straight-shot search algorithm (STSA) that identifies a large class of straight shots. We apply STSA to numerically generate more than 10,000 distinct straight-shot optimization paths and analyze their statistical distribution for each of the objectives of (i) state-to-state transitions, (ii) general observables, and (iii) unitary transformation generation. The results show a decrease in the nearest-neighbor distances among straight shots as the sample size increases, suggesting their prevalence in control space. We further conjecture that the starting locations of the straight-shot pathways on a landscape level set may form connected neighborhoods, motivating future investigation into their underlying topological structure. The present paper focuses on the use of STSA for locating massive numbers of straight solutions. However, the STSA is not a high-efficiency algorithm in its present form, but the discovered prevalence of control straight shots leaves open future advances to accelerate the discovery of straight shots as optimal controls.

</details>


### [179] [Adapted Fröhlich-Pizzo Model for Horizon-Induced Decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29363)
*Bik-Soon Sia*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 通过借鉴Fröhlich-Pizzo模型并结合U(1)手征流（chiral current）的局域代数（local algebras），在长保持时间极限下再现了Danielson-Satishchandran-Wald（DSW）视界诱导退相干（horizon-induced decoherence）机制的核心特征，并指出其有效动力学保留非马尔可夫（non-Markovian）特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原Fröhlich-Pizzo（FP）模型本用于非相对论极限下描述原子与电磁场相互作用；本文意在借助其框架刻画DSW提出的视界诱导退相干机制，并探讨量子事件（quantum events）在该动力学中的可能作用。

Method: 对FP模型进行改造，基于U(1)手征流（chiral current）的局域代数（local algebras）构建系统描述，并在大保持时间T的极限下与DSW退相干情形进行对照分析。

Result: 改造后的模型能够捕捉DSW视界诱导退相干机制的本质特征；分析表明该退相干过程的有效动力学（effective dynamics）仍具有非马尔可夫（non-Markovian）特征。

Conclusion: 该工作为视界诱导退相干提供了一种基于局域代数的模型化途径，并初步揭示量子事件（quantum events）在其中的潜在角色，但相关结论限于大保持时间T极限。

Abstract: The Fröhlich-Pizzo (FP) model is a quantum-mechanical model originally developed to describe the interaction between an atom and the electromagnetic field in the nonrelativistic limit $c\to\infty$. We show that an adaptation of the FP model based on the local algebras of a $U(1)$ chiral current captures essential features of the horizon-induced decoherence mechanism identified by Danielson, Satishchandran, and Wald (DSW) in the limit of large holding time $T$. Our analysis suggests that the effective dynamics of the system undergoing decoherence in the DSW setting retains non-Markovian features. Our approach also offers a glimpse into the possible role of quantum events in the dynamics underlying horizon-induced decoherence.

</details>


### [180] [Quantum f-divergences from retrodiction: a unifying classical variational principle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29696)
*Francesco Buscemi*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 同一经典 f-散度（f-divergence）在量子态上存在多种不等价扩展，本文提出一个含参考先验（reference prior）的变分原理，证明同一谱实现可同时最小化所有算子凸 f 的可区分度，并通过改变参考态在标准型、测地线与极大（maximal）量子 f-散度之间插值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典 f-散度向量子态扩展的方式不唯一且彼此不等价，核心问题是这些不同扩展能否由某个共同的操作性变分原理统一导出。

Method: 推广 Matsumoto 的逆向检验优化（reverse-test optimization），引入参考先验态；最小化正向与逆向 prepare-and-measure 过程（经所选参考态回溯／retrodiction 相联系，并受规定量子平均值约束）之间完整记录（complete records）的经典 f-散度。

Result: 对有限维忠实态（faithful states）证明：同一谱实现（spectral implementation）对所有算子凸（operator-convex）f 同时达到最小值。平坦参考给出标准量子 f-散度并为 Nussbaum-Szkoła 分布提供最优记录的变分刻画；以任一规定态为参考恢复极大量子 f-散度与 Matsumoto 逆向检验；幂型参考实现测地线（geodesic）插值。

Conclusion: 参考态的选择统一刻画了量子 f-散度的不同扩展族；在相对熵（relative entropy）情形下，改变回溯先验即实现从 Umegaki 相对熵到 Belavkin-Staszewski 相对熵的过渡。

Abstract: The same classical f-divergence admits inequivalent extensions to quantum states. The question is whether these extensions can be obtained from a common operational variational principle. Here we generalize Matsumoto's reverse-test optimization by introducing a reference prior state. More explicitly, we minimize the statistical distinguishability between a forward and a reverse prepare-and-measure process, related by retrodiction through the chosen reference and subject to prescribed quantum averages. Distinguishability is quantified by the classical f-divergence between their complete records. For faithful states in finite dimension, we prove that the same spectral implementation attains the minimum simultaneously for every operator-convex f. Varying the reference allows us to interpolate between standard and maximal quantum f-divergences of the prescribed states, passing through the geodesic family. More precisely, a flat reference yields the standard quantum f-divergences, thereby giving the Nussbaum-Szkoła distributions a variational characterization as optimal records. Choosing either prescribed state as reference recovers maximal quantum f-divergences and Matsumoto's reverse tests, while power references realize the geodesic interpolation. In the relative-entropy case, this gives the passage from Umegaki to Belavkin-Staszewski relative entropy by changing the prior for retrodiction.

</details>


### [181] [Finite Classification of Transversal Diagonal Gates Beyond Stabilizer Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29955)
*Xi He,Chao Zhang,Shilin Huang,Bei Zeng*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明距离至少为二的精确量子码上的横向对角门（transversal diagonal gates）可在有限搜索空间内被穷尽分类，其物理阶上界仅由码参数决定；据此对 $((6,2,3))$ 码的非平凡对角部分给出枚举，得到 66 个候选对称类、15 个可实现类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 横向对角门虽可含连续相位，但其诱导的投影逻辑作用与离散对称性的可枚举性此前缺乏统一判据，需要确定分类是否可在有限空间内完成。

Method: 证明任意投影逻辑作用可由有限阶物理门实现，且该阶上界仅依赖码参数；将该阶界与子集和线性规划（subset-sum-linear-programming, SSLP）框架结合枚举离散对称候选，再用 Knill-Laflamme 方程确定残余连续码参数。

Result: 对精确 $((6,2,3))$ 码的非平凡对角扇区，SSLP 给出 66 个候选对称类，其中恰有 15 个可实现，支持阶为 2、3、5 的完整投影对角逻辑循环群；阶 5 情形在局域幺正与量子比特置换下唯一，等价于 arXiv:2504.20847 构造的码。

Conclusion: 横向对角门的分类具有有限性，可归约为有限离散枚举加连续参数求解；该码在完整投影横向群不可共轭入投影 Clifford 群（projective Clifford group）的精确 $((n,2,\ge 3))$ 码中达到最小长度 $n=6$。

Abstract: We establish that transversal diagonal gates on exact qubit codes of distance at least two can be exhaustively classified within a finite search space. Although code-preserving physical gates can have continuous phases, any projective logical action induced by a transversal diagonal gate can always be realized by a physical gate of finite order. Crucially, we prove that this physical order has an upper bound determined solely by the code parameters. This order bound enables the subset-sum-linear-programming (SSLP) framework introduced in arXiv:2504.20847 to exhaustively enumerate all candidate discrete symmetries, after which the Knill-Laflamme equations determine the remaining continuous code parameters. Applying this method to the nontrivial diagonal sector of exact $((6,2,3))$ codes, SSLP yields $66$ candidate symmetry classes, of which exactly $15$ are realizable, supporting full projective diagonal logical cyclic groups of orders $2$, $3$, and $5$. In particular, for order $5$, the code is unique up to local unitaries and qubit permutations, equivalent to the code constructed in arXiv:2504.20847. This code also attains the minimum length $n=6$ among exact $((n, 2, \ge 3))$ codes whose full projective transversal group cannot be conjugated into the projective Clifford group.

</details>


### [182] [Liouvillian Non-Invertible Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.23111)
*Ying-Cheng Yang,Jhen-Dong Lin,Yueh-Nan Chen*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文将希尔伯特空间中的不可逆对偶（non-invertible duality）推广到开放量子系统，构建了刘维尔空间（Liouville space）对偶框架，并在周期横场伊辛链中给出自对偶下的选择规则与动力学判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不可逆对称性（non-invertible symmetries）在量子场论与多体物理中作用重要，但开放量子系统中尚缺乏一般框架；本文旨在填补这一空白，将不可逆对偶扩展至马尔可夫动力学。

Method: 提出通用的刘维尔空间对偶框架，把希尔伯特空间不可逆对偶推广到开放系统，所得缺陷（defect）连接对偶的哈密顿量与耗散参数，并在自对偶（self-duality）时成为精确对称性；以含两个集体林布拉德通道（Lindblad channels）、由 Kramers–Wannier（KW）对偶交换的周期横场伊辛链为具体模型，结合 Z₂ 对称性导致的四个宇称扇区进行分析。

Result: 强 Z₂ 对称性将刘维尔空间分为四个宇称扇区，缺陷仅在偶-偶（even-even）扇区非平凡作用；自对偶时 KW 缺陷对刘维尔约当块（Jordan blocks）施加按动量分辨的选择规则——不同刘维尔动量或 KW 缺陷本征值的模式不能构成共同的例外约当块。三格点链中，全局 Rényi-2 KW 不对称性可动力学地识别自对偶条件；谱结构同时呈现半单简并（semisimple degeneracy）与具有微扰选择性响应的自对偶例外点（exceptional point）。

Conclusion: 研究将不可逆对偶扩展至马尔可夫量子动力学，并建立了基于对称性的刘维尔约当结构选择规则，为开放量子系统中的不可逆对称性提供了统一框架。

Abstract: Non-invertible symmetries extend conventional symmetries beyond group-like transformations and play an important role in quantum field theory and many-body physics. However, a general framework of non-invertible symmetries in open quantum systems is still lacking. Here, we formulate a general Liouville-space duality framework that extends Hilbert-space non-invertible dualities to open quantum systems. The resulting defect relates Liouvillians with dual Hamiltonian and dissipative parameters and becomes an exact symmetry at self-duality. We apply this framework to the periodic transverse-field Ising chain with two collective Lindblad channels exchanged by Kramers--Wannier (KW) duality. A strong $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry divides Liouville space into four parity sectors, while the defect acts nontrivially only in the even-even sector. At self-duality, the KW defect imposes a momentum-resolved selection rule on Liouvillian Jordan blocks. Modes with different Liouville momenta or KW-defect eigenvalues cannot form a common exceptional Jordan block. For a three-site chain, a global Rényi-2 KW asymmetry dynamically identifies the self-dual conditions. At the same time, the spectral structure exhibits both a semisimple degeneracy and a self-dual exceptional point with a perturbation-selective response. These results extend non-invertible dualities to Markovian quantum dynamics and establish a symmetry-based selection rule for Liouvillian Jordan structures.

</details>


### [183] [Interacting quantum reference frames](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28732)
*Leonardo Chataignier,Alexander R. H. Smith*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文在规范理论（gauge theories）的狄拉克量子化框架下形式化量子参考系（quantum reference frames），证明物理内积可按参考系取向选择书写而物理希尔伯特空间与之无关，并证明换帧映射（change of frame）在参考系与系统存在相互作用时仍为酉映射（unitary）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有基于相对化映射（relativization maps）与Page-Wootters约化的参考系描述，在处理参考系与被描述系统存在相互作用（interactions）、以及规范群（gauge group）不呈张量积表示的情形时面临非酉性（non-unitarity）等问题，需要更具一般性的形式化框架。

Method: 假设规范群G为幺模群（unimodular），将量子参考系定义为与作用于运动学希尔伯特空间（kinematical Hilbert space）某张量因子上参考系取向群G_R相联系的相干态（coherent states）系统；类比路径积分中的Faddeev-Popov程序，用带算符测度（operator measure）的参考系取向选择改写物理内积，并推广Page-Wootters约化映射以定义条件态与条件概率。

Result: 物理内积可由参考系取向选择加相应算符测度表达，但物理希尔伯特空间与该选择无关；条件概率对应关系型狄拉克可观测量（relational Dirac observables）的期望值；核心结果为换帧映射是酉的，即使G在H_kin的因子分解上不呈张量积表示。应用于时间参考系时，得到相互作用时钟与系统满足酉薛定谔方程（Schrödinger equation），并在适当极限下回到此前报道的时间非局域薛定谔方程；非酉性问题通过物理内积中的规范固定测度（gauge-fixing measure）得以避免；空间参考系情形下，一帧中的相干性（coherence）与另一帧中的纠缠（entanglement）之间的已知对应关系依赖于耦合强度。

Conclusion: 该形式化使量子参考系框架可容纳参考系与被描述系统之间的相互作用，并借助规范固定测度维持酉演化；同时表明相干性与纠缠的帧间对应并非普适，而取决于耦合。

Abstract: We formulate quantum reference frames within the Dirac quantization of gauge theories, where physical states are obtained from a kinematical Hilbert space $\mathcal{H}_{\rm kin}$ by group averaging. We assume the gauge group $G$ is unimodular, and we define a quantum reference frame as a system of coherent states associated with a group $G_R$ of frame orientations acting on a tensor factor of $\mathcal{H}_{\rm kin}$. In direct analogy with the Faddeev-Popov procedure in path integrals, we show that the physical inner product can be written in terms of a choice of frame orientation with an appropriate operator measure, but that the physical Hilbert space is independent of this choice of frame. This yields a generalization of the Page-Wootters reduction map used to define conditional states and conditional probabilities of measurement outcomes given a frame orientation. These probabilities correspond to the expectation values of relational Dirac observables obtained from generalizations of previously considered relativization maps. Our main result is that the map that implements a change of frame is unitary, even when $G$ does not act as a tensor-product representation across a factorization of $\mathcal{H}_{\rm kin}$. The formalism therefore accommodates interactions between reference frames and the systems described relative to them. We apply this formalism to temporal frames, showing that a unitary Schrödinger equation emerges for an interacting clock and system, and that a previously reported time-nonlocal Schrödinger equation is recovered in an appropriate limit. With this, problems of non-unitarity are avoided by the use of the gauge-fixing measure in the physical inner product. We also discuss the case of interacting spatial frames, where the known correspondence between coherence in one frame and entanglement in another is shown to depend on the coupling.

</details>


### [184] [Hydrodynamic description of correlation matrix eigenvalues in a finite temperature expanding free fermionic gas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30062)
*Atharva Naik,Madhumita Saha,David A. Huse,Manas Kulkarni,Abhishek Dhar*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出一种流体力学（hydrodynamic）描述，用于获得非零温度下自由膨胀一维费米气体（one-dimensional Fermi gas）微观关联矩阵（correlation matrix）的本征值，并据此计算系统与环境的熵（entropy）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前期工作在一维费米气体单缺陷情形下，用半经典流体力学得到的 Yang-Yang 熵与微观纠缠熵（entanglement entropy）一致并给出 Page 曲线，但环境 Yang-Yang 熵呈单调增长；本文旨在将该流体力学框架推广至非零温度并更准确地描述环境熵。

Method: 对非零温度自由膨胀费米气体建立关联矩阵本征值的流体力学描述：系统一侧由相空间密度（phase space density）直接给出本征值并经 Yang-Yang 公式得到 von-Neumann 熵；环境（reservoir）一侧需在 Yang-Yang 公式中修正相空间密度以纳入相干性（coherences）。此外基于半经典波包（wave-packet）图像给出本征值的启发式推导。

Result: 该描述可准确复现环境的熵；同时由此得到瞬态（transient regime）下有限系统全计数统计（full-counting-statistics）的 Levitov-Lesovik 型公式。

Conclusion: 流体力学方法可在非零温度下统一给出关联矩阵本征值、系统与环境熵及全计数统计，为 Page 曲线与量子输运统计提供半经典层面的一致描述。

Abstract: A recent work considered the Page curve for entanglement entropy of a freely expanding one-dimensional Fermi gas starting from a finite box (the system) in a zero-temperature initial state that then expands in to a semi-infinite vacuum (the environment). For the case with a single defect at the system-environment junction, it was shown that the Yang-Yang entropy of the system computed from semiclassical hydrodynamics agrees with the microscopically computed entanglement entropy, yielding the Page curve. On the other hand, the environment Yang-Yang entropy showed a monotonic increase. Here, we propose a hydrodynamic description to obtain the eigenvalues of the microscopic correlation matrix for a nonzero temperature freely-expanding Fermi gas. For the system, the phase space density directly gives the correlation matrix eigenvalues and thus, via the Yang-Yang formula, yields the von-Neumann entropy. For the reservoir, a modification of the phase space density is required in the Yang-Yang formula, to incorporate coherences, in order to reproduce accurately the entropy. The hydrodynamic description of correlation matrix eigenvalues also leads to a Levitov-Lesovik type formula for the full-counting-statistics for a finite system in the transient regime. We provide a heuristic derivation of the correlation matrix eigenvalues based on a semiclassical wave-packet picture.

</details>


### [185] [An Instability of the Quantum Markov Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.12929)
*David C. Newsom,Michael Levin*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 马尔可夫长度（Markov length）在弱局域信道（weak local channels）下普遍不稳定；即使从纯直积态出发，经任意接近恒等映射的深度二高斯信道（Gaussian channels）即可使条件互信息（conditional mutual information, CMI）由指数衰减变为代数衰减，马尔可夫长度发散。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 马尔可夫长度被视为刻画混合态物相（mixed-state phases of matter）的有力指标，但此前缺乏对其在弱局域微扰下稳定性的系统检验；本文旨在检验该指标能否作为混合态相等价性的判据。

Method: 在一维费米子链上构造精细调制的深度二最近邻高斯信道线路（depth-two circuit of nearest-neighbor Gaussian channels），将真空态映射为 CMI 代数衰减的态，并推广到普通量子比特链；同时给出关于平移不变态（translation-invariant states）的一般性论证。

Result: 即使在信道无限接近恒等映射时，CMI 仍呈代数衰减、马尔可夫长度为无穷；其根源是冯·诺依曼熵（von Neumann entropy）在秩亏密度算符（rank-deficient density operators）处的不解析性。进一步证明：任何平移不变态只要存在单个秩亏约化密度算符，马尔可夫长度即不稳定。

Conclusion: 马尔可夫长度不是弱局域信道下的稳定量，这对近期基于马尔可夫长度定义的混合态相等价性（mixed-state phase equivalence）构成挑战，提示需谨慎选用该指标作为相分类判据。

Abstract: The Markov length, the length scale governing the exponential decay of conditional mutual information (CMI) in a quantum many-body state, has proven useful for characterizing mixed-state phases of matter. Nevertheless, we show that a state's Markov length is not generally stable under weak local channels. The failure of stability occurs already at a pure product state. We demonstrate this explicitly in a one-dimensional fermionic chain, where a fine-tuned depth-two circuit of nearest-neighbor Gaussian channels maps the vacuum to a state whose CMI decays \emph{algebraically} and whose Markov length is therefore infinite, even when the channels are taken arbitrarily close to the identity. The algebraic decay is rooted in the nonanalyticity of the von Neumann entropy at rank-deficient density operators. We exhibit an analogous instability on an ordinary qubit chain. More generally, we show that the Markov length is unstable in any translation-invariant state with even a single rank-deficient reduced density operator. These results pose a challenge to recent definitions of mixed-state phase equivalence based on the Markov length.

</details>


### [186] [Tunable flat bands from a parent chiral Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.28848)
*Jun-Hyung Bae,Alexei Andreanov,Saurabh Maiti*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出基于母体二分系统（parent bipartite system）与希尔伯特空间投影（Hilbert space projections）生成平带（flat bands）族的方法，可规避精细调节（fine-tuning）并实现对简并度、能带接触点及分裂的可控设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平带系统（flat band systems）有望承载在色散带（dispersive bands）系统中难以观测的奇异量子现象，但既有构造方法多需精细调节或求解困难的逆本征值问题（inverse-eigenvalue problem），限制了其设计与应用。

Method: 以二分系统（bipartite system）为母体，通过希尔伯特空间投影（Hilbert space projections）生成一族平带，并推导哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）在指定能量与指定简并度（degree of degeneracy）下保证平带存在的条件。

Result: 该方法基本不受精细调节约束，可实现平带与色散带在布里渊区（Brillouin zone）任意期望点处的接触点控制，并识别出可保持平带或可控分裂平带的微扰（perturbations）。

Conclusion: 该方案为设计具所需性质的平带系统提供了便捷的可调性（tunability），在平带工程（flat band engineering）中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Systems with flat bands are interesting as they potentially host many exotic quantum phenomena that would be hard to observe in usual systems with dispersive bands. Multiple approaches were developed to construct flat band systems, with most of them requiring fine-tuning and/or solving a challenging inverse-eigenvalue problem. In this article we work with an idea that uses a parent bipartite system to generate a family of flat bands by means of Hilbert space projections. This method is largely free from fine-tuning constraints and allows us to identify conditions for a Hamiltonian to guarantee flat bands at desired energies, with desired degree of degeneracy. We also show that one can control the touching points of the flat band with dispersive bands at any desirable point in the Brillouin zone. In the process, we identify perturbations that preserve the flat bands or split them controllably. This approach provides easy tunability in designing flat band systems with desired properties.

</details>


### [187] [Boundary exceptional point driven chiral switch of topological edge states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29316)
*Bikashkali Midya,Barsa Rani Sahu*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 本文解析研究了有限Su-Schrieffer-Heeger（SSH）链中拓扑与非厄米边界势的相互作用，导出边界诱导奇异点（exceptional points, EPs）的精确坐标，并据此提出受拓扑保护的手性边缘态转移协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑边缘态与非厄米局域耗散/增益的耦合机制尚缺乏解析刻画，且如何在体带不受影响的前提下实现可控的拓扑态操控仍待解决。

Method: 在两端带非厄米势的有限SSH链中，通过调节边界增益与损耗参数，利用两个带隙内拓扑边缘态的有限尺寸杂化，解析导出边界诱导奇异点的精确坐标 Γ_EP = ±i v^N / w^{N-1}；并考察动力学环绕该奇异点的演化。

Result: 在两个边缘态发生合并（coalescence）的奇异点处，绕行方向决定边缘态的走向：可将局域于一侧边界的拓扑边缘态以完美保真度转移至对侧边缘，或经双重切换过程返回原位，形成手性边缘态转移。

Conclusion: 由于非厄米性严格局限于边界，该手性输运不改变体带的能谱；研究结果提供了一个可实验验证、可由边界非厄米驱动控制的大尺度拓扑手性开关的最小框架。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate the interplay of topology and localized non-Hermiticity in a finite Su-Schrieffer-Heeger chain characterized by non-Hermitian potentials at its boundaries. By tuning the boundary gain and loss parameters, we exploit the finite-size hybridization of two mid-gap topological edge states to analytically derive the exact coordinates of boundary-induced exceptional points (EPs), $Γ_{\rm EP}=\pm i v^{N}/w^{N-1}$, at which these states undergo coalescence. We demonstrate that dynamically encircling these boundary EPs facilitates a robust, chiral edge state-transfer protocol. Depending on the orientation of the encircling loop, this protocol allows a topological edge state localized at one boundary to be either transferred to the opposite edge with perfect fidelity or returned to its starting position through a double switching process. Importantly, since the non-Hermiticity remains strictly confined to the boundaries, this chiral transport occurs without affecting the energy spectrum of the bulk bands. These results establish a minimal framework for a large-scale topological chiral switch that is experimentally verifiable and capable of being controlled through boundary non-Hermitian driving.

</details>


### [188] [Quantum-Enhanced Matterwave Interferometry in a Ring Waveguide](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29562)
*Sriganapathy Raghav,Utpal Roy*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出基于环形玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate）分数复苏动力学（fractional-revival dynamics）结合受控相互作用淬火（interaction quench）的量子增强物质波干涉测量协议，可实现约 -14.57 dB 的自旋压缩（spin squeezing）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于利用环形凝聚体固有的分数复苏动力学与可控相互作用操作，构建量子增强的干涉测量方案，以提升相位测量灵敏度。

Method: 采用截断维格纳近似（Truncated Wigner Approximation）进行数值分析，考察非线性演化至四分之一复苏（quarter revival）所生成的自旋压缩，并引入在初始四分之一复苏分束操作后的受控相互作用淬火，以及在 t=3T_r/8 时的相位控制操作。

Result: 非线性演化至四分之一复苏仅产生适度的自旋压缩（-2.03±0.28 dB）且随后退化；而自非相互作用凝聚体出发、经相互作用淬火可获得显著更强的自旋压缩（-14.57±0.27 dB）。在 t=3T_r/8 的相位控制将压缩正交分量旋转至相位敏感方向 Ĵ_y，于 t=T_r/2 形成相位压缩态，后续四分之一复苏演化将其转化为可支持布居差读出（population-difference readout）的粒子数压缩态。

Conclusion: 该序列结合环形体系内禀的分数复苏动力学与受控相互作用及相位操作，为量子增强干涉测量提供了一条可行路径。

Abstract: We propose a quantum-enhanced matter-wave interferometry protocol based on fractional-revival dynamics of a Bose-Einstein condensate in a ring, combined with a controlled interaction quench. Using the Truncated Wigner Approximation, we show that nonlinear evolution to the quarter revival generates modest spin squeezing of $-2.03\pm0.28\;$dB, which subsequently degrades under further nonlinear evolution. In contrast, starting from a non-interacting condensate, a controlled interaction quench applied after the initial quarter-revival beam-splitter operation generates substantially stronger spin squeezing of $-14.57\pm0.27\;$dB. We further show that phase control at $t=3T_r/8$ rotates the squeezed quadrature onto the phase-sensitive direction $\hat{J}_y$, producing a phase-squeezed state at $t=T_r/2$. The subsequent quarter-revival evolution converts this phase-squeezed state into a number-squeezed state, enabling population-difference readout. The resulting sequence provides a route to quantum-enhanced interferometry using the intrinsic fractional-revival dynamics of a ring together with controlled interaction and phase operations.

</details>


### [189] [High-fidelity and robust controlled-Z gates for superconducting transmon qubits. I. Intrinsic errors and decoherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30056)
*Chia-Hsien Huang,Hsi-Sheng Goan*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出面向直接耦合、频率可调transmon之间受控Z（controlled-Z, CZ）门的最优控制（optimal control）框架，兼顾哈密顿量内禀误差与退相干机制，得到12 ns、内禀误差约2×10^-5的CZ门，并给出退相位甜点与抗噪运行区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 容错量子计算（fault-tolerant quantum computing）要求量子门兼具短时长、高保真度与抗噪性；随着量子处理器规模扩大，逐门穷举表征与频繁重校准（recalibration）不再可行，因此鲁棒性成为关键需求。

Method: 构建系统的优化控制框架，显式纳入内禀哈密顿量误差及能量弛豫（energy relaxation）与退相位（dephasing）等实际退相干机制，采用平滑的单次通量脉冲（single-shot flux pulses）；并建立经已发表实验数据校准的退相位噪声模型，涵盖室温控制电子学的快退相位与1/f磁通噪声的慢退相位。

Result: 获得12 ns的CZ门，接近11.6 ns的理论速度极限，内禀误差压低至2×10^-5量级；基于噪声模型识别出抑制退相位噪声敏感度的退相位甜点（dephasing sweet spots），并利用transmon不对称性作为设计参数进入不同的抗噪运行区间。

Conclusion: 该框架在实验上可实施，可在降低表征与重校准开销的同时平衡门速度、保真度与噪声鲁棒性。

Abstract: Fault-tolerant quantum computing requires quantum gates that combine short duration, high fidelity, and robustness to noise. As quantum processors scale, robustness becomes increasingly important because exhaustive characterization and frequent recalibration of individual gates become impractical. We present a comprehensive optimal-control framework for controlled-Z (CZ) gates between directly coupled, frequency-tunable transmons that systematically accounts for intrinsic Hamiltonian errors and realistic decoherence mechanisms, including energy relaxation and dephasing. Using smooth, single-shot flux pulses, we obtain 12-ns CZ gates---close to the theoretical speed limit of 11.6 ns---with intrinsic errors suppressed to the $2\times10^{-5}$ level. We construct dephasing noise models, calibrated against published experimental measurements, that capture fast dephasing from room-temperature control electronics and slow dephasing from $1/f$ flux noise. Using these models, we identify dephasing sweet spots that suppress sensitivity to dephasing noise. We further exploit transmon asymmetry as a design parameter to access distinct noise-resilient operating regimes. These results establish an experimentally applicable framework for balancing gate speed, fidelity, and noise robustness while reducing characterization and recalibration overhead.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [190] [Algebraic criteria for complete relaxation in autonomous and periodically driven open quantum systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29084)
*Paul Menczel,Kay Brandner*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文给出开放量子系统（open quantum system）动力学收敛到与初态无关的唯一长时态的充分判据，称为完全弛豫（complete relaxation），并基于有限维希尔伯特空间上的GKSL主方程（GKSL master equation）以初等数学论证统一处理自治、周期驱动及非周期驱动情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于为开放量子系统长时行为提供可验证的收敛性保证，即判定动力学是否收敛到唯一且与初始条件无关的长时态，并统一、推广已有分散的弛豫判据。

Method: 仅使用初等数学论证，针对有限维希尔伯特空间上的GKSL主方程推导充分判据：自治系统情形以林德布拉德算符（Lindblad operators）与哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）的代数条件表述；周期驱动系统情形则结合类似代数条件与含时GKSL生成元（GKSL generator）的局部连续性要求。

Result: 对自治系统，重新得到此前已有的三条弛豫判据；对周期驱动系统，获得一条新判据，并由此蕴含文献[J. Phys. A 52, 43LT01 (2019)]中较早提出的完全弛豫判据。此外还讨论了对周期调制经典主方程（classical master equation）以及无周期性约束的驱动系统的推广意义。

Conclusion: 该工作以统一且初等的框架刻画开放量子系统的完全弛豫，兼顾自治与驱动两类情形，并将新旧判据纳入同一体系，同时对经典主方程与非周期驱动系统的扩展给出启示。

Abstract: We derive sufficient criteria that guarantee convergence of open quantum system dynamics to a unique long-time state independent of the initial conditions. We refer to this behavior as complete relaxation. Focusing on systems described by Gorini-Kossakowski-Sudarshan-Lindblad (GKSL) master equations on finite-dimensional Hilbert spaces, our approach uses only elementary mathematical arguments. For autonomous systems, we recover three previously derived relaxation criteria, which can be expressed as algebraic conditions on the Lindblad operators and the Hamiltonian entering the GKSL equation. For periodically driven systems, we obtain a new criterion involving a similar algebraic condition and a local continuity requirement on the time-dependent GKSL generator. This result implies an earlier criterion for complete relaxation formulated in Ref. [J. Phys. A 52, 43LT01 (2019)]. We also discuss implications of our approach for periodically modulated classical master equations, as well as extensions to driven systems without a periodicity constraint.

</details>


### [191] [Stochastic resonance in time-delayed bistable active particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29601)
*Antonio A. Valido,Mattia Coccolo,Miguel A. F. Sanjuán*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究了欠阻尼活性粒子（active particle，Ornstein–Uhlenbeck 噪声）在含时滞反馈（time-delayed feedback）的双稳势中、并受附加白噪声驱动时的随机共振（stochastic resonance）。作者在微扰框架下给出了逃逸率（escape rate）与谱功率放大（spectral power amplification）的解析表达，并与数值模拟定性吻合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往关于有色噪声驱动过阻尼被动系统的随机共振结果，难以刻画惯性、活性噪声与非平衡条件共同作用的场景；本文旨在揭示惯性（inertia）、活性（activity）、环境涨落与时滞反馈在非平衡系统随机共振中的联合作用。

Method: 在活性噪声主导的双稳区、且时滞与活性噪声关联时间足够短的条件下，建立了不依赖细致平衡（detailed balance）的微扰理论，在线性响应理论（linear-response theory）框架下推导逃逸率与谱功率放大的解析式，并与直接数值模拟对比验证。

Result: 适度增大活性噪声关联时间可增强随机共振响应，这与过阻尼被动系统中有色噪声结果的结论显著不同；附加白噪声削弱共振响应，并使最优活性噪声强度向低值移动；时滞反馈改变了逃逸率与驱动频率的匹配条件，但保留共振机制；超出微扰区时，有限记忆效应（finite-memory effects）使共振峰展宽且振幅显著下降。

Conclusion: 结果为非平衡系统中惯性、活性、环境涨落与时滞反馈对随机共振的协同作用提供了新认识，并指出活性噪声关联时间的非单调增强效应与有限记忆区的共振退化特征。

Abstract: We investigate analytically and numerically stochastic resonance in an underdamped active (Ornstein--Uhlenbeck) particle confined in a bistable potential with time-delayed feedback and subjected to an additional (non-thermal) white-noise source. Restricting the analysis to the active-noise-dominated bistable regime, with sufficiently short delay and active-noise correlation times, we develop a perturbative theory that yields analytical expressions for both the escape rate and the spectral power amplification within the framework of linear-response theory, without invoking detailed balance. The analytical predictions are in qualitative agreement with direct numerical simulations throughout the perturbative regime. We show that a moderate increase in the active-noise correlation time can enhance the stochastic-resonance response, in marked contrast to previous results for overdamped passive systems driven by colored noise. Conversely, the addition of white noise weakens the resonant response and shifts its optimum toward lower active-noise intensities. Time-delayed feedback further alters the matching condition between the escape rate and the driving frequency while preserving the resonant mechanism. Beyond the perturbative regime, we find that finite-memory effects progressively broaden the resonance profile and substantially decrease its amplitude. Overall, these results provide new insight into the combined role of inertia, activity, environmental fluctuations, and time delayed feedback in stochastic resonance in nonequilibrium systems.

</details>


### [192] [Splitting Probability in Monitored Quantum Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29547)
*Prashant Singh,Sabine Tornow,David A Kessler,Eli Barkai*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文构建了频闪投影测量下双目标连续时间量子行走（continuous-time quantum walk）首次探测振幅的更新框架，给出分裂概率（splitting probabilities）的精确表达，并揭示暗态导致遍历性破缺（ergodicity breaking）引起的概率不连续跳变，同时在一维紧束缚链上刻画了临界采样周期下的普适行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多次投影测量与量子行走结合会产生与幺正演化截然不同的探测统计，双目标竞争场景下的首次探测规律及采样周期、初态结构的影响尚缺乏统一描述，尤其缺乏对分裂概率精确解与测量诱导特殊效应的系统分析。

Method: 将连续时间量子行走以周期 τ 频闪式地施加于两个不同目标上的投影测量，构造各目标首次探测振幅（first-detection amplitude）的一般更新（renewal）框架并导出分裂概率；对具反射对称性（reflection symmetry）的哈密顿量，把双目标首次探测振幅表示为单目标振幅的干涉；并以最近邻跃迁的一维紧束缚（tight-binding）晶格链为示例进行解析与数值研究。

Result: 在反射对称情形下获得分裂概率的精确表达式，并给出因暗态（dark states）相关遍历性破缺而出现逐点不连续跳变的特殊采样时刻。对一维链，存在临界采样周期 τ_c = 2π/ΔE（ΔE 为能量带宽）：当 0<τ<τ_c 且初态位置不与目标重合时，大尺寸体系的分裂概率趋于与初位置无关的 1/2；当 τ≥τ_c 时，分裂概率偏离 1/2 并随初始位置振荡，振荡波数为 Q = 2 sin^{-1}(rτ_c/τ)，r 为正整数。此外分析了相干初态各分量的相对相位与空间间隔的影响，以及有限测量次数对分裂概率的修正与特殊采样时刻共振的展宽。

Conclusion: 频闪投影测量下的量子行走首次探测统计可由统一的更新与干涉框架精确描述，采样周期相对于 τ_c 的大小决定分裂概率是否趋于对称均分，并可通过初态相位、空间结构与测量次数加以调控；暗态导致的遍历性破缺是特殊采样时刻不连续跳变的根源。

Abstract: We consider a continuous-time quantum walk stroboscopically subjected to projective measurements on two distinct targets with sampling time period $τ$. We construct a general renewal framework for the first-detection amplitude at each target, from which we obtain the associated splitting probabilities. For Hamiltonians with reflection symmetry, we further show that each dual-target first-detection amplitude can be viewed as an interference effect between a pair of single-target first-detection amplitudes. This formulation yields exact expressions for the splitting probabilities and gives special sampling times at which they undergo pointwise discontinuous jumps due to a form of ergodicity breaking associated with dark states. As an illustrative example, we apply the framework to a one-dimensional tight-binding lattice chain with nearest-neighbor hopping, and study the splitting probabilities as functions of the initial position and sampling time. Below a critical sampling time, $0<τ< τ_c$ where $τ_c = 2 π/ ΔE$ with $ΔE$ being the energy bandwidth, the splitting probabilities for large system sizes become equal to $1/2$, independent of the initial position, as long as this position does not coincide with target sites. Above the critical sampling time, $τ\geq τ_c$, the splitting probabilities deviate from $1/2$ and develop oscillations as a function of initial position. The wavenumber associated with these oscillations is $Q=2\sin^{-1}(rτ_c/τ)$, where $r$ is a positive integer. We also explore the effects of relative phase and spatial separation between different components of a coherent initial state on the splitting probabilities. Finally, we study how a finite number of measurements modifies the splitting probabilities and broadens the resonances at special sampling times.

</details>


### [193] [Energy-fed commitment: a minimal model of random selection followed by reinforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29670)
*Haidar Issa Abbas*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出一个单耦合常数的唯象随机模型，刻画对称性破缺后“选择”如何逐步变得难以逆转：回复扰动所释放的能量按比例强化已选分支，导致承诺（commitment）随驱动方式呈几何、线性或次线性增长，并使反转受到指数抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称不稳定性（symmetric instability）被跨越时，涨落需在若干等价分支中择一；核心问题是何种机制使这一选择随时间变得越来越难以撤销。作者旨在构建最小模型以定量刻画该“选择固化”过程。

Method: 采用含一个耦合常数的唯象闭合（phenomenological closure）随机模型，设定“能量强化的是已选分支而非选择分支”为明示的不推导假设；通过精确首达（first-passage）计算与随机模拟对比，并在位移控制、能量控制与力控制三种驱动协议下分析承诺增长。

Result: 承诺增长在位移控制下为几何式、能量控制下为线性、力控制下为次线性；强化使反转为指数抑制，超过有限承诺阈值后替代极小值消失，反转成为对不可逆阈值（irreversibility threshold）的稀有穿越。无界强化下选择以正概率永久保留；饱和强化下保留时间有限，且寿命随饱和水平指数增长。首达计算与模拟一致。

Conclusion: 模型后果是定量的，模拟可验证闭合与分析但无法检验其物理假设；作者提出可检验该假设的实验：将吸收能量与扰动次数分离的协议，以及具有非零维持热（housekeeping heat）、使热与吸收功不相等的驱动稳态。

Abstract: When a symmetric instability is crossed, fluctuations choose one of several equivalent branches. We study a minimal stochastic model of what can then make that choice progressively harder to undo. After selection, the stiffness of the selected state grows in proportion to the energy released each time the structure recovers from a perturbation; in this conservative model that energy equals, up to small and explicitly computed terms, the work absorbed from the perturbing agent. The model is a phenomenological closure with one coupling constant. That energy reinforces a branch already chosen, rather than choosing it, is an assumption of the model, which we state and do not derive. Its consequences are quantitative. The growth of commitment depends on how the structure is driven: geometric under displacement control, linear under energy control and sublinear under force control, so that committed states absorb more work per cycle under displacement control and less under force control. Reinforcement suppresses reversal exponentially; beyond a finite commitment the alternative minimum disappears and reversals become rare crossings of an irreversibility threshold. With unbounded reinforcement a choice is retained forever with positive probability under stated conditions; with saturating reinforcement retention is finite, with a lifetime that grows exponentially with the saturation level. Exact first-passage calculations agree with stochastic simulations; these verify the closure and its analysis but cannot test the physical postulate. We identify the experiments that could: protocols that separate absorbed energy from the number of perturbations, and driven steady states with nonzero housekeeping heat, in which heat and absorbed work differ.

</details>


### [194] [Morphological instability and negative geometric friction from contact with active and driven particles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29902)
*Forouh Maleki,Christian Maes*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 过阻尼粒子（overdamped particles）在可变形基底（deformable substrate）上响应度规微扰（metric perturbations）时产生几何摩擦（geometric friction）；非平衡动力学可使该摩擦转为负值，从而软化基底刚度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究粒子对可变形基底的度规微扰响应如何产生有效摩擦，以及非平衡效应能否改变其符号与基底力学性质。

Method: 以局部曲率半径（local curvature radius）为动力学变量的“呼吸圆（breathing circle）”作为主要模型，分析非平衡动力学将几何微扰与动力学效应耦合的方式；并以平衡情形作对照。

Result: 在平衡态下，摩擦退化为普通势响应（ordinary potential response）并保持正值；非平衡动力学可将几何摩擦翻转为负摩擦，软化基底刚性（rigidity），并有利于内陷（invaginations）与外凸（evaginations）。

Conclusion: 几何响应（geometric response）构成主动物质（active matter）与驱动物质（driven matter）重塑其环境的一条新途径。

Abstract: Overdamped particles on a deformable substrate induce a geometric friction through their response to metric perturbations. Nonequilibrium dynamics couples such a perturbation to kinetic effects, which can flip that friction negative, softening the substrate's rigidity and favoring invaginations and evaginations. A breathing circle, with local curvature radius as dynamical variable, is the main example. At equilibrium the friction reduces to an ordinary potential response and stays positive. Geometric response emerges as a new route by which active and driven matter reshape their environment.

</details>


### [195] [Stockmayer Fluid with a Shifted Dipole. III. Ion Solvation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30094)
*Ananya Venkatachalam,Pierre J. Walker,Samuel Varner,Bilin Zhuang,Zhen-Gang Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作通过分子动力学模拟揭示：Stockmayer粒子的偶极位移（dipole shift）位置（而非仅偶极强度）是调控离子溶剂化结构、溶剂化自由能与离子对（ion pairing）的独立几何控制参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Stockmayer流体中的离子溶剂化已研究较充分，但将点偶极（point dipole）偏离粒子中心的简单几何改动如何影响离子-溶剂结构与溶剂化热力学尚不清楚。

Method: 采用分子动力学模拟（molecular dynamics simulations），系统改变偶极位移量与偶极强度，分析中心离子周围的径向与取向溶剂结构、溶剂对阴阳离子的区分能力，以及溶剂介导的离子-离子有效相互作用。

Result: 偶极居中时阴阳离子对局部溶剂环境的偏好相等；偶极位移打破局部静电场的先后对称性（fore-aft symmetry），使偶极更倾向指向阴离子而较少远离阳离子。该结构不对称传递至溶剂化自由能：增大偶极位移使阴离子溶剂化更有利、阳离子溶剂化整体更不利；并增强短程离子-离子接触的溶剂屏蔽而削弱长程屏蔽，使溶剂分隔构型（solvent-separated configurations）相对更有利，而增大偶极强度则呈相反趋势。

Conclusion: 上述效应无法仅用体相介电屏蔽（bulk dielectric screening）解释，表明分子电荷几何在单离子溶剂化与溶剂介导的离子-离子相互作用中起重要作用；偶极位置与强度一样，可作为一般极性流体中离子特异性溶剂化与离子配对的几何调控手段。

Abstract: Though ion solvation is well understood for the standard Stockmayer fluid, the effect of displacing the point dipole of a Stockmayer particle, a simple geometric modification, on ion-solvent structuring and solvation thermodynamics remains less understood. We use molecular dynamics simulations to describe how a dipole shift affects radial and orientational solvent structure around centered ions, how strongly the solvent distinguishes cations and anions, and how the solvent mediates ion-ion pairing. We show that, with a centered dipole, cations and anions have equal preferences for the local solvent environment. Shifting the dipole breaks the fore-aft symmetry of the local electrostatic field, leading to a stronger preference for a Stockmayer particle's dipole to orient towards an anion and a weaker preference to orient away from a cation. These structural asymmetries propagate to the solvation free energies: increasing dipole displacement makes anion solvation more favorable, while cation solvation becomes less favorable overall across dipole strengths. The same asymmetry also manifests in the effective ion-ion interaction-increasing the dipole shift reorganizes the local solvation environment, enhancing solvent shielding of short-range cation-anion contact while weakening screening at longer range. As a result, solvent-separated configurations become comparatively more favorable, whereas increasing dipole strength produces the opposite trend. Such effects cannot be explained by bulk dielectric screening alone, indicating that molecular charge geometry plays an important role in both single-ion solvation and solvent-mediated ion-ion interactions.
