This work demonstrates that dipole location, not only magnitude, provides a geometric control on ion-specific solvation and ion pairing in general polar fluids.

</details>


<div id='nucl-th'></div>

# nucl-th [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Relative geometry of nuclear mean fields through differential forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30046)
*Rafael C. R. de Lima*

Main category: nucl-th

TL;DR: 论文提出基于微分形式（differential forms）的成对不变量（pair invariant）与三重不变量（triple invariant），用于度量并定位核平均场中质子与中子密度局部差异，并在磁化 Wigner-Seitz 解、Hartree-Fock 组态、碰撞与核意面（nuclear pasta）中检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质子与中子密度可在具有相近多极矩（multipole moments）的同时在局部存在显著差异，因此需要不依赖特定原点和坐标轴的局部化度量来描述这种差异。

Method: 用微分形式（differential forms）构造成对不变量，以两密度梯度的模长加权度量其夹角，并用余面积公式（coarea formula）将其贡献分解到等值面（level surfaces）上；三重不变量被精确分解为加权成对项与沿等值面交线度量第三场变化的方向项。

Result: 成对不变量能区分质子四极矩相近的单元解，在 ⁴⁴Mg 中比 ²⁴Mg 大 253 倍，网格细化到 0.6 fm 时变化不超过 1.3%；方向因子可区分三轴与长椭球形组态；碰撞中三重不变量在首次低密度连接前上升 7.3 倍，而加权成对项变化不超过 3%；核意面中平板与网络构型的三重不变量在细网格上相差约 370 倍。

Conclusion: 这些比较无需优选原点或坐标轴；非零的分辨三重不变量可排除存在共同对称轴，但零值不能证明存在对称轴；核意面结果仍需进一步的收敛性研究。

Abstract: Proton and neutron densities can have similar multipole moments while differing locally. We use differential forms to measure and locate such differences between nuclear mean fields. A pair invariant measures the angle between two density gradients, weighted by their magnitudes, and the coarea formula resolves its contribution over level surfaces. A triple invariant factorizes exactly into a weighted pair term and a directional term that measures the variation of a third field along the level-surface intersections. We test these quantities in 98 magnetized Wigner-Seitz solutions, five static Hartree-Fock configurations, an illustrative $^{16}$O + $^{34}$Mg collision, and periodic nuclear pasta (a slab and a connected network). The pair invariant distinguishes cell solutions with comparable proton quadrupole moments and is 253 times larger in $^{34}$Mg than in $^{24}$Mg; refinement to 0.6 fm changes it by at most 1.3% in the four configurations recomputed. The directional factor separates the sampled triaxial configurations from the prolate ones. In the collision, it accounts for the 7.3-fold rise of the triple before the first recorded low-density connection, while the weighted pair term changes by at most 3%. In nuclear pasta, slab and network configurations have similar integrated proton-neutron misalignment but different distributions over density levels; their triple invariants differ by a factor of about 370 on the finer grid, mainly due to the variation of the Coulomb potential along joint density levels. These comparisons require no preferred origin or axis. A resolved nonzero triple excludes a common symmetry axis, but zero does not establish one. The pasta results require further convergence studies.

</details>


<div id='hep-ex'></div>

# hep-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [197] [Cross Section Measurements and Searches for New Structures in $e^+e^-$ Annihilations at BESIII](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29371)
*Hao Liu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: BESIII合作组在1.84–4.95 GeV质心能量范围采集了大量数据，本文综述其近期在粲偶素截面与谱学、XYZ粒子新结构搜寻以及开粲阈值以上超子产生方面的成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该能量区间数据兼具低本底与宽能区覆盖的优势，为粲偶素（charmonia）截面测量与谱学研究提供了理想条件，因而有必要系统梳理相关进展。

Method: 基于BESIII在质心能量（center-of-mass energy）1.84至4.95 GeV采集的大统计量数据样本，对粲偶素截面与谱学以及相关强子产生过程进行综述性分析。

Result: 综述涵盖XYZ粒子（XYZ particles）新结构的搜寻成果，以及开粲阈值（open-charm threshold）以上超子（hyperon）产生的相关研究结果。

Conclusion: BESIII数据在低本底、宽能区条件下支撑了粲偶素谱学与奇特态（exotic states）研究，相关综述成果体现了该能区实验研究的近期进展。

Abstract: The BESIII collaboration has collected large data samples in the center-of-mass energy range from 1.84 to 4.95 GeV. These data provide a low-background environment and wide energy range for studying the cross sections and the spectroscopy of charmonia. In this article, we review the recent achievements by the BESIII collaboration, including for searching new structures of $XYZ$ particles and the hyperonic production over open-charm threshold.

</details>


### [198] [Study of $Λ$ $CP$ Asymmetry Parameter and Electric Dipole Moment at the Super Tau-Charm Facility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.29819)
*Mingyu Yu,Jianyu Zhang,Jinlin Fu,Xingtao Huang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 在超级陶粲装置（STCF）上利用量子纠缠（quantum entanglement）的 ΛΛ̄ 对的全角分布，可将 CP 不对称参数 A_CP 灵敏度推进至 10^{-4} 量级、Λ 电偶极矩（EDM）探测下限达 10^{-20} e·cm。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找超出标准模型（beyond the Standard Model）的新 CP 破坏（CP violation）机制是解释宇宙物质—反物质不对称的关键，本工作旨在评估未来 STCF 在此方向上的物理潜力。

Method: 通过分析 J/ψ 衰变产生的量子纠缠 ΛΛ̄ 对的全角分布（full angular distribution），基于 STCF 预期 3.4×10^{12} 个 J/ψ 事例，对 CP 不对称参数 A_CP^Λ 与 Λ 电偶极矩进行初步灵敏度估计。

Result: 预期 A_CP^Λ 的灵敏度可达 10^{-4} 水平，Λ 电偶极矩可探测至 10^{-20} e·cm 量级。

Conclusion: 结果强烈表明 STCF 将为搜寻新 CP 破坏机制提供前所未有的机会，并有望达到世界领先（world-leading）的测量精度。

Abstract: Searching for new mechanisms of $CP$ violation beyond the Standard Model is essential to explain the observed matter-antimatter asymmetry of the universe. By analyzing the full angular distribution of the quantum-entangled $Λ\barΛ$ pairs from $J/ψ$ decays, the preliminary sensitivity of $CP$ asymmetry parameter $A_{CP}$ and Electric Dipole Moment of $Λ$ is studied at the future Super Tau-Charm Facility (STCF). Based on $3.4 \times 10^{12}$ $J/ψ$ events at STCF, the projected sensitivity for $A_{CP}^Λ$ reaches the $10^{-4}$ level, while the $Λ$ EDM can be probed at $10^{-20}$ $e$ cm. These results highly indicate that STCF will provide an unprecedented opportunity to search for new mechanisms of $CP$ violation with an expected world-leading precision.

</details>
